### [1] [Care, Human Enfeeblement, and the Existential Implications of AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14520)
*B. Scot Rousse*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文将AI引发的"人类衰弱"风险重新解释为关怀能力的侵蚀，主张以关怀为核心设计AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stuart Russell将"人类衰弱"列为先进AI的长期社会风险，但既有理解多停留在认知外包与知识丧失层面，作者希望给出更深的解释并提供设计出路。

Method: 概念与哲学分析：综合Brian Christian、Alison Gopnik、David Spivak关于关怀与照护的近期工作，并接续Winograd与Flores的本体论设计传统；将关怀界定为一组可培养的技能星座，使我们卷入世界、彼此关联，并让生活值得过。

Result: 指出真正的衰弱风险不是认知卸载，而是与世界脱钩式的"不投入"（disengagement），其根源在于关怀能力的侵蚀；由此提出以支持人类关怀能力为目标的AI设计取向，回应Hassabis等人关于后AGI时代"意义与目的"之问。

Conclusion: 关怀应成为评估与设计先进AI的核心维度；以AI支撑而非替代人的关怀能力，可缓解衰弱风险并回应后AGI的意义危机。

Abstract: Stuart Russell has named "human enfeeblement" as a long-term societal risk of advanced AI. This paper gives a new interpretation of the enfeeblement danger by interpreting it in light of recent work by Brian Christian, Alison Gopnik, and David Spivak on care and caregiving, and connecting it with the ontological design tradition of Terry Winograd and Fernando Flores. The enfeeblement that threatens is not merely a matter of cognitive off-loading and loss of knowledge, but a form of disengagement with the world which I explain in terms of an erosion of our capacities to care. This paper articulates a conception of care as a constellation of cultivable skills that involve us in the world and with each other, while making life worth living. Designing AI to support our capacities to care addresses the worry recently raised by Demis Hassabis and others regarding the questions of "meaning and purpose" that are looming in a post-AGI world.

</details>


### [2] [Probe Before You Preach: Conformal Ambiguity Sets and Neuro-Symbolic Counterfactual Probing for Diagnosing Algebraic Misconceptions in Middle School Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14557)
*Caihong Xu,Xiangyu Yu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文指出同一错误答案可能由不同的数学迷思规则产生，单题作答无法唯一诊断；作者形式化了该"代数碰撞"并推导条件贝叶斯误差界，提出MACMS与Sym-EIG两种方法，并对公开数据集进行审计，强调不主张诊断或教学收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次作答的正确答案/错误答案具有歧义性：不同迷思规则可产生相同错误答案，导致基于单一响应的诊断不可识别。同时作者希望厘清"模型内部生成推理"是否真正提供学生特异性信息。

Method: 1) 形式化"代数碰撞"并推导条件贝叶斯误差界；2) 在显式条件独立假设下论证内部生成推理不超出其输入所含学生特异信息；3) 提出Mondrian Adaptive Conformal Misconception Sets（MACMS），在可交换性下提供课程分组的边缘覆盖保证；4) 提出Symbolic-Verified Counterfactual Micro-Probing（Sym-EIG），搜索能使候选规则输出分离的探测题；5) 对本地公开Eedi图进行审计。

Result: 审计显示2,258个有链接的构念中有2,118个与至少两个迷思ID相关联；但构念级关联本身不能确立"同答案碰撞"。其他项目性能表因脚本直接赋值数值而需重新计算。未报告诊断性能或课堂学习收益。

Conclusion: MACMS仅提供课程分组的边缘覆盖，不保证逐学生正确性；Sym-EIG的可识别性依赖候选集与响应模型。作者不作诊断性能或教学效果主张，并提出未来开展课堂研究进行验证。

Abstract: Distinct mathematical misconception rules can produce the same incorrect answer, making diagnosis from a single response ambiguous. We formalize this algebraic collision and derive a conditional Bayes error bound; internally generated reasoning does not add student-specific information beyond its inputs under an explicit conditional-independence assumption. We propose Mondrian Adaptive Conformal Misconception Sets (MACMS) and Symbolic-Verified Counterfactual Micro-Probing (Sym-EIG). MACMS has curriculum-group marginal coverage under exchangeability, not a per-student correctness guarantee. Sym-EIG searches for probes whose outputs separate modeled candidate rules; identification additionally depends on the candidate set and response model. An audit of a local public Eedi graph finds 2,118 of 2,258 linked constructs associated with at least two misconception IDs; construct-level associations alone do not establish same-answer collisions. Other project performance tables require recomputation because their script assigns numerical results directly. No diagnostic performance or classroom learning benefit is claimed here. A classroom study is proposed for future validation.

</details>


### [3] [Who (or what) are they prompting? How CS students conceptualize and use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14798)
*Nikitha Donekal Chandrashekar,Margaret Ellis,Sehrish Basir Nizamani,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究在CS1后续课程中引入LLM教学，通过期末开放式调查（N=283），用SOLO分类法将学生对LLM工作原理的理解分为四个层级，并从"人格类型"与"关系角色"两个维度编码其隐喻式概念化，发现学生多将LLM视为人类助手、偏好上下文丰富的提示方式，并初步揭示了概念化、机制理解与提示行为之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在计算机课程中被学生广泛使用，但计算机教育研究尚未充分考察教学与LLM的纳入如何塑造学生对LLM的概念化与理解，以及这些因素与后续使用行为的关系；已有工作多关注提示策略与AI素养，而"概念化—机制理解—提示行为"三者关系仍缺乏探讨。

Method: 选取一门介绍CS各子领域的CS1后续课程并改造为教授LLM且允许使用LLM；实施期末调查，开放式题目收集学生对LLM的表征与工作原理解释，封闭式题目测量提示风格与提示数量；对N=283名同意并提供可编码回答的学生进行定性编码，基于SOLO分类法将机制理解划为四个有序知识层级，并沿人格类型与关系角色两个维度编码隐喻式概念化。

Result: 学生最常将LLM概念化为人类助手，上下文丰富的提示风格为首选；探索性分析识别出概念化、机制理解与使用行为等构念之间的关联，表明对系统的理解与其使用方式相关。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了学生LLM隐喻式表征的二维刻画框架，并初步展示机制理解水平与提示行为之间的联系，为计算教育中LLM的教学设计与AI素养培养提供实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by students in computing courses. Yet computing education research has not fully examined how teaching and inclusion of LLMs shape students' conceptualization and understanding of LLMs, and how these factors relate to their subsequent use. Although prior work has examined prompting strategies and AI literacy, the relationships among students' conceptualizations of LLMs, their understanding of LLM mechanisms, and their prompting behavior remain underexplored. We studied these relationships in a post CS1 course that introduces students to the various CS subfields. We adapted the course to teach and allow the usage of LLMs. We administered an end-of-course survey, using open-ended items to elicit students' representations of LLMs and their explanations of how LLMs work, alongside closed-response measures of prompting style and prompt count (N=283 students consented and provided codable responses). Using qualitative coding, we categorized students' understanding of how LLMs work into four ordinal knowledge levels based on the Structure of Observed Learning Outcomes (SOLO) taxonomy and coded their metaphoric conceptualizations along two dimensions: persona type and relational role. Our results show that students most often conceptualized LLMs as human assistants, and context-rich prompting was the preferred style. The study contributes a two-dimensional characterization of students' LLM metaphoric representation and exploratory analyses also identified associations among these constructs showing how understanding these systems relates to their usage.

</details>


### [4] [The hidden solar cost of urban unfamiliarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14959)
*Lijia Liu,Yimeng Song*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建香港岛时间分辨三维步行模型，量化"隐藏日晒成本"，发现短距离绕行即可通过遮阴/室内路径显著降低直射日晒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不熟悉环境的行人在选路时只走最短路线，忽略附近遮阴、室内通道与多层连接，从而承受本可避免的直射日晒；作者希望把这种"隐藏的日晒成本"显式化并量化。

Method: 构建香港岛时间特定三维模型，融合室内外步行网络、建筑几何、LiDAR 植被制图与太阳位置；对 500 条行程，在 4 个日间时刻与 5 个额外距离上限下，比较最短路径与遮阴知情路径，并做户外遮阴/室内保护与多层连接的受控消融对比。

Result: 正午、额外距离上限 10% 时，遮阴路径使模型直射日晒较最短路径下降 13.5%，平均路径长度仅增 2.85%；5% 上限可获 10% 情形下 77% 的降幅。受控对比中户外遮阴信息贡献 86% 降幅、室内保护 14%；移除多层连接后，500 条行程仅 191 条仍在原长度限内可行。

Conclusion: 现有防护未进入路径决策信息时存在可避免的日晒成本；让受保护路线"可见"并保留其连通性，可在不显著绕行的情况下降低日晒暴露。

Abstract: Unfamiliar walkers may follow the shortest route and miss nearby shade, indoor passages and multilevel connections. We define the resulting hidden solar cost as the direct-sun exposure avoidable through shade-informed routing without substantial detours. We developed a time-specific three-dimensional model of Hong Kong Island that combines indoor and outdoor walking networks with building geometry, vegetation mapped by light detection and ranging (LiDAR), and solar position. For 500 modelled journeys, we compared shortest and shade-informed routes at four daytime snapshots and five extra-distance limits. At noon, shade-informed routes reduced modelled direct-sun exposure by 13.5% relative to shortest routes under a 10% extra-distance limit. Route length increased by 2.85% on average. A 5% extra-distance limit delivered 77% of the reduction obtained at 10%. In a controlled comparison, outdoor shade information accounted for 86% of the reduction and indoor-protection information for 14%. Removing multilevel connections left only 191 of 500 journeys feasible within the original route-length limit. These route comparisons identify an avoidable solar cost when existing protection is missing from the information used to choose a route. Making protected routes visible and preserving their connections can reduce sun exposure without large detours.

</details>


### [5] [Taxonomizing Synthetic Data for Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15489)
*Ignacio Cofone,Katherine J. Strandburg,Nicholas Tilmes*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文在Gal与Lynskey关于合成数据将重塑信息隐私、市场竞争与数据质量的观点基础上，提出"背景知识"与"真值假设"才是理解合成数据法律与政策影响的关键维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常将合成数据与"真实"收集数据完全对立，忽视不同类型合成数据带来的差异性风险；而风险不仅取决于合成数据与原始数据的关联程度，更取决于生成过程中所依赖的背景知识与真值假设。

Method: 对合成数据进行分类学重构：(1)转换数据——修改收集数据以保留特定统计特性；(2)增强数据——借助假设提升数据集对真值的保真度；(3)模拟数据——几乎完全依赖背景知识与真值假设。并据此分析各类别的隐私、偏见与竞争影响。

Result: 没有任何一类合成数据能防止基于群体推断的隐私伤害；增强与模拟数据可能在使数据集"看起来更完整"的同时放大偏见；合成数据也很少能帮助较小竞争者克服在位者的数据优势。

Conclusion: 政策制定者在激励、强制或抑制合成数据使用时，应重点考量生成合成数据所用真值假设的有效性。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly important in data usage and AI design, creating novel legal and policy dilemmas. All too often, discussions of synthetic data treat it as entirely distinct from "real," collected data, overlooking the risks posed by different kinds and uses of synthetic data. We build on Michal Gal and Orla Lynskey's argument that synthetic data will transform information privacy, market competition, and data quality. While the risks posed by synthetic data depend on its connection to collected data, we argue that background knowledge and assumptions about ground truth used to create it are at least as important. We bring that focus to Gal and Lynskey's taxonomy of synthetic data, arguing that this focus is essential to grasp synthetic data's legal and policy implications. As such, we divide synthetic data into (1) transformed data, which modifies collected data to preserve certain statistical properties for an end use; (2) augmented data, which relies on assumptions to bolster a collected dataset's fidelity to the ground truth; and (3) simulated data, which relies almost entirely on background knowledge and ground-truth assumptions. Applying this taxonomy, we show that no category of synthetic data prevents privacy harms from group-based inferences, that augmented and simulated data can amplify bias while making a dataset look more complete, and that synthetic data rarely lets smaller competitors overcome incumbents' data advantages. As policymakers weigh whether to incentivize, mandate, or discourage the use of synthetic data, they should consider the validity of the ground-truth assumptions used in producing that data.

</details>


### [6] [Scaling public input on AI policy with pluralistic agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16028)
*Jacy Reese Anthis,Ken Burke,David Madras,Crystal Qian,Meredith Ringel Morris,Logan Cross,Caroline Pantofaru,Utsav Prabhu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出并测试"多元代理"(pluralistic agents)——代表个体进行审议、以对齐多元人类视角的AI代理；实验显示代理能可靠地把政策推向其所代表人格的偏好方向，但影响力受提示指令(如冗长度、合作度)左右；746人预注册研究中，参与者认为代理有用且公平，并在满意度与公平感上弥合了大部分"亲自参与—基线"差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI部署于广泛的应用领域、文化环境与利益相关者之中，安全负责的AI开发需要持续吸纳多元视角。然而人类无法直接参与所有讨论，尤其涉及需要专业训练的技术议题，以及依赖企业与政府难以共享的隐私信息的政策选择，因此需要一种可扩展的视角代表机制。

Method: 开发并测试pluralistic agents：代表个人进行审议的AI代理代表。开展实验考察其机会、风险与感知；并实施一项预注册研究，由746名人类参与者实际参与多元代理审议流程，比较"自身参与"、"多元代理"与"单一AI推理模型基线"三种条件。

Result: 代理能可靠地将政策推向其代表人格的偏好方向，但影响幅度对人格指令敏感——被指示更冗长或更不合作时影响力更大，说明AI辅助审议中的影响力会随参与者提示方式与系统设计而变化。参与者认为多元代理有用且公平，并优于单一AI模型基线；多数人仍偏好亲自参与，但多元代理在满意度上弥补了基线—直接参与差距的68%、公平性上弥补82%。

Conclusion: 多元代理可应用于许多无法直接讨论的现实场景，是有望扩展AI安全政策制定的工具；应将其定位为人类输入的"放大器"而非替代品，并鼓励谨慎探索该方法。

Abstract: AI is being deployed across a vast range of application domains, cultural environments, and human stakeholders. Safe and responsible AI development requires continuously accounting for diverse perspectives. We develop and test pluralistic agents, AI agent representatives that deliberate on behalf of individuals to align AI systems with diverse human perspectives. Pluralistic agents could help people contribute to many more discussions than they could directly participate in, on technical topics that would require training, and on policy choices that rely on private information that corporations and governments cannot readily share. Our experiments identify opportunities, risks, and perceptions of pluralistic agents. Agents were able to reliably shift policies in the direction of their represented persona's preferences, but the extent of each agent's influence was sensitive to the instructions from its persona, such as having more influence when instructed to be more verbose or less cooperative. This suggests that influence in AI-assisted deliberation could vary based on how participants prompt their agents and how the system is designed. In a preregistered study, 746 humans participated in the pluralistic agents process. Participants viewed pluralistic agents as useful and fair, preferring agents over the baseline of providing input to a single AI reasoning model. Most participants preferred participating themselves over either pluralistic agents or the baseline, but pluralistic agents recovered 68% of the baseline-direct gap in reported satisfaction and 82% in fairness. Because pluralistic agents can be used in many real-world contexts where direct discussion is not, they could be a promising tool to scale AI safety policy development. We encourage cautious exploration of this method, playing to its strengths as an amplifier of human input rather than a replacement.

</details>


### [7] [One Future Fits All: Text-to-image AI and the Narrowing of Global Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16089)
*Stephanie Diepeveen,Somnath Batabyal,Edward Ademolu,Peter Chonka,Gabriele de Seta,Iginio Gagliardone,Elisabeth Gunawan,Fabio Gygi,Yidnekachew Haile,Yenn Lee,Chipo Mapondera,Craig Ryder,Matti Pohjonen,Michiel Baas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过907张图像、15种语言、12个国家的协作探索性研究，发现Bing Create（DALL·E 3）对"当前与未来城市"的生成呈现高度趋同的视觉语法，剥离冲突、贫困与政治，复刻东方主义想象与单一现代化叙事，体现一种殖民/发展主义世界观的"平庸性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法偏见研究已较成熟，但技术变迁下偏见未必以相同形式再现；文本到图像生成AI是新场景，需考察其如何复制、重塑不平等，尤其是地方、语言与权力交织下的表征政治。

Method: 采用"合成探针"（synthetic probes）概念，对Microsoft Bing Create（基于DALL·E 3）进行迭代式协作探索性研究：覆盖15种语言、12个国家、共907个文本提示的图像输出，分析输入与输出在地方、语言与权力语境中的关联。

Result: 输出可能性范围"惊人的简单"：显性冲突、贫困与政治被剔除；地方多样性退化为东方主义式想象；未来图像统一为"文明使命/现代化叙事"——悬浮车、城市花园、太阳能板，统一的蓝绿色调构图。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的视觉输出反映并再生产一种简单却普遍、持久的殖民/发展主义世界观；其威胁不在复杂性或智能，而在"平庸性"——即表征可能性的系统性窄化：趋向他者友好、去政治化、发展饱和的视觉语法，抹平矛盾并封闭多元未来主义。

Abstract: Persistence of algorithmic bias is well established in scholarship, reflecting, reproducing and reshaping inequalities and injustices. Yet, as technology changes, we cannot assume that inequalities will be reproduced in the same or similar forms. This article explores the reproduction of bias in text-to-image generative AI, looking at the tool, Microsoft Bing Create, based on DALL E 3. It draws on results of a collaborative exploratory study of 907 image-based outputs of text-to-image generation in 15 languages and concerning 12 countries. We utilise the notion of synthetic probes (de Seta, 2024) to iteratively explore correlations of inputs and outputs within the context of place, language and power. We find striking simplicity in the scope of possibilities through which Bing Create considers the current and future city. Stripping out (explicit) indications of conflict, poverty and politics, we are left with images whose localised diversity harkens back to versions of Orientalist imaginaries. Images of the future emerge as one civilising mission or modernisation narrative, with all places headed to one future of hovercrafts, urban gardens and solar panels, encased in a composition of shades of blue and green. We argue that the visual outputs of this generative AI tool reflect and reproduce a simple but pervasive and persistent colonial/developmental worldview. We explore explanations for this emergent narrative and its implications, suggesting the threat of generative AI is not in its complexity or intelligence, but rather its banality. With this, we propose banality not as mere repetition, but the systematic narrowing of representational possibility: a convergence on tourist-friendly, depoliticised, development-saturated visual grammars that smooth contradiction and foreclose plural futurisms.

</details>


### [8] [From State of THE ART to Human Prediction Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16464)
*Conghao Wong,Ziqian Zou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文以交互艺术与人体轨迹预测算法为对象，从感知、交互、时序三个维度分析两个领域的结构与发展的共性，指出量化指标提升的同时对人类行为、未来意图与社会交互的考量反而减弱，并提出艺术启发式算法可从数据中挖掘有趣的行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI预测算法在量化指标上不断刷新SOTA，但对人类行为、未来意图与社会交互本身的考虑却呈递减趋势，被数字与度量所掩盖。作者希望用不同的分析视角重新审视该领域，为理解与后续研究提供新见解。

Method: 以交互艺术作品与人体轨迹预测算法为案例，进行跨领域的结构与发展的共性分析，围绕感知（perception）、交互（interaction）、时序（timing）三个方面，探讨人类直觉与艺术如何与AI算法建立关联。

Result: 发现特定受艺术启发的预测算法能够从数据中推断出有趣的人类行为模式，为理解人类活动、SOTA与AI算法之间的关系提供新的潜在途径。

Conclusion: 交互艺术与轨迹预测算法在结构与演进上具有共性；引入艺术视角有助于超越纯指标导向，重建算法对人类行为、意图与社会交互的关注，并可作为后续研究的分析框架。

Abstract: Human curiosity and longing for the future have driven human progress from ancient times to the present. With the advancement of AI technology, human prediction algorithms have also drawn increasing attention from researchers. However, as AI technology continues to advance, we observe a counterintuitive phenomenon. Take human trajectory prediction algorithms as an example. Although models' quantitative prediction performance has improved, yielding one state-of-the-art model after another, the consideration of human behaviors, future thoughts, and social interactions themselves presents a gradually diminishing trend in the construction of these prediction algorithms, buried beneath numbers and metrics. In this manuscript, taking interactive art and human trajectory prediction algorithms as examples, we attempt to adopt a different analytical perspective to analyze the structural and developmental commonalities between these two fields. By examining specific interactive artworks and prediction algorithms, we aim to explore how human intuitions and art are associated with AI algorithms through three aspects, including perception, interaction, and timing, thereby providing new insights for the understanding and further research in this domain. We also observe an interesting phenomenon that certain art-inspired prediction algorithms can actually deduce intriguing patterns of human behaviors from the data, offering new potential avenues for understanding the relationship between human activities, state of The Art, and AI algorithms.

</details>


### [9] [A Code-Level Audit of X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16800)
*Luka Bekavac,Simon Mayer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代码级审计方法，对X平台开源的推荐流水线做反向可达性遍历，发现线上服务路径上存在13个透明性死胡同，且决定内容过滤与排序的组件落入最不可检查的两档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《数字服务法》(DSA) 要求超大型在线平台披露推荐流水线信息，并向经审核的研究者开放非公开数据（可能包括推荐代码），但代码释出能否支撑实质性的第三方审计尚不清楚，需要实证检验。

Method: 提出代码级审计方法：对仓库做反向遍历（backwards-walk），从用户端时间线出发，将每个与决策相关的组件追溯到其上游生产者，检验其能否从实际服务的流水线可达，并按四档可检查性（inspectability）方案分类；将该方法应用于自愿开源的X平台推荐流水线。

Result: 在线上服务路径上识别出13个透明性死胡同（代码依赖但未释出的模型、服务、标签列表与配置值）；决定哪些帖子被过滤及如何排序的组件落入可检查性最低的两档，且随着2025年专有Grok模型并入审核、标注与排序而加剧；释出代码中还保留2025年流水线已不再触达的2023年代码，其中36处参数引用若无可达性检验会被误判为死胡同。

Conclusion: 仅释出代码不足以支撑有意义的生产推荐系统审计；需补充额外制品（模型、服务、配置与版本对齐信息等），并讨论其具体构成。

Abstract: The transparency regime of recommender systems on Very Large Online Platforms (VLOPs) is undergoing significant change under the EU Digital Services Act (DSA). The DSA requires platforms to disclose information about their recommendation pipelines and grants vetted researchers access to non-public data, which may extend to recommender code. However, it is not known to what extent such code releases enable substantive and meaningful third-party auditing of a production recommender system. We address this question by proposing a code-level audit methodology for recommender systems and applying it to the X platform, which has voluntarily open-sourced its recommender pipeline. Our methodology is a backwards-walk traversal of the repository, tracing each decision-relevant component from the user-facing timeline back to its upstream producers, checking that it is reachable from the served pipeline, and classifying each under a four-tier inspectability scheme. Applying this procedure, we identify 13 transparency dead-ends on the live serving path: models, services, label lists, and configuration values that the public code depends on but the release does not contain. We find that the components that decide which posts are filtered and how they are ranked fall into the two least inspectable tiers, a pattern that intensifies with the 2025 integration of the proprietary Grok language model into moderation, annotation, and ranking. We further show that the release keeps 2023 code that the 2025 pipeline no longer reaches, with 36 parameter references that would pass for dead-ends without a reachability check. Finally, we discuss which additional artifacts would be required for meaningful auditing.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond Curiosity: Habit and Organizational Learning Capability as Predictors of Shadow AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16931)
*Nolan Lovett,Mohan Yang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究以510名多国在职生成式AI用户为样本，检验“影子AI使用”究竟源于好奇心驱动的探索倾向，还是源于习惯化的技术自动性；结果显示习惯是最强预测因素，组织学习支持性条件具独立加性作用，影子AI应被重构为“习惯—环境”现象而非个体探索现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 员工未经组织批准使用生成式AI（shadow AI）并普遍加以隐瞒，但对其心理机制理解不足；流行观点与早期评论多假设这是“冒险个体的好奇心探索”，作者希望用习惯性技术使用理论检验这一流行假设。

Method: 多国调查数据（N=510 在职生成式AI用户）；预测变量包括职场好奇心、习惯、技术接受信念、组织学习能力；分析方法为层次回归、带bootstrap置信区间的相对权重分析、全子集支配分析（512个预测变量子集），并对未经授权使用与隐瞒行为分别建模。

Result: 习惯为最强预测因素，解释28.3%的解释方差，并在全部512个预测子集中完全支配“愉悦探索”（最契合探索假说的好奇心维度）；愉悦探索仅贡献3.4%（95% CI [1.6, 7.4]），在纳入使用行为与习惯后不再显著；学习支持性组织条件额外解释24.1%，且以加性而非调节方式起作用；对未经授权使用与隐瞒的平行模型呈相同支配模式；感知到的AI使用社会支持仅对隐瞒具有保护作用。

Conclusion: 影子AI是“习惯—环境”现象，而非个体探索现象；研究采用横断面设计，因果推断仅为探索性。

Abstract: Generative AI has entered the workplace faster than organizations can govern it. A large share of employees now use AI tools their employer has not sanctioned, and many actively conceal that use, but the psychology of this covert technology behavior remains poorly understood. A common assumption, in popular accounts and early commentary, treats unauthorized (shadow) AI use as curiosity-driven exploration by adventurous individuals. We test this against a competing account grounded in the psychology of habitual technology use, in which automaticity rather than exploratory disposition sustains the behavior. Using survey data from 510 employed generative AI users across multiple countries, we adjudicated among individual and contextual predictors (workplace curiosity, habit, technology-acceptance beliefs, and organizational learning capability) with hierarchical regression, relative weights analysis with bootstrap confidence intervals, and full-subset dominance analysis. Habit was the strongest predictor, accounting for 28.3% of explained variance, and completely dominated joyous exploration, the curiosity dimension most aligned with the exploration account, across all 512 possible predictor subsets. Joyous exploration contributed 3.4% (95% CI [1.6, 7.4]) and fell to nonsignificance once use behavior and habit were modeled. Learning-supportive organizational conditions accounted for a further 24.1% and operated additively rather than by moderating dispositions. Parallel models decomposing the outcome showed the same dominance pattern for unauthorized use and concealment, and perceived social support for AI use was protective specifically against concealment. These findings reframe shadow AI as a habit-and-environment phenomenon rather than an individual-exploration phenomenon. The cross-sectional design means causal claims are exploratory.

</details>


### [11] [Data Center Governance Under AI Expansion Pressures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16942)
*Yacine Jernite,Sasha Luccioni,Bruna Trevelin,Angela Lee,Carolina Molina,Xinying Chu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 数据中心的收益是全球性的，但其负面外部性（能源账单、含水层、居住环境）高度本地化；在联邦法规范围存疑、联邦政府偏向AI竞争力与增长的背景下，地方与州政府正通过法案与诉讼、以及既有区划与环境法规对数据中心运营方施压，社区也因此获得表达关切并施压的窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注AI驱动下数据中心规模激增所带来的本地负外部性（电价、水资源/含水层、生活环境），而传统上政府给予税收抵免与豁免等激励，这一格局已发生转变。由于联邦立法（公共信托原则、污染预防法、清洁空气法、安全饮用水法）适用范围存在争议，且联邦层面倾向优先保障AI竞争力与增长，保护本地资源的责任落到区域政府与社区身上，因此有必要梳理地方可用的法规工具与施压路径。

Method: 概念性/政策分析（非实证研究）：以美国为背景，梳理联邦立法与原则（Public Trust Doctrine、Pollution Prevention Act、Clean Air Act、Safe Drinking Water Act）的适用性与争议；识别州与区域层面与数据中心建设运营相关的法律法规（因数据中心在本地需要大量土地、电力、水与基础设施）；并结合近期地方政府提出法案、提起诉讼的动向，归纳社区可用于表达关切与对运营方施压的既有监管工具。

Result: 近期数月出现地方政府提出法案与提起诉讼的动向，以确保数据中心对本地保持净正面影响；联邦法规适用范围仍具争议且联邦立场偏向AI增长，导致监管压力下沉至区域层面；州与地方层面的既有法规（源于数据中心对土地、电力、水与基础设施的大量本地需求）构成社区可实际动用的施压工具。

Conclusion: 在联邦保护缺位或不确定的情况下，数据中心外部性的治理实质落在州与地方层面；社区当前拥有表达关切的机会，并可借助既有法规对数据中心运营方形成压力，从而促使其建设与运营的负外部性得到约束。

Abstract: The promised benefits of data centers are global, but their negative impacts are very much local, affecting the energy bills, aquifers and living environments of citizens across the United States. While governments and authorities have historically provided data centers with incentives such as tax credits and exemptions, AI's aggressive growth in scale has changed this dynamic, and recent months have seen them issue bills and file lawsuits in order to ensure that data centers remain a net positive influence on their legislatures. Existing pieces of federal legislation -- such as the Public Trust Doctrine, the Pollution Prevention, Clean Air and Safe Drinking Water Acts -- exist to manage natural resources, but their scope remains contested, and the current federal government's stance on competitiveness and growth of AI has put the onus on regional governments to endeavor to protect local resources. There are also state- and regional laws and regulations that are relevant for building and operating data centers since, in practice, they require substantial land, electricity, water, and infrastructure at a local level. Given this situation, communities are currently provided with the opportunity to raise their concerns regarding the potential negative impacts of how data centers are built and operated, and have existing regulations that can be used to put pressure on data center operators.

</details>


### [12] [Scalable Conversational Assessment: Chat-Based Exams with a Two-Agent LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17220)
*Geoffrey Challen,Craig Zilles*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出"可扩展对话式评估"(SCA)——基于双智能体LLM架构的文本对话口试系统,访谈者与学生交互、隐藏评估者按评分量规跟踪进度并指导追问,经模拟人格测试与真实课程试用验证其可行性与待改进点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口试能要求学生阐述推理过程、并通过追问澄清模糊回答,但难以规模化。作者希望用LLM在保留交互式追问优势的同时解决人力扩展瓶颈,评估学生"构建与支撑论证"的能力。

Method: 设计双智能体架构实现职责分离并降低答案泄漏风险:interviewer仅接收"下一个问题"的指导并与学生直接对话;hidden evaluator掌握活动目标、依据rubric追踪学生进展,并决定对当前问题追问还是进入下一题。评估分两阶段:(1)用LLM persona构建模拟学生集合(特定能力水平及对抗策略,如答案提取者、prompt注入者、自信编造者);新颖之处在于采用虚构评估主题,防止本不应知晓信息的人格滥用预训练知识;(2)在一门小型AI素养选修课的形成性与终结性评价中实际部署。通过人工与事后AI审阅转录记录进行分析。

Result: 模拟学生测试与课堂部署均顺利完成(系统可行);转录审阅发现两类问题:追问生成质量仍有提升空间,以及评分可能误将访谈者而非学生贡献的观点计入学生得分。

Conclusion: 双智能体对话式评估可实现口试的规模化,但需改进追问生成策略,并确保评分只归功于学生本人贡献的观点。

Abstract: Oral exams are compelling because (1) students can be asked to provide their reasoning for an answer and (2) their interactive nature permits follow-up questions to resolve ambiguous answers. But oral exams scale poorly. In this paper, we present scalable conversational assessment, an LLM-based system that conducts interactive text-based conversations with students to assess their ability to make and support an argument. We use a two-agent architecture to separate concerns and reduce the likelihood that answers are leaked to the student: the interviewer interacts directly with the student, but only receives guidance about the next question to ask the student; the hidden evaluator knows the goals of the whole activity, tracks the student's progress using a rubric, and guides the interviewer on when to follow-up on the current question or proceed to the next question. The system was first tested using a collection of simulated students with LLM personas of a particular ability level or adversarial tactic (e.g., answer-extractors, prompt-injectors, confident fabricators). A novel element of this evaluation was that it required the use of a fictional assessment topic to prevent personas that shouldn't have information from using knowledge present in the LLM's pre-training. The system was also deployed in a small, elective AI literacy course in both formative and summative contexts. Through manual and post-facto AI review of the transcripts we identified opportunities to improve the follow-up question generation and ensure that grading only credited ideas that the student (and not the interviewer) contributed.

</details>


### [13] [The Overstated Cost of AI Fairness in Criminal Justice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01299)
*Ignacio Cofone,Warut Khern-am-nuai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过COMPAS数据集的因果推断分析，本文证明算法不仅复制而且放大种族偏见，并重构公平-准确率权衡：当结果变量本身有偏时，施加公平约束可纠正扭曲而不损失预测准确率，所谓"公平的代价"在政策辩论中被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为提升算法公平必然牺牲预测准确率、给社会带来成本。作者质疑该前提，并进一步挑战两种常见辩护：算法决策相对人类判断是中性改进，以及算法只是镜像既有偏见。若模型实际放大偏见，则公平-准确率权衡的理论与政策基础都需重新审视。

Method: 对COMPAS数据集进行实证分析，采用因果推断方法（如反事实/中介分析）识别并量化种族偏见在数据与模型训练中的传导与放大；随后在施加公平约束的条件下考察预测准确率变化，并将权衡问题重构为"有偏结果变量（再逮捕数据）造成的扭曲"问题。

Result: (1) 种族偏见在COMPAS中不仅存在，且被广泛使用的模型放大，而非简单复制；(2) 在刑事司法场景中施加公平约束并不必然降低预测准确率，在部分干预下几乎不产生政策辩论所预设的成本；(3) 该机制可推广至信贷、招聘与住房等领域，即有偏结果变量会独立于代理变量选择而强化不平等。

Conclusion: 公平-准确率权衡的论证错误地将无约束模型的预测视为最优基准，而忽略了结果变量（如再逮捕）内含的规范性选择与系统性差异。法律与政策在调整刑事司法中的算法公平性时，应把偏差纳入结果变量本身的质量评估，而非仅关注代理变量与公平约束的成本。

Abstract: A dominant critique of algorithmic fairness holds that increasing fairness reduces predictive accuracy, imposing a cost on society. We challenge that assumption by empirically analyzing the COMPAS dataset. We make two contributions. First, using causal inference methods, we show that racial bias is not only present in the COMPAS dataset but is also amplified by the models trained on it. Widely used models do more than replicate existing bias; they exacerbate it. This undercuts both the assumption that algorithmic decision-making offers a neutral improvement over human judgment and the weaker claim that it merely mirrors preexisting human bias. Second, we reframe the fairness-accuracy tradeoff. Applying fairness constraints does not necessarily cost predictive accuracy in criminal justice. Prediction systems operationalize concepts such as risk through implicit and often flawed normative choices about what to predict and how. The tradeoff claim assumes that the unconstrained model's prediction is an optimal baseline. Fairness constraints can instead correct distortions introduced by biased outcome variables: rearrest data, in this case, captures and magnifies systemic racial disparities. Under some interventions, therefore, fairness carries none of the cost presumed in policy debates. These dynamics extend beyond criminal justice to lending, hiring, and housing, where biased outcome variables reinforce inequality independently of proxy selection. We draw out what this implies for how law and policy should approach fairness adjustments in criminal law.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [14] [CAFIN: CUDA-accelerated Adaptive Fisher Information Matrix Network Optimization via GPU-Native Kernel Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14589)
*Maryam Arshad,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAFIN：面向二阶优化器AdaFisher的GPU原生CUDA核融合框架，在不改变优化数学形式的前提下，将FIM预条件构造、EMA更新、梯度预条件、动量累积、权重衰减与参数更新渐进融合进单一CUDA核，降低显存流量与中间分配，实现最多78.4%的FIM构造延迟下降与2.1–26.9%的优化器步延迟下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶优化器利用曲率信息提升训练效果，但实际效率受GPU执行开销制约。AdaFisher虽通过对角Kronecker结构与指数滑动平均降低了FIM近似成本，但其PyTorch实现仍依赖串行CUDA核启动、中间张量分配与重复访存，导致核启动开销与显存带宽浪费成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出CAFIN——一种GPU原生CUDA核融合框架。将FIM预条件子构造、指数滑动平均更新、梯度预条件、动量累积、权重衰减与参数更新逐步融合为少量融合CUDA核；配合共享内存复用、寄存器驻留计算与原地（in-place）操作，减少访存与中间张量分配，且不修改AdaFisher的优化公式。

Result: 在GTX 1660 Ti与RTX 3060 Laptop上，ResNet-18于CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100与Tiny ImageNet的实验中：孤立FIM构造延迟最多降低78.4%，优化器步延迟降低2.1–26.9%；每步消除约18.91 MB中间GPU显存分配；数值验证在预设FP32容差范围内一致，各数据集与随机种子下收敛精度可比。

Conclusion: CUDA核融合可显著加速二阶优化，同时保持AdaFisher的数学形式与学习行为不变，说明实现层（系统级）优化是提升二阶优化器实用效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Second-order optimizers improve deep neural network training using curvature information, but their practical efficiency is limited by GPU execution overhead. AdaFisher reduces Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) approximation costs through diagonal Kronecker structures and exponential moving averages, yet its PyTorch implementation requires sequential CUDA kernel launches, intermediate tensor allocations, and repeated memory accesses. We introduce CAFIN, a GPU-native CUDA kernel fusion framework that improves AdaFisher execution efficiency without changing its optimization formulation. The proposed approach progressively integrates FIM preconditioner construction, exponential moving average updates, gradient preconditioning, momentum accumulation, weight decay, and parameter updates into fused CUDA kernels. Shared-memory reuse, register-resident computation, and in-place operations further reduce memory traffic and intermediate allocations. Experiments using ResNet-18 on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet across NVIDIA GTX 1660 Ti and RTX 3060 Laptop GPUs demonstrate up to 78.4% lower isolated FIM construction latency and 2.1-26.9% lower optimizer-step latency. The implementation also eliminates approximately 18.91 MB of intermediate GPU memory allocations per optimizer step. Numerical validation confirms consistency within the predefined FP32 tolerance, while convergence accuracy remains comparable across evaluated datasets and random seeds. These findings demonstrate that CUDA kernel fusion can substantially accelerate second-order optimization while preserving AdaFisher's underlying mathematical formulation and learning behavior.

</details>


### [15] [Machine Learning Moment Closure for the Boltzmann Transport Equation: Enforcing Hyperbolicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14894)
*Bongseok Kim,Jiahao Zhang,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个数据驱动的多维线性Boltzmann方程Hermite矩系统闭合，通过对所有空间方向共享的对称正定对称化子在保留下阶输运方程的子空间上进行约束，使闭合具双曲性保证，并在二维过渡与散射主导区实验中以十矩三阶系统优于更高阶Pn截断且保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boltzmann输运方程求解速度依赖代价高；矩方法需对未解析高阶矩给出闭合，数据驱动闭合虽精确但缺乏结构约束时会产生复特征速度与不稳定模拟。已有的具双曲性保证的学习闭合仅限一维，而多维需在所有方向上同时满足双曲性。

Method: 网络学习一个所有空间方向共享、限制在保持已知低阶输运方程子空间上的对称正定对称化子，未解析行由对称补全得到，从而每个方向Jacobian对任意网络输出与任意截断阶均具实特征值与完备特征向量；仅用动理学参考解中未解析矩的空间导数训练，训练中不含求解器，并用部分守恒间断Galerkin方法演化。

Result: 二维过渡与散射主导区实验中，单一学习的三阶十矩系统在所有情形下稳定；时间平均精度在每个实验中都优于36矩与136矩的P7与P15截断，密度误差比同矩数的P3截断小约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 构造性双曲性可作为多维矩闭合的有效结构约束，使低阶数据驱动闭合在精度与稳定性上同时超越高阶经典截断，且训练无需耦合求解器。

Abstract: The Boltzmann transport equation describes particle transport across collisional regimes, but resolving its velocity dependence is expensive. Moment methods reduce this cost by evolving finitely many velocity moments, requiring a closure for unresolved higher-order moments. Data-driven closures can represent these moments accurately, yet without structural constraints they may produce complex characteristic speeds and unstable simulations. Learned Boltzmann closures with guaranteed hyperbolicity have so far been formulated in one spatial dimension; in multiple dimensions, hyperbolicity must hold in every direction simultaneously. We develop, to the best of our knowledge, the first data-driven closure for multidimensional Hermite moment systems of the linear Boltzmann equation that is hyperbolic by construction. The network learns a symmetric positive-definite symmetrizer shared by all spatial directions and restricted to a subspace that preserves the known lower-order transport equations; the unresolved rows follow from a symmetric completion. Every directional Jacobian thus has real eigenvalues and a complete set of eigenvectors for every network output and at any truncation order. The closure is trained only on spatial derivatives of unresolved moments from kinetic reference solutions, with no solver in the training loop, and evolved by a partially conservative discontinuous Galerkin method. In two-dimensional experiments in the transition and scattering-dominated regimes, a single learned third-order system with ten moments remains stable in all cases. Averaged over time, it is more accurate than the classical P7 and P15 truncations with 36 and 136 moments in every experiment, and its density error is about two orders of magnitude smaller than that of the P3 truncation with the same moments.

</details>


### [16] [An Asynchronous Federated Learning Platform for Lung Adenocarcinoma Grading via Foundation Models and Structural Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14590)
*Moon-Il Joo,Md Ariful Islam Mozumder,Hee-Cheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于Web的联邦学习系统，仅共享本地模型权重而不共享患者数据，结合UNI2基础模型与CLAM-SB对肺腺癌WSI进行PD/WMD分级；在FedAvg、FedProx、FedBN、FedNova四种聚合算法与L1范数结构化剪枝（20%/30%/40%）下系统比较，最终确定FedNova+40%剪枝为兼顾通信效率与分类性能的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺腺癌PD与WMD的区分对患者生存预测具有重要意义，但病理医师间存在观察者间变异，难以获得一致性分类；同时多中心数据因患者隐私限制难以集中收集。因此需要在不共享原始数据的前提下整合多机构数据以提升泛化能力。

Method: 1）采用UNI2基础模型与CLAM-SB进行WSI分类；2）构建四客户端联邦学习系统，NLST队列完全留作外部验证；3）在相同实验条件下比较FedAvg、FedProx、FedBN、FedNova；4）对模型施加L1范数结构化剪枝（20%、30%、40%稀疏度）以降低传输体积；5）开发Web应用，支持切片上传、本地训练、AI分类及注意力热图可视化。

Result: 集中式评估中，CPTAC与TCGA联合训练的模型在外部验证上优于单机构训练，验证了多中心数据整合的必要性。测试AUC排序为FedBN > FedProx > FedNova > FedAvg；FedNova在PD与WMD两类间分类性能最均衡。剪枝后，FedAvg、FedProx、FedBN的测试AUC呈下降趋势，而FedNova在所有稀疏度下AUC均有提升。

Conclusion: FedNova与40%剪枝的组合在保护患者隐私的同时提升了通信效率与分类性能，是一种具有实用价值的方案；配套Web系统实现了从切片上传到热图可视化的完整工作流。

Abstract: Classification of lung adenocarcinoma into poorly differentiated (PD) and well-to-moderately differentiated (WMD) subtypes plays an important role in patient survival; however, consistent classification is difficult due to inter-observer variability among pathologists. Collecting data from multiple hospitals is also challenging due to patient privacy concerns. This study proposes a web-based federated learning system that shares only locally trained model weights without sharing patient data between institutions. The UNI2 foundation model and CLAM-SB were used to classify pathological whole slide images. A four-client federated learning system was constructed, and the NLST cohort was reserved exclusively for external validation. In centralized evaluation, joint training with CPTAC and TCGA outperformed single-institution training on external validation, confirming the necessity of multi-institutional data integration. FedAvg, FedProx, FedBN, and FedNova were compared as federated learning algorithms under identical experimental conditions. Test AUC ranked as FedBN, FedProx, FedNova, and FedAvg. FedNova showed the most balanced classification performance between PD and WMD. L1-norm structural pruning at 20%, 30%, and 40% sparsity was applied to reduce model transmission size. FedAvg, FedProx, and FedBN showed a tendency of decreasing Test AUC under pruning, while FedNova achieved improved Test AUC across all sparsity levels. A web-based application was developed supporting slide upload, local training, AI classification, and attention heatmap-based visualization. The combination of FedNova and 40% pruning was confirmed as a practical approach that improves communication efficiency and classification performance while protecting patient privacy.

</details>


### [17] [Adversarial Risk in the Generative AI Era Necessitates Dropping the Small Epsilon Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14594)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil G. Marchant,Paul Montague,Jiani Xie,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者批判对抗机器学习中以 $\ell_p$ 范数 $\epsilon$-球为最小扰动的度量范式，认为它是"度量陷阱"，会误导防御者、错配真实系统风险，甚至可能加剧危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对抗机器学习研究高度集中于 $\epsilon$-球内的最小 $\ell_p$ 扰动，该度量与真实世界系统脆弱性并不对齐，给防御者传递了失真的风险感知，因而需要反思这一范式的合理性。

Method: 采用立场性/论证性（position paper）分析，从度量学（metrology）视角解构 $\ell_p$ 范数作为威胁模型代理指标的假设，论证其在风险沟通与攻防激励两方面的失效。

Result: 指出两条失效路径：其一，防御者被引向优化狭窄的数学鲁棒性，牺牲系统性安全；其二，攻击者被激励去探索与之不重叠的替代对抗路径。

Conclusion: $\ell_p$ 球的主导地位不仅未能减少危害，反而可能通过传递针对错误威胁的虚假安全感而主动促成危害，呼吁重新审视对抗威胁的度量方式。

Abstract: Adversarial Machine Learning research is currently centered on the search for the epsilon-ball: minimal perturbations designed to fool models while remaining imperceptible to humans. We argue that this fixation on $\ell_p$ norms is a metrological trap that communicates a distorted perception of risk to defenders, one that is unaligned with real-world system vulnerabilities. This paradigm misleads defenders into optimizing for narrow, mathematical robustness at the expense of systemic security, while simultaneously incentivizing attackers to exploit non-overlapping alternative adversarial pathways. Ultimately, we argue that the dominance of the $\ell_p$ ball does not just fail to reduce harm---it may actively facilitate it by communicating a false sense of security against the wrong threats.

</details>


### [18] [StructRL: Model-Free Reinforcement Learning with Replay Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14599)
*Ivo Nowak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StructRL 将强化学习中的经验构建为显式回放图，用动态规划提取“图策略”，再以动作偏好/目标方向的形式蒸馏给 DQN 与 SAC，显著提升稀疏奖励任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励下，无模型 RL（DQN、SAC）样本效率低、成功率低；而已观测经验中蕴含可被显式利用的转移结构，未被现有方法充分利用。作者希望把探索经验转化为可规划的图结构，以弥补无模型学习器在长程信用分配上的不足。

Method: 1) 将 RL 经验收集为显式回放图（replay graph）；2) 当图中出现通往目标的路径时，用动态规划在已观测状态空间上选择动作，得到仅在已观测转移处有定义的图策略（graph policy）；3) 将图策略迁移到无模型学习器：对 DQN 作为动作偏好（action preferences），对 SAC actor 作为沿最短观测路径的目标方向（target directions）；4) 将图策略蒸馏为一个新的 Q-model。

Result: DQN 在三个稀疏奖励 MiniGrid 任务上最终成功率显著提升：DoorKey 0.10→0.90，KeyCorridor 0.10→0.80，ObstructedMaze 0→0.50，且普通 DQN 用三倍预算仍追不上。SAC 在连续 PointMaze 上，图导出方向标签使七个到达目标的配对运行成功率由 0.17 提升至 1.0。蒸馏得到的 DoorKey 6x6 控制器可从 91% 的可达状态到达目标，而普通 DQN 为 38%。扩展实验识别出三个瓶颈：图支撑不完整、结构混叠（structural aliasing）、高维输入中图信息丢失。

Conclusion: 显式图结构与动态规划规划可为无模型 RL 提供高质量指导信号，在稀疏奖励与长程导航任务上带来数量级式的样本效率与成功率改进；但方法的可扩展性受制于图覆盖度、状态混叠与高维表征下信息保留三个问题，是后续工作的关键方向。

Abstract: StructRL turns the experience collected during reinforcement learning into an explicit replay graph. Once the graph contains a path to the task goal, dynamic programming selects actions on the observed part of the state space; these actions form a graph policy that is defined only where the required transitions have been observed. StructRL transfers these actions to model-free learners: as action preferences for a DQN Q-model and as target directions along shortest observed paths for a SAC actor. For DQN on three sparse-reward MiniGrid tasks, final success rises from 0.10 to 0.90 (DoorKey), 0.10 to 0.80 (KeyCorridor) and 0 to 0.50 (ObstructedMaze); plain DQN does not catch up with three times the budget. For SAC on continuous PointMaze, graph-derived direction labels raise final success from 0.17 to 1.0 on seven matched runs that reached the goal. Distilling the graph policy into a fresh Q-model gives a DoorKey 6x6 controller that reaches the goal from 91% of reachable states, against 38% for plain DQN. Scaling experiments identify three bottlenecks: incomplete graph support, structural aliasing, and loss of graph information in high-dimensional inputs.

</details>


### [19] [KernelMosaic: An Open Dataset and Agentic Framework for CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14604)
*Ningyuan Xi,Yuhang Lai,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelMosaic：面向高效CUDA内核生成的开源数据集，含多样化任务、已验证解与优化轨迹；小模型SFT后KernelBench L1/L2/L3正确率由4%/0%/0%提升至88%/82%/16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大模型生成高效CUDA内核需要覆盖多样计算需求并包含迭代优化过程的监督信号，但异构规范难以统一，且可用实现依赖复杂的迭代测试与精修，构建此类监督数据十分困难。

Method: 构建KernelMosaic数据集：通过人工引导评审与多样性感知选择，将任务以自然语言描述、参考程序、数学规格及其交叉形式表达；使用智能体框架驱动强LLM，结合可复用的CUDA指导与执行反馈，完成实现、正确性修复与性能优化，生成解与轨迹。

Result: 在小开源模型上做监督微调后，KernelBench L1/L2/L3正确率由4%、0%、0%升至88%、82%、16%，且各级别中内核快于PyTorch eager的任务比例均提升；对比实验显示轨迹监督显著优于直接回答监督，并促进持续的测试与修订行为。

Conclusion: KernelMosaic可使通用模型在CUDA内核生成上获得显著性能提升，验证了多样化任务与高质量优化轨迹监督的有效性。

Abstract: Training large language models to generate efficient CUDA kernels requires supervision spanning diverse computational requirements and iterative code optimization. Constructing this supervision is challenging because of heterogeneous specifications, and useful implementations emerge through iterative complicated testing and refinement. We introduce KernelMosaic, an open dataset pairing diverse CUDA tasks with verified solutions and trajectories. Human-guided review and diversity-aware selection curate tasks expressed as natural-language descriptions, reference programs, mathematical specifications, and their intersections. To build the solutions and trajectories, our agentic framework guides a strong LLM through implementation, correctness repair, and performance optimization with reusable CUDA guidance and execution feedback. Supervised fine-tuning of a small open-source model on KernelMosaic raises correctness from 4%, 0%, and 0% to 88%, 82%, and 16% on KernelBench L1, L2, and L3, respectively, while increasing the fraction of tasks with kernels faster than PyTorch eager at every level. Further training experiments show that supervision from the curated trajectories substantially outperforms direct responses and promotes sustained testing and revision. These results demonstrate that KernelMosaic enables a general-purpose model to achieve substantial gains in CUDA kernel generation.

</details>


### [20] [Thompson Sampling using Prior-fitted Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14656)
*Sihwa Park,Jingsen Zhu,Vinamr Jain,Sheng-Yen Chou,Alexander Terenin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出先验拟合扩散Transformer，在推理时上下文内执行Thompson采样，并配套非高斯先验，突破高斯过程限制，在全局优化基准上优于传统GP流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续全局优化中，Thompson采样几乎受限于高斯过程先验，因为只有GP的后验计算在实践中可处理；而GP无法表达如单峰性等非高斯特征。

Method: 基于prior-fitted network范式预训练扩散模型（prior-fitted diffusion transformers），使其在推理阶段以in-context方式执行Thompson采样；设计一组适用于一般黑箱函数的实用非高斯先验（含单峰函数先验）；并量化模型规模与预训练迭代次数的权衡，检验缩放规律。

Result: 模型呈现类似其他模态Transformer的缩放规律；在多个全局优化基准上，带非高斯先验的prior-fitted diffusion transformers性能优于基于高斯过程的传统流程。

Conclusion: 扩散Transformer可通过先验拟合范式将Thompson采样扩展到非高斯先验，摆脱对GP的依赖，为黑箱连续全局优化提供更具表达力且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Thompson sampling is natural randomized strategy for optimizing unknown functions in a black-box manner. In continuous global optimization, Thompson sampling has largely been restricted to Gaussian process priors, as these are essentially the only class for which computing the posterior distribution has been tractable in practice. In this work, we introduce \emph{prior-fitted diffusion transformers}, which are diffusion models that perform Thompson sampling in-context at inference time, after being pre-trained using the prior-fitted network paradigm. To apply these models, we develop a set of practical non-Gaussian priors for general black-box functions---including those that have characteristics that cannot be achieved under Gaussianity, such as priors over unimodal functions. We also quantify the tradeoff between model size and the number of pre-training iterations, and show that these models follow scaling laws akin to those observed in transformers defined over other modalities. Across a set of global optimization benchmarks, we show that prior-fitted diffusion transformers with non-Gaussian priors can achieve improved performance compared to traditional pipelines built on Gaussian processes.

</details>


### [21] [Closing the Loop: Co-Evolving EM for Irregular Time Series Generation in Lifted Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14659)
*Idan Arbiv,Gal Fadlon,Omri Azencot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出"先一次性插补、再冻结训练生成器"的开环范式存在结构性缺陷，提出插补器-生成器协同演化的蒙特卡洛EM框架，并配以PSLR后验采样，在十个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非规则时间序列生成规则时间序列通常被简化为一次性插补：补全每条部分序列后冻结，再在替代数据集上训练生成器。作者认为该开环策略结构上有缺陷：确定性点插补会把歧义缺失区域坍缩为条件均值，随机插补器则随生成器演化而过时。真正的约束在于"迭代"，而非插补器设计。

Method: 提出协同演化的蒙特卡洛EM框架，闭合插补器-生成器回路：E步从当前生成器后验中采样缺失值，M步用这些补全重新训练生成器，从而保持生成器的无条件训练目标。由于扩散先验工作在提升的二维表示中、而观测位于时间序列空间，进一步提出PSLR（Posterior Sampling for Lifted Representations），在E步中强制算子一致的条件化、不确定性保留与流形一致性。

Result: 在10个数据集、4种损坏机制下达到SOTA：判别分数提升59%，预测分数提升15%，Context-FID提升73%，特征相关性误差提升71%（相对最强基线）。消融显示相较单步基线判别分数平均降低73%，大幅缩小与干净数据oracle的差距。

Conclusion: 迭代式的插补-生成协同演化优于一次性插补的开环范式，验证了"迭代而非插补器设计"是关键约束这一论断；代码已开源。

Abstract: Regular time-series generation from irregular time series is typically reduced to one-shot imputation: complete each partial sequence once, freeze the completions, and train a generator on the resulting surrogate dataset. We show that this open-loop strategy is structurally flawed. Point imputers collapse ambiguous missing regions to conditional averages, while stochastic imputers become stale as the generator evolves; the binding constraint is therefore iteration, not imputer design. We introduce a co-evolving Monte Carlo EM framework that closes the imputer-generator loop. Each E-step samples missing values from the posterior of the current generator, and each M-step retrains the generator on these completions, preserving unconditional generator training. Since our diffusion prior operates in a lifted 2D representation while observations live in time-series space, we further introduce Posterior Sampling for Lifted Representations (PSLR), which enforces operator-consistent conditioning, uncertainty preservation, and manifold consistency during the E-step. Across ten datasets and four corruption regimes, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving over the strongest baselines by 59% in discriminative score, 15% in predictive score, 73% in Context-FID, and 71% in feature-correlation error. Ablations show that our approach reduces average discriminative score by 73% over single-step baselines, closing most of the remaining gap to the clean-data oracle. Code is available at https://github.com/azencot-group/PSLR.

</details>


### [22] [Trait Hitchhiking in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14683)
*Adil Soubki,Elizabeth S. Z. Tan,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将进化生物学中的Price方程引入语言模型RL后训练，提出"性状搭便车(trait hitchhiking)"形式化模型，解释未受奖励直接约束的性状如何在训练中被放大或抑制，并在GRPO训练的模型上验证Price估计能紧贴采样性状动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL已成为语言模型后训练的标准环节且算力投入快速上升。它虽能提升能力（尤其在可验证奖励任务上），却也会改变奖励未明确针对的行为与内部属性，而这种非目标性状变化的机制缺乏形式化刻画。

Method: 建立描述RL后训练中"性状"演化的形式体系，并由此推导出进化生物学的Price方程；据此构建"性状搭便车"的数学模型——训练在completion上重分配概率时导致非目标性状被放大或抑制；随后在GRPO训练的语言模型上对该Price更新表达式进行数值评估。

Result: Price估计值与实际采样的性状动态高度吻合，表明该表达能较好地预测性状演化轨迹。

Conclusion: 性状搭便车是RL后训练在非显式目标下悄然放大不良性状的一种机制，为理解和监控后训练的副作用提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is now a standard stage of language model post-training, and the compute devoted to it is growing rapidly. While it has been shown to improve capabilities, particularly for tasks with verifiable rewards, it can also change behaviours and internal properties that the reward does not explicitly target. We present a formalism to describe the evolution of "traits" during RL post-training and, in doing so, recover the Price equation from evolutionary biology. We use this to develop a mathematical model for what we call "trait hitchhiking": the amplification or suppression of non-targeted traits as training redistributes probability across completions. We evaluate this Price update expression on language models trained with GRPO and show that the Price estimates closely track the sampled trait dynamics. These results identify trait hitchhiking as a mechanism by which RL post-training can silently amplify undesirable traits.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Row-Level Prediction: Evaluating and Learning Composition for Reusable Table Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14697)
*Ali Younes,Saeed Ghoorchian,Maximilian Schambach,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出表级嵌入评测基准（一致性、可分辨性、鲁棒性、效率四项要求），发现轻量指纹最强；用SCDM学习组合规则可显著提升TabPFN/ConTextTab的组合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格内容分布于schema、单元格、行、列，检索、重叠估计、去重等任务需要可复用的表级嵌入；但现有表格表示方法多以行级预测或监督下游任务评估，未直接衡量表级嵌入质量。

Method: 构建基准，通过四项要求（部分视图一致性、跨标签粒度可分辨性、对良性扰动的鲁棒性、效率）评估表级嵌入；在合成与真实语料上比较12种方法与5种无参池化规则；冻结ConTextTab，仅学习组合规则，采用自监督的支撑条件分布匹配（SCDM）损失，将嵌入相似度分布与表内/跨表分区重叠分布对齐。

Result: 轻量schema与文本指纹表现最强；TabPFN与ConTextTab虽行级预测强，但其状态经mean、max等池化后不及最强指纹；学习组合规则后，15种头配置下合成数据Spearman相关从0.04升至0.43–0.50，三元组排序、聚类、检索均改善，真实数据增益较小但系统一致；父表检索下游任务验证结论。

Conclusion: 局部状态的组合方式及其训练目标显著影响表级嵌入质量；瓶颈更多在于组合规则而非编码器。

Abstract: Tables are compositional, with content spread across a schema, cell values, rows, and columns. Large-scale operations such as retrieval, overlap estimation, deduplication, and data curation need these local signals composed into one reusable embedding vector per table. Table representation methods, however, are mostly evaluated through row-level prediction or supervised downstream tasks, which do not measure table-level embedding quality directly. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates table-level embeddings through four desiderata: consistency under partial views, discriminability across label granularities, robustness to benign perturbations, and efficiency. Across twelve methods on synthetic and real corpora, lightweight schema- and text-based fingerprints are among the strongest. The sharpest contrast comes from two tabular foundation models, TabPFN and ConTextTab: although both are strong row-level predictors, their states, composed by mean, max, or three further parameter-free pooling rules, trail the strongest fingerprints. We therefore ask whether the gap lies in the encoder or in the compositional rule. Keeping ConTextTab frozen, we learn only the compositional rule over its cell states, using support-conditioned distribution matching (SCDM), a self-supervised loss we propose that matches the distribution of embedding similarities to the distribution of partition overlaps within and across tables. Across all fifteen head configurations, the learned rule raises the Spearman correlation between embedding similarity and partial-view overlap from 0.04 to 0.43-0.50 on synthetic data and improves triplet ranking, clustering, and retrieval, with smaller but systematic gains on real data. A downstream parent-table retrieval task confirms these findings. How local states are composed, and with what objective, substantially affects the quality of table-level embeddings.

</details>


### [24] [Code as Explanations: Executable and Verifiable LLM Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14704)
*Nhi Nguyen,Shauli Ravfogel,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"代码解释"（code explanations）：用一个程序跨数据集解释LLM行为，并以其执行结果与LLM输出的吻合比例（Consistency指标）实现自动验证，从而替代无法跨输入核验的自由文本CoT解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正越来越多地参与具有后果性的决策，需要理解其得出答案的方式。理解模型行为必须在多个输入上观察，但CoT等自由文本解释是逐实例生成的，难以在新输入上验证，形成"解释生成方式"与"解释评估方式"之间的错配。

Method: 以"程序"为载体表达跨输入的LLM行为规律，通过程序执行实现自动验证；定义Consistency指标（程序复现LLM输出的输入占比），用于评估与筛选候选程序；在3个开源代码能力LLM与8个任务上评测，并与CoT长度、无信息基线对比；另以Claude Opus 5.5开展一致性反馈+代码执行实验。

Result: 多设置下LLM可产出高一致性代码解释；代码解释长度仅为CoT的1/6至1/100，且在24个设定中的9个上与模型行为更吻合；但在语言成分更复杂的任务上一致性显著下降，程序复现率不高于无信息基线，且常依赖较长硬编码模式。

Conclusion: 可执行代码是一种新颖、可验证且紧凑的LLM行为解释范式；提供一致性反馈与代码执行可提升解释一致性，但复杂任务上的差距仍显著，验证反馈是改进解释一致性的有前景方向。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly make consequential decisions and solve problems alongside humans, we must understand how models arrive at their conclusions. Understanding how an LLM behaves requires observing it across multiple inputs, yet free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought (CoT) are generated per instance and cannot be easily verified on new inputs. We propose {code explanations to address the mismatch in how LLM explanations are generated versus how they are evaluated: a program explains LLM behavior across a dataset of inputs, and its execution enables automatic verification. We define Consistency metric that measures the fraction of inputs on which the program reproduces the LLM's outputs, and use it to evaluate and select candidate programs. Across three open-source code-capable LLMs and eight tasks, we find that LLMs can produce highly consistent code explanations in several settings. Code explanations are also 6$\times$ to 100$\times$ shorter than CoT, and better align with model behavior in 9 out of 24 settings. However, consistency is significantly lower on tasks with more complex linguistic components, where generated programs reproduce model outputs no more frequently than an uninformative explanation baseline, and these programs often rely on longer hard-coded patterns. In experiments with Claude Opus 5.5, providing the model with consistency feedback and code execution improves explanation consistency across tasks, but consistency remains significantly lower on more complex tasks. Our findings establish executable code as a novel, verifiable, and compact approach to explaining LLM behavior and highlight verification feedback as a promising direction for improving explanation consistency. The code for this paper is at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations.

</details>


### [25] [UniPipe: Unified Inference Serving and Fine-Tuning for Deadline-Constrained DNN Surrogate Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14748)
*Jaiaid Mobin,Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniPipe：在单个边缘GPU上以单上下文融合DNN代理模型的推理服务与在线微调，通过共享前向、选择性反向和多项式时间调度器降低时延抖动与截止期错过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流（如X射线光源的叠层成像）需在硬性单样本截止期内处理高速传感器流。DNN代理比迭代求解器快100–1000倍，但输入分布漂移会降低精度；现有部署要么冻结预训练模型，要么用独立CUDA上下文并发微调，多上下文并发造成GPU资源争用，抬高推理时延均值与方差，导致截止期错过。

Method: 单上下文统一系统：(1) 将训练与推理数据融合进一次前向传播，仅对训练子集执行选择性反向传播；(2) 利用现代GPU单样本时延随批量非线性变化的特性，构建多项式时间调度器计算批量组成；(3) 利用负载跨区间重复的特性进行离线调度规划。

Result: 在真实叠层成像重建工作流（传感器速率达5000 patterns/s）上：重建MSE较预训练基线最多降低30%，推理MSE最多降低50%；相比多上下文基线，截止期错过率降低13%–22%；时延离散度从均值的106%降至57%；总吞吐量亦高于多上下文基线。

Conclusion: 在单边缘GPU上以单上下文统一推理与微调可同时兼顾精度自适应与硬截止期保障，优于冻结模型与多上下文并发两种现有方案。

Abstract: DNN surrogates are increasingly used to accelerate scientific workflows that must process high-rate sensor streams in near real time, exemplified by Ptychographic imaging at next-generation X-ray light sources. Such surrogates deliver 100-1000x higher throughput than iterative solvers, enabling faster steering of experiments. To preserve surrogate accuracy during an experiment, as the input distribution evolves, state-of-the-art deployments freeze the pretrained surrogate or run a separate CUDA context that fine-tunes concurrently with serving. However, multi-context concurrency causes significant contention on the GPU, inflating both the mean and the variance of inference latency, triggering deadline misses in workflows where sensor data has to be processed within a hard per-unit deadline. To address this challenge, we propose UniPipe, a single-context system that unifies inference serving and fine-tuning of a DNN surrogate on a single edge GPU. UniPipe is driven by three key insights: both serving and fine-tuning require a forward pass and can be fused into one; the per-sample latency of modern GPUs is non-linear in batch size; and the workload is repetitive across intervals, enabling offline scheduling. Specifically, UniPipe fuses training and inference data in a single forward pass with a selective backward pass on the training subset, and uses a polynomial-time scheduler that exploits the latency curve to compute the batch composition. We evaluate UniPipe on a real-world Ptychographic reconstruction workflow with sensor rates up to 5000 patterns/sec.; UniPipe lowers reconstruction MSE by up to 30% and inference MSE by up to 50% over a pretrained baseline, and reduces the deadline-miss rate by 13%-22% over a multi-context baseline. UniPipe's unified deployment reduces the latency spread from 106% to 57% of the mean, and increases total throughput compared to multi-context baseline.

</details>


### [26] [From Nodes to Pixels: Topological and Structural Two-View Graph Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14750)
*Md Joshem Uddin,Soham Changani,Sai Karthik Navuluru,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: G2Image 提出一种固定预算的"图成像"框架，将每个图转换为两种互补的图像式视图（TopoGrid 多持续同调表面网格 + GraphGrid 边密度聚合网格），实现置换不变、分辨率可控且固定尺寸的张量输入，可用轻量 2D 编码器处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图学习缺乏类似视觉中的 patch 或语言中的 token 那样可广泛复用的输入接口：图不规则、规模与密度差异大、且在等价节点标号下不变，难以跨任务和架构标准化输入。

Method: 将图转为两个互补视图：(1) TopoGrid——编码内蕴双滤流（bifiltration）为紧凑的多持续同调表面网格，提供稳定拓扑视图；(2) GraphGrid——按内蕴节点分数分组并聚合组间边密度，提供更高带宽结构视图。两视图均具置换不变性并附分辨率控制的稳定性保证；因输出为固定尺寸张量，可用轻量 2D 编码器处理，并通过监督式跨视图对比目标对齐。

Result: 在图分类与分子预测基准上取得较强平均性能；优于单视图与标准融合变体；无需改变整体架构即可扩展到含属性的分子图。

Conclusion: 固定预算图图像可作为图表示学习中实用且可复用的输入接口。

Abstract: Graph learning still lacks a broadly reusable input interface comparable to patches in vision or tokens in language. Unlike images or text, graphs are irregular, vary in size and density, and are invariant under many equivalent node labelings, making it difficult to standardize inputs across tasks and architectures. We introduce G2Image, a fixed-budget graph imaging framework that converts each graph into two complementary image-like views. TopoGrid provides a stable topological view by encoding an intrinsic bifiltration as a compact multipersistence surface grid, while GraphGrid provides a higher-bandwidth structural view by aggregating edge densities between groups defined by intrinsic node scores. Both views are permutation-invariant and come with resolution-controlled stability guarantees. Because the resulting representations are fixed-size tensors, they can be processed by lightweight 2D encoders and aligned through a supervised cross-view contrastive objective. Across graph classification and molecular prediction benchmarks, G2Image achieves strong average performance among the compared methods, improves over single-view and standard fusion variants, and extends to attribute-rich molecular graphs without changing the overall architecture. These results suggest that fixed-budget graph images offer a practical and reusable interface for graph representation learning.

</details>


### [27] [Graph Label Alignment: A Diagnostic Atlas for Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14760)
*Neelam Akula,Varun Shiralkar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"图标签对齐"(graph label alignment)诊断框架，用无需训练图编码器的SLA与FLA两个探针，将图分类基准成功归因于"受限边际视图"中的标签可得性，并据此解释基准难度、揭示超越池化表示的结构-特征耦合需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图分类基准难度差异巨大：部分基准可被简单池化基线解决，另一些对GNN与图Transformer仍困难。作者希望以数据为中心回答：基准成功究竟源于真实的结构-特征推理，还是源于在受限边际视图中已可获取的标签信号。

Method: 提出轻量诊断"图标签对齐"：不训练图编码器，仅用两个监督探针测量标签可得性——结构标签对齐(SLA)，基于置换不变的拓扑与谱摘要；特征标签对齐(FLA)，基于去边后的池化节点特征。在真实图分类基准与多种池化骨干上绘制SLA–FLA二维平面。进一步设计受控的"交互主导"压力测试：异配ego-graph，以及各类边际分布匹配但角色-特征耦合不同的合成Twins任务。

Result: SLA–FLA平面能强有力地组织测试准确率；低对齐数据集形成一致区域，标准池化流程在该区域普遍表现不佳。在高对齐数据集上，高对齐度可解释大部分"简单"基准性能；在低对齐数据上，成功取决于超越标准池化表示、保留结构-特征对应关系。

Conclusion: 基准上的成功与标签在受限边际视图中的可得性高度相关，提示标准基准存在"可被边际捷径解决"的隐患；低对齐及交互主导任务构成真正需要结构-特征耦合推理的评测区域，为更严谨的基准设计与建模提供依据。

Abstract: Graph classification benchmarks vary widely in difficulty: some are solved by simple pooled baselines, while others remain challenging for GNNs and graph transformers. We take a data-centric view and ask whether benchmark success is driven by genuine structure--feature reasoning or by label signal already accessible from restricted marginal views. We introduce graph label alignment, a lightweight diagnostic that measures label accessibility through two supervised probes computed without training a graph encoder: structural label alignment (SLA), based on permutation-invariant topological and spectral summaries, and feature label alignment (FLA), based on pooled node features with edges removed. Across real graph classification benchmarks and multiple pooled backbones, the resulting SLA--FLA plane strongly organizes test accuracy, with low-alignment datasets forming a consistent regime where standard pooled pipelines struggle. To probe what lies beyond marginal accessibility, we introduce controlled interaction-dominated stress tests, including heterophilic ego-graphs and synthetic Twins tasks whose structural and feature marginals are matched across classes but whose role--feature couplings differ. These results show that high alignment explains much of easy benchmark performance, while low alignment reveals regimes where success requires preserving structure--feature correspondence beyond standard pooled representations.

</details>


### [28] [SPIRE: Task-agnostic embodied continual learning through autonomous situation inference and causal motor-primitive reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14840)
*Yiyang Jia,Mingyang Lyu,Gongce Cao,Feifei Zhao,Yinqian Sun,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIRE是一种循环脉冲神经网络系统，无任务标签与边界信息下从动作-结果证据推断潜在情境，在线学习情境特定运动执行器，并将成熟执行器作为受保护源与新目标情境残差组合，实现自主情境恢复与运动技能的因果复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身持续学习要求控制器在无任务标签、无已知任务边界的条件下感知情境变化、保留既有行为并复用运动技能。核心难点在于判断何时恢复先前行为、以及哪些技能可支撑新学习。

Method: 提出SPIRE（Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment）：循环脉冲系统从动作-结果证据推断潜在情境并在线学习情境特定运动actor；成熟actor成为受保护源，与目标情境残差共同驱动循环脉冲运动群体。在坐标控制、三关节控制与隐力控制三类任务上评估，并设置回退干预与七种循环持续学习/模块化控制基线对比。

Result: SPIRE保持控制能力并自主推断情境、保留先前行为；对未见源组合的学习，达到准则所需平均交互次数较匹配的新控制器减少约40%；每次匹配干预中回退兼容源actor均损害早期习得，证明因果复用；相比七个对照，平均先验学习优势为各配对流中最强无任务标签替代方案的2.4倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明在连续具身控制中可实现运动技能的自主恢复与因果复用，支持新学习；但作为摘要级结果，其泛化性与回退干预的细节机制仍需正文实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Embodied continual learning requires controllers to infer changing situations, retain prior behavior and reuse motor skills without task labels or known boundaries. The challenge is deciding when to recover earlier behavior and which skills can support new learning. We introduce Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment (SPIRE), a recurrent-spiking system that infers latent contexts from action-outcome evidence and learns context-specific motor actors online. Mature actors become protected sources that combine with a target-context residual to drive recurrent-spiking motor populations. Across coordinate, three-joint and hidden-force control, SPIRE maintained control, autonomously inferred situations, retained prior behavior and accelerated acquisition of an unseen source composition, reducing mean interactions to criterion by approximately 40% relative to matched fresh controllers. Rewinding compatible source actors impaired early acquisition in every matched intervention, demonstrating causal reuse. Against seven recurrent continual-learning and modular-control comparators, SPIRE achieved 2.4 times the mean prior-learning advantage of the strongest eligible task-free alternative selected for each paired stream. These results demonstrate autonomous recovery and causal reuse of motor skills for new learning in continuous embodied control.

</details>


### [29] [Owned Edits to a Shared Decision Rule: Learning Private Exceptions as Typed, Gated, Withdrawable Weight Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15917)
*John J. O'Hare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种以“自有编辑”为变更单位的学习机制：将已发布决策规则精确编译为小型ReLU网络作为结构化先验，各方在其类型化声明空间中经选择感知证据门控搜索，实现私有证据下的非汇聚式协作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个组织共用同一已发布决策规则时，各自掌握“该规则在本地方案上错误”的私有证据，但不愿共享。需要一种既能利用私有证据、又能保证不泄露、不改变他人决策且可撤回的学习机制。

Method: 将发布规则精确编译为小型ReLU网络（结构化先验）；每个参与方的学习器在小型类型化（typed）声明空间（subject、scope、form、size等命名值）中搜索，仅当自身记录通过“选择感知证据门控”时才提出一个编辑；学习即围绕先验的门控搜索。受理机构依据声明重算每条编辑并写入该方专属预留单元。

Result: 两项预注册分析（20个隐藏真值合成数据）：每方30条记录时，学习器以96.6%对未改规则91.7%，在2分以内非劣于全部七个调优基线（受限梯度学习器、Ditto、FedRep、联邦平均、两个汇聚网络、汇聚梯度提升树）；去掉先验或门控的同类搜索仅84.0%。第三项预注册测试中，基于共享数据训练网络的类型化编辑比该网络高5.4分，比基于发布规则的同类编辑高3.1分。机制按构造保证：不改变任何其他主体的决策、可精确撤回、泄漏至多log2(类型空间大小)比特、除声明值外无可隐藏。失败情形：所有方共享的真值、放宽型编辑、仅10条记录、某预先声明的门控标准两次均未通过、以及语言模型在单次抽样上准确率相当。

Conclusion: “自有编辑+类型化门控搜索+先验”的机制在私有证据、不汇聚设定下可行且具可验证的隐私与局部性保证，但在共享真值、放宽、极少样本等情形下失效；在仅规则基线上，一张自有例外表可复现相同决策。

Abstract: Organisations that apply the same published decision rule often hold private evidence that the rule is wrong for their own cases, and will not pool it. We describe a learning mechanism for that setting whose unit of change is an owned edit. The published rule is compiled exactly into a small ReLU network, a structured prior. Each party's learner searches a small typed space of declarations, a few named values such as subject, scope, form and size, and proposes one only when its own records pass a selection-aware evidence gate. The learning is that gated search around the prior. In two analyses registered before they ran, on synthetic data with 20 sampled hidden truths, the learner at 30 records per party was non-inferior, within 2 points, to all seven tuned baselines (a confined gradient learner, Ditto, FedRep, federated averaging, two pooled networks and pooled gradient-boosted trees), scoring 96.6% against 91.7% for the unchanged rule; the same search without the prior or the gate scored 84.0%. In a third registered test, typed edits over a network trained on shared records beat that network alone by 5.4 points and the same edits over the published rule by 3.1. An acceptance body recomputes each edit from its declaration and writes it into units reserved for that party, so by construction an edit changes no decision about any other subject, withdraws exactly, discloses at most log2 of the typed space's size in bits, and can hide nothing beyond its declared values. We report where it loses: truths shared by all parties, relaxations, ten records, a pre-stated gate criterion that failed in both runs, and a language model given the records that decided about as accurately on one draw. On a rule-only base, a table of owned exceptions makes the same decisions.

</details>


### [30] [Wayfinder: Adaptive Resource Routing from Agent Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16053)
*Miguel Romero,George Karypis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自适应路由器 Wayfinder，以下游智能体引用的文档作为反馈更新资源表示，从而在语义重叠资源环境下实现更优的路由；并构建并发布三个受控测试床（NFCorpus、FEVER、Tech Startup）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体需跨聊天、issue 跟踪、邮件、知识库等多资源收集上下文，但不知道信息所在位置，导致在连续交互中重复探索同一资源。常见做法将每个资源视为一个工具、依赖高层描述泛化到未见环境，但这类描述难以区分相似或重叠资源，且无法从后续交互中改进。

Method: Wayfinder 将下游智能体引用的文档作为"相关信息位置"的反馈信号，据此更新内部资源表示，使后续交互优先访问可能含相关信息的资源；同时在离线强化学习中引入资源增强（匿名化并置换资源身份），以抑制对"某资源承载某主题"的训练期过拟合。

Result: 消融表明资源增强在训练环境较少时收益最大；在共享闭环协议下与监督式、工具检索和上下文赌博机基线对比，Wayfinder 在资源内容语义重叠、静态描述无法区分时路由效果最佳；而当资源来自彼此独立的语料时，基于描述的工具检索已足够。

Conclusion: 以引用文档为反馈的自适应路由优于静态描述式工具检索，但优势依赖于资源语义重叠程度；同时现有公开专业环境缺失，故需构建受控测试床以支撑该问题的系统评估。

Abstract: To act effectively, agents must gather contextual information across resources such as chats, issue trackers, email and knowledge bases, to answer questions like "what was decided in the last project meeting?" or "where is the FAQ for our internal tooling?" Where this information lives in a user's environment is unknown to the agent, so it re-explores the same resources across consecutive interactions. Common routing approaches represent each resource as a tool and rely on high-level descriptions to generalize to unseen environments. Such descriptions often fail to separate similar or overlapping resources and cannot improve from the agent's subsequent interactions. We introduce Wayfinder, an adaptive router that uses the documents cited by the downstream agent as feedback about where relevant information resides. These citations update Wayfinder's internal resource representations, which subsequent interactions use to prioritize the resources likely to contain relevant information. We also introduce resource augmentation during offline reinforcement learning, which anonymizes and permutes resource identities to discourage training-time overfitting of which resource holds which topic; our ablations suggest it helps most when few training environments are available. Because no openly available professional environments pair realistic queries with resource-access patterns, we construct and release three controlled testbeds derived from existing datasets: NFCorpus, FEVER and Tech Startup. Compared with supervised, tool-retrieval and contextual bandit baselines under a shared closed-loop protocol, Wayfinder gives the best routing when resources contain semantically overlapping content that static descriptions cannot distinguish, while description-based tool retrieval suffices when resources are drawn from distinct corpora.

</details>


### [31] [Generalize Then Personalize: Wearable-Based Heart Rate Forecasting Under Scarce Ruck-March Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14849)
*Muntaqim Ahmed Raju,Ruizhe Ma,John W. Ramsay,David J. Cornell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在负重行军场景的稀缺数据条件下，比较三种训练范式（仅人群训练POT、从零个性化PFST、人群预训练微调PPFT），发现PPFT在各网络结构与预测时域下总体优于POT和PFST，预训练+个性化微调是可行的穿戴式心率预测策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心率预测可为负重行军中的近未来心血管响应提供提前信息，支撑生理监测与负荷管理决策；但大规模行军数据难以采集，形成"必须跨被试泛化、又要用少量个人数据适配个体"的稀缺数据问题。

Method: 使用8名美国陆军现役卫生兵、13次行军会话的数据，对比POT、PFST、PPFT三种训练范式；骨干网络为LSTM、Attention-LSTM、CNN-LSTM，并以persistence（持续性）作为基线；评估30 s与60 s两种预测时域，指标为MAE、RMSE、终点MAE；采用被试级配对分析与三个随机种子平均，并做Holm多重比较校正。

Result: 在所有神经结构与两种时域上，PPFT的平均MAE、RMSE、端点MAE均低于POT与PFST；相对PFST，PPFT将轨迹MAE降低2.22–4.30 bpm、端点MAE降低1.24–2.88 bpm。6项轨迹MAE比较经Holm校正后全部显著（p_adj=0.0469），6项端点MAE比较中4项仍显著。30 s时persistence的MAE最低（3.555±1.274 bpm），CNN-LSTM PPFT的RMSE最低（4.669±1.916 bpm），LSTM PPFT端点MAE最低（5.093±2.365 bpm）；60 s时Attention-LSTM PPFT的MAE（5.573±1.939 bpm）与RMSE（7.134±2.260 bpm）最低，CNN-LSTM PPFT端点MAE最低（7.553±2.357 bpm）。

Conclusion: 人群预训练后再进行被试特异微调，是稀缺数据条件下穿戴式心率预测的一种实用策略；但短时域上简单persistence基线在MAE上仍具竞争力，说明模型收益主要体现在RMSE与端点预测及较长时域。

Abstract: Heart rate (HR) forecasting may provide advance information about near-future cardiovascular response, potentially supporting physiological monitoring and workload-management decisions during ruck marching. However, robust model development is constrained by the difficulty of collecting large-scale ruck-march datasets, creating a scarce-data problem in which models must generalize across participants while adapting to individuals from limited personal data. To address this problem, we benchmark population-only training (POT), personalized-from-scratch training (PFST), and population-pretrained fine-tuning (PPFT) for HR forecasting during loaded ruck marching using data from eight active-duty U.S. Army medics across thirteen sessions. LSTM, Attention-LSTM, and CNN-LSTM backbones were evaluated against a persistence baseline at 30 s and 60 s horizons. Across all neural architectures and horizons, PPFT yielded lower mean MAE, RMSE, and endpoint MAE than both POT and PFST. Participant-level paired analysis after averaging across three training seeds showed that, relative to PFST, PPFT reduced trajectory MAE by 2.22-4.30 bpm and endpoint MAE by 1.24-2.88 bpm. All six trajectory-MAE comparisons remained significant after Holm adjustment ($p_{adj}$ = 0.0469), while four of six endpoint-MAE comparisons remained significant after adjustment. At 30 s, persistence achieved the lowest MAE (3.555 $\pm$ 1.274 bpm), while CNN-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest RMSE (4.669 $\pm$ 1.916 bpm); LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest endpoint MAE (5.093 $\pm$ 2.365 bpm). At 60 s, Attention-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest MAE (5.573 $\pm$ 1.939 bpm) and RMSE (7.134 $\pm$ 2.260 bpm), while CNN-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest endpoint MAE (7.553 $\pm$ 2.357 bpm). These results suggest that population pretraining followed by participant-specific fine-tuning may provide a practical strategy for scarce-data wearable HR forecasting.

</details>


### [32] [Robustness Within a Fairness Budget: Parity Frontiers and Equal-Opportunity Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16031)
*Pablo Rivas,Jeremy Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将二分类建模为平方软群体均等（DP）预算下的PGD对抗风险最小化，用投影乘子与单侧惩罚更新求解；在Adult Income上，对抗训练与DP约束可联合获得稳健且更平等的操作点，但单一公平指标不能替代完整的群体率审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练能保护攻击下的预测性能，却不决定决策在受保护群体间的分布；因此需要研究稳健性与群体公平能否同时实现，以及单一公平指标是否足以刻画群体差异。

Method: 将二分类表述为平方软DP预算下的PGD对抗风险最小化问题，采用投影乘子法与单侧惩罚更新优化；在Adult Income数据集上（二元性别字段仅用于审计、不参与预测），以逻辑回归和单隐层MLP为模型，在3组配对随机种子、4种PGD强度、5种预算下做对照实验，并与ERM和纯PGD训练比较。

Result: ε=0.3时，PGD训练相较ERM将平衡准确率分别提升7.77和11.45个百分点，并消除攻击导致的硬DPD增量的85.7%和89.5%。在内点τ=0.015处，约束训练使DPD相对PGD训练下降52.2%和49.2%，同时平衡准确率仍高于ERM 3.18和8.34个百分点。全部8条种子平均的模型-攻击路径上，预算路径严格排序DPD与准确率，并使平均带符号机会均等差异（EOpp）发生反转；速率定理给出反转所需的精确真阳率偏移，实测群体率满足该条件。

Conclusion: 在该受控特征空间研究中，稳健性与均等性存在可用的联合操作点；但DPD下降与EOpp反转可能同时出现，说明单一公平指标无法替代完整的群体率审计。

Abstract: Adversarial training protects predictive performance under attack but does not determine how decisions are distributed across protected groups. We formulate binary classification as projected gradient descent (PGD) adversarial-risk minimization under a squared soft demographic-parity (DP) budget, optimized with projected multiplier and one-sided penalty updates. On Adult Income, using the binary sex field supplied by the benchmark for group auditing but excluding it from prediction, we test logistic regression and a one-hidden-layer multilayer perceptron (MLP) across three paired seeds, four PGD strengths, and five budgets. At $\varepsilon=0.3$, PGD training improves balanced accuracy over empirical-risk minimization (ERM) by $7.77$ and $11.45$ percentage points and removes $85.7\%$ and $89.5\%$ of attack-induced growth in hard demographic-parity difference (DPD). At the representative interior value $τ=0.015$, constrained training cuts DPD relative to PGD training by $52.2\%$ and $49.2\%$, while remaining $3.18$ and $8.34$ balanced-accuracy points above ERM. Across all eight seed-averaged model-attack paths, the sampled budget path strictly orders DPD and accuracy and reverses mean signed equal-opportunity difference (EOpp). A rate theorem gives the exact true-positive-rate shift required for reversal, and the measured group rates satisfy it. Within this controlled feature-space study, robustness and parity admit useful joint operating points, but one fairness metric cannot replace a full group-rate audit.

</details>


### [33] [FOE-Dreamer: Deployment-Efficient Learning of Cyber Defense Policies in Operational Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14880)
*Yinuo Du,Christopher Kiekintveld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOE-Dreamer：一种基于模型的强化学习框架，可在真实云网络（OpenStack测试床）中在线直接训练防御者，无需仿真器阶段，通过分解潜在状态与对手嵌入提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主网络防御多用深度强化学习，但多在仿真器中训练，存在保真度差距（认证延迟、部分动作失败、服务依赖、用户流量模式等未被建模），导致迁移性能下降；而在真实网络中训练虽消除差距，却交互缓慢昂贵，样本效率成为瓶颈。基于模型的强化学习已在游戏与机器人上大幅降低样本需求，但尚未用于在运营网络中训练的防御者。

Method: 提出FOE-Dreamer（Factored Opponent-Enhanced Dreamer）：将潜在状态中的三类变异源分离——由防御者动作驱动的可控分量、由正常用户活动驱动的外生分量、以及仅从防御者可观测信号推断的对手嵌入。在"想象"中规划时，分量分离降低了策略梯度方差。

Result: 在8主机OpenStack测试床上，针对两种脚本化攻击者配置、用户活动由GHOSTS框架模拟，FOE-Dreamer在单GPU、3天预算内完成训练；在同等算力下，回合损失（episode loss）约为Rainbow和IQN基线的2倍改善。消融显示分解机制与对手模型均有贡献，且学到的潜在表示与预期分离一致。

Conclusion: 基于模型的强化学习结合状态分解与对手建模，可有效用于真实运营网络中的在线自主防御训练，显著提升样本效率并消除仿真到现实的保真度差距。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber defense is widely studied with deep reinforcement learning, but most work trains policies inside simulators because learning requires many trial-and-error interactions. Simulator-based training carries a fidelity gap: trained policies face authentication delays, partial action failures, service dependencies, and user traffic patterns that the simulator did not represent, and degrade on transfer. Training directly in the network being defended removes the gap but makes every interaction slow and costly, so sample efficiency becomes the binding constraint. Model-based reinforcement learning has reduced sample requirements on games and physical robots by orders of magnitude by planning inside a learned world model, but it has not been applied to defenders trained in operational networks. We present FOE-Dreamer (Factored Opponent-Enhanced Dreamer), a model-based reinforcement learning framework that trains a defender online and directly in a network on cloud infrastructure, without a simulator stage.
