FOE-Dreamer separates three sources of variation in the latent state: a controllable component driven by defender actions, an exogenous component driven by benign user activity, and an opponent embedding inferred from defender-observable signals alone. Separating the components reduces the variance in policy gradients during planning in imagination. Against two scripted attacker profiles with user activity emulated by the GHOSTS framework on an 8-host OpenStack testbed, FOE-Dreamer trains within a 3-day budget on a single GPU and achieves about 2$\times$ better episode loss than Rainbow and IQN baselines under matched compute. Both the factoring and the opponent model contribute to the gain, and the learned latents are consistent with the intended separation.

</details>


### [34] [Social Engineering Attack Detection in Corporate Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14897)
*Hanzla Hamid,Pappu Jha,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用真实数据集构建基线机器学习模型检测钓鱼邮件，并进一步探索可解释的心理操纵/说服线索特征（紧迫感、信任、贪婪等）在钓鱼检测中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会工程攻击日益复杂，邮件、聊天与电话中真假通信难以区分，可能泄露商业秘密并形成从基层员工到高管的攻击链条，因此需要基于真实数据研究并构建检测方法。

Method: 使用真实世界数据集构建TF-IDF机器学习模型对邮件进行钓鱼/安全分类；在合并邮件数据集上提取基于词典的可解释操纵指标（紧迫、信任、贪婪及其他说服线索），并独立评估这些操纵导向特征的判别能力及跨邮件来源的可迁移性。

Result: TF-IDF分类模型准确率超过90%；仅使用操纵导向特征即取得ROC-AUC 0.74（在留出测试集上），并检验了其向其他留出邮件来源的迁移效果。

Conclusion: 表层的词法特征（TF-IDF）已能高效检测钓鱼邮件，而心理操纵与说服线索本身也携带显著信号，为可解释、互补的钓鱼检测提供了依据。

Abstract: Social Engineering attacks continue to pose a significant risk to the corporate world with a growing level of sophistication. It is difficult to distinguish between authentic and phishing communication via email, chat, and phone. They pose a risk of exposing trade secrets and confidential communications and can create a chain of attack from lower-level employees up to executives. Therefore, we study this growing threat using real-world datasets to build baseline machine-learning models. Our TF-IDF models classify emails as phishing or safe, achieving accuracy above 90%. We build on this by exploring whether psychological manipulation and persuasion indicators can be measured to play a significant role in detecting phishing. Instead of relying solely on surface-level lexical features, we derive interpretable lexicon-based indicators of urgency, trust, greed, and other persuasion cues from the combined email dataset. By evaluating these manipulation-oriented features on their own, we measure how much signal they carry (ROC-AUC of 0.74 on a held-out test split) and test whether they transfer to held-out email sources.

</details>


### [35] [Epigraph Form Reinforcement Learning-based Safe Trajectory Tracking Control for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14903)
*Xiaoke Yang,Jin Chen,Yafei Wang,Qi Zhang,Ming Li,Haiwen Wu,Chengliang Yin,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STTC-EFRL强化学习框架，将安全轨迹跟踪建模为epigraph形式min-max优化，用递减标量调节策略梯度方向以获得训练稳定性，并设计射线投射法量化安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV在高动态环境中的安全轨迹跟踪（STT）具有挑战性：非学习方法为满足实时性采用简化车辆与交互模型，跟踪精度次优；主流Lagrangian DRL因拉格朗日乘子增大导致策略梯度幅值放大，训练不稳定。

Method: 将STT问题表述为epigraph形式min-max优化问题；基于该表述设计学习机制，用一个与epigraph相关的递减标量调整策略梯度下降方向而不放大梯度幅值；并提出计算高效的射线投射方法，用于计算多面体缩放因子以量化安全性。

Result: 实验（含Vehicle-in-the-Loop测试）表明：STTC-EFRL跟踪精度高于所比较的非学习基线，训练稳定性优于常用Lagrangian DRL方法，并在网联环境下满足安全要求。

Conclusion: epigraph形式建模配合递减标量梯度调节，可在不增大梯度幅值的前提下实现稳定训练，兼顾跟踪精度与安全性，适用于高动态CAV场景。

Abstract: Safe trajectory tracking (STT) aims to enable autonomous vehicles to follow a reference trajectory accurately while avoiding collisions. This problem is particularly challenging for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) in highly dynamic environments. Existing non-learning-based STT methods often use simplified vehicle and interaction models to meet real-time requirements, which may result in suboptimal tracking accuracy. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) can help address this limitation, widely used Lagrangian DRL methods often suffer from unstable training because increasing Lagrange multipliers amplify policy-gradient magnitudes. To address these issues, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework for STT, called STT Control Epigraph Form RL (STTC-EFRL). In this framework, the STT problem is formulated as an epigraph-form min-max optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we develop a learning mechanism that uses a decreasing scalar related to the epigraph to adjust the policy gradient descent direction without increasing the gradient magnitude, thereby improving training stability. We also develop a computationally efficient ray-casting method to compute a polyhedron scaling factor for safety quantification. Experimental results, including Vehicle-in-the-Loop tests, show that STTC-EFRL achieves higher tracking accuracy than the considered non-learning-based baseline and more stable training than widely used Lagrangian DRL methods, while satisfying safety requirements in connected environments.

</details>


### [36] [Rethinking "RL Generalizes, SFT Memorizes": The Role of SFT Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14912)
*Yunlong Hou,Fengzhuo Zhang,Yuan Cheng,Jiachun Pan,Xingyao Li,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了SFT与RL在推理和泛化能力上的差异，指出SFT的训练数据（来源与规模）是关键，匹配正确轨迹数量或总rollout预算后SFT可达到甚至超越RL的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为带可验证奖励的RL训练能带来强推理与广泛泛化，而SFT更易记忆、迁移有限。作者希望从SFT训练数据角度重新检视该区分，探究差距究竟源自训练范式还是数据本身。

Method: 在Qwen2.5与Qwen3系列模型上开展实证研究：(1) 考察数据来源，比较精心混合的SFT数据集与更大模型单独生成的数据；(2) 考察数据规模，对齐SFT与RL的正确rollout数量及总rollout预算；(3) 用LLM标注刻画训练rollout中的解法类型，分析尾部解法的可泛化信号；(4) 通过分析浅层Transformer在RL与SFT下的训练动力学给出理论支撑。

Result: 精心混合的SFT数据集显著优于仅由更大模型生成的数据；匹配正确rollout数量可大幅提升SFT泛化，匹配总rollout预算则使SFT泛化与RL相当；两者结合可使SFT泛化优于RL。大 数据集覆盖更多尾部解法，这些尾部解法提供可泛化的推理信号。

Conclusion: SFT与RL的泛化差距在很大程度上可由训练数据的来源与规模解释，而非范式固有缺陷；SFT在合适数据条件下可匹配甚至超过RL，理论分析亦支持该结论。

Abstract: Large language models trained with Reinforcement Learning (RL) with verifiable rewards exhibit strong reasoning ability and broad generalization, whereas models trained with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) are often viewed as more prone to memorization and limited transfer. This paper rethinks this distinction through the lens of SFT training data on widely used Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 series models. First, we study the role of data source and show that it is critical: a carefully mixed SFT dataset substantially outperforms data generated solely by a larger model. Second, we study the role of data scale and show that matching the number of correct rollouts between SFT and RL greatly improves SFT generalization, while matching the total rollout budget enables SFT to generalize as well as RL. Combining these two factors further enables SFT to generalize even better than RL. Third, by using LLM annotations to characterize the solution methods in training rollouts, we show that larger datasets cover more tail methods and that these tail methods provide generalizable reasoning signals. Finally, we support these empirical findings theoretically by analyzing the training dynamics of shallow transformers under both RL and SFT.

</details>


### [37] [A Transferable Pretrained 3D Model for Impedance Inversion with Sparse-Well Prior Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14922)
*Gen Li,Zhaoyun Zong,Diqiong Jiang,Man Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可迁移三维地震阻抗反演框架，将离线预训练与目标工区部署解耦，冻结网络即可用单口井完成反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的阻抗反演通常需针对每个目标工区重新训练，且在井控极稀疏时难以泛化到井位以外区域。

Method: 用掩码自编码器在多工区无标签数据上学习地震结构表征；将冻结编码器嵌入反演网络，并用合成地质模型（含稀疏井导出的低频先验）训练；部署时反演网络全部固定，仅用可用井构建低频先验并对齐全局阻抗范围。

Result: Overthrust模型上全数据体NRMSE最低，P95道级NRMSE由0.1310降至0.1074（相对最佳目标训练基线），不同井位下局部大误差更少；实际工区（1口条件井、3口盲井）盲井平均NRMSE较目标训练U-Net降低20.95%，相关性最高。

Conclusion: 该框架能以低部署成本、无需重训反演网络地把阻抗信息迁移到条件井位之外。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based seismic impedance inversion commonly requires retraining for each target survey, which may generalize poorly beyond the conditioning locations when well control is extremely sparse. We propose a transferable three-dimensional inversion framework that separates offline learning from target-survey deployment. A masked autoencoder first learns seismic structural representations from unlabeled multi-survey data, after which the frozen encoder is incorporated into an inversion network trained on synthetic geological models with sparse-well-derived low-frequency priors. During deployment, the complete inversion network remains fixed; the available conditioning well is used only to construct the low-frequency prior and align the global impedance range. Experiments on the Overthrust model show that the proposed method achieves the lowest full-volume NRMSE and reduces the P95 trace-wise NRMSE from 0.1310 to 0.1074 relative to the best target-trained baseline, indicating fewer localized large errors under different well locations. In a field survey with one conditioning well and three blind wells, it reduces the mean blind-well NRMSE by 20.95% relative to the target-trained U-Net while maintaining the highest correlation. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework transfers useful impedance information beyond the conditioning location with low deployment cost and without retraining the inversion network.

</details>


### [38] [Binding Mode Matters: Hotspot-Aware Drug Discovery via Explorative Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14956)
*Dingshuo Chen,Hao Yang,Kuangqi Zhou,Zhixun Li,Qiang Liu,Xiangxiang Zeng,Shu Wu,Yaosen Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出BindMol，将基于靶点的药物设计重构为多目标探索任务，每个目标对应一个特定热点的相互作用增强，并用定制多目标强化学习生成兼具高亲和力、结构新颖性与多样结合模式的分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式方法主要优化单一标量对接分数，掩盖了各关键结合决定因素的独立贡献，难以获得多样化的结合模式，而后者对实验验证至关重要。

Method: 将靶点导向的药物设计表述为多目标探索问题，每个子目标显式对应增强与某一特定蛋白热点的相互作用；构建BindMol生成框架，由定制化的多目标强化学习算法驱动，并在训练中引入探索性偏好以高效发现具有多样且理想结合特征的分子。

Result: 实验表明BindMol能够发现兼具高亲和力、结构新颖性与多样结合模式的化合物；在基于靶点的药物发现和多性质优化任务上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 以热点级多目标强化学习替代标量打分优化，为面向目标的药物发现提供了一种通用范式。

Abstract: Discovering hit molecules requires not just high binding affinity, but also the identification of diverse binding modes, which are critical for experimental assays. Existing generative approaches have predominantly relied on optimizing a scalar docking score, obscuring the distinct contributions of key binding determinants. To this end, we introduce a paradigm shift by formulating target-based drug design as a multi-objective exploration task, where each objective explicitly corresponds to enhancing interactions with a specific hotspot. Here, we introduce BindMol, a novel generative framework driven by a customized multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithm. By incorporating explorative preferences during training, our approach efficiently uncovers molecules with diverse and desirable binding profiles. Empirical results demonstrate that BindMol facilitates the discovery of high-affinity compounds characterized by both structural novelty and diverse binding modes. Validated across target-based drug discovery and multi-property optimization tasks, our approach provides a versatile paradigm for goal-oriented drug discovery.

</details>


### [39] [Poison-then-Hide: Finetuning-Activated Backdoor Attack on Pretrained Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14970)
*Qixuan Jin,Abinitha Gourabathina,Vinith Menon Suriyakumar,Walter Gerych,Marzyeh Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Poison-then-Hide 后门攻击：在预训练编码器中植入休眠后门，经干净下游数据全模型微调后激活，六个数据集与三种架构上ASR最高97%，且标准防御无法彻底移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"微调激活型"后门攻击假设域偏移有限或编码器层冻结，因而在全模型微调（domain transfer 下的完整参数更新）场景下失效，需要一种在该设定下仍隐蔽有效的攻击。

Method: 三部分组成：(1) 触发器优化；(2) 基础编码器投毒（在预训练编码器中植入休眠后门）；(3) 定向遗忘（targeted unlearning）以隐藏后门。关键发现是联合学习良性目标任务与投毒过程对攻击成功至关重要。

Result: 在6个数据集、3种模型架构上评估，攻击成功率达SOTA水平（最高97%）；标准检测与缓解防御无法完全移除后门，且后门在良性微调后可重新出现。

Conclusion: 全模型微调场景下的休眠后门攻击可行且隐蔽，联合学习良性任务与投毒是成功关键，现有防御对此类攻击不足，亟需更强的后门检测与清除方法。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks threaten the integrity of machine learning models by allowing attackers to control model behavior through triggers. Models can be compromised during finetuning by backdoor attacks through either poisoned data or adversarial training objectives. Because existing finetuning-activated attacks assume limited domain shift or frozen encoder layers, they often fail under full-model finetuning. We target a stealthy finetuning-activated attack where a dormant backdoor is implanted in a pretrained encoder, and later activated by finetuning on clean downstream data. We propose Poison-then-Hide, a novel attack that remains effective when the entire model is finetuned in a domain transfer. Our approach consists of three components: trigger optimization, base encoder poisoning, and targeted unlearning to conceal the backdoor. We evaluate our method on six datasets and three model architectures, and achieve state-of-the-art (up to 97%) attack success rates. We find that jointly learning the benign target task alongside the poisoning is critical to the attack's success. We demonstrate standard detection and mitigation defenses cannot fully remove the backdoor, which can reappear after benign finetuning.

</details>


### [40] [EASTAR: Event-Anchored Semantic and Task-Adaptive Region Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14990)
*Peng Liang,Genan Dai,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EASTAR：以线下活动事件为语义锚点，将聚合出行表示分解为多目的语义通道，并用任务自适应提示路由适配下游任务的城市区域表示学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于移动性的区域表示方法多从聚合OD矩阵学习，不同活动目的驱动的出行被混合为"目的无关"的交互模式；POI、土地利用等静态属性虽能补充语义，但未将移动交互显式锚定到活动目的信号上，导致下游适配需在纠缠嵌入上进行，难以选取任务相关的功能语义。

Method: 提出EASTAR框架：(1) 以线下活动事件作为稀疏语义锚点，将聚合移动性表示分解为多个与目的相关的语义通道；(2) 引入自由通道（free channels）捕捉事件数据未覆盖的潜在移动模式，并设全局通道保留整体空间结构；(3) 设计任务自适应提示路由器，以LLM先验初始化任务-通道相关性，并通过下游监督细化路由权重。

Result: 在两个真实城市数据集、四个预测任务上，EASTAR稳定优于代表性基线，MAE最高降低39.15%；消融与可解释性分析验证了事件锚定、语义通道构建与任务自适应路由的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式以活动目的信号锚定移动交互、并按任务自适应选择语义通道，可有效缓解目的无关嵌入的纠缠问题，提升城市区域表示在下游任务中的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Urban region representation learning supports a wide range of urban analytics tasks by encoding regional functions and interactions, with human mobility serving as a central signal because it directly captures inter-region interactions. However, most mobility-based methods learn from aggregated origin-destination matrices, where trips driven by different activity purposes are mixed into purpose-agnostic interaction patterns. Although static urban attributes such as POIs and land use can enrich region-level semantics, they do not explicitly ground mobility interactions in activity-purpose signals. As a result, downstream adaptation is often performed over entangled embeddings, making it difficult to select task-relevant functional semantics. To address these limitations, we propose EASTAR, an event-anchored and task-adaptive framework for urban region representation learning. EASTAR treats offline activity events as sparse semantic anchors and uses them to factorize aggregated mobility representations into multiple purpose-related semantic channels. It further introduces free channels to capture latent mobility patterns not covered by event data and a global channel to preserve overall spatial structure. To adapt the learned channel space to different downstream objectives, EASTAR designs a task-adaptive prompt router that initializes task-channel relevance from LLM-derived priors and refines the routing weights through downstream supervision. Experiments on two real-world urban datasets across four prediction tasks show that EASTAR consistently outperforms representative baselines, achieving up to 39.15% reduction in MAE. Ablation and interpretability analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness of event anchoring, semantic channel construction, and task-adaptive routing.

</details>


### [41] [AI4Plasma I: Data-Efficient Surrogate Modeling of Capacitively Coupled Plasma Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15002)
*Zhenyu Zou,Junjue Liao,Hao Yang,Luowei Yin,Chenyu Wang,Yufeng Wei,Zhichao Peng,Chun Chen,Daniel Zhengyu Huang,Kangxin Liu,Haoran Guan,Rubing Han,Yipei Chen,Peiting You,Yuwei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较五种代理模型（Coordinate MLP、PCA-Net、U-Net、FNO、Transolver）在CCP等离子体仿真中的**数据效率**，以达到1%/2%平均相对L2误差所需的参考仿真数量为指标。FNO在八项比较中的七项取得最低或并列最低需求，被视为所测CCP设定下数据高效的基线架构，但最优架构依赖精度目标与参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCP参考仿真能给出空间分辨的等离子体场，但跨工况生成成本高昂；需要评估哪类代理架构能以最少参考仿真数据达到给定精度，从而降低仿真成本。

Method: 在1D平板与2D轴对称GEC CCP基准上，对比Coordinate MLP、PCA-Net、U-Net、FNO、Transolver五种架构，预测RF周期平均的电子密度、离子密度、电势与电子温度；以参数内部与参数边界测试用例达到1%和2%平均相对L2误差所需的参考仿真数为度量；并分析FNO谱模态与padding、U-Net跳跃连接、Transolver自适应切片、PCA-Net投影误差与预测误差关系等架构机制。

Result: FNO在8项比较中7项最低或并列最低。1D：FNO以50例达1%内部误差，Transolver需100例、U-Net需200例。2D：FNO与U-Net各以100例达1%内部误差；FNO与Transolver各以200例达2%参数边界误差，U-Net需400例。更严格的2D 1%参数边界目标下，Transolver需400例，FNO需800例。

Conclusion: FNO是所测CCP设定下数据高效的基线架构；但首选架构取决于精度目标和运行参数区域（内部vs边界、1%vs2%），不存在单一占优方案。

Abstract: Reference capacitively coupled plasma (CCP) simulations provide spatially resolved plasma fields but are costly to generate across operating conditions. We compare the data efficiency of five surrogate architectures--Coordinate MLP, PCA-Net, U-Net, Fourier neural operator (FNO), and Transolver--for predicting RF-cycle-averaged electron density, ion density, electric potential, and electron temperature in 1D planar and 2D axisymmetric GEC CCP benchmarks. We assess the number of reference simulations needed to achieve 1% and 2% mean relative L2 error for parameter-interior and parameter-boundary test cases, and examine architecture-specific mechanisms. FNO achieves the lowest or joint-lowest reference-case requirement in seven of eight comparisons. In 1D, FNO reaches 1% interior error with 50 training cases, compared with 100 for Transolver and 200 for U-Net. In 2D, FNO and U-Net each reach 1% interior error with 100 cases; FNO and Transolver each reach 2% parameter-boundary error with 200 cases, compared with 400 for U-Net. At the stricter 1% 2D parameter-boundary target, Transolver requires 400 cases versus 800 for FNO. Additional analyses investigate FNO spectral modes and padding, U-Net skip connections, Transolver adaptive slicing, and PCA-Net projection versus prediction error. The results support FNO as a data-efficient baseline for the tested CCP settings, while showing that the preferred architecture depends on the accuracy target and operating-parameter region.

</details>


### [42] [A Cardiorespiratory Foundation Model Pretrained on Ten Physiological Signals for Perioperative and Critical Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15004)
*Cheol-Hui Lee,Wookyung Kim,Hyeonhoon Lee,Garam Lee,Da-in Eun,Hyung-Chul Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARMEN——一个在10种心肺波形（2.27百万模态小时）上无标签预训练的统一编码器，通过LSCNorm恢复归一化所丢失的绝对信号水平，在19项跨韩国与美国队列的任务中，冻结编码器全面超越监督模型与现有基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理波形基础模型几乎只基于心电图（ECG）和光电容积脉搏波（PPG），而动脉压、静脉压、肺动脉压、颅内压及呼吸机波形同样指导手术室与ICU的临床决策，缺乏覆盖多种心肺模态的统一基础模型。

Method: 构建单一编码器CARMEN，在10种共记录的心肺波形（2.27百万模态小时）上无标签预训练；输入支持10秒至5分钟、任意共记录信号组合；提出条件机制LSCNorm，在归一化后恢复绝对信号水平；以下游冻结编码器方式在来自韩国与美国队列的19项任务上评估。

Result: 冻结编码器在15项任务中的12项优于监督模型；在19项任务的14–17项上优于3个基础模型（1个通用模型、2个生物信号模型），且无任何任务被超越；仅输入ECG或PPG时，多数比较中达到或超过信号专用基础模型；缺失通道情况下性能保持稳定。消融显示LSCNorm至关重要：去除后术中低血压AUROC由0.894降至0.780。

Conclusion: 多模态心肺波形的统一无标签预训练可学习可迁移的通用表征，绝对信号水平信息对低血压等任务不可或缺，CARMEN在跨队列、跨模态缺失条件下均表现出强鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Arterial, venous, pulmonary artery and intracranial pressures and ventilator waveforms guide decisions in operating rooms and intensive care units, yet foundation models for physiological waveforms have been built almost entirely on the electrocardiogram and photoplethysmogram. We present CARMEN, a single encoder pretrained without labels on ten cardiorespiratory waveforms (2.27 million modality-hours). CARMEN accepts any combination of co-recorded signals from 10 s to 5 min, and a conditioning mechanism, LSCNorm, restores the absolute signal level that normalization removes; without it, the AUROC for intraoperative hypotension fell from 0.894 to 0.780. Across 19 tasks from Korean and US cohorts, the frozen encoder outperformed supervised models on 12 of 15 and three foundation models, one general-purpose and two for biosignals, on 14 to 17 of 19, with none exceeding it on any task. Given the electrocardiogram or photoplethysmogram alone, it matched or exceeded signal-specific foundation models in most comparisons, and it retained its performance when channels were missing.

</details>


### [43] [From Forecasting to World Modeling: Can Temporal Foundation Models Recover Causal Response?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15009)
*Juwon Hong,Minha Hwang,Venkatesh Shankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预测精度高并不代表价格效应估计准确；表格/时序基础模型的额外上下文只改善预测不改善因果效应；工具变量仅在特定形式下降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售商需要理解销量对价格变化的因果响应，而现有基础模型多以预测精度为评价标准，本文检验"高预测精度是否意味着准确的价格因果效应"这一被忽视的假设。

Method: 评估已发布的表格与时间序列基础模型，以及一个专门预训练用于因果效应估计的基础模型；在真值已知的模拟数据与真实零售销售记录上，比较预测精度与价格效应估计精度，并考察额外上下文与工具变量（原始形式 vs. 特定形式）的影响。

Result: 三项发现：(1) 在模拟数据和真实零售数据上，预测精度均不指示价格效应预测的准确度；(2) 对表格基础模型，增加上下文改善预测但未改善价格效应估计；(3) 工具变量作为标准计量经济学补救手段，仅在以特定形式提供时降低误差，而在混杂情形下以原始工具变量形式提供时对所有模型都会增大误差。

Conclusion: 预测能力与因果效应估计能力存在脱节，不应以预测精度作为因果推断质量的代理指标；基础模型在价格弹性等因果任务上的应用需谨慎，工具变量的使用形式对结果至关重要。

Abstract: A model that forecasts sales accurately can still mislead a retailer about how sales respond to a price change. We evaluate released tabular and time-series foundation models, as well as a foundation model pretrained specifically to estimate causal effects. We report three findings. First, forecast accuracy does not indicate the accuracy of the predicted effect of price, both on simulated data in which the true effect is known and on real retail sales records. Second, for the tabular foundation models, additional context improves forecasts but not the predicted effect of price. Third, instrumental variables, the standard remedy in econometrics, reduce this error only when supplied in a specific form and increase it for every model under confounding when supplied as raw instruments.

</details>


### [44] [SkinAdvisor: An AI-Driven Decision Framework for Skin Production Planning via Multi-Modal Transformer Sales Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15023)
*Ge Fan,Kai Wang,Chaoyun Zhang,Yingjie Li,Yuze Liu,Junyang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出已上线的 AI 皮肤生产决策框架 SkinAdvisor，其核心预测器 SkinCast 为多模态 Transformer，单次非自回归解码即可预测未发布皮肤 180 天逐日销售轨迹，并接入 ROI 排序服务流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网游的皮肤收入是主要营收支柱，但"做哪些皮肤、何时发布、如何定价"仍依赖直觉与事后分析，缺乏前瞻性的量化决策支持；同时需在线上低延迟约束下满足生产级可用性。

Method: SkinCast 融合四个编码器：预训练 Informer 编码玩家偏好时序、预训练 Player-Hero-Skin 图嵌入提供关系先验、两个轻量 Transformer 分别编码皮肤属性与发布上下文；经带局部+全局 day-x 位置编码的共享骨干融合，由 N 层 Informer 一次性非自回归解码。SkinAdvisor 将预测接入 Select→Fill→Infer→Sales→ROI→Ranking 服务流水线，按预测 ROI 对候选皮肤排序并支持多候选对比。

Result: 离线实验中 SkinCast 较最强基线 Informer 降低 RMSE 约 20%，较经典方法降低约 60%；16 核 CPU 服务器压力测试下 p99 延迟低于 8ms，峰值吞吐超过 1100 rps；系统已完成一个完整发布周期的线上运行。

Conclusion: 两条部署经验：生产决策只需数量级精度即可；预训练嵌入可能泄漏时间信息，需采用基于赛季的数据划分加以规避。

Abstract: Skins, cosmetic in-game items, are a primary revenue stream for modern online games, yet production decisions (which skins to make, when to release, how to price) remain driven by intuition and retrospective analytics. We present SkinAdvisor, an AI-driven decision framework for skin production planning, powered by SkinCast, a deployed multi-modal Transformer forecasting 180-day per-day sales trajectories for unreleased skins. SkinCast couples four encoders: a pretrained Informer for player-preference time series, a pretrained Player-Hero-Skin graph embedder for relational priors, and two lightweight Transformers for skin attributes and release context, fused through a shared backbone with a local-plus-global day-x positional encoding and decoded by an N-layer Informer in a single non-autoregressive pass. SkinAdvisor plugs these forecasts into a serving pipeline (Select -> Fill -> Infer -> Sales -> ROI -> Ranking) that ranks candidate skins by forecast-based ROI and supports multi-candidate comparison. The system has been in production for a full release cycle. Offline experiments show SkinCast reduces RMSE by ~20% over the strongest baseline (Informer) and ~60% over classical methods; stress tests on a 16-core CPU server confirm p99 latency under 8ms and peak throughput above 1100 rps. We report two deployment lessons: order-of-magnitude accuracy suffices for production decisions, and pretrained embeddings can leak temporal information, requiring season-based splitting to prevent.

</details>


### [45] [Mask-Pattern Position Augmentation: Training-Free Long-Context Extrapolation for Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15029)
*Gaurav Sarkar,Syed Affan Daimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mask-Pattern Position Augmentation (MPPA)，利用dLLM特有的掩码演化信号做位置外推，在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上把4096窗口扩展到8192且困惑度反低于窗口内水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLM)靠双向注意力迭代去掩码生成，但RoPE仍使其受限于固定预训练窗口。现有免训练外推方案（如LongLLaDA）直接照搬自回归模型的NTK-RoPE缩放，在每个位置、每步去噪中施加同一个仅依赖长度的静态变换，忽略了dLLM独有的信号——部分解码序列中不断演化的掩码模式。

Method: MPPA分两步：先将位置索引线性插值压缩进预训练窗口；再依据累积掩码密度为每个位置、每个去噪步添加偏移量。该偏移补偿插值所造成的旋转相位分辨率损失，并随token被确定而逐渐衰减，使后期步骤趋近于干净的插值几何。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上，于4096窗口的2倍长度（8192 tokens）测试：困惑度由标准解码的19.51降至3.12，低于窗口内水平；passkey检索准确率100%，needle-in-a-haystack准确率不变。

Conclusion: 掩码条件化的位置工程是扩散原生的长上下文生成路径，初步2倍外推结果支持该方向。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate by iteratively unmasking tokens with bidirectional attention, but rotary position embeddings (RoPE) still confine them to a fixed pretraining window. Existing training-free extrapolation, notably LongLLaDA, copies NTK-based RoPE scaling from autoregressive (AR) models and applies one static, length-only transform at every position and denoising step. That transplant ignores a signal unique to dLLMs: the evolving mask pattern of the partially decoded sequence. We propose Mask-Pattern Position Augmentation (MPPA). MPPA linearly interpolates indices into the pretrained window, then adds a per-position, per-step offset from cumulative mask density. The offset restores rotary phase resolution that interpolation collapses, and it shrinks as tokens are committed, so late steps see near-clean interpolated geometry. On LLaDA-8B-Instruct at twice its 4096-token window, MPPA reduces perplexity from 19.51 under standard decoding to 3.12 at 8192 tokens, below the in-window figure, with 100% passkey retrieval and unchanged needle-in-a-haystack accuracy. These preliminary $2\times$ results motivate mask-conditioned position engineering as a diffusion-native path to long-context generation.

</details>


### [46] [AROMA: Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation for Vision-Language Model Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15041)
*Zhaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AROMA是一个冻结VLM的控制框架，将可解释性定位与可控性解耦为五个子问题（干预位置、几何与增益、到达目标、选择目标、何时干预），并通过因果验证的注意力头维度实现有限规模的有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制定位本身不能带来可用控制：因果敏感度可能只占很小激活能量、准确的局部方向在有限尺度下失效、可达的数值目标未必是正确目标。因此需要把定位、驱动、可达性、目标质量和选择性部署当作不同的控制问题分别处理。

Method: 提出AROMA（自适应表示导向多模态驱动）框架，在冻结的Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct上工作：先用因果验证的跨注意力头，量化其4个维度对基数敏感度与激活能量的解释比例；采用幅度归一化的因果子空间驱动；再提出迭代最小努力驱动（MEA）在匹配步数预算下逼近目标；并将目标与门控在评估前冻结，配合前瞻性路由与跨头/跨模型复现及归因引导基线对比。

Result: 4个因果维度解释89.82%的基数敏感度但仅9.52%的中位激活能量；幅度归一化后，在未触碰的程序化数据上精确计数准确率提升+8.00pp。MEA在匹配步数预算下达到81.25%的oracle目标，冻结方向仅15.18%。在N=630的新自然Stage-2队列上，目标与门控冻结的定向MEA将此前冻结策略从34.444%提升至38.730%（配对p=4.63e-7）；全部7,992例上为65.741% vs 65.153%，仅编辑63个输入。

Conclusion: 结果将定位、驱动、可达性、目标质量与选择性部署区分为独立的控制问题，说明因果定位须配合几何/增益归一化、迭代最小努力与前瞻路由才能转化为可用控制，并具备跨头、跨模型的复现性与对小样本编辑的高效性。

Abstract: Mechanistic localization does not by itself yield usable control: causal sensitivity may occupy little activation energy, an accurate local direction may fail at finite scale, and a reachable numeral target may be wrong. We introduce AROMA (Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation), a frozen-VLM control framework separating where to intervene, which geometry and gain to use, how to reach a target, which target to pursue, and when to act. In Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct, four dimensions of a causally validated cross-attention head explain 89.82% of cardinality sensitivity but only 9.52% of median activation energy; magnitude-normalized causal subspace actuation converts this mismatch into finite control, improving exact-count accuracy on untouched procedural data by +8.00 pp. Iterative Minimal-Effort Actuation (MEA) reaches 81.25% of oracle targets versus 15.18% for a frozen direction under matched step budgets. With target and gate frozen before evaluation, targeted MEA improves the previous frozen policy from 34.444% to 38.730% on a new natural Stage-2 cohort (N=630; paired p=4.63e-7) and yields 65.741% versus 65.153% over all 7,992 examples while editing only 63 inputs. Prospective routing, replication across heads and models, and comparisons with attribution-steering baselines further characterize each stage, separating localization, actuation, reachability, target quality, and selective deployment as distinct control problems.

</details>


### [47] [PhysXAI: A Theoretical Framework for Diagnosing Physical Hallucinations in Weather Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15053)
*Anjan Mahapatra,Nikhil Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhysXAI诊断框架，检测、定位并归因全球天气预报模型的"物理幻觉"（违反质量守恒、静力平衡、谱能量标度等物理规律的状态），将预测误差与物理合理性显式解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ML天气预报模型精度高，但常产生违反基本物理定律的状态（物理幻觉）。现有事后指标套件无法区分预测误差与物理不合理性，缺乏可解释的归因手段，亟需一个能检测、定位并归因物理不一致性的严谨诊断系统。

Method: 以数值残差与物理残差的联合条件形式化问题；构建多分支编码器，从守恒、动力学、谱、跨变量、时空残差中提取表征；提出物理一致性对比学习目标，理论上证明潜在表征近似等距于物理残差空间；推导带梯度加权自适应权重的多目标损失以平衡诊断目标；输出空间分辨的幻觉图、违规类型分类及校准的贝叶斯不确定性。

Result: 在ERA5、WeatherBench 2、MERRA-2上，针对GraphCast、Pangu-Weather、NeuralGCM的预报开展综合对比评估，代码可复现；理论分析与实验表明框架可有效检测并归因物理不一致性。

Conclusion: PhysXAI为诊断物理幻觉提供了有原理依据的框架，可产出空间定位、违规分类与可解释归因，推动天气预报可信AI的发展。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models for global weather forecasting achieve remarkable predictive accuracy, yet they frequently produce states that violate fundamental physical laws, including conservation of mass, hydrostatic balance, or spectral energy scaling. We term these outputs physical hallucinations and propose PhysXAI, a rigorous diagnostic framework that detects, localises, and attributes such inconsistencies. Unlike existing post-hoc metric suites, PhysXAI is a learnable system that explicitly decouples forecast error from physical plausibility. We formalise the problem through a novel joint condition on numerical and physical residuals, and introduce a multi-branch encoder that extracts representations from conservation, dynamical, spectral, cross-variable, and spatiotemporal residuals. A key innovation is a physics-consistency contrastive learning objective, which we theoretically show the latent representation to be approximately isometric to the physical residual space, ensuring that diagnostic decisions remain grounded in physically meaningful deviations. We further derive an adaptive multi-objective loss with gradient-based weighting to balance competing diagnostic objectives. The framework produces spatially resolved hallucination maps, violation-type classifications, and calibrated Bayesian uncertainty, enabling both detection and interpretable attribution of physical inconsistencies. We conduct a comprehensive comparative evaluation of PhysXAI across ERA5, WeatherBench 2, and MERRA-2, using forecasts from GraphCast, Pangu-Weather, and NeuralGCM, with Python-written code and a fully reproducible experimental protocol. Our theoretical analysis and empirical evaluation establish PhysXAI as a principled framework for diagnosing physical hallucinations and advancing trustworthy AI for weather forecasting.

</details>


### [48] [Probabilistic State Space Enumeration in Neural Network Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15062)
*Victor O. K. Li,Yang Han,Jacqueline C. K. Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将Transformer MLP的稠密计算精确表示为通道组二值包含状态上的期望，提出NORDER-II在概率递减顺序下枚举联合状态并截断，实现按概率质量与状态预算控制的任意时刻近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似方法对单个计算组件（通道）排序并保留，忽视联合状态结构；作者提出疑问：稠密神经计算能否精确表示为组合状态空间上的期望，并仅通过评估最可能配置来近似。

Method: 构建概率状态空间框架：将中间维度划分为通道组，定义输入相关的二值包含状态分布，并用重要性校正的掩码输出使完整期望精确还原稠密MLP输出。核心算法NORDER-II无需构造或排序指数级状态空间，即可按概率递减顺序枚举完整联合状态；截断该序得到由累积概率质量与状态预算控制的anytime近似。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B与Gemma-2-2B上，B=2048时NORDER-II的MLP输出近似精度为92.4%和93.8%，Channel Top-k为76.9%和79.8%；联合近似首、中、末MLP层时WikiText-2困惑度较稠密推理偏差仅0.06%和0.54%；相对expected-count Grouped Top-k精度提升82.6%–126.4%，额外局部解析FLOPs仅0.18%–0.19%。

Conclusion: 结果凸显超越原子显著性的联合状态结构的重要性，提出将稠密层视为组合计算状态上结构化概率推断的新范式，为预训练网络中的自适应、按概率质量控制的计算开辟路径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks typically realize dense computation by propagating information through all intermediate channels, while many approximation methods rank and retain individual computational components. We ask whether dense neural computation can instead be represented exactly as an expectation over a combinatorial state space, and approximated by evaluating only its most probable configurations. We introduce a novel probabilistic state-space framework for Transformer MLPs. The framework partitions the intermediate dimension into channel groups, defines an input-dependent distribution over binary inclusion states, and uses importance-corrected masked outputs such that the full expectation exactly recovers the dense MLP output. The key algorithmic novelty is NORDER-II, which enumerates complete joint states in decreasing probability without constructing or sorting the exponentially large state space. Truncating this ordering yields an anytime approximation governed by cumulative probability mass and a state budget. On Llama-3.2-1B and Gemma-2-2B, NORDER-II at a state budget of B=2048 achieves 92.4% and 93.8% MLP-output approximation accuracy, compared with 76.9% and 79.8% for Channel Top-k. When the first, middle, and last MLP layers are approximated jointly, WikiText-2 perplexity remains within 0.06% and 0.54% of dense inference. Relative to expected-count Grouped Top-k, NORDER-II yields 82.6% to 126.4% relative accuracy gains with only 0.18% to 0.19% additional local analytic FLOPs. These results establish the significance of joint-state structure beyond atomic salience and point to a new paradigm for neural computation: dense layers can be viewed as structured probabilistic inference over combinatorial computational states. This framework opens a path toward adaptive, probability-mass-controlled computation in pretrained neural networks.

</details>


### [49] [CoLaX: Context-Aware Local Explanations for Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15067)
*Kexuan Zhang,Paolo Frazzetto,Xiaobei Zou,Gustau Camps-Valls,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoLaX：面向时间序列分类器的上下文感知事后解释框架，用全序列表示对局部形状片段的相关性进行条件化，再以Shapley归因赋权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序分类器的事后解释方法仅对局部模式（子序列、shapelet）打分，忽略序列其余部分；但同一motif在不同全局时间条件（趋势、状态/regime）下预测含义不同，需要上下文条件化的解释。

Method: 以shapelet驱动的Shapley归因为基础，引入学习到的全序列表示作为全局上下文，在两级起作用：(1) 生成样本特定的原型权重；(2) 生成时间分辨信号，对局部激活图重新加权。由此得到的上下文条件化片段作为Shapley归因博弈的"参与者"，其取值仅来自冻结的黑盒分类器。

Result: 在相同motif跨上下文改变相关性的合成基准上，AUPRC较最强基线平均提升30%；在真实数据上于ECG取得最高AUPRC。消融实验表明增益来自输入依赖的条件化机制，而非额外的可学习参数。

Conclusion: CoLaX最适用于重复出现的局部motif在序列级上下文间相关性发生变化的场景，强调上下文条件化对时序解释的必要性。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanations for time series classifiers usually score local patterns, such as subsequences or shapelets, without regard to the rest of the sequence. Yet the same motif can carry different predictive meaning under different global temporal conditions, such as trends or regimes. We propose CoLaX, a context-aware post-hoc explanation framework that builds on shapelet-driven Shapley attribution and conditions local-pattern relevance on a learned representation of the full sequence. Global context acts at two levels: it produces sample-specific prototype weights and a temporally resolved signal that reweights the local activation map. The resulting context-conditioned segments are the players of a Shapley attribution game whose values come only from the frozen black-box classifier. On synthetic benchmarks where identical motifs change relevance across contexts, CoLaX improves AUPRC over the strongest baseline by 30\% on average. On real-world data, it achieves the highest AUPRC on ECG. Additional ablations indicate that the gains come from input-dependent conditioning rather than from additional learnable weights. CoLaX is therefore most useful when recurring local motifs change relevance across sequence-level contexts.

</details>


### [50] [When Does Causality Help in Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15073)
*Long Zhang,Wei Huang,Aishui Rao,Jian Hu,Binxiang Wu,Jie Li,Chen Gong,Linxi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文以固定因果引导自编码器DeCauAE为受控实验，系统回答"因果评分何时有效"，指出图质量决定收益、异常类型决定目标、评测协议决定排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测中因果感知评分被广泛使用，但文献很少说明其适用条件与收益边界，缺乏对"何时有用"的显式刻画。

Method: 保持DeCauAE不变，对真值分别扰动图质量（二十变量DAG逐步破坏）、异常类型（含条件结构破坏强度s）与评测协议（point-adjusted F1 与 range-aware VUS）；并在真实基准上考察因果模块的启用条件（密度导致的系统资格、跨子样本可复现性筛选实体）。

Result: 图质量决定收益：DAG被破坏时相对无图自编码器的增益随之衰减；异常类型决定目标：破坏条件结构时窗口F1从0.800升至0.976，破坏强度低于s=0.3时优势消失；协议决定排名：point-adjusted F1下随机评分可与完整模型持平，range-aware VUS能区分自编码器族与经典基线；真实基准上因果模块仅在有密度资格且经跨子样本可复现性筛出的实体上带来收益，全局启用损失0.012 VUS-ROC；结构因子还可迁移至经典检测器。

Conclusion: 因果感知评分的价值依赖图质量、异常类型与评测协议三者的联合条件；应条件化启用因果模块而非普遍部署，并采用range-aware VUS等对区间敏感的指标进行评价。

Abstract: Causality-aware scoring is common in multivariate time-series anomaly detection, yet the literature rarely states when it helps. Holding one causality-guided autoencoder (DeCauAE) fixed, we vary graph quality, anomaly type and protocol against ground truth. Graph quality fixes the payoff: on a twenty-variable DAG the gain over a graph-free autoencoder decays as the graph is corrupted. Anomaly type fixes the target: the structural factor lifts window F1 from 0.800 to 0.976 where anomalies break conditional structure, and its advantage vanishes below a break severity s 0.3. Protocol fixes the ranking: point-adjusted F1 lets a random score match the full model, while range-aware VUS separates the autoencoder family from the classical baselines. On real benchmarks the causal module pays only where density makes a system eligible and cross-subsample reproducibility selects entities inside it; enabling it everywhere costs 0.012 VUS-ROC. The structural factor also transfers to classical detectors .

</details>


### [51] [Profile-Based Auditing of Domestic Violence Survey Selection Across South Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15106)
*Mohammad Abu Obaida Mullick,Rezaur Rahman Ratul,Mymuna Nourin,Md. Motaharul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用南亚五国DHS数据，通过可解释机器学习检验人口、社会经济与家庭特征能否形成可复现的总体画像，并评估这些画像对家庭暴力模块样本选择（入样）的预测与审计价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DHS的家庭暴力（DV）数据通过专门模块采集，其住户层面抽样与隐私要求形成独特的观察过程，可能导致差异性的覆盖/选择偏差；作者希望判断标准人口社会经济特征是否能刻画这种选择性入样，从而为敏感模块的覆盖审计提供工具。

Method: 使用阿富汗、印度、缅甸、尼泊尔、巴基斯坦五国DHS Individual Recode数据，纳入796,374名15–49岁女性；统一整合61个数值特征与8个类别特征，经PCA降维提取出6个可解释画像；在PSU（初级抽样单元）感知的留出验证下比较22种机器学习模型，并设置仅基于设计的基线，进一步做分国别分析与留一国交叉验证以检验可迁移性。

Result: 最优模型HistGradientBoosting取得ROC-AUC 0.9167、PR-AUC 0.9612，优于仅设计基线的0.8475与0.9203；加入女性个体层面特征带来最大增量提升，而显式交互项对样本外性能提升甚微；分国别结果异质性显著，留一国评估显示增量画像信号的可迁移性有限。

Conclusion: 可解释机器学习可用于审计敏感调查模块中超出正式抽样设计之外的差异性覆盖问题；DV模块入样存在可被人口社会经济画像部分解释的结构性选择，但该信号具有较强的国别特异性。

Abstract: Domestic violence (DV) data in the Demographic and Health Surveys (DHS) are collected through a specialized module whose household-level sampling and privacy requirements create a distinct observation process. We ask whether demographic, socioeconomic, and household characteristics form reproducible population profiles and whether these profiles contain information about selection into the DV module across South Asia. Using DHS Individual Recode data for 796,374 women aged 15-49 across Afghanistan, India, Myanmar, Nepal, and Pakistan, we harmonize 61 numeric and 8 categorical features, reduce the profile space with PCA, and identify six interpretable profiles. We then evaluate 22 machine-learning models under primary-sampling-unit-aware holdout validation. The best model, HistGradientBoosting, achieves ROC-AUC 0.9167 and PR-AUC 0.9612, versus 0.8475 and 0.9203 for a design-only baseline. Adding woman-level characteristics yields the largest incremental gain, while explicit interaction terms add little out-of-sample performance. Country-specific analyses reveal substantial heterogeneity, and leave-one-country-out evaluation shows that transferability of the incremental profile signal is limited. These results demonstrate that interpretable ML can audit differential coverage in sensitive survey modules beyond the formal sampling design.

</details>


### [52] [Serving-Aligned Entire-Space Conversion Modeling via Click-Through Rate Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15137)
*Pavel Dolin,Xun Zou,Jiatian Wu,Deguang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用知识蒸馏将线上服务的pCTR分数监督ESMM辅助pCTR塔，缓解pCTCVR的训练-服务不一致；在Ali-CCP上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESMM联合训练pCVR与辅助pCTR塔、但线上仅部署pCVR塔，并与独立训练的pCTR模型拼接计算pCTCVR；当两者预测不一致时，pCTCVR存在训练-服务不一致（training-serving mismatch）。

Method: 以线上记录的serving pCTR分数作为知识蒸馏教师信号，监督ESMM辅助pCTR塔；变体记为KD-α，α控制硬标签损失权重。在Ali-CCP上与ESMM基线对比，并测试保留5%未点击样本的负采样场景。

Result: KD-0.3在serving pCTCVR上最优：相比ESMM，serving pCTCVR与pCVR的ROC-AUC分别提升2.1%与1.6%，PR-AUC提升9.9%与8.8%；负采样下KD-0.3的平均AUC分别提升1.2%与0.9%，PR-AUC提升8.5%与4.5%。

Conclusion: 用服务端pCTR分数蒸馏辅助塔可有效对齐训练与服务的pCTCVR分布，并同步提升pCVR性能，且在负采样条件下依然有效。

Abstract: Recommendation systems may use predicted click-through conversion rate (pCTCVR), the product of predicted click-through rate (pCTR) and post-click conversion rate (pCVR), to guide ranking decisions. Many pCVR models build on the Entire Space Multi-Task Model (ESMM), which jointly trains pCVR and auxiliary pCTR towers using pCTR and pCTCVR losses, but deploys only the pCVR tower. At serving time, this tower may be paired with a separately trained pCTR model, creating a training-serving mismatch in pCTCVR when its predictions differ from those of the auxiliary pCTR tower. In this paper, we use knowledge distillation (KD) from logged serving pCTR scores to supervise ESMM's auxiliary pCTR tower and improve training-serving pCTCVR alignment. We denote variants by KD-$α$, where $α$ controls the hard-label loss weight. On the Ali-CCP dataset, KD-0.3, the best variant on serving pCTCVR, increases ROC-AUC not only for serving pCTCVR but also for pCVR by 2.1% and 1.6%, respectively, and PR-AUC by 9.9% and 8.8%, respectively, relative to the ESMM baseline. We also test negative downsampling with 5% nonclick retention. Downsampled KD-0.3 increases mean AUC for serving pCTCVR and pCVR by 1.2% and 0.9%, respectively, -and PR-AUC by 8.5% and 4.5%, respectively, relative to downsampled ESMM.

</details>


### [53] [Knowledge Transfer for Conditional Flow Matching: Frozen Estimation and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15150)
*Yian Huang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向条件流匹配的迁移学习框架TFM-Freeze与TFM-Adapt，可处理源域与目标域响应分布及维数均不同的情形，并给出条件平方Wasserstein风险界、信息下界与独立验证选择程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标域数据有限时，条件生成建模可从相关源域学到的表示中获益；但已有方法难以应对源域与目标域响应分布不同、维数不一致的情况。

Method: 构建条件流匹配迁移框架：TFM-Freeze固定源表示、仅估计目标速度头；TFM-Adapt在函数锚定惩罚下联合学习速度头与表示适配。对显式神经网络类推导条件平方Wasserstein风险界（涵盖源估计、目标拟合、表示失配与数值积分误差），并提出用于条件生成器选择的独立验证程序。

Result: 当源表示存在光滑修正时，适配将以学习修正的代价加可控正则偏差替代冻结失配项；信息下界表明存在适配严格优于任何受限于冻结上下文信息的生成器的情形。选择程序满足oracle不等式，切换规则以高概率保证条件能量风险不高于仅用目标域训练的生成器。仿真与真实数据验证了方法收益及目标域验证对负迁移的防护作用。

Conclusion: 表示迁移在条件生成中可带来理论可证的速率改进，且配合目标域验证选择机制能有效规避负迁移。

Abstract: Conditional generative modeling with limited target data can benefit from representations learned in a related source population. We develop a transfer learning framework for conditional flow matching that accommodates distinct source and target response distributions and dimensions. Two procedures exploit a representation learned from source data: TFM-Freeze estimates a target velocity head with the representation fixed, whereas TFM-Adapt jointly learns the head and a representation adaptation under a functional anchoring penalty. For explicit neural network classes, we establish bounds on conditional squared Wasserstein risk that account for source estimation, target fitting, representation mismatch, and numerical integration. When the source representation admits a smooth correction, adaptation replaces the frozen mismatch contribution by the cost of learning the correction and a controllable regularization bias. An information lower bound identifies regimes in which adaptation attains a strictly faster conditional squared Wasserstein risk rate than any generator restricted to the frozen context information. We also develop independent validation procedure for selecting among fitted conditional generators. We show that the selection procedure satisfies an oracle inequality, and the switching rule guarantees, with high probability, no increase in conditional energy risk relative to a target-only generator. Simulations and real data applications illustrate the gains from our proposed methods and the effectiveness of target validation in guarding against negative transfer.

</details>


### [54] [PRELUDE: Separating Common Components from Target-Specific Dependencies for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15155)
*Dongqing Song,Jianyu Xu,Haobin Ding,Xinyi Guo,Jiahao Du,Ruiqi He,Ruiqi Liu,Yuntong Xie,Chao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多变量时间序列预测中跨变量模块的增益主要来自共同成分而非依赖关系，作者提出分离读取框架PRELUDE分别处理共同成分与依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨变量模块（截面池化、变量间注意力、图结构）虽能显著降低预测误差，但通过在推理时干预跨变量读取发现，模型对所有目标的读取几乎相同，说明增益并非来自变量间依赖，而是来自共同成分，即"伪依赖"现象。

Method: 将伪依赖归因于共同成分混杂：共同成分存在于每个变量中，影响读取对来源的区分，使读取在各目标间被共享并集中于少数相似均值。理论分析表明共同成分与依赖对读取有不同要求，故提出PRELUDE分离读取框架：通过截面池化与多个共同因子整体估计共同成分；在预白化得到的创新空间中，按lead-lag关系为每个目标读取依赖。

Result: 在来自四个领域的16个真实数据集上实验，PRELUDE有效缓解伪依赖，24个短期与空气质量设置中有23个取得最优MSE，相较最强基线平均MSE降低6.1%。

Conclusion: 共同成分与依赖应分离处理；PRELUDE通过共享共同成分估计与目标特定的创新空间依赖读取，实现更优的预测性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting often improves the prediction of a target by modeling the interactions among variables, and cross-variable modules such as cross-sectional pooling, inter-variable attention, and graph structures are used to construct such interactions. However, by intervening on the cross-variable reading of existing models at inference time, we find that although these models substantially reduce forecasting error through cross-variable interaction, their reading is nearly identical for all targets: the gain appears to come from dependencies among variables but mainly comes from the common component. We term this phenomenon spurious dependency and attribute it to common-component confounding: present in every variable, the common component affects how reading distinguishes sources, so that reading tends to be shared across targets and concentrates on a few similar averages; dependencies thus make up a small share of the read-in content, and a common component composed of several drivers is only partly captured. Theoretical analysis further shows that the common component and dependencies place different requirements on reading and should therefore be handled in different ways. Accordingly, we propose PRELUDE, a separated-reading framework that estimates the common component holistically through cross-sectional pooling and multiple common factors, and reads dependencies for each target via lead-lag relations in the innovation space obtained by prewhitening. Extensive experiments on 16 real-world datasets from four domains show that PRELUDE effectively mitigates spurious dependency and achieves the best overall performance: it attains the best MSE on 23 of the 24 short-horizon and air-quality settings and reduces the average MSE by 6.1% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [55] [Stealthy, High-Impact False Data Injection Attacks in Smart Grid Using Physics-Constrained GAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15162)
*Md. Mainul Islam,Hasan Kurban,Abdulrahman Takiddin,Muhammad Ismail,Rachad Atat,Erchin Serpedin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出检测器无关的物理约束生成对抗网络（PCGAN），从已部署传感器的历史量测中学习解耦潮流灵敏度，用于生成兼顾隐蔽性与物理影响的虚假数据注入（FDI）攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FDI攻击方法难以兼顾隐蔽性与攻击影响，且通常假设攻击者掌握电网内部信息、全节点量测或防御方模型，实际可行性不足。

Method: 构建检测器无关的PCGAN：从历史量测学习有功-相角、无功-电压幅值解耦潮流灵敏度，约束扰动仅作用于被攻陷通道，并以对抗训练生成符合实际的时空量测模式。在IEEE 300节点系统上，基于PJM一年逐时负荷与合成节点负荷曲线，将PCGAN与7种现有攻击在10%/30%/60%/100%传感器攻陷率下对比，采用10种机器学习检测器与常规不良数据检测（BDD）评估隐蔽性，用非线性交流加权最小二乘状态估计量化物理影响。

Result: 四类场景下PCGAN平均机器学习规避率84.28%、平均空间负荷误判率22.31%，隐蔽性-影响权衡得分18.69%（最强对比攻击仅5.61%）；卡方BDD规避率83.45%，最大归一化残差BDD规避率86.02%。

Conclusion: PCGAN在不同传感器接入水平下实现了更优的隐蔽性-影响平衡，可为FDI检测器评估提供高难度攻击样本。

Abstract: False data injection (FDI) attacks can distort power system state estimation while retaining plausible measurement patterns. Existing FDI methods trade stealth for impact or vice versa. They often assume access to internal grids, all-bus sensing, or defender models. In this paper, we propose a detector-agnostic physics-constrained generative adversarial network (PCGAN) that learns decoupled power-flow sensitivities from historical measurements of deployed sensors. The learned active-power-angle and reactive-power-voltage-magnitude relationships guide perturbations restricted to compromised channels, while adversarial training promotes realistic spatiotemporal patterns. We implement PCGAN and seven existing FDI attacks on the IEEE 300-bus system using one year of observed hourly demand from PJM Interconnection, a regional transmission organization in the United States, and synthetic bus-level load profiles. We compare their performance across 10%, 30%, 60%, and 100% compromised sensors. Attack stealth is assessed against ten machine-learning (ML) detectors and conventional bad-data detection (BDD) methods, while physical impact is quantified through nonlinear alternating-current weighted least-squares state estimation. PCGAN achieves 84.28% mean ML evasion and 22.31% mean spatial load misperception across the four scenarios. Its mean stealth-impact trade-off score reaches 18.69%, compared with 5.61% for the strongest competing attack. PCGAN also achieves 83.45% chi-square BDD evasion and 86.02% largest-normalized-residual BDD evasion. These results demonstrate a favorable stealth-impact balance under varying sensor-access levels and provide challenging attack cases for evaluating FDI detectors.

</details>


### [56] [Anytime-Valid Empirical Likelihood Inference from LLM Annotations with Adaptive Human Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15197)
*Hongfu Gao,Jiang Hu,Pengfei Liu,Wang Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AAELI：以LLM输出引导自适应人工审计，通过可预测经验似然e-过程构造恒时有效置信序列，在有限审计预算下估计人工标签总体均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被用作低成本标注替代人工，但其输出相对人工标签存在偏差与失准，直接使用会导致误导性分析与推断；需在有限人工审计预算下获得对人工标签总体均值有效、可靠的推断。

Method: 提出AAELI在线框架：用LLM输出自适应地指导人工审计分配，并将审计标签与LLM输出通过可预测经验似然e-过程结合，构建恒时有效（anytime-valid）置信序列；核心是引入可预测学习的高效投影，并给出基于连续混合的下注式构造。

Result: 理论：可预测学习的高效投影在自适应审计下保持一阶profile经验似然统计量，且无需对投影估计量设定收敛速率；得到精确恒时有效下注构造，其连续混合形式仅产生标量而非向量维度的混合惩罚。辅助结果给出自适应经验似然渐近性，并证明所学审计规则在相同审计概率下限与长期期望预算下达到该增广估计方程设计类的oracle方差。实验：合成数据及三个真实数据集上，AAELI维持经验恒时覆盖率，且达到目标精度所需人工审计次数少于最强基线。

Conclusion: AAELI在有限审计预算下实现了对人工标签总体均值的恒时有效、高效推断，兼顾统计有效性与审计成本，优于现有最强基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate unlabeled data as a fast, low-cost alternative to human annotation. However, their outputs can be biased and miscalibrated relative to human labels, potentially leading to misleading analysis and inference. We propose AAELI (Adaptive Human Auditing and Anytime-Valid Empirical Likelihood Inference), an online framework for estimating the population mean of human labels under a limited auditing budget. AAELI uses LLM outputs to guide adaptive human auditing and combines them with audited labels through predictable empirical-likelihood $e$-processes to construct anytime-valid confidence sequences. Our main theoretical result shows that a predictably learned efficient projection preserves the first-order profile empirical-likelihood statistic under adaptive auditing, without requiring a prescribed convergence rate for the projection estimator. This yields an exactly anytime-valid betting construction whose continuous-mixture form incurs a scalar rather than vector-dimensional mixing penalty. Supporting results establish adaptive empirical-likelihood asymptotics and show that a learned audit rule attains the oracle variance within the stated class of augmented estimating-equation designs under the same audit-probability floor and long-run expected budget. Experiments on synthetic data and three real-world datasets demonstrate that AAELI maintains empirical anytime coverage while requiring fewer human audits than the strongest competing baseline to reach a target precision.

</details>


### [57] [DGPExplainer: Generating Prototype Sequence Explanations for Dynamic Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15224)
*Jinlong Hu,Shixu Fan,Chongzhang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DGPExplainer：面向动态图神经网络（DGNN）的模型级、原型式事后解释器，将复杂决策转化为可类比的案例式解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN已成功用于图结构数据学习，但动态图（节点与边随时间演化）上的GNN解释性尤为困难。静态GNN的解释技术成熟，却难以直接迁移到动态GNN：时序动态与时空依赖带来额外复杂度，尤其缺乏模型级（model-level）解释方法。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出DGPExplainer，一个事后、基于原型的解释器，分三步：(1) 用局部解释方法在动态图序列的各时间快照中识别重要元素；(2) 在每个时间步将图分解为不同的原型（prototypes），从而把动态图序列"符号化"；(3) 设计序列生成方法，利用符号化序列学习并生成类别特定的动态图模式序列，作为原型解释。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够为DGNN模型在多个类别上有效生成基于原型的解释。

Conclusion: DGPExplainer首次针对DGNN提供模型级、原型式的案例类比解释，验证了将局部解释—原型分解—序列生成相串联的可行性，为动态图模型的时空可解释性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been successfully applied to learning from graph-structured data. However, applying them to dynamic graphs-where nodes and edges evolve over time-presents significant challenges, particularly regarding explainability. While explanation techniques for static GNNs are well established, they do not readily extend to dynamic GNNs (DGNNs) due to the added complexity introduced by temporal dynamics and spatiotemporal dependencies, especially when generating model-level explanations. To address this gap, we introduce DGPExplainer, a post-hoc, prototype-based explainer specifically designed for DGNN models that transforms their complex decision-making processes into human-understandable, analogical, case-based explanations. DGPExplainer first employs local explanation methods to identify important elements within temporal snapshots of dynamic graph sequences. It then decomposes the graph at each time step into distinct prototypes, thereby symbolizing the dynamic graph sequence. Finally, we design a sequence generation method that uses these symbolized sequences to learn and generate class-specific dynamic graph pattern sequences that serve as prototype explanations. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method effectively produces prototype-based explanations for DGNN models across multiple classes.

</details>


### [58] [MOON-RL: Multi-Objective Optimization via Objective Space Navigation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15227)
*Andrii Artomov,Soheyl Massoudi,Jürg Schiffmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MOON-RL：将约束多目标优化建模为目标空间中的偏好导向导航，用轻量PPO策略结合非支配解档案与元算法，在真实执行器设计问题上于感兴趣区域取得有竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计问题常含多个冲突目标与严格可行性约束，主流种群优化器需大量评估、且对搜索聚焦区域缺乏在线控制，难以支持交互式偏好引导设计。

Method: 将约束多目标搜索表述为面向用户指定权衡方向的目标空间导航；轻量PPO策略以目标空间与约束相关观测为输入，作为实例自适应搜索过程学习设计更新；配套元算法周期性重置策略、从持续更新的非支配解档案中重采样起点，并调节步长与惩罚调度。

Result: 在真实执行器设计问题与收敛困难约束基准上，于等评估预算下与经典进化算法对比：聚焦搜索场景中在感兴趣区域性能有竞争力；消融显示RL自适应采样与元算法均有贡献；框架亦可兼容非RL自适应采样器，而PPO在固定目标、偏好导向导航中仍为强选择。

Conclusion: 将强化学习训练重用于目标空间导航，可作为交互式偏好引导设计工具的可行构建模块。

Abstract: Many engineering design problems involve multiple conflicting objectives and strict feasibility constraints. They are typically solved with population-based optimizers that require many evaluations and offer limited online control over where the search is focused. We present MOON-RL, a reinforcement-learning-based optimization framework that formulates constrained multi-objective search as preference-directed navigation in the objective space. In MOON-RL, a lightweight PPO policy receives objective-space and constraint-related observations and acts as an instance-specific adaptive search process. It learns how to update the design so that the current solution advances along a user-specified trade-off direction, while a simple meta-algorithm periodically resets the policy, resamples starting designs from a continuously updated archive of non-dominated solutions, and adjusts step-size and penalty schedules. We evaluate MOON-RL on real-world actuator-design problems and additional convergence-hard constrained benchmarks, comparing against established evolutionary algorithms under matched evaluation budgets. In focused search scenarios, MOON-RL achieves competitive performance in the region of interest, and ablation studies show that both the RL-driven adaptive sampling process and the meta-algorithm contribute to this behaviour, suggesting that repurposing RL training as objective-space navigation is a viable building block for interactive, preference-guided design tools. Additional experiments show that the framework also accommodates adaptive non-RL samplers, while PPO remains a strong choice for fixed-target, preference-directed objective-space navigation.

</details>


### [59] [Winter wheat improves soil carbon sequestration with reduced gains in warmer climates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15232)
*Georgios Giannarakis,Vasileios Sitokonstantinou,Dimitrios Bormpoudakis,Ilias Tsoumas,Nikiforos Samarinas,Gustau Camps-Valls,Charalampos Kontoes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究采用因果机器学习方法，基于立陶宛2018-2022年全国田间数据，评估冬小麦轮作对土壤有机碳（SOC）的异质性影响，发现其增碳效果随气候变暖而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续农业集约化需精确理解不同环境条件下土地利用决策对SOC固存的影响，而传统方法难以刻画处理效应的空间与气候异质性。

Method: 构建包含作物类型、管理措施、土壤属性与环境条件的五年全国田间数据集，运用因果机器学习估计冬小麦轮作的条件平均处理效应（CATE），并结合未来气候情景进行外推预测。

Result: 冬小麦轮作总体上提升SOC水平，但效应受气候调节：较冷地区SOC增益更高，较暖地区效应减弱甚至为负；高排放情景下其固碳潜力可能下降。

Conclusion: 应制定考虑气候变异性的区域化农业策略，以最大化SOC固存并保障土壤长期可持续性。

Abstract: Sustainable agricultural intensification requires a precise understanding of how land use decisions influence soil organic carbon (SOC) sequestration under varying environmental conditions. We apply causal machine learning to evaluate the heterogeneous impact of winter wheat-based rotations on SOC at the field-level across Lithuania. Using a nationwide dataset collected over five years (2018-2022), including crop types, management practices, soil properties, and environmental conditions, we estimate how the effects of winter wheat-based rotation vary across space and climate. Our results indicate that winter wheat generally enhances SOC levels, but its effectiveness is modulated by climate. Cooler regions experience higher SOC gains, whereas warmer regions show diminished or even negative effects. Future climate projections suggest that the potential for winter wheat carbon sequestration may decline under high-emission scenarios. These findings highlight the importance of localized agricultural strategies that account for climate variability to maximize SOC sequestration and ensure long-term soil sustainability.

</details>


### [60] [Schedule-Calibrated Commitment Order in Blockwise Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15234)
*Gaurav Sarkar,Umang Pandey,Pratik Prabhanjan Brahma,Zicheng Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用Spearman秩恒等式将掩码扩散语言模型的全局位置-承诺相关性分解为“调度参考项+块内加权均值”，并据此校准LLaDA-8B-Instruct的实验结果，指出块大小128的更大原始残差并不代表更强的局部位置顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型在顺序块调度下会呈现明显的从左到右token承诺外观，但这一外观可能由调度本身造成，而非模型内部真实的位置顺序；需要区分调度伪影与真实的块内顺序性。

Method: 针对等长连续块、按位置顺序无交错处理、不可逆且不共享的承诺，利用Spearman秩恒等式把全局位置-承诺相关表示为确定性的调度参考项加块内相关性的加权均值；对块大小64与128给出尖锐界，并由已报告的汇总统计推导校准区间。

Result: LLaDA-8B-Instruct在500个数学与代码提示、256个生成位置/更新上，块大小由64增至128使全局平均相关从0.9845降至0.9149；而均匀随机块内参考值分别为0.9375和0.7500。校准后块内平均相关为0.751与0.660，说明128时更大的原始残差并不表示更强的局部位置顺序。

Conclusion: 承诺顺序需结合调度进行解释；本研究仅限一个检查点和两种调度，且未评估输出质量与推理速度，受限于仅发布聚合统计。

Abstract: Sequential block schedules can make token commitment in masked diffusion language models appear strongly left-to-right even when within-block order is random or reversed. For equal contiguous blocks processed without interleaving in positional order, with untied irreversible commitments, we use a Spearman-rank identity to express global position-commitment correlation as a deterministic schedule reference plus a weighted mean within-block correlation. In a reported paired study of LLaDA-8B-Instruct on 500 math and code prompts with 256 generated positions and updates, increasing block size from 64 to 128 lowers mean global correlation from 0.9845 to 0.9149. Uniform-random within-block references are already 0.9375 and 0.7500. After calibration, mean within-block correlations are 0.751 and 0.660, so the larger raw residual at block size 128 does not indicate stronger local positional ordering. We give sharp bounds and derive calibrated intervals from reported summaries, while documenting the limitations of the aggregate-only empirical release. The analysis supports schedule-aware interpretation of commitment order; its empirical scope is one checkpoint and two schedules, without evaluation of output quality or inference speed.

</details>


### [61] [Regret Bounds for Quantum Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15237)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究有界奖励的d维量子线性bandit，证明下界Ω(d^{3/2}log(1+T/d^{3/2}))，闭合了上界d^{3/2}依赖是否必要的问题，并对固定动作集提出Soft-G-Elim与QG-Elim两种分阶段消除算法改进后悔界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有一般动作集算法达到O(d^{3/2}polylog(T))后悔，而先前下界仅为Ω(d log(1+T/d))，二者存在d^{1/2}差距，因此需要判定上界中d^{3/2}依赖是否本质必要，并进一步优化固定动作集下的对数因子。

Method: 下界：构造困难实例族，使每个动作的奖励间隔正比于其到最优动作的平方距离；建立带量子内存的自适应量子查询的后验Fisher信息界，将关于最优方向的信息获取与后悔关联，并将所选动作视为最优方向的估计，用矩阵Cramér–Rao论证导出期望后悔下界。上界：对大小K的固定动作集提出两个分阶段消除算法；通过改造量子相位估计构造SoftQME均值估计器（偏差小、尾部可控）；Soft-G-Elim在设计支撑上以独立SoftQME输出估计存活动作的均值奖励，控制偏差与波动。

Result: 证明极小极大下界Ω(d^{3/2}log(1+T/d^{3/2}))，与已有上界匹配（至多对数因子）。Soft-G-Elim达到后悔O(d log T log(dT)√log(KT))，将先前界中的log(KT)loglog(dT)因子降为√log(KT)；QG-Elim进一步将该因子降为min{√log(KT),√d}。

Conclusion: d^{3/2}依赖是量子线性bandit的本质复杂度；结合量子相位估计的均值估计与分阶段消除设计可显著削减对数因子，QG-Elim给出目前更优的固定动作集后悔界。

Abstract: We study $d$-dimensional quantum linear bandits with bounded rewards, where the learner selects an action and queries its quantum reward oracle or its inverse in each round. For general action sets, existing algorithms achieve regret $O(d^{3/2}\operatorname{polylog}(T))$ over $T$ rounds. Prior work established a lower bound of $Ω(d\log(1+T/d))$, leaving open whether the $d^{3/2}$ dependence in the upper bound is necessary. We resolve this question by proving a minimax lower bound of $Ω(d^{3/2}\log(1+T/d^{3/2}))$. To prove this result, we construct a family of hard instances in which the reward gap of each action is proportional to its squared distance from the optimal action. We establish a posterior Fisher information bound for adaptive quantum queries with quantum memory, relating the information acquired about the optimal direction to the regret incurred. Viewing each selected action as an estimate of the optimal direction, we use a matrix Cramér--Rao argument to derive a lower bound on expected regret. When the action set is fixed and has size $K$, we propose two phased elimination algorithms, Soft-G-Elim and QG-Elim. We adapt quantum phase estimation to construct SoftQME, a mean estimator with small bias and controlled tails. Soft-G-Elim estimates the mean reward of every surviving action from independent SoftQME outputs on the support of an experimental design. Controlling the bias and fluctuations of these estimates yields regret $O(d\log T\log(dT)\sqrt{\log(KT)})$, reducing the $\log(KT)\log\log(dT)$ factor in the previous regret bound to $\sqrt{\log(KT)}$. QG-Elim further reduces this factor to $\min\{\sqrt{\log(KT)},\sqrt d\}$.

</details>


### [62] [KQML-Liver: Interpretable Hybrid Quantum Machine Learning with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Liver Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15241)
*Nhat-Long Nguyen,Viet-Loi Dinh,Trong-Nghia Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KQML-Liver混合框架，将量子机器学习(QML)与Kolmogorov-Arnold网络(KAN)结合，在严格防泄漏预处理下于ILPD数据集实现82%准确率和100%召回率，并提供更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝病仍是全球重大健康问题，需要可靠、易获取的早期检测手段。现有机器学习、深度学习与量子机器学习方法普遍存在可解释性不足，以及因数据泄漏或过于简化的评估协议导致性能虚高的问题，削弱了其临床实用可靠性。

Method: 提出KQML-Liver混合框架：以参数化量子电路提供表达性强的特征表示，结合KAN的基于函数的映射以可解释方式捕捉非线性关系；在印度肝病患者数据集(ILPD)上采用严格防止数据泄漏的预处理协议进行评估，并与现有基线方法对比。

Result: 在ILPD数据集上取得82%分类准确率与100%召回率（检出全部阳性病例），优于现有基线，同时提升了临床预测的透明度。

Conclusion: 混合KAN-量子架构为可解释的AI辅助医学诊断提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Liver disease remains a major global health concern, highlighting the need for reliable and accessible early detection methods. Although recent advances in machine learning, deep learning and quantum machine learning (QML) have introduced promising diagnostic tools, many approaches suffer from limited interpretability and inflated performance caused by data leakage or overly simplistic evaluation protocols. These constraints reduce their reliability in real-world clinical practice. This paper proposes KQML-Liver, a hybrid framework that integrates QML with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) to enhance predictive performance and interpretability. The architecture combines parameterized quantum circuits for expressive feature representation with KAN-based functional mappings that capture nonlinear relationships in an interpretable manner. Evaluated on the Indian Liver Patient Dataset (ILPD) under a rigorous preprocessing protocol preventing data leakage, the proposed model achieves 82% classification accuracy and 100% recall, detecting all positive cases, outperforming existing baselines while providing improved transparency in clinical predictions. These results suggest that hybrid KAN-quantum architectures offer a promising direction for interpretable AI-assisted medical diagnosis.

</details>


### [63] [Dual Representation Enhanced Diffusion Policy for Flexible Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15246)
*Yunyi Zhao,Wei Zhang,Cheng Xiang,Vadakkepat Prahlad,Zuming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DredRL：以自注意力/交叉注意力构建"工序-机器"双重增强表征，并用扩散模型作为策略网络生成调度决策，在合成、公开、大规模与随机FJSP基准上优于现有学习型调度器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FJSP是智能制造中的核心优化问题；现有DRL调度方法虽能学习有效策略，但在问题规模扩大与加工时间不确定性增加时性能显著退化。

Method: DredRL采用双重表征增强的扩散策略强化学习框架：首先通过自注意力与交叉注意力机制分别捕获工序、机器的实体内部与实体间依赖，构建增强双重表征；再将扩散模型作为策略网络，以编码状态为条件，通过迭代去噪生成调度决策，以提升策略表达能力与鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成、公开、大规模及随机FJSP基准上，DredRL在多数配置下优于SOTA学习型调度器；合成实例上最大完工时间最多降低5.44%；在四个公开基准中的三个取得最佳学习型性能；在随机基准上优于考虑随机性的算法。

Conclusion: 增强双重表征与扩散式策略的结合，为复杂环境下大规模可扩展FJSP调度提供了一种有效且鲁棒的方法。

Abstract: The Flexible Job-Shop Scheduling Problem (FJSP) is a fundamental and challenging optimization problem in smart manufacturing. Although recent deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approaches can learn effective scheduling policies through interaction with the environment, their performance often degrades as problem scale and processing-time uncertainty increase. In this paper, we propose DredRL, a dual representation enhanced diffusion-policy reinforcement learning scheduler. DredRL first constructs enhanced dual representations of operations and machines through self- and cross-attention mechanisms to capture both intra-entity and inter-entity dependencies. A diffusion model is further employed as the policy network, generating scheduling decisions through iterative denoising conditioned on the encoded state to enhance policy expressiveness and robustness. Extensive experiments on synthetic, public, large-scale, and stochastic FJSP benchmarks demonstrate that DredRL outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based schedulers across most configurations. Compared with SOTA learning-based methods, DredRL reduces makespan by up to 5.44% on synthetic instances. It also achieves the best learning-based performance on three of four public benchmarks and outperforms the stochasticity-aware algorithms on stochastic benchmarks. These results demonstrate that combining enhanced dual representations with diffusion-based policies provides an effective and robust approach for large scale scalable FJSP scheduling in complicated environment.

</details>


### [64] [Protected Unbiased Sparsification: A Sharp Loewner Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15247)
*Ziyang Wang,Menghao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机稀疏化：用仅有少量非零项的随机概率向量替代原概率向量，要求估计无偏、精确保持给定线性统计量，并对任意下游线性响应的协方差给出保证。作者导出了尖锐的协方差上界系数，并给出对应的采样算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机稀疏化在注意力、采样等场景中广泛使用，但现有做法难以同时满足无偏性、线性统计量精确保持与协方差可控三项要求；尤其缺少对"在保护若干线性统计量的条件下，协方差能达到的最优水平"的刻画。

Method: 设p有k个正坐标，A指定受保护统计量，其有效秩为d≤k−2。对每个d+1≤m≤k，构造至多m个非零项的随机概率向量Q，使其无偏、逐实现满足AQ=Ap，且Cov(Q)⪯h·C_{p|A}，其中h=(k−m)/((m−d)(k−d−1))，C_{p|A}为对受保护统计量做最优线性调整后的类别协方差。另构建残差协方差桥接，将并发无保护曲线推广到任意逐路径线性保护；刻画类内均匀权重划分的实例最优常数并给出尖峰权重下的界；对有理输入给出期望多项式时间、因子1+ε的精确Las Vegas采样器；并提出保持块质量的快速块枢轴采样器。

Result: 该协方差系数在实例上一致尖锐，即便在偏估计器中以二阶矩衡量误差时亦如此。块枢轴采样器在协方差序上不劣于块内独立采样。合成权重与语言模型注意力实验验证了理论；在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct解码中快速采样器保持块质量，与VarOpt的比较统计上不显著，而top-m截断在RULER上得分最高。

Conclusion: 本文为带线性统计量保护的随机稀疏化给出了尖锐的协方差最优界及可实现的采样方案，统一了无偏性、统计量精确保持与协方差控制，并在理论上与实验中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Random sparsification replaces a probability vector by a random vector with few nonzero entries. We ask for unbiasedness, exact preservation of prescribed linear statistics, and a covariance guarantee for every downstream linear response. Let $p$ have $k$ positive coordinates, let $A$ specify the protected statistics, and let $d\le k-2$ be their effective rank. For every $d+1\le m\le k$, we construct an unbiased random probability vector $Q$ with at most $m$ nonzero coordinates, $AQ=Ap$ on every realization, and $\operatorname{Cov}(Q)\preceq h C_{p\mid A}$, where $h=(k-m)/((m-d)(k-d-1))$ and $C_{p\mid A}$ is the categorical covariance after optimal linear adjustment for the protected statistics. This coefficient is sharp uniformly over instances, even among biased estimators when error is measured by its second moment. A residual covariance bridge extends the concurrent unprotected curve to arbitrary pathwise linear protection. We also characterize instance-optimal constants for partitions with uniform weights within classes and derive bounds for peaked weights. For rational inputs, an exact Las Vegas sampler attains the curve within a factor $1+ε$ in expected polynomial time. A separate fast block-pivotal sampler preserves block masses and is no worse in covariance order than independent sampling within blocks. Experiments illustrate the theory on synthetic weights and language-model attention. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct decoding, the fast sampler preserves block masses, while comparisons with VarOpt remain statistically inconclusive and top-$m$ truncation scores highest on RULER.

</details>


### [65] [CastWorld: Benchmarking Context-Aware Agentic Time Series Forecasting Beyond Predictive Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15262)
*Bokai Pan,Ruijie Yang,Mingyue Cheng,Jiahao Wang,Xiaoyu Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CastWorld——首个面向上下文感知时间序列预测的智能体基准，包含8个领域25个真实数据集的上下文丰富数据套件、49个工具与状态自适应循环的预测原生执行框架，以及涵盖预测质量、过程执行、行为可信度与资源效率的模型级评测和系统级评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体时间序列预测基准在三个方面支持不足：缺乏对多样上下文信息（外部变量、事件、历史案例）的集成支持；缺乏预测任务专用的自适应交互机制；缺乏对预测智能体的系统化评测体系。

Method: 构建CastWorld基准：1）上下文丰富的数据套件，覆盖8领域25个真实数据集，在统一上下文规范下组织外部变量、事件与历史案例；2）轻量模块化的预测原生执行框架，提供49个核心工具与状态自适应循环，跟踪预测版本并支持迭代式证据收集与预测修订，中间预测暴露信息缺口，新证据引导后续修订与最终版本选择；3）模型级评测（基于决策轨迹与预测版本历史评估预测质量、过程执行、行为可信度、资源效率）与系统级评测（比较不同预测系统的准确率）。

Result: 实验发现：准确率相近的智能体之间存在明显不同的决策行为；额外的交互并不必然提升预测效果；智能体可能无法从自身已生成的预测中选出最优者；在预测精炼过程中允许证据获取可在三个被测模型上一致提升任务感知预测得分。

Conclusion: CastWorld为在可控条件下评测智能体式时间序列预测提供了可复用的基础平台，揭示了行为层面与预测质量之间的分离现象，表明过程评测对预测智能体研究具有独立价值。

Abstract: Agentic time series forecasting extends conventional approaches beyond one-shot numerical prediction toward autonomous, context-aware interaction. However, existing benchmarks offer limited integrated support for diverse contextual information, forecasting-specific adaptive interactions, and systematic evaluation of forecasting agents. In this paper, we introduce CastWorld, an agentic benchmark for context-aware time series forecasting. First, its context-rich dataset suite spans 8 domains and 25 real-world datasets, covering external variables, events, and historical cases under a unified context specification. Second, a lightweight, modular forecasting-native harness provides 49 core tools and a state-adaptive loop that tracks forecast versions and enables iterative evidence gathering and forecast revision. Intermediate forecasts can expose information gaps, while newly acquired evidence guides subsequent revisions and final version selection. Third, model-level evaluation assesses forecast quality, process execution, behavioral trustworthiness, and resource efficiency through decision trajectories and forecast-version histories. Also, systems-level evaluation compares predictive accuracy across forecasting systems. Experiments reveal distinct decision behaviors among agents with comparable accuracy. Additional interactions do not necessarily improve forecasts, and agents may fail to select their best previously generated predictions. Allowing evidence acquisition during forecast refinement improves task-aware forecast scores across all three evaluated models. CastWorld provides a reusable foundation for evaluating agentic time series forecasting under controlled conditions.

</details>


### [66] [A hybrid machine learning-analog framework for downscaling unprecedented climate extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15284)
*Nina Effenberger,Florian E. Roemer,Maybritt Schillinger,Maxim Samarin,Erich Fischer,Reto Knutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种混合框架，将机器学习降尺度与基于类比的降尺度结合，用于从粗分辨率全球模式集合订正事件中高效生成高分辨率极端热浪情景，并证实ML生成的热浪在物理上真实可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气事件对气候影响评估至关重要。物理模型虽能通过集合订正（ensemble boosting）模拟前所未有的极端事件，但计算代价高昂，通常只能输出粗空间分辨率结果；而概率生成降尺度有望高效采样包含极端尾部在内的完整气候分布。

Method: 先在巴黎区域验证ML生成的热浪是否具备物理真实性；再借助模拟的机器学习数据集，对粗分辨率全球气候模式集合订正事件执行基于类比（analog-based）的降尺度，形成混合框架。

Result: ML生成的热浪被证实物理上真实；混合框架在高分辨率区域细节与稀有事件采样之间实现有效衔接，可作为极端事件降尺度的高效工具，并能刻画多模式不确定性。

Conclusion: 该混合降尺度框架为极端事件的高效降尺度提供了新工具，兼顾高分辨率与稀有极端采样，并保留多模式不确定性信息。

Abstract: Extreme weather events are critical for climate impact assessments. While physical models can simulate unprecedented extremes, for example, through ensemble boosting, this approach is computationally demanding, often restricting output to coarse spatial resolutions. Probabilistic generative downscaling offers a solution by enabling efficient sampling from the entire climate distribution, including its extreme tails. Here, we first demonstrate that machine learning generated regional climate model heatwaves over Paris are physically realistic. We then leverage an emulated machine learning dataset to perform analog-based downscaling of coarse global climate model ensemble boosting events. Our hybrid framework effectively bridges high-resolution regional detail with rare-event sampling, providing an efficient tool for extreme event downscaling and capturing multi-model uncertainty.

</details>


### [67] [Learning Rate Transfer and Feature Learning Across Depth for Constrained Spectral Optimizers: Complete Scion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15291)
*Eline Pot,Laurent Lefebvre,Mathieu Segond,Antonio Silveti-Falls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明对于Frank-Wolfe与投影非欧几里得归一化最速下降等约束优化方法，$1/L$残差缩放规则同样成立，从而支持学习率在深度与宽度方向的迁移，并在nanoGPT、DeiT、MLP-Mixer上验证了该迁移与特征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期CompleteP框架为Adam族优化器建立了$1/L$残差缩放规则，实现了深度方向的学习率迁移；但该结论是否能推广到更新由线性最小化预言机决定的约束优化方法（Frank-Wolfe、投影非欧几里得归一化最速下降）尚不明确。

Method: 为上述约束优化方法推导残差缩放规则，分析其与Adam族论证的差异；据此刻画Scion算法学习率在深度与宽度方向的迁移条件，并保证特征学习、避免落入kernel regime。

Result: 推导出的缩放规则确认$1/L$残差缩放对约束方法同样适用；实验在NLP transformer（nanoGPT/FineWeb）、视觉transformer（DeiT/ImageNet）与视觉MLP（MLP-Mixer/CIFAR-10）上均观察到深度方向学习率迁移及特征学习。

Conclusion: $1/L$残差缩放是跨优化器族的普适性规律，其理论依据虽与Adam族不同但结论一致；合理选择残差缩放即可在Scion中实现学习率跨深度与宽度迁移，同时保持特征学习。

Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer across model depth is instrumental in training deep models, as it enables learning rates calibrated on shallow networks to be effectively applied to deeper architectures. The recent CompleteP framework establishes a $1/L$ residual scaling rule that demonstrates this for Adam-family optimizers. We study whether similar transfer extends to constrained optimization methods, specifically Frank-Wolfe and projected non-Euclidean normalized steepest descent, where updates are determined by linear minimization oracles. We derive scaling rules for these methods which confirm that the $1/L$ residual scaling applies, although the arguments differ from the Adam-family optimizers. Our analysis supports depth-wise transfer and width-wise transfer of the learning rate for the Scion algorithm while ensuring feature learning and avoiding the kernel-regime. In experiments on NLP transformers (nanoGPT on FineWeb), vision transformers (DeiT on ImageNet), and vision MLPs (MLP-Mixer on CIFAR-10), we observe learning rate transfer across depth and feature learning for appropriately chosen residual scaling.

</details>


### [68] [Sharp Covering-Number Bounds for Feedforward ReLU Networks via Parameter Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15300)
*Ruizhi Deng,Guohao Shen,Tangrui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画 ReLU/Leaky ReLU 网络（全连接、卷积、残差架构及一般连续分段线性激活网络）参数空间的对称性（正通道缩放与通道置换），通过商掉这两种对称得到覆盖数的紧上下界，并据此给出经验风险最小化的统计风险保证，解释权重再平衡与损失平坦方向现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 名义参数量会高估网络的功能复杂度，因为不同参数配置常实现同一输入—输出映射。需要刻画这些冗余（参数空间对称性），以得到反映真实功能复杂度的容量度量。

Method: 证明保持网络函数不变的拟解析参数映射必为正通道缩放与通道置换的复合；对两种对称做商，推导一致的覆盖数上界：正缩放把复杂度指数从总参数数 S 降至 S−H（H 为可缩放通道数），置换对称使上界除以通道置换数 G 的阶乘；并构造匹配下界（指数 S−H 一般紧，除数 G 在一隐层与一般可辨识网络中可达）；结果推广至一般连续分段线性激活网络；最后用覆盖数界推导经验风险最小化的统计风险保证与非 i.i.d. 下的容量控制。

Result: 得到指数为 S−H、除数为 G 的覆盖数上下界（上界来自对称性商化，下界证明其锐性），并将风险保证与非 i.i.d. 容量控制建立在这些覆盖数界之上；同时说明对称性可解释函数逼近中的权重再平衡与经验风险优化中的平坦方向。

Conclusion: 网络参数空间的本质对称性仅为正通道缩放与通道置换，商掉它们可显著收紧容量界（指数降 H、界除以 G），这为理解网络真实复杂度、推广性及权重再平衡/平坦性等现象提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: In neural networks, distinct parameter configurations often realize the same input--output mapping, indicating that nominal parameter counts can overstate functional complexity. In this paper, we characterize the parameter-space symmetries of ReLU and Leaky ReLU networks across fully connected, convolutional, and residual architectures. We prove that every quasianalytic parameter map preserving the network function is a composition of positive channel scaling and channel permutation. By quotienting out both symmetries, we derive uniform covering-number upper bounds in which positive scaling reduces the complexity exponent from the total parameter count $S$ to $S-H$ (with $H$ denoting the number of scalable channels) and permutation symmetry divides the bound by the factorial count $G$ of channel permutations. We establish matching lower bounds showing that the exponent $S-H$ is sharp in general and that the divisor $G$ is attained for one-hidden-layer and generically identifiable networks. We further extend these results to general continuous piecewise-linear activated networks. Finally, we apply our covering-number bounds to derive statistical risk guarantees for empirical risk minimization and capacity control beyond i.i.d.\ settings, and show how the underlying symmetries help explain weight rebalancing in function approximation and flat directions in empirical risk optimization.

</details>


### [69] [TabOmics: A Small, Open Tabular Foundation Model for Wide Omics Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15329)
*Andrea López-García,Luis Bote-Curiel,Francisco Javier Gimeno-Blanes,José Luis Rojo-Álvarez,Sergio Muñoz-Romero*

Main category: cs.LG
