TL;DR: TabOmics 是一个仅 7.3M 参数、完全在合成表格上预训练的开放表格基础模型家族，联合处理预处理后保留的全部特征，单次前向即输出预测、attention 特征重要性与样本嵌入；在高维小样本的 46 个分类任务上以八成员集成取得平均 AUROC 0.943，优于 8 成员 TabPFN-3.5 且参数量约少 30 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组学表格通常具有上万分子特征而样本极少（宽表、高维小样本），现有表格基础模型多依赖 in-context learning，对宽表常需子采样、分组或特征压缩，难以联合建模全部特征。

Method: 提出 TabOmics：7.3M 参数的小型开放表格基础模型家族，仅用合成表格预训练；对预处理后保留的全部特征联合建模，单次前向传播同时给出预测、基于 attention 的特征重要性评分与测试样本嵌入。

Result: 在三个基准的 46 个高维低样本分类任务上：v2.5 八成员集成平均 AUROC 0.943，为评测最高，在 32 个任务上超过八成员 TabPFN-3.5（参数量约少 30 倍）；单成员 0.941，对比 TabPFN-3.5 八成员 0.942，任务级配对分析无显著差异。合成表格上特征重要性恢复效果与 TabPFN-Wide 相近，在 30,000 特征下预测仍优于随机。乳腺癌任务中排名靠前基因包含定义亚型标签的 PAM50 基因，属与标签定义一致而非独立生物学验证。

Conclusion: 小型、纯合成预训练的表格基础模型可在高维小样本组学任务上以极低参数量达到与大型模型相当或更优的性能，并提供可解释的特征重要性；但生物学有效性尚未独立验证。代码、权重、训练配方与实验产物均公开。

Abstract: Omics studies often produce tables with thousands to tens of thousands of molecular features and few samples. Tabular foundation models use diverse strategies to predict labels for such tables, with some relying on in-context learning, while approaches to wide tables may involve subsampling, grouping, or compressing features. We present TabOmics, a small, open family of tabular foundation models with 7.3M parameters, pretrained exclusively on synthetic tables. TabOmics processes all features retained after preprocessing jointly and derives predictions, attention-derived feature-importance scores, and test sample embeddings from the same forward pass. On 46 classification tasks from three benchmarks of high-dimensional, low-sample-size tables, TabOmics v2.5 with eight ensemble members achieves the highest mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) among the evaluated models (0.943), outperforming TabPFN-3.5 with eight members on 32 tasks despite having about 30 times fewer parameters. With a single member, TabOmics v2.5 reaches a mean AUROC of 0.941, versus 0.942 for TabPFN-3.5 with eight members, with no significant paired difference under the reported task-level analysis. On synthetic tables, its feature-importance scores achieve feature recovery similar to that of TabPFN-Wide, and its predictions remain above chance at 30,000 features. On breast cancer tasks, the top ranked genes include PAM50 genes, which define the subtype labels, indicating concordance with the label definition rather than independent biological validation. The code, model weights, training recipes, and experimental artifacts are publicly available.

</details>


### [70] [MechBench: A Domain-Specific LLM Benchmark for Parallel Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15342)
*Zhihao Xia,Tingting Wang,Hui Li,Yongheng Xing,Chunxu Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MECHBENCH（2541题）基准，从专业知识、机构分析与设计三方面评估LLM在并联机构上的能力，并通过MECHFORGE构建、MECHGPT诊断域适配模型，揭示知识表现无法转化为可执行推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联机构的有效推理依赖机构拓扑、闭环约束、几何、关节类型与建模假设的联合作用，而现有LLM基准缺乏对该领域知识、约束敏感分析与构造式设计的系统覆盖。

Method: 构建2541题基准MECHBENCH，含标准与挑战两档、六大能力类别、九项可执行分析与设计任务；用MECHFORGE框架构建（专家组织知识、LLM辅助出题、程序化实例生成、两阶段专家审核、任务感知评分：精确匹配/评分细则/程序验证）；并开发域适配诊断模型MECHGPT。

Result: 领先闭源模型标准档>95分，挑战档仅47–60分；MECHGPT较Qwen3-8B基线显著提升，但在约束分析与设计上仍有较大差距。

Conclusion: 强知识表现并不直接转化为可执行机构推理，MECHBENCH可作为系统测量机构科学领域LLM能力的测试平台。

Abstract: Parallel mechanisms pose distinctive challenges to large language models (LLMs), as valid reasoning depends jointly on mechanism topology, loop closure, geometry, joint types, and modeling assumptions. Yet existing LLM benchmarks provide little systematic coverage of such domain-specific knowledge, constraint-sensitive analysis, and constructive design. To fill this gap, we introduce MECHBENCH, a 2,541-question benchmark for evaluating LLMs in parallel mechanisms across professional knowledge, mechanism analysis, and design. MECHBENCH contains standard and challenge tiers spanning six capability categories and nine executable analysis and design tasks. It is constructed through MECHFORGE, a domain-grounded framework that combines expert-organized knowledge, LLM-assisted question generation, programmatic instance generation, two-stage expert review, and task-aware scoring with exact matching, rubric-based evaluation, and program verification. We further develop MECHGPT as a diagnostic domain-adapted model. Experiments show that strong knowledge performance does not directly translate into executable mechanism reasoning: leading closed-service models achieve over 95 on the standard tier but only 47-60 on the challenge tier, while domain adaptation substantially improves MechGPT over its Qwen3-8B baselines but leaves a large gap in constrained analysis and design. These results establish MECHBENCH as a systematic testbed for measuring domain-specific LLM capabilities in mechanism science.

</details>


### [71] [Breath-Dx: A Time Series Classification Benchmark for Disease Screening with eNose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15348)
*Ivan Poddiakov,Ivan Kruzhilov,Galina Zubkova,Svetlana Erofeeva,Andrey Savchenko,Pavel Blinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Breath-Dx，首个面向呼气式疾病筛查的时间序列分类方法系统性基准，基于迄今最大的公开临床电子鼻数据集S-OH（1,234名患者、9个诊断组、17通道多元时间序列），采用显式考虑传感器漂移的时间划分，系统评估10种方法。结果显示所有方法在时序验证下AUC下降0.05–0.12，最佳方法在部分肺部疾病上ROC AUC为0.74–0.76，循环架构表现最稳定，并公开数据与协议以促进可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子鼻呼气分析具备便携、无创的疾病筛查潜力，但领域缺乏系统性基准：现有研究多依赖小型私有数据集与不一致协议，结果不可比；且传感器漂移这一已知失效模式很少被量化，阻碍了方法比较与研究进展。

Method: 构建Breath-Dx基准，基于S-OH公开临床电子鼻数据集（1,234例、9个诊断组、每例17通道多元时间序列），提供显式考虑传感器漂移的时间训练/测试划分；系统评估跨四类家族的10种方法：基于特征的表学习方法、卷积模型、图模型与时间序列专用架构，并与随机交叉验证对比，同时分析人口学混杂因素（如年龄）的影响。

Result: 所有方法在时间验证下均显著退化，AUC较随机交叉验证下降0.05–0.12，表明漂移本身携带强时间信号；在此现实评估下，最佳方法在部分肺部疾病上达到ROC AUC 0.74–0.76，其余疾病接近随机水平；循环架构跨疾病表现最一致，专用时间序列方法取得最高峰值结果；人口学混杂因素具有影响，呼气信号对部分疾病提供独立诊断价值，对另一些疾病在年龄之外增益有限。

Conclusion: 传感器漂移是电子鼻疾病筛查中不可忽视且可量化的失效模式，随机交叉验证会高估性能，应采用时间划分进行现实评估；不同方法家族在稳定性与峰值性能上各有取舍。作者公开S-OH数据集、全部时间划分、评估协议与代码，以支持可复现研究并加速临床呼气组学进展。

Abstract: Electronic nose (eNose) technology offers a portable, non-invasive route for disease screening via exhaled breath analysis. However, progress in the field is hindered by the lack of systematic benchmarks that compare machine learning methods under realistic conditions, particularly sensor drift, a known but rarely quantified failure mode. Most existing studies rely on small, private datasets with inconsistent protocols, rendering results non-comparable and slowing progress. We introduce Breath-Dx, the first comprehensive benchmark of time series classification methods for breath-based disease screening. Our work leverages the Scent of Health (S-OH) dataset, the largest public clinical eNose collection to date, comprising 1,234 patients across nine diagnostic groups, each providing a 17-channel multivariate time series with explicit temporal train/test splits that account for sensor drift. We systematically evaluate 10 methods spanning four families: feature-based tabular learners, convolution-based models, graph-based, and time series architectures. All methods degrade significantly under temporal validation (0.05-0.12 AUC drop compared to random cross-validation), confirming that drift embeds a strong temporal signal. Under this realistic evaluation, the best methods achieve ROC AUC of 0.74-0.76 for some lung conditions, though others remain near chance. Recurrent architectures show the most consistent performance across diseases, while specialized time series methods achieve the highest peak results. Demographic confounders matter: breath signals provide independent diagnostic value for some conditions but add little beyond age for others. We release the S-OH dataset, all temporal splits, evaluation protocols, and code to enable reproducible research and accelerate progress in clinical breathomics.

</details>


### [72] [A Modified Glicko Model for Rating Soccer Clubs and National Teams Worldwide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15378)
*Andrei Shelopugin,Alexander Sirotkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于改进Glicko模型的统一评分框架，将全球俱乐部与国家队置于同一概率尺度，用约120万场俱乐部比赛和1.26万场国家队比赛验证其预测精度优于Elo、与基于梯度提升期望进球的比分矩阵模型（Dixon-Coles、马尔可夫链）相当，跨国赛事尤具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俱乐部多在各自国内联赛比赛、国家队多在所属大洲联合会内比赛，跨体系交锋稀少，导致难以比较几乎不交手的球队。需要一个能把全球俱乐部与国家队放在共同概率尺度上的评分框架，并支持球员评估、转会分析与赛果预测。

Method: 在近期带顺序效应与强度相关平局概率的Glicko扩展基础上做出足球化改造：加入进球数调整；分解主场优势；处理加时赛；引入联赛感知的评分初始化，以及不抬高联赛整体水平的升降级规则；国家队以所属大洲联合会水平为起点。数据为252个联赛（166国）约120万场俱乐部比赛（2000–2026）与1.26万场国家队比赛（1998–2026），以留出赛季做评估，基线包括Elo与基于梯度提升模型进球期望构建的比分矩阵模型（Dixon-Coles、马尔可夫链）。

Result: 在留出赛季上，改进Glicko明显比Elo更准，与Dixon-Coles和马尔可夫链等比分矩阵模型精度相当，而在国际俱乐部赛事中显著更准；将评分模型与马尔可夫链模型的预测取平均优于任一单一模型。国家队方面，该模型显著优于所有基线，在跨联合会球队相遇的决赛阶段尤为突出。

Conclusion: 所提评分可解释、可迁移，能用于球员评估、转会分析和赛果预测；比赛数据、评分与代码已在GitHub公开（https://github.com/andreyshelopugin/SportsRatings）。

Abstract: Comparing soccer teams that rarely or never meet is a fundamental challenge in sports analytics: clubs play mostly within their national leagues, and national teams within their confederations. We propose a rating framework that places clubs and national teams worldwide on a common probabilistic scale. Building on a recent extension of Glicko with order effects and strength-dependent draw probabilities, we adapt the model to soccer with goal-scoring adjustments, a decomposition of home advantage, extra time, league-aware rating initialization and rules of promotion and relegation that do not inflate the levels of leagues; national teams start from the levels of their confederations. The models are evaluated on about 1.2 million club matches of 252 leagues from 166 countries (2000-2026) and 12.6 thousand national team matches (1998-2026). On held-out seasons, the modified Glicko model is clearly more accurate than Elo and as accurate as score-matrix models built on the goal expectations of a gradient boosting model, including the Dixon-Coles and Markov chain models, while it is substantially more accurate in international club matches. Averaging the predictions of the rating model and the Markov chain model is more accurate than either model alone. For national teams, the model is significantly more accurate than all baselines, especially in the final tournaments, where teams of different confederations meet. The resulting ratings are interpretable and can be used for player evaluation, transfer analysis and match forecasting. The match data, the ratings and the code are publicly available at https://github.com/andreyshelopugin/SportsRatings

</details>


### [73] [Is the Importance Ratio Necessary for Stable Reinforcement Learning in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15404)
*Shuibai Zhang,Junhyuck Kim,Gyeongman Kim,Jaewoong Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出似然门控策略优化（LGPO），以基于似然的门控实现软信任域约束，省去旧策略似然计算，在保持稳定性的同时将训练时间平均降低约18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等主流RL方法需同时计算旧策略与当前策略的似然以构造重要性采样比，带来不可忽视的计算开销；同时基于比率裁剪的信任域约束在完全on-policy训练下失效（比率恒为1），导致训练崩溃。

Method: LGPO通过基于似然的门控机制施加软信任域约束，从而无需计算旧策略似然及重要性采样修正项。实验采用消融分析分别去除重要性采样修正项与信任域机制，考察二者对训练稳定性的影响。

Result: 去除重要性采样修正项不损害训练稳定性，而移除信任域机制会导致崩溃；在GRPO稳定的标准设置下，LGPO稳定性与峰值性能相当，训练时间平均减少约18%；在GRPO失效的完全on-policy训练中LGPO仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 基于比率的裁剪式信任域存在根本缺陷，似然门控是更稳健的替代方案；LGPO实现了更高效、更鲁棒的大模型RL后训练，适用于多种训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training large language models (LLMs). However, popular RL methods like GRPO incur non-negligible overhead by computing both old-policy and current-policy likelihoods to form importance sampling ratios. In this work, we propose Likelihood-Gated Policy Optimization (LGPO), which enforces a soft trust region constraint via likelihood-based gating, eliminating the need to compute old-policy likelihoods. Empirically, we show that removing the importance sampling correction term does not harm training stability, whereas removing the trust region mechanism can lead to collapse. Moreover, ratio-based clipping can fail in fully on-policy training: the importance ratio stays at 1, so the ratio-based trust region constraint never activates. Under standard training settings where GRPO is stable, LGPO achieves comparable training stability and peak performance while reducing training time by ~18% on average relative to a standard implementation that recomputes old-policy likelihoods. In fully on-policy training, where GRPO fails, LGPO remains stable, enabling more efficient and robust LLM RL post-training across training regimes.

</details>


### [74] [MORA: Correcting Time Series Forecasts with Coarse Future Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15413)
*Hu Li,Kenan Li,Haonan Xu,longlong,Shuqin Gao,Yucheng Zhang,Feng Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的残差适配器MORA，通过估计目标序列的分段均值并对骨干预测进行分阶段校正，利用可预测的粗尺度残差提升冻结预测器的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析多种架构的预测器发现：残差能量大量集中在少数低频分量上，且历史输入仍含有关预测均值误差的预测信息，说明粗尺度残差可被显式利用。

Method: MORA为模型无关适配器，无需额外输入，作用于冻结骨干的输出。它通过结构外推与学习估计得到目标序列的分段均值，而非直接预测骨干特定的残差；再将估计均值与骨干预测对应统计量之间的差异转换为分阶段校正，并按估计可靠性对每步更新进行缩放。

Result: 在12个基准、6个预测骨干上，长期预测平均MSE降低7.2%，短期预测降低28.1%，288个案例中96.2%取得更低MSE；在3个时间序列基础模型的不同上下文长度下同样有效。

Conclusion: 可预测的粗尺度残差能够被有效利用以改进现有预测，MORA作为一种通用后处理适配器具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Recent architectural advances have improved time-series forecasting. However, our analysis of forecasters with diverse architectures reveals a common pattern: a substantial share of residual energy is concentrated in a small number of low-frequency components. Simple linear probes further show that the forecasters' historical inputs still contain predictive information about errors in the forecast mean. Building on these findings, we propose Model-Output Residual Adaptation (MORA), a model-agnostic residual adapter that refines the outputs of frozen forecasters without requiring additional inputs. MORA estimates segment-wise means of the target sequence through structural extrapolation and learned estimation, rather than directly predicting backbone-specific residuals. It then converts discrepancies between these estimates and the corresponding statistics of the backbone forecast into stagewise corrections, scaling each update according to estimation reliability. Extensive experiments on twelve benchmarks with six forecasting backbones show average MSE reductions of 7.2% for long-term forecasting and 28.1% for short-term forecasting, with lower MSE in 96.2% of the 288 evaluated cases. The benefits also extend to three time-series foundation models evaluated at different context lengths. Together, these results demonstrate that predictable coarse-scale residuals can be effectively exploited to improve existing forecasts. Code repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MORA-B0B0/.

</details>


### [75] [Overcoming the Resolution Limit: Significance-Aware Regularization for Intersectional Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15427)
*Antonio Ferrara,André Panisson,Francesco Cozzi,Alan Perotti,Francesco Bonchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出显著性-超额（significant-excess）公平性度量族与SAFER正则化方法，用似然比证据门控只惩罚统计显著的子群差异，缓解稀疏交叉子群下的公平性过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉性公平评估需细分到细粒度子群，但子群样本量随粒度下降而急剧减少，公平性估计高度不稳定。现有in-processing方法"盲于不确定性"：直接惩罚观测到的差异，无法区分真实不公平与抽样噪声，导致在稀疏场景下为"修正"虚假差异而牺牲效用（公平性过拟合）。

Method: 提出significant-excess度量族：连续、含不确定性、仅在给定置信水平下度量超出抽样变异可解释范围的那部分子群差异。基于该度量构建SAFER——可微in-processing正则项，用软计数（soft-count）似然比证据统计量对公平性惩罚进行门控，并配合阈值平滑与致密尾部项。

Result: 理论证明安全性质：当所有子群差异显著低于门控阈值时，无约束最优点对SAFER近似驻定，门控组件的反传贡献指数级小。三个基准数据集及专门构造的压力变体上的机制消融显示，移除阈值平滑、显著性门控或致密尾部项中任一组件，都会在至少一个压力变体上出现理论预测的失效。稀疏交叉子群场景下SAFER的公平-效用Pareto前沿优于基线，且在不存显著不公平时保持效用。

Conclusion: 将统计显著性显式嵌入公平性正则化可有效抑制稀疏子群下的公平性过拟合，在无显著不公平情形下实现"几乎不干预"，为细粒度交叉公平提供兼具理论保证与实证优势的方案。

Abstract: Intersectional fairness requires evaluating models across fine-grained subgroups. However, as group definitions become more granular, the sample size per subgroup shrinks, causing fairness estimates to become increasingly unstable. Existing in-processing methods are certainty-blind: they penalize observed disparities without distinguishing statistically certified unfairness from sampling noise. This leads to fairness overfitting, where models sacrifice utility to "fix" spurious disparities in sparse regimes. To address this, we introduce the significant-excess metric family, a continuous, uncertainty-aware fairness score that measures only the portion of a subgroup's disparity exceeding what sampling variation can explain at a specific confidence level. Building on this metric, we propose SAFER (Significance-Aware Fairness Regularizer), a differentiable in-processing method that gates fairness penalties using a soft-count likelihood-ratio evidence statistic. SAFER satisfies a provable safety property: when every subgroup disparity lies well below the gate threshold, the unconstrained optimum is nearly stationary for SAFER. At that optimum, the back-propagated contribution of its significance-gated component is exponentially small, and the additional fairness contribution comes from a small ungated term with a fixed weight. Mechanism ablations across three benchmark datasets and purpose-built stress variants show that removing any component of SAFER (threshold smoothing, the significance gate, or a dense-tail term) produces the failure the theory predicts on at least one stress variant. In sparse intersectional settings, SAFER achieves a better fairness-utility Pareto frontier than the baseline methods, and it preserves utility when certified unfairness is absent.

</details>


### [76] [DETER: Suppression During Pretraining for Tamper Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15479)
*Maria Ios Glarou,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在预训练阶段主动抑制知识（梯度上升与标准预训练交替），比仅过滤语料或事后遗忘更能增强模型通过对抗性微调获取目标知识的难度，且随模型规模增大呈现更优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型可能获得生物、化学或网络滥用的知识，而权重发布后的知识限制必须经得起微调。事后遗忘易被快速逆转，预训练语料过滤抗性较强，但作者想探究：在预训练过程中主动抑制知识能否进一步提升微调获取所需成本。

Method: 在过滤后的预训练语料上，将简单的梯度上升与标准预训练交替进行（方法名 DETER），以抑制医学等目标知识；保留知识用留出数据和下游任务评估，并通过不同模型规模与抑制强度考察微调难度与留出损失的权衡。

Result: 在 1B 参数模型上，达到未过滤模型的 PubMed 留出损失所需微调 token 数为仅过滤情形的 20 倍以上；随模型规模变化，抑制强度增强使微调难度与保留数据留出损失之间的权衡日益有利。

Conclusion: 预训练是使不良能力难以通过后续微调获得的更有效阶段，比单纯过滤或事后遗忘更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Open-weight language models can acquire knowledge that could enable biological, chemical, or cyber misuse. Any attempt to limit such knowledge must remain effective after the model weights are released and can be fine-tuned. Unlearning applied after pretraining can be rapidly undone, whereas filtering the pretraining corpus offers greater resistance. We ask whether actively suppressing knowledge during pretraining can further increase the fine-tuning required to acquire it. Even simple gradient ascent interleaved with standard pretraining on the filtered corpus makes medical knowledge harder to acquire through adversarial fine-tuning than filtering alone or post-hoc unlearning. At 1B parameters, reaching the held-out PubMed loss of an unfiltered model requires over 20$\times$ as many PubMed fine-tuning tokens as after filtering alone. Retained knowledge is evaluated using both held-out data and downstream tasks. Across model sizes, varying suppression strength reveals an increasingly favorable trade-off between fine-tuning difficulty and held-out loss on retained pretraining data. These results point to pretraining as an effective stage for making unwanted capabilities harder to acquire through subsequent fine-tuning. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/MEGlr/DETER}{\texttt{github.com/MEGlr/DETER}}.

</details>


### [77] [The Illusion of Diversity: Aligning LLM Exploration via Effective Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15493)
*Xiaoliang Fu,Jiaye Lin,Yangyi Fang,Cong Qin,Binbin Zheng,Chaowen Hu,Zekai Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"有效熵"（Effective Entropy）概念，仅在有效（正确）子空间内度量多样性，并据此实例化 VEPO 算法，将有效熵直接引入 RLVR 训练，以自适应吸引机制提升低概率有效轨迹，实验证明其优于 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 将大模型对齐从主观偏好转向客观正确性，但探索多样性与推理有效性之间存在脱节。现有方法依赖"盲"熵（token 级或全局序列级熵），无法区分有效推理与潜在幻觉，导致高熵往往意味着混乱退化而非有价值的探索，形成"多样性幻觉"。

Method: 定义有效熵：仅在有效子空间内量化多样性，系统过滤错误轨迹噪声。基于该概念实例化 VEPO（Validity-aware Entropy Policy Optimization），将有效熵直接融入 RLVR 训练，形成自适应吸引动态，提高探索不足的有效轨迹的概率；该机制适配大规模自回归模型中序列概率极小的实际情形。

Result: 在 1.5B 与 7B DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen 模型、六个数学推理基准上，相较 GRPO 取得一致的总体准确率提升，重复采样成功率具竞争力；在匹配正确数的直接评估中，MATH500 常见题上呈现更多不同解法，并有配对统计检验与三随机种子训练对比支撑。

Conclusion: 以有效性感知的熵替代盲目熵度量，可在不牺牲正确性的前提下获得真实而非虚假的探索多样性，为 RLVR 的探索机制提供了原理简洁且可扩展的改进方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has shifted the alignment paradigm of large language models from subjective preference to objective correctness, yet a fundamental disconnect persists between exploration diversity and reasoning validity. Existing strategies predominantly rely on "blind" entropy metrics, e.g., token-level or global sequence-level entropy, which structurally fail to distinguish between effective reasoning and potential hallucinations. This misalignment creates an illusion of diversity, where high entropy tends to signal chaotic degeneration rather than valuable exploration. To bridge this gap, we introduce Effective Entropy, a validity-aware entropy concept that quantifies diversity exclusively within the valid subspace and systematically filters out the noise of incorrect trajectories. Built on this concept, we instantiate VEPO, a simple Validity-aware Entropy Policy Optimization method that directly incorporates effective entropy into RLVR training. VEPO induces an adaptive attraction dynamic that drives up the probability of under-explored valid trajectories, a mechanism that matches the practical regime of large-scale autoregressive models where sequence probabilities remain extremely small. Experiments on six mathematical reasoning benchmarks with 1.5B and 7B DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen models show consistent aggregate accuracy gains over GRPO and competitive repeated-sampling success. A direct evaluation with matched correct counts finds more distinct strategies on commonly solved MATH500 problems, supported by paired statistical tests and three-seed training comparisons.

</details>


### [78] [Selective State-Space Neural Tucker Factorization for Temporal-aware Traffic Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15502)
*Zhenhao Li,Peng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出选择性状态空间神经Tucker分解模型（S3NTF），结合选择性状态空间机制与神经Tucker分解，实现线性复杂度长时序建模与高阶时空交互学习，用于鲁棒交通数据插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通数据具有强长期时间依赖，准确建模长时序动态是高保真插补的核心前提。现有方法在长序列建模上存在梯度消失、效率低、长程时序相关性捕获不足等固有缺陷。

Method: 1）将选择性状态空间机制（Selective State Space）与神经Tucker分解（Neural Tucker Factorization）相结合；2）设计基于状态空间的时间编码器，以线性复杂度提取长序列依赖；3）构建潜在时空特征交互学习模块，基于Tucker分解建模高阶跨模态交互；4）捕获复杂空间相关性与长期时序动态。

Result: 在六个真实交通数据集上的大量实验表明，该模型在插补准确率上优于现有模型。

Conclusion: S3NTF通过状态空间长时序编码与Tucker高阶交互建模的有效融合，缓解了长序列建模的效率与长程依赖瓶颈，验证了其在交通数据插补任务中的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal traffic data imputation is a key task of intelligent transportation systems (ITS), and urban traffic data inherently exhibits strong long-term temporal dependencies. Therefore, accurate modeling of these long-term temporal dynamics is the core prerequisite for high fidelity traffic data imputation. However, existing traffic data imputation methods still suffer from inherent limitations in long-sequence modeling, such as gradient vanishing, low efficiency, and insufficient capability to capture long-range temporal correlations. To address these issues, this paper proposes a Selective State Space Neural Tucker Factorization model (S3NTF) for robust traffic data imputation. This model combines the Selective State Space mechanism with Neural Tucker Factorization to capture complex spatial correlations and long-term temporal dynamics. A state space-based time encoder is proposed to effectively extracts long sequence dependencies with linear complexity, while the latent spatiotemporal feature interaction learning module models high-order cross modal interactions based on Tucker decomposition. Extensive experiments on six real traffic datasets have shown that our model outperforms existing models in terms of accuracy.

</details>


### [79] [ReShap: Interpretable Reinforcement Learning for High-Dimensional Multi-Action Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15507)
*Yiyu Qian,Pengfei Guo,Liyuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReShap 将 Shapley 可解释性通过策略蒸馏扩展到高维多动作环境，用决策树/线性/逻辑回归三类代理模型对比保真度与回报，并在 Atari、Box2D 与用户研究中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度 RL 策略不透明，现有 Shapley 可解释工作（如 SILVER）受限于低维状态、二值动作和单一线性代理模型。

Method: 从原始图像观测提取紧凑特征表示；做 SHAP 特征归因刻画各特征对决策的贡献；沿策略访问分布用全部 (state, policy-action) 对将深度 RL 策略蒸馏为可解释代理；代理实例化为决策树、线性回归、逻辑回归三类。

Result: 在 Atari 与 Box2D 上用三种深度 RL 算法评估：ReShap 保持有竞争力的任务性能；决策树总体优于线性与逻辑回归；线性回归在多数设置中最弱；用户研究评估策略清晰度与可信度。

Conclusion: Shapley 可解释性可经策略蒸馏推广到高维多动作场景，决策树是较优的可解释代理选择。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) plays a crucial role in tackling complex tasks, yet its reliance on deep neural networks makes the learned policies opaque. To enhance the trustworthiness of RL policies, existing work (e.g., the SILVER framework [Li et al., 2025]) has focused on Shapley-based interpretability, but is typically restricted to low-dimensional state spaces, binary-action settings, and a single class of linear surrogate. We propose ReShap, a policy-distillation framework that extends Shapley-based interpretability to high-dimensional, multi-action environments. ReShap extracts compact feature representations from raw image observations, performs SHAP-based feature attribution to characterize how each feature contributes to the policy's decisions, and distills the deep RL policy into an interpretable surrogate trained on the full set of (state, policy-action) pairs along the policy's visited distribution. We instantiate the surrogate with three interpretable model families (decision trees, linear regression, and logistic regression) and compare their fidelity and task return. We evaluate ReShap on Atari and Box2D environments using three deep RL algorithms and conduct a human-subject study to assess the clarity and trustworthiness of the resulting policies. Results show that ReShap preserves competitive task performance, that decision trees generally outperform linear and logistic regression, and that linear regression is weakest in most settings.

</details>


### [80] [An Approach to Hinton's Mother-Child Proposal: Neural Networks Learning and Preserving Care in a Finite World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15523)
*Csaba Balogh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"先学关怀、网络当孩子"的框架：用危害词表、可学习未来可接受度度量与执行否决机制训练有限世界中的"关怀"，并论证在表示、监督与执行三方面明确条件下，习得关怀可在母体离场、新情境与后续学习中存续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hinton主张应赋予AI"母性本能"、如母待子般对待人类；作者认为护理/关怀能力不能预设，必须先被学习，且学习主体应是网络自身（网络作为孩子）。核心问题：在有限世界中习得的关怀能否经受母体（监督者）离开、新情境与继续学习而不失效。

Method: 机制由三部分组合：危害词表、习得的"未来可接受度"度量、执行阶段的否决（veto）。有限角色证书支持在声明的"世界族"内迁移；隐表示变化时，更新仅在关怀读出（care readout）以规定间隔恢复所需判断且权重有界时被接受。采用固定精确角色编码与受保护更新，证明在正确后继模型下认证度量在任意自身奖励下保持关怀边界；给出商条件（quotient condition）实现证书跨族迁移，并用"两请求"与"有限库存"反例刻画角色编码失效的情形；给出读出可行性的充要条件及反复接受更新下的保持性证明。水与言语实验中，执行仅用学习到的门控，无需每步动作做世界模型检验。

Result: 受保护网络在270个言语回合中未产生有害词，并在全部486个回合中无可避免伤害；与未来感知shield的决策相匹配，而去掉前瞻则出现可避免失误。奖励与来源污染阈值及见证伪造（witness-falsification）试验界定了该构造的依赖条件。

Conclusion: 习得关怀可以在有限世界中、在关于表示、监督与执行的显式条件下经受进一步学习而存续；但角色编码与证书的有效性受限于声明的世界族与读出可行性条件。

Abstract: Hinton proposed that artificial intelligence should be given "maternal instincts" and relate to us as a mother to her child. We argue that care must first be learned, with the network itself as the child. We construct this upbringing in a finite world and study whether care survives the mother's departure, new situations and further learning. The mechanism combines a vocabulary of harm, a learned measure of future acceptability and an execution veto. A finite role certificate supports transfer within a declared world family. When the hidden representation changes, an update is accepted only if a care readout recovers the required judgements with a prescribed margin and bounded weights. With a correct successor model, fixed exact role encoding and protected updates, the certified measure preserves the care boundary under arbitrary own reward. An explicit quotient condition transfers the finite certificate across the declared family, while two-request and finite-stock counterexamples identify when the role code ceases to suffice. We give a necessary and sufficient condition for readout feasibility and prove preservation under repeated accepted updates. In the water-and-speech experiment, protected networks complete all 270 speech episodes without a harmful word and incur no avoidable harm across 486 episodes. Execution uses the learned gate without a world-model check at each action. A matched comparison reproduces a future-aware shield's decisions, whereas removing future lookahead permits avoidable lapses. Reward and source-contamination thresholds, together with witness-falsification trials, identify the conditions on which the construction depends. The construction shows how learned care can survive further learning in a finite world under explicit conditions on representation, supervision and execution.

</details>


### [81] [One Hierarchy, Many Sports: Hierarchy-Aligned Multi-Task Learning for Next-Shot Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15529)
*Chao-Wei Cai,Min-Te Sun,Sheng Kuang Wu,Hung-Hsuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MT-HTA——一种层次对齐的多任务Transformer，利用球拍类运动（乒乓球、羽毛球、网球）中"击球→回合→计分单元"的共同层次结构，联合预测下一击属性，并在三运动四数据集上取得最高的平均任务F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球拍类运动的下一击预测需处理赛事事件数据在比赛结构、记录属性与标签词表上的差异；但乒乓球、羽毛球、网球共享"击球构成回合、回合构成更高层计分单元"的层次结构，该共性是构建可复用模型的切入点。

Method: MT-HTA为层次对齐的多任务Transformer，含三条路径：时序Transformer刻画事件演进；特征token路径建模语义字段间（而非时间轴）的交互；任务特定注意力从层次摘要与球员上下文中检索信息。架构支持三级与四级层次及随运动变化的任​​务集合，可适配异构事件数据。

Result: 在三种运动的四个数据集上，MT-HTA的平均任务F1在对比主模型中最高。乒乓球的消融表明层次编码、任务注意力与特征token交互的组合有效；附加模块主要改变分类分数与空间误差之间的权衡，而非全面提升各项指标。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种可复用于异构球拍类运动事件建模的架构，代码已开源以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Next-shot prediction in racket sports requires learning from event data with different match structures, recorded attributes, and label vocabularies. Table tennis, badminton, and tennis nevertheless share a hierarchy in which shots form rallies and rallies form higher scoring units. We present MT-HTA, a hierarchy-aligned multi-task Transformer that uses this structure to jointly predict the attributes of the next shot. A temporal Transformer captures event progression, a feature-token path models interactions among semantic fields rather than along the time axis, and task-specific attention retrieves information from hierarchical summaries and player context. The architecture supports three- and four-level hierarchies and sport-dependent task sets. Across four datasets from three sports, the final MT-HTA achieves the highest mean task-averaged F1 among the compared main models. Ablations on table tennis support the combination of hierarchical encoding, task attention, and feature-token interaction. Additional modules alter the balance between classification scores and spatial error rather than improving every metric. These results support a reusable architecture for heterogeneous racket-sport event data. We release the code for reproducibility. https://github.com/gndd1221/For-Sports-event-modeling-with-rally-and-score-hierarchical-A-Multi-Task-Hierarchical-Transformer

</details>


### [82] [What Conformal Selection Can Certify in CRISPR Off-Target Design:Benchmark Leakage, Free Baselines, and a Finite-Sample Cohort Requirement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15555)
*Mohammad Malekpouri,Somayeh Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出，基于现有公开脱靶数据无法实现CRISPR向导设计的共形选择"安全证书"：共形p值存在1/(n+1)的算术下界，导致候选集几乎总为空；同时揭示CIRCLE-seq基准文件中的分组交叉验证泄漏问题，并给出修正协议与基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形选择被提出作为机器学习CRISPR向导设计的分布无关、有限样本误差控制"安全层"，作者质疑在现实验证的脱靶数据下这种保证是否可达。

Method: 通过算术分析给<b>出共形p值的下界约束，推导Benjamini-Hochberg选择所需的校准队列规模；审计标准CIRCLE-seq数据文件，发现按序列列分组交叉验证导致训练/测试共用同一向导（泄漏），修正后重新评估无训练的位置权重打分与近匹配位点计数基线，并与训练型oracle比较。

Result: 公开队列仅提供10个验证向导，而2000候选池、10%错误率需20000样本，导致超过96%的划分中接受集为空；泄漏修正后，配对级判别与逐向导保真度均显著下降，训练型oracle也未能超越简单的免训练打分与近匹配位点计数。

Conclusion: 现有数据不足以支撑共形认证流水线；作者给出修正后的评估协议、新模型必须超越的基线，以及认证流水线所需的最小队列规模。

Abstract: Conformal selection promises distribution-free, finite-sample control of the error rate of a decision, and has been proposed as a safety layer for machine-learning CRISPR guide design. We ask whether such a certificate is attainable from the validated off-target data that exist. The constraint is arithmetic: a conformal p-value cannot fall below one divided by the calibration cohort size plus one, so a Benjamini-Hochberg selection certifies a single candidate only when that cohort is at least the candidate pool size divided by the tolerated error rate. Public cohorts supply ten validated guides, whereas a pool of two thousand at a ten percent error rate needs twenty thousand; the accepted set is consequently empty in over ninety-six percent of splits. Two benchmarking findings explain why this went unnoticed. The standard CIRCLE-seq file lists ten guides under eighty on-target strings, so grouping cross-validation on the sequence column trains and tests on one guide; corrected, pair-level discrimination and per-guide fidelity collapse. With that leakage removed, a training-free position-weight score and a bare count of near-match genomic sites are not beaten by a trained oracle. We give the corrected protocol, the baselines a new model must beat, and the cohort size a certified pipeline needs.

</details>


### [83] [Observations Drift, Structures Remain: Structural Pretraining with Time Alignment for Electromagnetic Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15566)
*Wenjin Gui,Junyu Shen,Haibo Xu,Yuchuang Sun,Luqing Luo,Wupeng Xie,Qian Huang,Fengxiang Wang,Yangang Sun,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异构I/Q电磁信号预训练中的"观测偏置"问题，提出结构预训练+时间对齐（RoPE-fs + 离散结构单元掩码预测）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁信号建模趋向自监督预训练，但观测波形由信号生成、传播环境与采集过程共同决定，混杂了可复用结构规律与观测特定变异。现有方法在混合观测上以重建目标统一预训练，导致两类错配：传播引起的"外观错配"与采集采样坐标引起的"时间错配"，使模型更易重建观测特定外观与采样坐标而非稳定信号结构，形成观测捷径（observation-biased pretraining）。

Method: 提出Structural Pretraining with Time Alignment：(1) RoPE-fs，将token位置映射到按采样率归一化的物理时间位置，缓解时间错配；(2) 以离散结构单元的掩码预测替代波形重建，减少对外观捷径的依赖；结构单元分为原子结构单元与涌现结构单元，后者捕获超出固定patch边界的变长组合模式。

Result: 在多个电磁基准上，相比现有电磁预训练基线，在全监督与少样本适配两种设定下均取得提升，表明所学表征在波形外观与时间尺度漂移时保持了更好的结构一致性。

Conclusion: 将预训练目标从观测级重建转向结构级预测，并结合物理时间对齐，可有效抑制观测偏置，提升异构电磁信号表征的鲁棒性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Electromagnetic signal modeling is increasingly moving toward self-supervised pretraining on heterogeneous I/Q observations. However, an observed waveform is jointly shaped by signal generation, propagation environments, and acquisition processes, which mix reusable structural regularities with observation-specific variations. Existing methods usually pursue unified pretraining with reconstruction objectives on mixed observations. This heterogeneity creates two key mismatches for reconstruction pretraining: (1) appearance mismatch, induced by propagation-dependent waveform variations; and (2) temporal mismatch, induced by acquisition-dependent sampling coordinates. These mismatches make observation-specific appearances and sampling coordinates easier to reconstruct than stable signal structures, inducing observational shortcuts. We identify this failure mode as observation-biased pretraining. To address this, we propose Structural Pretraining with Time Alignment for heterogeneous EM signals. It mitigates temporal mismatch with RoPE-fs, which maps token positions to sampling-rate-normalized physical-time positions, and reduces reliance on appearance shortcuts by replacing waveform reconstruction with masked prediction of discrete structural units. These units are organized into atomic structural units and emergent structural units that capture variable-length compositional patterns beyond fixed patch boundaries. Across diverse EM benchmarks, our method improves both full-supervision and few-shot adaptation over existing EM pretraining baselines, showing that the learned representations better preserve structural consistency when observations drift in waveform appearance and temporal scale.

</details>


### [84] [Learning a calibrated decision head from logged bandit feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15572)
*Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何在仅有日志化bandit反馈（只知道被选中选项是否正确）的条件下，对LLM的决策头进行校准式适配，提出CAPS方法，并系统检验了REINFORCE类奖励的失效、全标签下bandit方法不优于交叉熵、以及CAPS在仅日志反馈下优于仅用确认正例微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的决策头通常只能观察到"所选选项是否正确"的bandit反馈，而非完整标签。作者希望回答：如何从这种部分反馈中适配（LoRA）决策头，以及常见的bandit奖励信号是否真的可用。

Method: 在Qwen3-0.6B-Base与1.7B-Base上对决策头做LoRA适配。对比方法包括：以采样动作自身概率的Brier/log score作为REINFORCE奖励、路径梯度（pathwise）把概率当损失、REINFORCE奖励r与r−p；全标签条件下四种bandit方法与交叉熵+单一温度缩放对比（四个未见过标签schema上的平均ECE）；仅日志反馈下提出CAPS（对选中动作概率施加proper scoring rule的直接法），与仅用已确认答案（丢弃负例）的微调对比；并考察倾向权重、已知翻转率下的噪声通道校正与早停。

Result: (1) 以Brier/log score作REINFORCE奖励不可用：错误动作获得不低于正确动作的奖励，均值基线使最低概率错误动作获得最大优势，两选项时信号为零；某次运行中有序量表准确率从.636降到.111；路径梯度损失及r、r−p奖励不崩。(2) 全标签下四种bandit方法在四个未见schema的平均ECE上均未超过交叉熵+温度缩放。(3) 仅日志反馈下CAPS优于仅用确认答案微调：Banking77准确率+2.6点、ECE低.053、选择性风险更低（3种子对1基线种子）；在记录器常出错的emotion数据上单种子提升21.7点；N=5000时CAPS在0.6B与1.7B上分别低于全标签训练1.5/0.6点；倾向权重无增益；已知翻转率下置信度平均跟随噪声通道，反向校正可恢复大部分干净校准，基于留出日志结果的早停可防止记忆噪声样本。

Conclusion: 概率型反馈不应直接用作REINFORCE奖励；在仅有日志bandit反馈时，对选中动作概率施加proper scoring rule的直接法（CAPS）是更可靠的校准式适配途径，接近全标签训练效果，并能结合噪声通道建模与早停稳健工作。

Abstract: A decision head reads a state and a list of options and returns a probability per option in one forward pass. Once deployed, it usually observes only whether the option it chose was right: logged bandit feedback. We adapt such a head, LoRA on Qwen3-0.6B-Base and 1.7B-Base, from that feedback and report three results. First, the Brier or log score of the sampled action's own probability is not a usable REINFORCE reward: every wrong action earns at least gold's reward, with a mean baseline the lowest-probability wrong action gets the largest advantage, and at two options the signal is zero; in one run it cut accuracy on ordered-scale questions from .636 to .111. Scoring the probability as a loss by the pathwise gradient avoids the collapse, as do the REINFORCE rewards r and r minus p. Second, with full labels, none of four tested bandit arms beat cross-entropy plus one temperature on mean expected calibration error (ECE) over four unseen label schemas. Third, from logged feedback alone, Chosen-Action Proper Scoring (CAPS), the direct method with a proper score on the chosen action's probability, beats fine-tuning on confirmed answers only, which discards negatives: on Banking77 by 2.6 accuracy points, .053 lower ECE and lower selective risk (three seeds against one baseline seed), and by 21.7 points on one seed on emotion data with an often-wrong logger. At N 5000, CAPS stays 1.5 points below full-label training at 0.6B and .6 points at 1.7B against one full-label seed, and propensity weights add nothing on this log. Under feedback flipped at a known rate, one seed per rate, CAPS's confidence tracks the noisy channel on average, backward correction recovers most of the clean calibration, and early stopping on held-out logged outcomes prevented memorising noisy draws in all three runs. Code, adapters, logs and per-record results are public.

</details>


### [85] [Constructing and Validating Memorisation Audits for EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15578)
*Inigo Stibbe,Joshua Placidi,Padmanabhan Ramnarayan,A. Aldo Faisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIMIC-IV上GPT-2风格EHR模型表现出弱队列级记忆，但在token重构、敏感属性披露与患者级canary攻击下未出现可靠的患者级泄露，提示需临床情境化的推理期审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归EHR基础模型可能在推理时泄露训练数据，但临床可预测性使患者层面的记忆与泛化难以区分，且现有审计缺乏临床上下文下的可靠区分手段。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV上训练GPT-2风格模型，设置早停、最佳验证、最终三类检查点；评估固定窗口token重构、敏感属性披露、随机患者级canary三类攻击，并加入1000名患者的过拟合阳性对照以检验检测灵敏度。

Result: 重构精度随上下文增加而提升，但训练-留出差异很小；含有限临床信息的轨迹唯一提示未产生超额敏感披露；匹配对照中训练患者canary标签恢复AUROC由0.512升至0.549，留出仍为随机水平；过拟合阳性对照达近完美恢复。

Conclusion: 在所评估攻击下存在较弱的队列级记忆，但无可可靠复现的患者级披露；应开展基于临床情境的推理期审计，以在无需canary重训练的前提下检测已有模型的患者特定记忆。

Abstract: Autoregressive electronic health record (EHR) foundation models may disclose training information at inference, yet clinical predictability makes patient-specific memorisation difficult to distinguish from generalisation. We evaluate fixed-window token reconstruction, sensitive-attribute disclosure, and random patient-level canaries in GPT-2-style models trained on MIMIC-IV, across early, best-validation, and final checkpoints. Reconstruction improves with additional context, but training--held-out differences remain small. Trajectory-unique prompts containing limited clinical information produce no excess sensitive disclosure for training patients. In a matched model with random binary patient-level canaries, training-patient label-recovery AUROC increases from 0.512 to 0.549 while held-out recovery remains at chance. An overfit 1,000-patient positive control achieves near-perfect recovery, confirming sensitivity to strong memorisation. These results reveal weak cohort-level memorisation but no reliable patient-level disclosure under the evaluated attacks. They motivate clinically grounded inference-time audits that can detect patient-specific memorisation in existing models without retraining with canaries.

</details>


### [86] [AI4Plasma II: Geometry-Aware Surrogate Modeling for Variable Electrode Gap GEC Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15584)
*Hao Yang,Zhenyu Zou,Wenshuai Jie,Luowei Yin,Yufeng Wei,Daniel Zhengyu Huang,Zhichao Peng,Chun Chen,Kangxin Liu,Rubing Han,Haoran Guan,Yipei Chen,Peiting You,Yuwei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在压力、电压、频率和电极间距构成的参数空间上，比较六种几何感知代理模型预测完整等离子体场与晶圆近处离子密度剖面的数据需求，指出基于预设映射的Geo-FNO与Transolver在2%平均误差下仅需100个训练算例，并引入电荷分离加权损失将5%剖面误差所需样本从1000降至100。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业等离子体工艺开发需广泛探索操作与几何参数，其中电极间距会影响放电结构与处理均匀性；但用高保真仿真重复遍历该参数空间计算代价高昂（每个参考算例平均5.32 CPU h），而几何变化会导致计算域与离散化随样本改变，使常规代理模型难以直接应用。

Method: 构建涵盖压力、电压、频率与电极间距的仿真数据集，比较六类几何感知代理模型：预设几何映射、学习型几何映射、扩展域表示、连续坐标表示以及几何原生离散化（如预设映射Geo-FNO与Transolver等）；以全等离子体场与晶圆近处离子密度剖面的预测误差为目标，统计各模型达到误差阈值所需训练算例数，并在训练损失中引入电荷分离加权以加强近晶圆区域的拟合。

Result: 在四类等离子体场达到2%平均误差时，预设映射Geo-FNO与Transolver仅需100个训练算例，其余模型需250–1000个；晶圆近处离子密度剖面预测难度更高且模型间差异更大，预设映射Geo-FNO所需训练算例最少；加入电荷分离加权后，预设映射Geo-FNO达到5%剖面误差的最少测试训练需求由1000降至100。

Conclusion: 在具备合适几何映射的前提下，预设映射Geo-FNO可作为本文变间距问题的实用起点；而训练数据充足时Transolver取得最高预测精度。

Abstract: Industrial plasma process development requires broad exploration of operating and geometric parameters. Geometric parameters, particularly electrode spacing, affect discharge structure and processing uniformity. Exploring these parameter spaces through repeated high-fidelity simulations can be computationally expensive. Surrogate models can offer rapid predictions, but geometric variation complicates their application because the computational domain and discretization may change across samples.
  In this work, six geometry-aware surrogate models based on prescribed and learned geometry mappings, extended-domain representation, continuous coordinates, and geometry-native discretizations are compared in terms of their data requirements for accurate predictions of full plasma fields and ion density profiles near the wafer. The simulation dataset spans pressure, voltage, frequency, and electrode gap, with each reference case requiring an average simulation cost of 5.32 CPU h.
  To achieve a 2% mean error across the four plasma fields, prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO and Transolver require 100 training cases, compared with 250-1000 for the other models. Predicting ion density profiles near the wafer is more demanding and reveals larger differences among models, with prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO requiring the fewest training cases for the profile accuracy targets considered. To further reduce the data required for accurate predictions near the wafer, we introduce charge-separation weighting into the training loss, which reduces the prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO's minimum tested training requirement for a 5% profile error from 1000 to 100 cases.
  These results support prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO as a practical starting point for the variable-gap problem studied here when a suitable geometry mapping is available, while Transolver achieves the highest prediction accuracy with sufficient training data.

</details>


### [87] [Imputing Missing Near-Surface Air Temperature using Graph Attention Networks with Distance-Aware Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15598)
*Emma C. Weber,Dominique Guillot,Daniyar Abykhanov,Julien Emile-Geay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于图注意力网络的近地面气温场重建方法，以网格为节点构建k近邻几何图，避免极区投影畸变，在历史稀疏观测下重建误差显著低于克里金、GraphEM与LaMa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史气温记录自19世纪中期起覆盖不全，早期与高纬度地区缺测严重；主流深度学习方法将全球视为平面经纬度图像处理，导致极区严重畸变，重建效果受限。

Method: 将每个网格单元视为节点并连接至k个最近邻，注意力权重由节点特征、距离和陆海过渡共同决定，构建球面几何图；用CMIP6 CESM2历史模拟并以1850–2014年时变HadCRUT5观测覆盖掩膜进行训练；在留出实现上与普通克里金、GraphEM和LaMa修复网络对比，并开展消融实验。

Result: 图模型取得最低空间RMSE（0.997 K），较LaMa低10.1%，较克里金与GraphEM低约31%；增益集中于北极与南极，LaMa在观测密集的热带略占优势；随覆盖度提升图模型误差下降更快，1850s至2000s相对LaMa的优势扩大逾三分之一；消融表明性能主要来自几何图构建而非辅助物理协变量。

Conclusion: 球面几何图注意力结构是稀疏历史气温场重建的有效归纳偏置，尤其适合高纬与早期缺测场景；相较平面图像修复与插值/统计方法具有稳定且随覆盖度增加而扩大的精度优势。

Abstract: We introduce a graph attention network for reconstructing near-surface air temperature fields from incomplete observations. The model represents each grid cell as a node connected to its $k$ nearest neighbors, with attention weights that depend on node features, distance, and land-ocean transitions. This graph representation avoids the polar distortions associated with treating the globe as a flat latitude-longitude image, as most deep learning reconstruction methods do. This is particularly relevant for historical temperature records, which extend back to the mid-nineteenth century but remain incomplete, with the largest gaps early in the record and at high latitudes. We train our model on CMIP6 CESM2 historical simulations masked with the time-varying HadCRUT5 observational coverage from 1850 to 2014. We then compare our approach with ordinary kriging, GraphEM, and the LaMa inpainting network on a held-out realization. The graph model achieves the lowest spatial RMSE (0.997 K), 10.1% lower than LaMa and approximately 31% lower than kriging and GraphEM. The largest gains occur in the Arctic and Antarctic, while LaMa remains slightly more accurate in the densely observed tropics. The graph model's error also falls faster as coverage increases, so its advantage over LaMa grows by more than a third from the 1850s to the 2000s. An ablation study shows that the model's skill comes mainly from the geometric graph construction rather than from auxiliary physical covariates.

</details>


### [88] [Are Scaling Laws of Universal MLIPs Universal?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15603)
*Theo Keane,Anthony Onwuli,Richard Tomsett,Robert M. Forrest,Jonathan J. Bean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究M3GNet、MACE、PET三种MLIP在10k–1.16M结构规模下的数据缩放规律，比较均匀随机、三种化学/结构分层、迭代DIRECT和基于嵌入的最远点采样等策略，发现缩放指数、误差下限与采样效率随架构、目标量、采样策略和测试分布变化，采样策略排名可因测试分布而反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放律可预测增大训练集的收益，但MLIP数据集异构且生成昂贵，相同结构数量未必包含相同信息量，因此需要研究"结构数量"之外的采样方式如何影响缩放行为。

Method: 固定模型配置，在MatPES-MP-ALOE合并训练池的子集（10k至640k及全部116万结构）上训练M3GNet、MACE、PET；对比均匀随机采样、三种化学与结构信息驱动的分层策略、迭代DIRECT和基于三个预训练MLIP嵌入的最远点采样；以凝聚能、力、应力及综合损失在母分布留出测试集与分布偏移的MP-r²SCAN测试集上评估。

Result: 随数据量增加呈幂律改进，但拟合缩放指数、表观误差下限和相对样本效率取决于模型架构、目标量、采样策略与测试分布；采样策略排名在不同测试分布间可反转——化学分层提升跨数据集性能却损害分布内性能，嵌入空间多样性策略常不如随机采样；相对损失与测试-训练化学空间差异强相关。

Conclusion: 结构数量是MLIP缩放的必要但非充分变量；额外第一性原理计算的回报不仅取决于结构数量，还取决于其化学与构型内容与模型预期用途的匹配程度。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws enable the prediction of returns from larger training datasets, but machine-learned interatomic potential (MLIP) datasets are heterogeneous and expensive to generate, and equal numbers of structures need not contain equal amounts of useful information. We study data scaling under fixed model configurations for M3GNet, MACE, and PET trained on subsets of a combined MatPES-MP-ALOE training pool. Across training structure subsets from 10,000 to 640,000, together with the full 1.16-million-structure training set, we compare uniform random sampling, three chemistry- and structure-informed stratification policies, iterative DIRECT, and furthest-point sampling using embeddings from three pretrained MLIPs. Models are evaluated using cohesive-energy, force, stress, and aggregate losses on both a held-out test set from the parent distribution and the distribution shifted MP-r$^2$SCAN test set. We observe power-law scaling improvements with increasing data, but the fitted scaling exponent, apparent error floor, and relative sample efficiency depend on the model architecture, target quantity, data sampling policy, and test set distribution. Sampling-policy rankings can reverse across test distributions: chemical stratification can improve cross-dataset performance while degrading in-distribution performance, whereas the embedding-space diversity policies frequently under-perform random sampling. Relative loss is strongly associated with test-to-train chemical-space divergence. These results show that structure count is a necessary but insufficient scaling variable for MLIPs. For training MLIPs, the return on additional first-principles calculations depends not only on how many additional structures are included, but also on how their chemical and configurational content aligns with the model's intended use.

</details>


### [89] [FADO: Learning Causal Explanations with Prior-Fitted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15607)
*Seth Flaxman,Jess Carr,Annabel Jakob,Marek Masiak,Dino Sejdinovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FADO：用先验拟合网络在合成SCM数据上摊销学习do-算子，实现仅凭观测表格数据预测特征干预效应Δ_i，在代理变量、混杂、对撞与安慰剂等场景下优于预测归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML可解释性文献（SHAP、置换重要性等）多聚焦"预测归因"，本质捕捉相关性而非因果性；而实际问题常需回答"操纵特征X_i是否会改变Y"。直接从观测数据估计因果干预效应需要强因果假设，存在困难。

Method: 定义特征干预效应Δ_i=E[Y|do(X_i=+1)]−E[Y|do(X_i=-1)]。提出FADO（Feature-wise Amortized Do-Operators）：基于在由结构因果模型（SCM）生成的合成表格数据集上训练的先验拟合网络（prior-fitted networks）进行摊销推断。训练时数据生成过程已知，可计算真值Δ_i，从而学习从纯观测表格数据映射到干预效应的模型，测试时直接预测。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法易于训练，在代理变量、混杂变量、对撞变量及安慰剂等标准预测归因失效的情境下给出更好的解释；在真实、合成与半合成数据集（含/不含隐藏混杂）上与现有预测归因方法和先进因果推断方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: FADO将因果可解释性转化为摊销推断问题，借助已知SCM的合成训练规避强因果假设，为表格数据提供可行的因果归因替代方案；在预测归因失败的多类场景中表现更优。

Abstract: In regression and classification tasks with tabular datasets, most of the literature on explanation in ML (e.g. SHAP, permutation importance) has focused on predictive attribution: does feature $X_i$ help predict outcome $Y$? At its heart, predictive attribution captures correlation, not causation. We instead ask a causal question: would manipulating feature $X_i$ change outcome $Y$? We define a natural quantity to answer this question, the feature intervention effect: $Δ_i = E[Y\mid do(X_i=+1)]-E[Y\mid do(X_i=-1)]$. In general, estimating this quantity from observational data alone would entail strong causal assumptions. We propose FADO (Feature-wise Amortized Do-Operators), an amortized inference method using prior-fitted networks trained on synthetic tabular datasets generated by structural causal models. During training, the synthetic data-generating process is known, so we can compute ground-truth intervention effects $Δ_i$, and train a model to predict them at test time from a purely observational tabular dataset. As we demonstrate through extensive experiments, our new causal explainability method is easy to train and provides better explanations under a range of settings for which standard predictive attribution fails, including proxy variables, confounders, colliders, and placebos. We evaluate it on real, synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets, with and without hidden confounders, and compare it to existing predictive attribution methods and state-of-the-art causal inference methods.

</details>


### [90] [GLCR: Global-Local Collaborative Routing for Multi-Task Electromagnetic Signal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15609)
*Fang Lu,Yao Lu,Zhuangzhi Chen,Sicheng Zhang,Shanqing Yu,Yun Lin,Qi Xuan,Guan Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HeteroSignal-7多任务路由基准（14万样本、7数据集、6类任务、7个专家）与两阶段路由方法GLCR，实现96.16%路由准确率和76.30%系统得分，并以1.43ms/样本的最低调度延迟将期望FLOPs降低5.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一任务的电磁信号专用模型缺乏跨任务迁移能力，而电磁基础模型虽覆盖任务广但预训练与推理成本高昂；需在精度与效率之间取得平衡，因此采用模型路由将未知信号动态分派给异构专家池中的匹配专家。

Method: 构建HeteroSignal-7基准：7个数据集共140,000样本，覆盖6类任务，配套7个分类与波形恢复专家及统一评测协议。提出GLCR两阶段路由：第一阶段用轻量一维CNN加additive-margin头预测7类路由类别；第二阶段用梯度提升树专家基于4个物理统计量，对前二候选构成校验集已自动识别的混淆对的样本进行再判别。全SNR范围联合训练，推理时无需SNR标签与噪声估计。

Result: GLCR优于全部19个基线，路由准确率96.16%、系统得分76.30%，分别比次优基线高1.16%与1.14%；路由延迟1.43 ms/样本为对比方法中最低；相比执行全部专家，期望系统FLOPs降低5.9倍。

Conclusion: 该工作为电磁信号处理中的异构专家路由提供了公开评测平台与高效、可复现的基线，验证了"全局分派+局部细判"两阶段路由在精度、延迟与算力开销上的综合优势。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for electromagnetic signal processing are typically developed as single-task experts with fixed inputs and objectives, limiting cross-task transferability. Electromagnetic foundation models offer broader task coverage but incur substantial pretraining and inference costs. To balance accuracy and efficiency, we adopt model routing to dynamically dispatch unknown signals to matched experts within a heterogeneous pool. We construct HeteroSignal-7, a multi-task routing benchmark with 140,000 samples from seven datasets covering six task types, paired with seven classification and waveform-recovery experts and a unified evaluation protocol. We then propose GLCR, a two-stage routing method combining global dispatch with local discrimination of similar tasks. First, a lightweight one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN) with an additive-margin head predicts one of seven routing classes. Second, a gradient-boosted-tree specialist uses four physical statistics to re-evaluate samples whose top two candidates match a confusion pair automatically identified from validation errors. The pipeline is trained across the full signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range and requires neither SNR labels nor noise estimation at inference time. GLCR outperforms all 19 baselines, achieving 96.16% routing accuracy and a 76.30% system score, exceeding the respective second-best baselines by 1.16% and 1.14%. It achieves the lowest routing latency among the compared methods at 1.43 ms per sample and reduces expected overall system FLOPs by 5.9 times compared with executing all experts. This work provides a public evaluation platform and an efficient, reproducible baseline for heterogeneous expert routing in electromagnetic signal processing.

</details>


### [91] [Data-Free Metrics Don't Work: The Missing Invariance Under Functionally Preserving Reparameterisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15621)
*Gabryel Mason-Williams,Israel Mason-Williams,Fredrik Dahlqvist*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明随机矩阵理论（RMT）导出的无数据层级指标（如α-ReQ/α-Smoothness）与模型性能和泛化无关：通过对"鲁棒"层进行保持功能的重新参数化，可使这些指标取任意值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMT无数据指标被宣称能逐层预测预训练模型质量、识别模型病理，从而被用于预训练优化与训练后压缩。作者希望检验这一宣称是否成立，即这些指标是否真正反映层"强/弱"与泛化能力。

Method: 利用神经网络"临界性"现象：部分层（鲁棒层）可被重新初始化或随机化而几乎不改变模型输出。对鲁棒层做此类操作即得到保持功能的重新参数化，在此变换下观测RMT指标的变化范围；在多种架构与规模上实验，并考察指标与泛化的关联以及指标"良好区间"是否受初始化影响。

Result: 保持功能的重新参数化可使RMT指标取任意值，说明其与性能、训练过程无因果关系；RMT指标间的关系与泛化只是虚假相关；许多预训练模型落在指标"良好"区间，部分取决于模型初始化。

Conclusion: RMT类无数据层级指标不具备所宣称的预测与诊断能力，基于它们的预训练优化与压缩策略缺乏依据，使用前须谨慎。

Abstract: Data-free methods for analysing and understanding the layers of neural networks offer many metrics for quantifying notions of 'strong' versus 'weak' layers, with the promise of increased interpretability. In particular, random matrix theory (RMT) offers data-free metrics that claim predictive power over the quality of pre-trained models at a layerwise precision and the ability to identify model pathologies, indicating a unique relationship with generalisation. As a result, metrics from RMT are championed for pre-training optimisation strategies and post-training compression. We establish that RMT-based metrics are unrelated to performance or training by showing that functionally preserving reparametrisations of a pre-trained model can have arbitrary metrics. We show this across a range of architectures and scales. To create functionally preserving reparametrisations, we exploit the well-established phenomenon of criticality: some layers can be re-initialised or re-randomised without affecting the functional behaviour of the model -- they are called robust -- while others cannot -- they are called critical. Re-initialising or re-randomising robust layers provides functionally preserving reparametrisations for which RMT-based metrics are arbitrary. Moreover, we show that relationships between metrics offered by RMT are spuriously related to generalisation. We conclude by showing that if many pre-trained models have data-free metrics in a 'good' range, it is, in part, dependent on model initialisation. Project page available at: https://gmw99.github.io/data-free-metrics-dont-work

</details>


### [92] [A Mechanistic Interpretability Study of an Astronomy Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15653)
*Rachel C. Zhang,Alberto Bietti,Wes Gurnee,Alessandro Favero,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个针对天文基础模型AION-1的因果机制可解释性研究，揭示其多模态红移预测优势的机制：优势集中于编码器第一个block，且"可线性解码性"与"因果可定位性"解耦——空间延展信息分布冗余，尘埃衰减信息则由单个注意力头承担且无法用已知天体物理操作解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在科学领域广泛应用，但其内部计算是否对应已知科学操作尚不清楚；弄清机制对评估可靠性、识别失效模式以及从模型中学习新科学至关重要。

Method: 对天文基础模型AION-1开展因果机制可解释性研究，聚焦红移预测中"成像+测光"优于"仅测光"的多模态优势；结合线性探测、注意力头消融、单头保留实验，按星系属性（空间延展性、尘埃衰减）分层进行因果定位分析。

Result: 多模态优势在空间延展且尘埃衰减强的星系上最大，且主要定位于十二个block中第一个block。该block内，属性的线性可解码性不能预测其可定位性。空间延展信息被因果使用，单个头在探测中占主导，但消融该头对红移预测影响可忽略——信息跨头分布，并在下一层可重新解码。尘埃衰减呈相反模式：全编码器中仅弱可解码，但单个头对大部分多模态优势是必要的（尤其对强衰减星系），且仅保留该头可保持几乎全部优势。所测试的天体物理动机操作均无法解释该头的计算内容。

Conclusion: 线性可解码性与因果必要性不一致，注意头间存在显著冗余；关键头的计算无法归约为已知天体物理操作，究竟是可解释性方法的局限还是非已知天体物理计算仍待解决，这是将可解释性作为科学发现工具的前提。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used across the sciences, but whether their internal computations correspond to known scientific operations or proceed through different mechanisms remains unclear. Understanding these mechanisms is essential for assessing reliability, identifying failure modes, and learning new science from the models. We present, to our knowledge, the first causal mechanistic interpretability study of an astronomy foundation model, AION-1, focusing on its multimodal advantage in redshift prediction, where combining imaging with photometry outperforms photometry alone. We find that this advantage is largest for spatially extended and dust-attenuated galaxies, and that most of it is localized to the first of the encoder's twelve blocks. Within that block, how well a property is linearly decodable does not predict whether it can be localized. Spatial extent is causally used, and one attention head dominates linear probing for it, yet ablating that head has negligible effect on redshift prediction: the information is distributed across heads and is decodable again one layer after we erase it. Dust attenuation shows the opposite pattern: it is only weakly decodable anywhere in the encoder, yet a single head is necessary for most of the multimodal advantage, particularly for the most dust-attenuated galaxies, and keeping that head as the only active head in its block preserves nearly all of the advantage. None of the astrophysically motivated operations we tested explains what this head computes. Whether this reflects a limitation of current interpretability methods or a computation that does not correspond to any known astrophysical operation remains open, and resolving it is a prerequisite for using interpretability as a tool for scientific discovery.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Reweighting: Certified Improvements from New Transitions in Discrete MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15668)
*Yishan Xi,Tinglan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"转移支撑设计"框架，同时考虑允许的转移与平稳流，用于刻画、认证并利用可逆MCMC中固定图结构带来的效率局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定图上仅优化转移概率（重加权）会受结构限制，可能造成显著的估计效率损失，因此需要判断并认证"增边"是否值得。

Method: 针对固定目标分布、可观测量与节点流预算，推导支撑增广能严格降低渐近方差最优值的充要对偶条件，并给出可计算的原-对偶证书；同时研究能量启发的边选择与受约束平稳流优化。

Result: 构造出一族图：相对原支撑最优重加权，仅加一条边即可将积分自相关时间降低Ω(n)倍；实验在分子与Ising状态图上表明，同一增广Ising支撑下全权重重优化在24/24条件下认证严格改进，而基于度数的Metropolis–Hastings加权无一认证。

Conclusion: 该框架为在显式有限状态空间中刻画、认证与利用转移支撑改进提供了有原则的方法，并区分了"新转移的潜在价值"与"在其上构造的核的实际性能"。

Abstract: Optimizing transition probabilities on a fixed grap may leave substantial limitations on the efficiency of reversible Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). We develop a framework for transition-support design that considers both allowed transitions and their stationary flows. For a fixed target distribution, observable, and node-flow budget, we derive a necessary and sufficient dual condition for support augmentation to strictly reduce the optimal value of the asymptotic variance, together with computable primal--dual certificates that establish improvements over every admissible original-support reweighting. We also construct a family in which adding a single edge reduces integrated autocorrelation time by an $Ω(n)$ factor relative to optimal original-support weighting, demonstrating that structural limitations can impose arbitrarily large relative losses in estimation efficiency. To realize these gains, we investigate energy-informed edge selection and constrained stationary-flow optimization. Experiments on molecular and Ising state graphs distinguish the potential value of new transitions from the performance of kernels constructed on them: on identical augmented Ising supports, full weight optimization certifies strict improvements in 24/24 conditions, whereas degree-based Metropolis--Hastings weighting certifies none. Our results provide a principled approach to characterizing, certifying, and exploiting transition-support improvements in explicitly represented finite state spaces.

</details>


### [94] [The Noise Floor of a Quantization Comparison: Engine, restart, batch and kernel drift, measured against 8-bit and 4-bit effects in two decision models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15671)
*Aleks Blagojevich,Trevor Sunderland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文量化评测中引入"非量化噪声基线"，发现重启、引擎切换、batch size、kernel 选择本身就会翻转 0–0.92% 决策；真正的量化效应（Q8_0 至 NVFP4，0.88%–5.79%）须以此为参照解读，且 4-bit 区间排除全部非量化来源；准确率会掩盖这些变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化模型通常直接与 BF16 原模型对比，但该对比同时改变了服务引擎、服务器实例、请求批处理方式乃至库自动选择的 kernel，因此测得的差异并非纯量化效应。作者希望把这些非量化来源逐一量化，从而给出"超额"意义上的真实量化影响。

Method: 在两个 Cloudflare 决策模型 Clef-flash（9B）与 Clef（27B）上，于单张 NVIDIA GB10 上对同一组 2,400 条公开决策记录进行测量：(1) llama.cpp 与 vLLM 的重启可复现性（含多实例对比）；(2) BF16 下的引擎切换；(3) batch size 变化；(4) transformers 自动选择的 Gated DeltaNet kernel；(5) 单引擎内的量化（Q8_0、FP8、Q4_K_M、NVFP4）。所有量化效应均以对这些非量化来源的超额量表述，并采用预注册的五项预测进行检验。

Result: 非量化来源翻转 0–0.92% 的决策：llama.cpp 重启逐条位一致；vLLM 重启在 BF16/FP8/NVFP4 下分别翻转 0、5、20 条（Clef-flash）与 4、0、22 条（Clef）；第三实例（探索性）使 NVFP4 落在 4–22 条的实例依赖区间。BF16 下换引擎翻转 6–16 条，改 batch size 8–15 条，Gated DeltaNet kernel 被动选择翻转 10–14 条。量化效应更大：Q8_0 为 0.88%/1.04%，FP8 为 2.12%/1.62%，Q4_K_M 为 5.79%/3.50%，NVFP4 为 5.79%/5.67%（Clef-flash/Clef）；两模型上所有 4-bit 区间均排除全部非量化来源。五项预注册预测中两项失败（在 27B 上复现同样失败）：FP8 并不落在重启噪声内；换引擎影响的决策数少于 8-bit 量化。准确率掩盖了这些差异：31,275 题上 Clef 的 Q4_K_M 净变化仅 6 题（−0.02 点，[−0.20, +0.16]），但 436 条决策变差（探索性）；NVFP4 使 Clef-flash 降 0.95 点、Clef 降 0.65 点。代码、量化配方、逐条输出与预注册均已公开。

Conclusion: 量化评测必须建立非量化噪声基线，否则重启、引擎、批处理与 kernel 选择等混杂因素会被误计入"量化效应"；4-bit 量化的影响显著超出该基线，而 8-bit/FP8 的量级与基础设施噪声相近，需谨慎归因。聚合准确率对逐条决策翻转极不敏感，应报告逐条一致率并预注册假设。

Abstract: A quantized model is usually judged by comparing it with its BF16 original, but that comparison changes more than the weights. It often changes the serving engine, it always runs on a different server instance, it may change how requests are batched, and on some stacks the library selects a different kernel depending only on which packages import. We measure each of these sources on the same 2,400 public decisions for two of Cloudflare's decision models, Clef-flash (9B) and Clef (27B), on an NVIDIA GB10, and state every quantization effect as the excess over them. Sources that are not quantization move between 0 and 0.92% of decisions. llama.cpp restarts are bit-identical on every record for both models. vLLM restarts flip 0, 5 and 20 decisions at BF16, FP8 and NVFP4 on Clef-flash and 4, 0 and 22 on Clef; a third instance of Clef's quantized builds (exploratory) leaves FP8 at 0 and puts NVFP4 at 4 to 22, depending on which two instances are compared. An engine change at BF16 flips 6 to 16, a batch-size change 8 to 15, and the Gated DeltaNet kernel transformers selects unasked 10 to 14. Quantization inside one engine moves more: for Clef-flash and Clef, 0.88% and 1.04% at Q8_0, 2.12% and 1.62% at FP8, 5.79% and 3.50% at Q4_K_M, and 5.79% and 5.67% at NVFP4. On both models, every 4-bit interval excludes every non-quantization source. Two of our five pre-registered predictions failed, and the same two fail when the tests are rerun on the 27B: FP8 is not inside restart noise, and changing engine moves fewer decisions than 8-bit quantization does. Accuracy hides most of this. Over the full 31,275 public questions, Clef's Q4_K_M build moves accuracy by a net six questions (-0.02 points [-0.20, +0.16]) while 436 of its decisions get worse (exploratory), and NVFP4 costs Clef-flash 0.95 points and Clef 0.65. We release the code, quantization recipes, per-record outputs and the pre-registration.

</details>


### [95] [SpaceMCP: Vision Language Model Spatial Reasoning via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15680)
*Ameen Izhac,Nobuko Yoshida,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpaceMCP 通过 MCP 风格的结构化函数调用，让 VLM 查询由前馈三维重建与开放词汇分割实时构建的场景图，无需训练即在三个空间推理基准上达到 SOTA，较基座模型提升 15–22 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）的空间推理能力仍然有限且进展缓慢，而前馈三维重建已能提供精确、实时的几何理解。作者希望把这一快速进步转化为 VLM 可调用的空间推理能力，而非依赖模型原生空间能力的缓慢提升。

Method: 提出 SpaceMCP 框架：用前馈三维重建恢复度量几何，结合开放词汇分割定位场景中相关物体，实时构建场景图；再以 MCP 风格的结构化函数调用增强 VLM，使其能查询距离、物体位置、相机属性等空间原语，并由 VLM 的推理能力组合这些原语求解复杂空间任务。全流程无需训练。

Result: 在 VSI-Bench、MMSI-Bench 与 VSTI-Bench 上取得 SOTA，优于近期经空间后训练的 VLM，并相较基座 VLM 在每个基准上提升 15–22 点。

Conclusion: 鉴于 VLM 原生空间推理进展相对缓慢、前馈三维视觉发展迅速，该"工具调用 + 实时场景图"范式为稳健空间推理提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Despite impressive advances in large language models (LLM), vision-language models (VLM) still exhibit limited spatial reasoning capabilities. Recent progress in feed-forward 3D reconstruction has enabled precise, real-time geometric understanding from visual inputs. In this work, we propose SpaceMCP, a framework that leverages these advances to introduce an MCP-style paradigm for spatial reasoning. Our approach augments a VLM with structured function calls, allowing it to query a scene graph for spatial information such as distances, object positioning, and camera properties. The scene graph is constructed in real time using feed-forward 3D reconstruction to recover metric geometry, combined with open-vocabulary segmentation to identify and localize relevant objects within the scene. Paired with the VLM's strong reasoning capabilities, the VLM is able to combine this primitive information to solve more complex spatial tasks. Without any training, SpaceMCP achieves state-of-the-art results on VSI-Bench, MMSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench, outperforming recent spatially post-trained VLMs and improving its base VLM by 15-22 points per benchmark. We argue that, given the contrast between the relatively slow progress in native VLM spatial reasoning and the rapid advances in feed-forward 3D computer vision, this paradigm offers a promising path forward for enabling robust spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [96] [Characterizing Predictive Multiplicity in Ranking and Its Fairness Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15705)
*Ainhize Barrainkua,Mikel Malagón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将预测多重性（predictive multiplicity）从分类扩展到基于分数的排序问题，给出排序多重性的形式化定义与理论保证，并揭示其与算法公平性的双重关系：多重性既是潜在不公平来源，也可作为搜索更少歧视性排序的原则性途径，并通过合成与真实数据实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测多重性研究集中于分类任务，而排序框架在高风险社会决策（如福利资源与社会救助分配）中至关重要，其多重性影响尚未被探索；同时多重性与公平性在排序中的关系不明。

Method: 引入刻画排序设定下多重性的形式化定义，并给出基础理论保证；分析预测多重性与算法公平性的对偶关系，论证模型多重性可成为不公平来源，同时展示利用该决策空间可发现歧视更少的排序；在合成与真实数据集上进行实证评估。

Result: 理论分析给出排序多重性的定义性保证，并阐明其与公平性的双向联系；实证结果验证了分析结论，说明多重性在排序中的现实相关性及利用其获得更公平结果的潜力。

Conclusion: 预测多重性在排序问题中具有重要现实意义，既可威胁公平性，也可作为实现更公平排序的有效杠杆。

Abstract: Prediction problems often admit competing models that achieve comparable performance yet produce conflicting outputs, a phenomenon known as predictive multiplicity. While this challenge has been extensively studied in classification tasks, its implications remain largely unexplored in ranking frameworks, which are critical for high-stakes societal decisions such as the allocation of welfare resources and social assistance. This paper investigates the phenomenon of predictive multiplicity in score-based ranking problems. We introduce formal definitions to capture multiplicity in ranking settings, supported by foundational theoretical guarantees. Furthermore, we examine the dual relationship between predictive multiplicity and algorithmic fairness in ranking problems: we analyze how model multiplicity can act as a potential source of unfairness, while demonstrating how exploring this decision space provides a principled avenue for discovering less discriminatory rankings. Finally, we complement our theoretical contributions with insightful empirical evaluations on both synthetic and real-world datasets, validating our analytical findings and illustrating their practical implications. Taken together, this work highlights the real-world relevance of predictive multiplicity in ranking and sheds light on the potential of leveraging this effect to achieve fairer outcomes.

</details>


### [97] [Efficient Knowledge Transfer in Federated Bayesian Optimization through Neural Network Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15707)
*Alexander Gräfe,Max van Gemmeren,Paul Brunzema,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GLOBAFED 框架，用共享主干+代理特定头（带变分贝叶斯末层）的概率神经网络代理模型，实现异构相关目标间的联邦贝叶斯优化知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦贝叶斯优化（FedBO）依赖近同构目标函数或共享 GP 超参数等强假设，在真实异构场景中常被违背，限制了知识迁移的有效性。

Method: 采用概率神经网络代理：共享特征提取主干 + 代理特定输出头，头内含变分贝叶斯末层以提供不确定性估计；优化过程中通过联邦学习在主干上协同学习公共隐特征表示，各代理头独立适配自身目标。

Result: 相较 SOTA FedBO 基线，显著加速收敛并获得更优最终性能；代理可利用已学到的全局特征进行热启动优化。

Conclusion: 通过解耦共享表示与个体目标建模，GLOBAFED 在保证隐私的前提下放宽了 FedBO 的同构假设，实现异构相关目标间的高效迁移。

Abstract: Federated Bayesian Optimization (FedBO) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Bayesian Optimization (BO) in distributed, privacy-sensitive settings, enabling agents to accelerate the optimization of their local objectives by sharing knowledge about their similarities without exposing raw data. However, existing methods rely on restrictive assumptions, such as near-identical objective functions or shared Gaussian process hyperparameters that are often violated in heterogeneous real-world applications. We present GLOBAFED, a novel framework that enables efficient knowledge transfer across different but related objectives without compromising privacy. At its core is a probabilistic neural network surrogate with a shared feature-extracting backbone and agent-specific heads equipped with varia- tional Bayesian last layers to provide uncertainty estimates. During optimization, agents use federated learning on the shared backbone to collaboratively build a common latent feature representation, while agent-specific heads adapt to their individual objectives. Our empirical results demonstrate that GLOBAFED significantly accelerates convergence and reaches better final performance compared to state-of-the-art FedBO baselines, and enables agents to warm-start their optimization from previously learned global features.

</details>


### [98] [Curiosity-0826-4B: Generative Medical Event Sequence Modeling Across Pediatric and Adult Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15710)
*Mani Nagarathnam,Luke Benham,Paul Blazek,John Cashy,Kevin Chung,John Muenning,Hank Voeller,Shane Waxler,Davis White,Alison Callahan,Kevin Dysart,Andrew Loza,Michal A. Mankowski,Mattheus Ramsis,Nigam H. Shah,Karen K. Wong,Rahul Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 介绍 Curiosity-0826-4B 医疗事件生成式基础模型，基于 Epic Cosmos 训练，参数量 4.3B、上下文 16K，可零样本模拟患者时间线完成临床预测，并在实验室指标、儿科疾病事件及死亡预测等任务上取得改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展生成式医疗事件基础模型的覆盖人群与临床事件表征，使零样本预测能力延伸至儿科人群和更多结局，从而无需为每个任务单独训练模型。

Method: 在 Epic Cosmos 上训练更新版 Curiosity-0826-4B：（1）扩大患者人群并丰富临床事件表示；（2）依据缩放定律分析确定计算最优的初始预训练，并进行上下文扩展的持续预训练；（3）模型规模升至 4.3B 参数、上下文窗口 8K→16K，训练 token 减少 56%，训练 FLOPs 为前代的 2.2 倍；（4）将通用零样本预测策略扩展至儿科患者及新增结局。

Result: 在两者共同支持的评估任务上总体持平或优于 Curiosity-0226-1B；实验室值预测 MAE 降低 18–46%。2–17 岁儿科队列中新发哮喘 AUROC 0.742–0.785、哮喘急性加重 0.829–0.852，与成人参考队列相当；镰状细胞危象 AUROC 0.820–0.847，低于成人（0.915）。显式表示有记录的死亡使成人临终关怀、住院和门诊场景的死亡预测成为可能（AUROC 0.821–0.942）。

Conclusion: Curiosity-0826-4B 通过扩展至儿科人群和新增结局，拓宽了无需任务专用模型即可开发临床与运营应用的途径；后续需验证本地校准、不同患者亚组与事件频率下的表现、时间线完成率、新场景泛化性，并开展临床与运营效用的前瞻性评估。

Abstract: We introduce Curiosity-0826-4B, an updated version of the generative medical event foundation model trained on Epic Cosmos that simulates patient timelines to produce zero-shot clinical predictions. Curiosity-0826-4B incorporates a broader patient population and richer clinical event representations, while generally matching or exceeding Curiosity-0226-1B, the largest model in the previous Curiosity-0226 series, on evaluated tasks supported by both models. Laboratory value prediction improved, with MAE 18--46% lower than that of Curiosity-0226-1B. We evaluated prediction of disease events across pediatric cohorts aged 2--17 years. AUROCs for new asthma diagnosis (0.742--0.785) and asthma exacerbation (0.829--0.852) were comparable to those in the adult reference cohort, while AUROCs for sickle cell crisis (0.820--0.847) were lower than in adults (0.915). Explicit representation of documented death enabled new mortality prediction tasks among adults across hospice, inpatient, and ambulatory settings (AUROC 0.821--0.942). Scaling-law analysis guided compute-optimal initial pretraining followed by continued pretraining with context extension. The resulting Curiosity-0826-4B model has more parameters (4.3B versus 1B) and a longer context window (16K versus 8K). It processes 56% fewer training tokens and requires 2.2 times the training floating point operations (FLOPs) compared with its predecessor. By extending a common zero-shot prediction strategy to pediatric patients and additional outcomes, Curiosity-0826-4B broadens opportunities to develop clinical and operational applications without training separate task-specific models. Further validation should assess local calibration, performance across patient subgroups and event frequencies, timeline completion rates, and generalization to new settings, alongside prospective evaluation of clinical and operational utility.

</details>


### [99] [Orbit: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on IT Operations Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15718)
*Sathwik Narkedimilli,Rakshit Verma,Rahul Soni,Ankit Kushwaha,Balaji Ganesan,Kaustabha Ray,Sunil Saumya,Pratibha Moogi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ITOps基准ORBIT，含选择题、开放题与排障题三种题型，每题配参考答案与来源；审计剔除约十分之一的模板缺陷项，并用真实从业者问答重建排障题。独立评审下，五个LLM选择题表现好、开放题与排障题明显更差；模型自评会误接受缺陷项答案，故统一采用独立评审。词面重叠即可解出过半选择题。ORBIT定位为诊断与监督助手选型的工具，而非部署测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ITOps基准只呈现LLM的分数高低，很少解释模型为何失败；同时生成式题目存在质量缺陷、模型自评存在偏袒，导致评测结论不可靠。

Method: 构建ORBIT基准：三种题型（多选题、开放题、排障题），每题附参考答案与可追溯来源；通过审计识别并剔除约10%存在模板缺陷的生成项；排障题改用真实从业者讨论串及被采纳答案重建；由单一独立评审对五个LLM统一评分，并在更宽松的根因评分标准下评测；另做词面重叠基线分析。

Result: 独立评审下，五个模型多数选择题答对，开放题正确率约三分之一到略高于一半，排障题仅有少数正确（即便采用更宽松的根因评分）。模型自评时会接受大量针对缺陷题目的答案；仅靠与题干词的词面重叠就能定位正确选项的比例超过半数选择题。

Conclusion: ORBIT作为诊断性工具，用于模型选型与监督，而非部署准入测试；同时揭示生成题目质量、自评偏差与词面泄漏等因素对ITOps评测有效性的影响。

Abstract: LLMs now support IT operations (ITOps), but benchmarks rarely show why a model fails. We present ORBIT, a benchmark of ITOps questions in three formats, namely multiple-choice, open-ended, and troubleshooting, with each question accompanied by a reference answer and a recorded source. An audit found template defects in about a tenth of the generated items, so we drop them and rebuild the troubleshooting format from real practitioner threads with accepted answers. Under one independent judge, five LLMs answer most multiple-choice items correctly, between a third and just over half of the open-ended items, and a minority of the troubleshooting items, even under a more lenient root-cause rubric. When models judged their own answers, they accepted many answers to defective items, so we use one independent judge for every model. Word overlap with the question finds the correct option for more than half of the multiple-choice items. ORBIT is a diagnostic aid for selecting and supervising assistants rather than a deployment test.

</details>


### [100] [Latent Imagination for Parameterized Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15744)
*Atahan Cilan,İhsan Mert Muhacıroğlu,Ümit Can Bekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyLI是一种基于世界模型的PAMDP智能体，完全在潜空间想象中学习混合策略并以单次前向传播决策，在7个基准上兼具样本效率与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动作MDP（PAMDP）要求智能体同时选择离散动作及其依赖该选择的连续参数。无模型方法决策快但交互次数多，模型方法样本效率高但每步都需规划、决策慢，二者难以兼得。

Method: 提出HyLI（Hybrid Latent Imagination）：在世界模型的潜在想象中学习混合策略，执行时仅需一次前向传播。针对PAMDP结构带来的学习难题，引入两项机制——反事实模式评估（counterfactual mode evaluation），为每个离散动作在每个想象状态提供学习信号；成功窗口回放（success-window replay），在训练中保留稀有成功样本。

Result: 在7个标准PAMDP基准上，HyLI在所有任务上均达到或超过对比中最强无模型基线的公开分数（误差在一个公开标准差以内）；以少2.4倍的环境步数达到该分数的90%（计入其无奖励预训练则为5.0倍）；在DLPA公开规划设置下决策速度快67倍。

Conclusion: HyLI是对比中唯一同时比最强无模型基线更样本高效、且快到足以用于实时控制的方法。

Abstract: Parameterized-action MDPs (PAMDPs) ask an agent to choose a discrete action together with continuous parameters whose meaning depends on that choice. Model-free PAMDP methods act fast but need many interactions; model-based methods are sample-efficient but plan at every decision, which makes them slow to act. We introduce HyLI (Hybrid Latent Imagination), a model-based agent that learns a hybrid policy entirely in the latent imagination of a world model and acts with a single forward pass. To address learning problems that the PAMDP structure creates, HyLI adds counterfactual mode evaluation, which gives every discrete action a learning signal at every imagined state, and success-window replay, which keeps rare successes in training. On seven standard PAMDP benchmarks, HyLI is within one published standard deviation of, or above, the published score of the strongest model-free baseline in our comparison on all 7 tasks, reaches 90% of that score with 2.4x fewer environment steps than that baseline (5.0x when its reward-free pre-training is counted), and decides 67x faster than the model-based planner DLPA at its published planner settings. It is the only method in our comparison that is both more sample-efficient than that baseline and fast enough for real-time control.

</details>


### [101] [Sensitivity-Aware Surrogate Modeling with Random Feature Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15750)
*John E. Darges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出spike-and-slab随机特征展开(SAS-RFE)，用总Sobol'敏感性指数确定各输入在随机Fourier特征中的纳入概率，迭代学习特征采样分布，无需显式筛选变量即可提升代理建模精度与敏感性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征展开(RFE)是灵活的代理建模方法，但其精度依赖于特征采样所采用的分布；如何从数据中自适应地学习该分布而非人为设定，是待解决的问题。

Method: 采用spike-and-slab分布对每个输入在随机采样Fourier特征中的纳入与否赋予概率；纳入概率由总Sobol'敏感性指数估计值决定，而非显式选择重要输入或交互项。通过信息论准则证明总Sobol'指数所诱导的分布可近似模型的输入交互结构；设计迭代算法，利用逐次RFE代理的敏感性估计更新采样概率，并推导随机Fourier特征展开Sobol'指数的解析公式，使更新无需额外目标函数评估。

Result: 在六个基准问题(输入维度最高达100维)上的数值实验表明，该方法在代理逼近精度与敏感性估计两方面均有改进。

Conclusion: 以总Sobol'指数驱动的spike-and-slab采样分布可有效捕捉输入交互结构，迭代更新策略在无额外函数评估代价下改善了高维RFE代理的性能。

Abstract: Random feature expansions (RFEs) provide a flexible approach to surrogate modeling, but their accuracy depends on the distribution used to sample features. We propose spike-and-slab random feature expansions (SAS-RFE) to learn the feature sampling distribution from sensitivity analysis. The method employs a spike-and-slab distribution that assigns probabilities to the inclusion of individual inputs in randomly sampled Fourier features.
