These inclusion probabilities are determined by estimates of total Sobol' sensitivity indices, rather than by explicitly selecting important inputs or interaction terms. An information theoretic criterion shows that the total Sobol' indices yield a distribution that approximates the input interaction structure of the model. An iterative algorithm updates the sampling probabilities using sensitivity estimates from successive RFE surrogates. Analytical formulas for the Sobol' indices of random Fourier feature expansions enable these updates without additional evaluations of the target function. Numerical experiments on six benchmark problems, with up to 100 input dimensions, demonstrate improvements in surrogate approximation and sensitivity estimation.

</details>


### [102] [SAME: Semantic Watermark Embedding for Open-Source Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15771)
*Xinyao Song,Jialiang Shen,Hui Kang,Huajie Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Jiyang Zheng,Huaxi Huang,Zhenliang Lu,Chris Russell,Runqi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义水印嵌入方法SAME，通过私有词表与原生语义对齐构建可组合、可迁移且抗微调的水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型易被未授权复制与修改，现有基于触发-响应模式的水印需针对任务数据定制，跨下游任务迁移性差，且在良性微调后不持久。

Method: 引入固定私有词表，为每个原生token指定私有embedding对应项，并在模型表示空间中对齐两套词表的语义表达能力；私有token可灵活组合为语义触发器，产生有意义响应，从而突破预定义触发-响应对的限制；水印与模型演进中的语义能力耦合，随下游微调保持关联。

Result: 在多种规模和架构的LPM上实验表明，SAME提供可靠且鲁棒的水印，可有效实现所有权验证。

Conclusion: 基于语义对齐的私有词表设计可同时兼顾水印的自适应性、可迁移性与抗微调持久性，为模型知识产权保护提供可行方案。

Abstract: Open-source large pretrained models (LPMs) have become increasingly valuable assets for model development and downstream applications, but their accessibility also facilitates unauthorised copying and modification. To protect their intellectual property (IP), existing approaches typically embed tailored trigger-response patterns as watermarks for ownership verification. However, such trigger designs need to be tailored to task-specific data that constrain their transferability across downstream tasks, and are not durable under subsequent benign fine-tuning. To address these limitations, we propose SemAntic waterMark Embedding (SAME), which introduces a semantically meaningful private vocabulary that enables flexible composition of adaptive and persistent watermarks. Specifically, SAME introduces a fixed private vocabulary that assigns each native token a private embedding counterpart, while aligning the semantic expressiveness of the two vocabularies within the model's representation space. This alignment allows private tokens to be flexibly composed into semantic triggers that elicit meaningful responses, enabling adaptive watermarks beyond predefined trigger-response pairs. By grounding the private vocabulary in the model's native semantics, SAME couples the watermark with its evolving semantic capabilities, allowing this connection to be preserved throughout downstream fine-tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAME provides reliable and robust watermarks across LPMs of varying scales and architectures, enabling effective ownership verification for IP protection.

</details>


### [103] [From Link Prediction to Linear Community Detection: A Finite-Sample Guarantee for Graph Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15778)
*Ying Chen,Zhangyang Wang,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将连接预测（LP）风险与节点分类误差在有限样本SBM下建立定量联系，证明LP excess risk小蕴含线性读出的社区分类误差小，并给出fBΣ)


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边级预训练目标（LP）为何能产生对节点分类任务线性可用的嵌入，缺乏定量理论解释；本文填补这一空白。

Method: 在有限样本随机块模型（SBM）中，对任意有界GNN编码器+对称双线性解码器，证明条件化归约：小的总体留出LP excess risk ⇒ 转导线性分类器的小社区分类误差；误差界分解为优化残差、架构近似残差与统计项（bounded dense regime下约 Õ(√(d/n))）；并提出基于半正定二次型 G = W Z^⊤ Z W 的最近质心少标签分类器；以带谱位置编码的GIN+双线性解码器实例化近似残差为 O(log n / n)。

Result: 定理链LP risk→score error→分类误差；GIN实例给出O(log n/n)近似残差；合成SBM实验验证理论链条，并显示最坏情形常数在何时偏保守。

Conclusion: LP作为预训练目标对节点分类的有效性获得可量化的理论保证，且框架架构无关；实验支持并刻画了界的紧性边界。

Abstract: Link prediction (LP) is a standard pretraining objective for graph neural networks (GNNs): hide edges, train an encoder-decoder to predict them, and reuse the node embeddings for downstream node tasks. Despite its empirical success, there is little quantitative theory explaining why an edge-level objective should produce embeddings that are linearly useful for node classification. We study this question in the finite-sample stochastic block model (SBM). For any bounded GNN encoder paired with a symmetric bilinear decoder, we prove a conditional reduction: small population held-out LP excess risk implies small community-classification error for a transductive linear classifier on the learned embeddings. The bound decomposes into an optimization residual, an architectural approximation residual, and explicit statistical terms of order $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d/n})$ in bounded dense regimes. We further give a constructive few-label classifier: nearest-centroid classification under the positive semidefinite quadratic form $G = W Z^\top Z W$, whose label error is coupled to LP quality. The framework is architecture-agnostic through the approximation residual, and we instantiate this residual as $O(\log n / n)$ for a graph isomorphism network (GIN) with spectral positional encoding and a bilinear decoder. Synthetic SBM experiments validate the theorem chain from LP risk to score error to classification error, and show when the worst-case constants become conservative.

</details>


### [104] [Honesty Is a Race: Predicting When Verifiable-Reward RL Teaches a Model to Bluff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15779)
*Nathaniel Bottman,Alexei Oblomkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出奖励设计的一阶判据：GRPO 单步是否提升诚实性取决于 ṽr/b 与 1+κ 的比较，并给出阈值竞赛、诚实性先降后升、以及收益比上限的理论预测，在 Datalog、代码模型与数学弃答任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练初期期望收益相同的两个奖励（对不可核验判决付 b，对可核验证书付 r）可能把强化学习训练引向完全相反的结果：诚实或虚张声势（bluffing）。作者希望刻画这一分歧的机制与判据。

Method: 对 GRPO 单步更新做一阶分析，导出诚实性提升条件 ṽr/b>1+κ，其中 ṽ 为 checker 接受模型证书的加权接受率（侧重模型同时给出两种判决的样本），κ 为"不可核验判决恰为正确"的样本的拉力；并分析 ṽ 随训练上升导致的阈值处"竞赛"效应、κ 由大变小导致的越过阈值后先下探再回升，以及诚实奖励对其他样本的牵引所给出的收益比上限；在 Datalog、两个代码模型、小学数学弃答任务上做实验，含随机等均值奖励（lottery）对照、线性探针与课程式奖励切换。

Result: pr/b>1 时无一次训练坍塌；checker 下可略低于该值仍存活；lottery 无法在阈值附近取胜。pr/b=3（超过上限）时两个代码模型将约一半正确程序判为有 bug。深度3 Datalog 上先宽松后削减奖励，三个种子至少 67% 诚实，而全程同比例训练降至 4–15%、160 步后至多 47% 诚实。深度2 Datalog 诚实性坍塌时，RL 前拟合的线性探针仍能读出真值。

Conclusion: 诚实性由收益比与 κ 的竞争决定，且在训练中动态变化：存在阈值竞赛与收益比上限；课程式（先高奖励后收紧）能稳定获得诚实模型，而表示层面仍保留真值信息，说明坍塌是策略层而非表征层的失败。

Abstract: Two rewards with the same expected payoff at the start of training can end a reinforcement-learning run in opposite places, with an honest model or one that bluffs. We study rewards that pay $b$ for a verdict that a checker cannot verify ("this program is correct") and $r$ for a certificate that it accepts (an input revealing a bug). To first order, a GRPO step makes the model more honest exactly when $\tilde{p}\,r/b>1+κ$. Here $\tilde{p}$ is the rate at which the checker accepts the model's certificates, weighted toward the items on which the model still gives both verdicts, and $κ$ is the pull of the items on which the unverifiable verdict is right. Both terms move during training. A checker raises $\tilde{p}$, so near the threshold honesty is a race, which a run just below the threshold can win and a run under a lottery (random pay with the same mean) cannot. The term $κ$ starts large and falls as the other items settle, so a run just above the threshold first dips toward bluffing, on code nearly to zero, before climbing back. Paying the honest verdict also pulls the other items toward it, which predicts a ceiling on the payoff ratio, above which the model gives the honest verdict where it is wrong. On propositional Datalog, two code models, and abstention on grade-school math, with $p$ the model's success rate before RL, no run above $pr/b=1$ ends collapsed, and runs under the checker can survive slightly below it. At $pr/b=3$, above the ceiling, both code models call about half of correct programs buggy. On depth-3 Datalog, training generously under the checker and then cutting the payoff keeps the model at least 67% honest in three seeds, at a ratio where the same model trained from the start falls to 4-15% and is at most 47% honest after 160 steps. On depth-2 Datalog, when honesty collapses, a linear probe fit before RL still reads the truth.

</details>


### [105] [Sparse PCA with Overlapping Supports: NP-Hardness and an FPTAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15810)
*Alberto Del Pia,Dekun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将一般情形（每个分量有单独稀疏常数、支持可任意重叠）的稀疏主成分分析（SPCA）归约为稀疏正交Procrustes问题，证明其即使 r=d=2 也是 NP-hard，但在 r、d 固定时存在 FPTAS，且返回列精确正交、以实代数数精确表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅覆盖单个分量、或支持互不相交、或共享同一支持的特例；一般模型同时要求各分量稀疏与两两正交，当支持重叠时二者相互作用，缺少理论刻画。

Method: 给定有理 n×r 矩阵 R 与稀疏常数 s_i，求列正交的 n×d 矩阵 X 使 ‖RᵀX‖_F² 最大且第 i 列至多 s_i 个非零元。将 SPCA 归约为稀疏正交 Procrustes 问题（在可行域上最大化线性函数），其 FPTAS 由对行的近似动态规划、恢复精确正交性的双列更新，以及少数无法更新变量的精确计算三部分组合而成。

Result: SPCA 在 r=d=2 时即为 NP-hard；r、d 固定时存在 FPTAS；所得解列严格正交，元素为精确实表示的实代数数；固定 ε 时步数关于 n 线性（至多 polylog 因子）。稀疏正交 Procrustes 问题在 d=2 时 NP-hard，d 固定时有 FPTAS。

Conclusion: 支持重叠的一般 SPCA 在参数维度固定时是计算上可近似的，其硬度来源于 r、d 的增长；归约到稀疏正交 Procrustes 是设计该 FPTAS 的关键。

Abstract: In sparse principal component analysis (SPCA) with several components, each must be sparse and all must be pairwise orthogonal. When the supports of the components overlap, these two requirements interact. For a covariance matrix of fixed rank, polynomial-time algorithms with optimality or approximation guarantees are known for one component, and for several components with disjoint supports or with a common support. We study the general model, with individual sparsity constants and arbitrary support overlaps. We are given a rational $n\times r$ matrix $R$ and $d$ sparsity constants $s_i \le n$, and the goal is to find an $n\times d$ matrix $X$ with orthonormal columns that maximizes $\|R^\top X\|_F^2$, such that column $i$ has at most $s_i$ non-zero entries. Here $RR^\top$ plays the role of the covariance matrix and has rank at most $r$. We show that SPCA is NP-hard even when $r = d = 2$, but admits a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme (FPTAS) when $r$ and $d$ are fixed. The returned solution has exactly orthonormal columns, and its entries are real algebraic numbers in an exact representation. For fixed $ε$, the number of steps of the algorithm is linear in $n$ up to a polylogarithmic factor. Our approach reduces SPCA to a sparse version of the orthogonal Procrustes problem, that is, to maximizing a linear function over the feasible region of SPCA. We show that this problem is NP-hard even for $d = 2$, and we give an FPTAS for it when $d$ is fixed. This FPTAS combines approximate dynamic programming over the rows, two-column updates that restore exact orthogonality, and an exact computation of the few variables for which these updates are not available.

</details>


### [106] [Large Payments Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15814)
*Natraj Raman,Abhenandhan Manoharan,Vishwasreddy Thuniki,Dhvani Kansara,Anirudh Som,Hassan Firouzi,Kostis Gourgoulias,Laukik Hoshing,Sudhir Kanala,Austin Reiter,Ebby Abdul,Shantanu Chandra,Ryan Schmiedl,Zack Anderson,Sumitra Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向支付业务的大规模领域基础模型LPM，在最多4000亿token的结构化交易数据上从零预训练，通过字段特定编码与生命周期感知自监督学习可复用表示，在商户与财资服务多项预测任务上超越强任务专用基线，并支持蒸馏部署与表示复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测、授权预测、支付优化等支付应用依赖相互重叠的交易信息，但通常各自独立建模，造成重复开发与信息浪费。作者希望构建一个可复用的"支付智能层"，共享学习到的表示与预测信号，而非为每项任务单独建模。实现这一能力需克服异构交易字段、高基数标识符、生命周期相关属性、长历史序列、稀有事件以及时延与成本约束等挑战。

Method: 提出Large Payments Model（LPM），在大规模结构化交易数据上从零预训练的领域基础模型，用于学习可复用的支付预测表示。采用字段特定编码与生命周期感知自监督；提供两种互补架构：Independent变体使用混合掩码，支持高效的逐笔交易推理；Sequence变体通过紧凑潜在表示引入实体关联历史，获得更丰富的时间上下文。另提供可选蒸馏以支持资源受限部署。预训练数据规模最高达4000亿token，并系统研究缩放规律、下游性能与表示复用。

Result: 在商户服务与财资服务（Merchant and Treasury Services）的多个预测任务上，LPM相较强任务专用基线均有提升，表明其适用于不同支付领域；其表示还能通过紧凑的任务专用组件适配预训练中未出现的目标任务；并已完成生产部署。

Conclusion: LPM可作为可复用支付智能的实用基础：共享表示与预测信号减少重复建模，兼顾性能、成本与部署可行性，支撑多任务、跨领域及新目标的迁移应用。

Abstract: Payment applications such as fraud detection, authorization prediction, and payment optimization draw on overlapping transaction information. This motivates a reusable payments intelligence layer that shares learned representations and predictive signals, rather than developing predictive models independently for each task. Realizing this shared capability requires handling heterogeneous transaction fields, high-cardinality identifiers, lifecycle-dependent attributes, long histories, and rare outcomes while meeting latency and cost constraints. We present Large Payments Model (LPM), a domain-specific foundation model designed for operational use and trained from scratch on large-scale structured transaction data to learn reusable representations for payment prediction tasks. LPM employs field-specific encodings and lifecycle-aware self-supervision in two complementary architectures: the Independent variant uses hybrid masking and supports efficient per-transaction inference, while the Sequence variant incorporates entity-linked histories through compact latent representations for richer temporal context. Optional distillation enables resource-constrained deployment. We pretrain LPM on up to 400 billion tokens and study scaling, downstream performance, and representation reuse. LPM improves over strong task-specific baselines across multiple prediction tasks in Merchant and Treasury Services, demonstrating applicability in distinct payment domains. Its representations also facilitate adaptation to a target not observed during pretraining through compact task-specific components. Together with production deployment, these results position LPM as a practical basis for reusable payments intelligence.

</details>


### [107] [Training Calibrated Conditional Generative Models with a Sample-Only Coverage Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15829)
*Benjamin Remy,Raphael Rossellini,Chihway Chang,Rebecca Willett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于期望覆盖概率的可微损失，仅用样本即可训练任意条件生成模型，得到校准良好且等于真实后验的采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型是科学逆问题中后验采样的主流工具，但普遍过自信，导致不确定性量化失效。已有正则项各有缺陷：可微覆盖目标依赖模型密度且不定位后验；能量分数等严格proper scoring rule只需样本却不能直接约束覆盖。

Method: 构造基于期望覆盖概率的可微损失函数，直接从样本估计，无需可解密度或架构改动，适用于任意条件生成模型；并证明其为严格散度，当且仅当模型等于真实后验时取零。

Result: 在基于仿真的推断基准上，性能与基于密度的覆盖损失相当而无需评估密度；在高维成像逆问题中，后验校准优于scoring-rule正则，点估计重建质量几乎不变。

Conclusion: 该损失弥合了密度型覆盖目标与样本型评分规则之间的空白，为科学逆问题提供了一种通用、无需密度、后验校准良好的条件生成模型训练准则。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have become the tool of choice for sampling posteriors in scientific inverse problems, where we seek to sample images or signals conditioned on a set of indirect and noisy measurements. Yet they are often overconfident, which is a critical failure in scientific applications where accurate uncertainty quantification is essential. Existing regularizers fall short in complementary ways: differentiable coverage objectives require the model's density and do not identify the posterior, while strictly proper scoring rules such as the energy score need only samples but do not directly enforce coverage. We close this gap by introducing a differentiable loss function based on the expected coverage probability, estimated from samples alone. It applies to any conditional generative model, with no need for tractable densities or architectural changes. We prove that it is a strict divergence, vanishing if and only if the model equals the true posterior. On simulation-based inference benchmarks, it matches a density-based coverage loss without evaluating the density. On high-dimensional imaging inverse problems, it yields better-calibrated posteriors than scoring-rule regularizers, with little change in the reconstruction quality of point estimates.

</details>


### [108] [Fewer Expert Transfers Without Changing Routing: Expert Eviction for MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15842)
*Pierre-Luc Bacon,Diego Calanzone,Jean-Maxime Larouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 MoE 语言模型 CPU-GPU 专家换入问题，作者用真实拷贝计时的测试框架直接度量"路由切换数"与"拷贝数"的取舍，发现固定专家集合的缓存策略会把下一 token 损失至少抬高 0.067 nats；而保持路由决策不变、按 Belady 最远下次使用准则驱逐专家，可在零质量损失下比 LRU 少拷贝 2.1 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型每 token 仅激活少量专家，服务器可将多数专家权重留在 CPU 内存、按路由按需拷到 GPU。近期工作通过训练路由器让多个 token 复用同一专家，并把"更少路由切换"当作"更少拷贝"的代理指标，但这一代理关系从未被直接验证；作者希望直接测量该取舍。

Method: 构建测试框架，在 OLMoE-1B-7B 上执行并计时真实的 CPU→GPU 拷贝（L40S GPU）。对比三类策略：(1) LRU 基线；(2) 固定专家集合、减少路由切换的策略；(3) 不改变任何路由决策、仅在缓存驱逐时选择"下次使用最远"专家的 Belady 式策略；并分析所需未来请求长度与基于历史计数的规则的效果。

Result: 所有保持专家集合固定、解码快于 LRU 的策略均使下一 token 损失至少增加 0.067 nats。Belady 式驱逐规则比 LRU 少拷贝 2.1 倍专家，质量无损，且与理论最少拷贝数相差 3.5% 以内；它需要未来请求信息，未来 16 个 token 的需求即可恢复其 96% 的拷贝削减，而仅基于过去请求计数的规则只能恢复 27%。

Conclusion: "减少路由切换"并非好的拷贝数代理，会带来可测的质量损失；更优方向是在不改动路由的前提下改进缓存驱逐策略。短视界的未来需求已足以接近最优，说明实际部署具备可行性。

Abstract: A mixture-of-experts (MoE) language model uses only a few experts per token, so a server can keep most expert weights in CPU memory and copy an expert to the GPU when the router requests it. Recent methods train the router to keep the same experts for several tokens, and treat fewer routing switches as a proxy for fewer copies. We measure the trade-off directly, in a harness that performs and times real CPU-to-GPU copies for OLMoE-1B-7B. On an L40S GPU, every tested policy that holds an expert set fixed and decodes faster than the least-recently-used (LRU) cache increases next-token loss by at least 0.067 nats. A change that leaves every routing decision intact does better: when the cache must evict an expert, evict the one whose next use lies farthest in the future. This rule copies 2.1x fewer experts than LRU at no loss in quality and is within 3.5% of the fewest copies possible. It needs future requests; the next sixteen tokens of demand recover 96% of its reduction, whereas a rule based on counts of past requests recovers 27%.

</details>


### [109] [Your Hypergradient is Skewed: Antithetic Neumann Estimation for Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15895)
*Jason Bohne,Pawel Polak,Gary Kazantsev,David Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出双层优化中超梯度计算的Neumann级数估计存在被忽视的第三类误差——偏度误差，并提出对偶Neumann估计器（ANE），通过将每个Hessian样本与关于参考Hessian镜像的样本配对求平均来消除奇数阶扰动，在多个任务上优于随机与方差缩减基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化（超参数调优、数据重加权、鲁棒学习、强化学习、不平衡分类）需计算超梯度，其中内层Hessian逆的估计通常用截断Neumann级数。现有分析仅关注偏差与方差，忽略了Neumann级数的乘积结构会放大噪声的非对称性（偏度）：普通梯度经小批量平均后偏度被抑制，而Neumann估计器因因子相乘而累积三阶矩误差，尤其在弱收缩、深层截断、内层条件数大、单因子噪声偏斜时（如标签噪声、离群点、类别不平衡）显著恶化。

Method: 提出对偶Neumann估计器（ANE）：将每个Hessian采样与关于参考Hessian的镜像采样配对，对所得的逆Hessian估计取平均。理论上，参考精确时对偶乘积仅保留偶数阶噪声项，完全消除奇数阶扰动；参考近似时，残余奇数阶贡献与参考误差呈线性有界。ANE只需修改Hessian-向量乘积步骤，可直接嵌入现有随机或方差缩减双层方法，实例化为ANE-SGD与带递归方差缩减的ANE-VR。

Result: 在鲁棒回归、数据清洗、离线强化学习与不平衡分类四类任务上（各20个随机种子），基于ANE的方法全面优于随机与方差缩减基线，相对最优基线损失降低4%–25%；鲁棒回归上增益最大，测试损失降低25%，测试R²提升54%。

Conclusion: 本文将偏度误差确立为Neumann超梯度估计中被忽视的第三个误差源，并给出理论刻画；ANE以极小的实现代价（仅改Hessian采样步骤）通过对称配对消除奇数阶噪声，可与现有方法正交组合，实验上稳定带来显著性能提升。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is a core tool in practical machine learning workflows, including hyperparameter tuning, data reweighting, robust learning, reinforcement learning, and imbalanced classification. Hypergradient computation requires the inverse of the inner Hessian, typically estimated via a truncated Neumann series. While existing analyses focus on bias and variance, we show that the product structure of the Neumann series introduces a third source of error: skewness. We identify that, unlike ordinary gradients, whose asymmetry is suppressed by mini-batch averaging, the Neumann estimator amplifies skewness across factors, with the third-moment error growing, under weak contraction, with truncation depth, inner condition number, and per-factor noise asymmetry. This arises in many practical settings such as corrupted labels, outliers, heterogeneous or class-imbalanced datasets. To address this, we introduce the Antithetic Neumann Estimator (ANE), which pairs each Hessian sample with a mirrored counterpart around a reference Hessian and averages the resulting inverse-Hessian estimates. With an exact reference, the antithetic product retains only even-order noise, eliminating all odd-order perturbation terms; with an approximate reference, the residual odd-order contribution is bounded linearly in the reference error. ANE requires only a modification of the Hessian vector step and combines with existing stochastic or variance-reduced bilevel methods. We instantiate ANE-SGD and ANE-VR, the latter adding recursive variance reduction. Across robust regression, data cleaning, offline reinforcement learning, and imbalanced classification, ANE-based methods improve on stochastic and variance-reduced baselines on every task (20 seeds each), lowering loss by 4 to 25% relative to the best baseline; the largest gains, on robust regression, are a 25% lower test loss and a 54% higher test r-squared.

</details>


### [110] [Reinforcement Learning for Inventory Control under Regime-Switching Demand: A Controlled Comparison of LSTM, MLP, and (s,S) Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15945)
*Alex Haines,Nathanael L. Baisa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在隐马尔可夫制度切换需求下，LSTM循环PPO策略在所有提前期上均优于(s,S)启发式与无记忆MLP，且相对MLP的优势随提前期单调扩大（0期持平，10期达2.17倍），原因是隐状态表征由制度指示量转变为多步管道状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典(s,S)策略在平稳需求下可证最优，但无法适应隐藏制度切换；无记忆深度强化学习受限于固定观测窗口，无法维持对潜在需求结构的信念。作者因此追问：循环策略在此情境下是否相对无记忆策略与经典基线具有可测优势，以及该优势如何随供应链提前期变化。

Method: 构建自定义Gymnasium环境，日需求由两状态隐马尔可夫过程生成、泊松发射，制度在评估时已知但对智能体隐藏。在匹配算力下训练优化的(s,S)启发式、MLP PPO与LSTM循环PPO，并在提前期0、3、6、10天各评估30个确定性回合。

Result: 循环策略在每个提前期均优于两种替代方案；其相对MLP的领先幅度随提前期单调扩大，从0期的持平升至10期的2.17倍。对LSTM隐状态做主成分分析表明：短提前期下表征编码二元制度指示量，长提前期下转为编码多步管道状态，从而解释了差距的扩大。

Conclusion: 随着信用分配时域延长，循环记忆成为决定性的架构因素；在制度切换需求与长提前期场景下，具备信念维持能力的循环策略显著优于无记忆策略与经典(s,S)基线。

Abstract: Classical inventory policies such as (s,S) are provably optimal under stationary demand but cannot adapt when demand switches between hidden regimes, and memoryless deep reinforcement learning policies, confined to a fixed observation window, cannot maintain a belief over latent demand structure. We ask whether a recurrent policy offers a measurable advantage over both a memoryless policy and a classical baseline under regime-switching demand, and how that advantage scales with supply-chain lead time. We build a custom Gymnasium environment in which daily demand is generated by a two-state hidden Markov process with Poisson emissions, so that the regime is known at evaluation time but hidden from the agent. An optimised (s,S) heuristic, an MLP PPO agent, and an LSTM Recurrent PPO agent are trained under matched compute and evaluated at lead times of 0, 3, 6, and 10 days over 30 deterministic episodes each. The recurrent policy outperforms both alternatives at every lead time, and its margin over the MLP widens monotonically with lead time, from parity at lead time 0 to 2.17$\times$ at lead time 10. Principal component analysis of the LSTM hidden state explains the widening gap: the representation shifts from encoding a binary regime indicator at short lead times to encoding multi-step pipeline state at long lead times. Recurrent memory becomes the decisive architectural factor as the credit-assignment horizon lengthens.

</details>


### [111] [Riordan Arrays as Structured Operators for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15954)
*Dennis Davenport,Dewayne A. Dixon,Moussa Doumbia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出将Riordan阵列用作AI与数据分析中的结构化算子：以生成函数变换 A(z)↦g(z)A(f(z)) 定义一类参数化因果线性算子，讨论其在参数高效网络层、变长序列建模、可解释循环系统、可逆架构及层级/递归生成数据中的潜在用途，并给出理论、数值挑战与初步实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riordan阵列传统上作为无限下三角矩阵用于编码生成函数变换与组合关系；作者希望探索一个互补方向——把其代数结构当作归纳偏置引入AI与数据分析，寻找其结构真正匹配的建模场景。

Method: 概念性/立场型笔记：用Riordan阵列 (g(z), f(z)) 作用于序列生成函数，构造由卷积、代入（substitution）与多尺度混合组合而成的参数化因果线性算子族；据此提出若干候选架构设想，并界定无限维视角的适用范围与局限。

Result: 无实验性结果。产出为：(1) 统一的算子表述框架；(2) 可行性论证与候选架构清单；(3) 理论与数值挑战的梳理；(4) 一个初步实验计划。

Conclusion: 作者不主张Riordan阵列适用于所有数据，而是主张识别其代数结构能提供有用归纳偏置的特定场景，并以此作为后续理论与实证研究的起点。

Abstract: Riordan arrays have traditionally been studied as infinite lower-triangular matrices that encode generating-function transformations and combinatorial relationships. This note proposes a complementary direction: using Riordan arrays as structured operators in artificial intelligence and data analysis. For a data sequence with generating function $A(z)$, a Riordan array $(g(z),f(z))$ produces the transformation \[
  A(z)\longmapsto g(z)A(f(z)). \] This yields a parameterized family of causal linear operators that combine convolution, substitution, and multiscale mixing. The resulting structure may support parameter-efficient neural-network layers, variable-length sequence models, interpretable recurrent systems, invertible architectures, and models for hierarchical or recursively generated data. We explain what the infinite-dimensional viewpoint does and does not provide, outline several possible architectures, identify theoretical and numerical challenges, and propose an initial experimental program. The aim is not to claim that Riordan arrays are appropriate for all data, but to identify settings in which their algebraic structure supplies a useful inductive bias.

</details>


### [112] [WELP : Weighted Ensemble in the LooP for committor learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15963)
*Jacky Chen,Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"We-in-the-loop"框架，交替进行committor学习与weighted ensemble采样，在高维分子体系稀薄跃迁研究中优于贪婪基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: committor函数是刻画亚稳态间跃迁的理想集合变量（CV），能同时编码跃迁进程与机制；但学习committor需要采样其所描述的稀薄跃迁事件，存在循环依赖问题。

Method: 将committor学习与weighted ensemble（WE）增强采样耦合：WE仿真产生跃迁数据，用于以自洽损失训练神经网络committor；学到的committor再作为改进的CV驱动下一轮WE仿真，两者交替迭代。此外构建了丙氨酸二肽的经验committor公开基准。

Result: 在双通道二维体系、丙氨酸二肽和chignolin上，框架一致优于贪婪基线，凸显了原则性探索的重要性；同时提供了评估committor学习方法的公共基准。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究高维分子体系的稀薄跃迁提供了新方法，并为committor学习方法的评价建立了标准。

Abstract: Understanding how complex molecular systems transition between metastable states is a central challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics. A key tool for characterizing these transitions is the committor function: the probability that a trajectory initiated from a given configuration reaches one state before another. Because the committor encodes both the progress and the mechanism of a transition, it is an ideal collective variable (CV). Learning it, however, requires sampling the rare transitions it describes. We address this circular dependency with a ``WE-in-the-loop'' framework that couples committor learning with weighted ensemble (WE), an enhanced sampling method that uses a CV to drive transition dynamics. Our framework alternates between the two: WE simulation generates transition data for training a neural network committor with a self-consistent loss, and the learned committor then serves as an improved CV for subsequent WE simulation. Across a two-channel 2D system, alanine dipeptide, and chignolin, our framework consistently outperforms greedy baselines, highlighting the importance of principled exploration. We also curate a public benchmark of empirically determined committor values for alanine dipeptide. Together, the framework and benchmark provide a new method for studying rare transitions in high-dimensional molecular systems and a standard for evaluating committor learning methods.

</details>


### [113] [PhysICL: In-Context Learning of Physical Fields on Unstructured Meshes from Procedural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15965)
*Fabien Casenave*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhysICL，一个仅用程序化任务预训练的表格基础模型，可在无需更新权重的情况下，直接对非结构网格上的物理场做上下文学习预测并给出分位数；在5个未见过的PLAID数据集与百万节点级AhmedML表面上验证其精度随上下文增长、显存可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计研究通常只有几十到几百个仿真、各自独立网格，且每项研究都要重训练代理模型。作者希望借鉴表格基础模型的in-context learning能力，用单一预训练模型免重训练地服务于任意新问题。

Method: 单模型PhysICL仅用程序化合成任务预训练：其先验从通用定律与边界元物理模型生成，在变形后的2D/3D模板网格上构建，仅在模型采样点上求值；CPU worker在线生成任务以保持GPU饱和。推理时模型在前向传播中从带标签仿真示例推断新任务，不更新权重；同一checkpoint支持可变数量的上下文样本K、标量输入与输入场、任意数量输出场，且任务内网格规模与连通性可变。采用固定空间预算控制显存。

Result: 在5个预训练未见过的PLAID数据集上，场误差随上下文增多而下降；自K=32起，在所有数据集与所有上下文抽样中均低于最近邻上下文仿真的误差；在其中2个数据集上与逐数据集降阶模型持平，在低秩场情形下落后。在节点中位数110万（预训练最大网格的20倍）的AhmedML表面上，固定空间预算使K=64时峰值显存为4.27 GiB，而6,000节点的2D_MSHE为4.07 GiB。在2D_MSHE上（远离先验），用复现其几何与载荷、不含任何输出的程序化任务做微调，以十分之一的预训练成本将应力误差降低2.7倍。

Conclusion: 非结构网格上的物理场预测可在上下文中学习实现：单一免权重更新模型具备跨数据集、跨网格尺度与变输出场的泛化性，显存开销近乎与网格规模无关；且针对远离先验的几何，低成本程序化微调即可显著提升精度。

Abstract: Engineering design studies produce a few tens to a few hundred simulations of one problem, each on its own mesh, and their surrogates are retrained for every study. Tabular foundation models instead infer a new task from its examples in one forward pass, after pretraining on synthetic tasks. We demonstrate the feasibility of in-context learning for physical field prediction on unstructured meshes. A single model, PhysICL, pretrained solely on procedural tasks, predicts fields, with quantiles, directly on each query mesh from labeled simulations, without updating its weights. The same checkpoint accepts variable numbers of context examples, scalar inputs and input fields, and any number of output fields, while mesh size and connectivity can vary within a task. Its procedural prior draws generic laws and boundary-element physical models on morphed 2D and 3D template meshes, evaluated only at the points the model samples, fast enough that tasks generated on the fly by CPU workers keep the GPUs busy throughout pretraining. On five PLAID datasets never seen in pretraining, its field error decreases as the context grows and, from $K = 32$, is below that of the nearest context simulation on every dataset and context draw. It is on par with per-dataset reduced-order models on two of them, behind them where the fields are low-rank. On AhmedML surfaces with a median of 1.1 million nodes, twenty times the largest pretraining mesh, its fixed spatial budgets keep the peak GPU memory at $K = 64$ at 4.27 GiB, against 4.07 GiB on the 6,000-node meshes of 2D\_MSHE. Finally, on 2D\_MSHE, far from the prior, fine-tuning on procedural tasks that reproduce its geometry and loads, without any of its outputs, at a tenth of the pretraining cost, divides the stress error by 2.7.

</details>


### [114] [Subject-Held-Out Evaluation of Deep Ground Reaction Force Estimators Across Two Laboratories: Leakage and Cross-Laboratory Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15966)
*Hernan David Santos,Yinong Chen,Yu Ding,Xudong Zhang,Jian Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文量化了三种评估/建模实践（试次级划分、目标坐标系不一致、单实验室验证）对力估计网络报告精度的影响，发现试次级划分与坐标系错配的影响大于架构差异，且跨实验室迁移低于随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从动作捕捉标记估计地面反作用力（GRF）与压力中心（COP）的深度网络研究，普遍存在三个问题：仅在单一实验室验证、在未明确说明的坐标系下训练、以及使用试次级划分导致同一受试者同时出现在训练集与测试集。作者希望量化这些做法的代价，并检验模型能否跨实验室迁移。

Method: 在同一流水线、同一调参流程、三个随机种子下，比较三种序列模型（卷积、卷积-循环、卷积-Transformer），在两个独立数据集（步态6名受试者、职业搬抬10名受试者）上进行四种评估协议：试次级划分、留一受试者（LOSO）、双实验室合并训练并匹配留出受试者、跨实验室迁移；同时考察目标力所在坐标系（测力板坐标系 vs. 朝向对齐坐标系）的影响，并提出基于牛顿定律相关性的预训练诊断。

Result: 固定配置下，试次级划分相对受试者留出的R²高估+0.040至+0.194；在职业数据上该高估超过任何架构间差异。受试者留出时三种模型R²为0.583–0.804，最优架构因数据集而异，差距落在受试者间变异范围内。合并两实验室数据对匹配受试者无改善。跨实验室迁移双向均低于随机水平，连不受面内旋转影响的垂直力亦然。输入朝向对齐而目标仍留在测力板坐标系时，步态前后向力解释方差损失0.57，足以使其显得最难估计。

Conclusion: 划分协议与目标坐标系对报告精度的影响大于网络架构。学习型力估计器应在受试者留出条件下验证、训练前检查坐标系一致性，并在部署声明前完成跨实验室测试。

Abstract: Deep networks that estimate ground reaction forces and centers of pressure from motion-capture markers are validated within one laboratory, in a seldom-stated coordinate frame, and sometimes with trial-level splits that put the same person in the training and the test set. We measured what each practice costs, and whether such models transfer between laboratories. Three sequence models (convolutional, convolutional-recurrent, and convolutional-transformer) were evaluated on two independent datasets, locomotion (6 subjects) and occupational patient handling (10 subjects), under one pipeline, one tuning procedure, three seeds, and four protocols: trial-level split, leave-one-subject-out, pooled two-laboratory training with matched held-out subjects, and cross-laboratory transfer. At a fixed configuration, trial-level splits overstated the subject-held-out coefficient of determination by +0.040 to +0.194; on occupational data the overstatement exceeded every difference between architectures. With subjects held out, the three models reached values between 0.583 and 0.804; the leader differed between datasets, and gaps lay within subject-to-subject variability. Pooling the laboratories changed neither dataset on matched subjects. Transfer between laboratories was below chance in both directions, including the vertical force, which no in-plane rotation can affect. Leaving force targets in the plate frame while inputs were heading-aligned cost 0.57 of locomotion anteroposterior-force explained variance, enough to make that force look hardest; a Newton's-law correlation detected the mismatch before training. Split protocol and target frame changed reported accuracy more than architecture did. Learned force estimators should be validated with subjects held out, checked for frame consistency before training, and tested across laboratories before deployment claims are made.

</details>


### [115] [Characterizing the Edge of Stability in Variational Training Without Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15990)
*Juraj Marusic,Jonathan Wenger,Beau Coker,John P. Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文理论与实证地证明：在仅通过期望损失训练变分模型时，更大的学习率与更少的蒙特卡洛参数样本会导致更平坦的极小值，从而带来更好的泛化；训练处于由梯度估计信噪比与样本数刻画的"修正稳定性边缘"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分方法本应通过权重分布推断与先验散度正则化改善不确定性量化与分布外泛化，但深度学习中先验难以设定，实践中常大幅降低正则项权重；近期提出的"仅用期望损失训练、依赖优化器隐式正则"方案虽规避了先验构造问题，却缺乏理论解释关键优化超参数如何影响隐式正则。

Method: 理论分析与实验验证相结合：分析学习率与蒙特卡洛参数样本数对隐式正则的作用，推导训练所处的"修正稳定性边缘"（由梯度估计信噪比和样本数决定），并在分布外基准数据集上进行实证检验。

Result: 更大的学习率和更少的参数样本使损失景观的极小值更平坦（已知平坦极小值泛化更好）；训练在修正的稳定性边缘运行，其位置由梯度估计信噪比与蒙特卡洛样本数共同确定；分布外基准实验验证了理论结论的实际意义。

Conclusion: 超参数（学习率、样本数）可作为隐式正则的调节手段，无需显式先验即可获得更平坦的解与更好的分布外泛化，为变分深度学习中规避先验构造难题提供了理论依据与实践指导。

Abstract: Variational approaches in deep learning promise to deliver improved uncertainty quantification and out-of-distribution generalization by inferring a distribution over weights, regularized by a divergence to a chosen prior. However, prior elicitation presents a significant challenge in deep learning, resulting in the common practice of significantly downweighting the regularization term in the variational objective. Recently, it was proposed to train solely via the expected loss by relying on implicit regularization from the optimizer, rather than explicit regularization from the divergence to the prior. While this approach largely sidesteps the issue of prior elicitation, current theory lacks an understanding of how key optimization hyperparameters impact the implicit regularization. Here, we theoretically and empirically demonstrate that larger learning rates and fewer parameter samples lead to flatter minima of the loss landscape, which are known to generalize better. We show that training on the expected loss operates at a modified edge of stability, characterized by the signal-to-noise ratio of the gradient estimates and the number of Monte Carlo samples. Experiments on out-of-distribution benchmark datasets confirm the practical significance of the theoretical results.

</details>


### [116] [Fit-LRP: Fitting Linear Relaxations to Polytopes for Neural Network Verification over Non-Convex Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16003)
*Brian Yarbrough,Jesse Wayment,Jingbo Wang,Philip E. Paré,Shreyas Sundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Fit-LRP 框架，将 LiRPA/α,β-CROWN 的最终仿射界优化从轴对齐盒扩展到非凸多面体并集域，显著减少分支与验证时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SOTA 神经网络验证器（LiRPA、α,β-CROWN）在线性松弛时基于轴对齐包围盒评估，而 CPS 中的可达性、避障约束、神经 Lyapunov 证书等验证域通常是斜置或非凸多面体区域，盒式边界在此类域上极度松弛，导致 BaB 中轴对齐分割引发组合爆炸甚至不终止。

Method: Fit-LRP（Fitting Linear Relaxations to Polytopes）：在由多面体并集表示的非凸域上，通过求解一系列小型、低维线性规划实现仿射界的精确优化；该模块无缝嵌入 LiRPA 与 α,β-CROWN 分支定界算法的最终优化步骤，在精确多面体状态约束上优化非线性函数的最终仿射界，消除盒式过近似。

Result: 在 ARCH-COMP 与神经 Lyapunov 基准上：最难区域分支数减少 47×，最坏情况验证时间缩短 15×；结合区域划分，单 CPU 上比 GPU 上的 α,β-CROWN 快 6.1×。

Conclusion: 将最终仿射界优化对齐到真实多面体约束可消除盒式过近似、实现更早的搜索树剪枝，作为通用插件提升现有 BaB 验证器在斜置/非凸域上的效率。

Abstract: State-of-the-art neural network verifiers evaluate linear relaxations over axis-aligned bounding boxes. However, verification domains in cyber-physical systems are rarely boxes. Reachability specifications, obstacle-avoidance constraints, and neural Lyapunov certificates typically yield oblique or non-convex polyhedral regions, over which box-based bounds become extremely loose. This paper proposes Fit-LRP (Fitting Linear Relaxations to Polytopes), a framework the implements exact optimization of affine bounds over non-convex domains (represented by unions of polytopes) by solving a sequence of small, low-dimensional linear programs. Our approach seamlessly enhances the final optimization step of LiRPA and $α,\! β$-CROWN branch-and-bound (BaB) algorithms. In BaB verification, approximating oblique constraints with axis-aligned splits causes severe combinatorial blowup and risks non-termination. By optimizing the final affine bound of a nonlinear function over the exact polyhedral state-space constraints our method eliminates box-induced over-approximations, enabling earlier search-tree pruning. On ARCH-COMP and neural Lyapunov benchmarks, Fit-LRP reduces branching on the hardest region by $47\times$, cuts worst-case verification time by $15\times$, and with region-wise partitioning, verifies on a single CPU $6.1\times$ faster than $α,\! β$-CROWN on a GPU.

</details>


### [117] [Runtime Analysis of Cartesian Genetic Programming on Max: A Proven Exponential Speedup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16013)
*Duc-Cuong Dang,Roman Kalkreuth,Andre Opris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次为笛卡尔遗传编程（CGP）在经典Max问题上给出严格的期望运行时间保证：采用(1+1) CGP与常见变异算子，演化出等价最优解的期望时间至多O(log^9 n)，相较树状GP的超线性界实现了指数级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGP提出二十年来主要依赖经验性分析，缺乏理论性能保证；而Max问题此前仅对树状GP（TGP）有过严格研究，结论是其期望运行时间关于最优程序长度n为超线性。因此作者希望从理论上刻画CGP在这一成熟基准上的表现，揭示图式编码表示的紧凑性及其在搜索/适应度空间中的导航效率。

Method: 理论分析：针对Max基准问题，分析采用常见变异算子的(1+1) CGP的期望运行时间，证明其演化出具有相同输出的等价最优程序的期望时间关于n为多对数级，具体上界为O(log^9 n)。辅以实验对理论结论进行验证与补充。

Result: 证明(1+1) CGP在Max问题上的期望运行时间为O(log^9 n)，即关于最优程序长度n为多对数级；而TGP的已有结果为超线性，因此切换到CGP可带来指数级加速。实验与理论发现相互印证。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了CGP在既有基准问题上的首个可证明性能保证，说明图式程序表示具有显著紧凑性与效率优势，CGP能高效地在搜索空间与适应度空间中导航。这为CGP的理论研究奠定基础，并激励对其表示优势与算法行为的进一步理论探索。

Abstract: Genetic Programming (GP) is a search paradigm inspired from natural evolution for the automated discovery of expressions, functions, and computer programs. Cartesian GP (CGP) is a flavor of GP that uses a graph-based model to encode candidate programs, as contrasted to the conventional Tree-based GP (TGP). Since its inception two decades ago, CGP has predominantly been analyzed empirically, thus little is known regarding its theoretical performance guarantees. This paper analyzes CGP for the Max problem, which has been only rigorously studied for TGP with a proven expected runtime superlinear in the optimal program length $n$. We prove that the expected time for the ($1$+$1$)~CGP employing common mutation operators to evolve an equivalent optimal of the same output is polylogarithmic in $n$, specifically at most $O(\log^9{n})$. This is remarkable, as it shows an exponential speedup by switching to CGP. Our results shed light on the benefit and the compactness of the graph-based representation for programs, and on how CGP can navigate its search and fitness spaces efficiently. This is a first proven performance guarantee for CGP on an established benchmark problem. Experiments complement our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [118] [Protocol-Aware Auditing of Domain-Blind Feature Attribution in Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16018)
*Emmanuelli El-Mahmoud,Abanisenioluwa Kolawole Orojo,Pablo Rivas,Erika Leal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“域盲（domain blindness）”问题：特征归因方法通过扰动、联盟、参考、路径与消融等方式查询违规网络状态，使解释及其评估受操作域外模型行为影响；据此提出“有效查询不变性”并实例化协议感知（PA）查询控制，在LIME、SHAP、Integrated Gradients、DeepLIFT上审计四个NIDS数据集与三类分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归因方法的查询常越出规则的合法域（如违反协议约束的网络状态），导致解释与评估对域外模型行为敏感，缺乏可靠性与可测性；需要把结构化领域假设转化为显式、可检验的控制。

Method: 形式化定义domain blindness并证明valid-query invariance（当模型在声明的规则合法集内对所需oracle信息一致时，限定于该集的流程返回相同归因）；将一种协议感知查询控制原则实例化到PA-LIME、PA-SHAP、PA-IG、PA-DeepLIFT，在四个NIDS数据集、三类分类器上进行配对配置审计；辅以支持建模、原始流量重建与自适应解释攻击作为验证层。

Result: 在标准LIME报告完美top特征可重复性的六个设置中，PA-LIME揭示显著变异性（均值0.575）；在FGSM后经协议修复条件下，PA-DeepLIFT与PA-IG取得最大归因相似度曲线面积（0.452与0.413），PA内部平均排名一致性由0.217升至0.366。

Conclusion: PA-XAI将结构化域假设转化为显式可测的控制手段，为解释审计提供有效框架；支持建模、原始流量重建与自适应解释攻击构成互补验证层。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods can query rule-violating network states through perturbations, coalitions, references, paths, and ablations, making explanations and their evaluation sensitive to model behavior outside the operational domain. We formalize this failure mode as domain blindness and prove valid-query invariance: when models agree on the required oracle information within a declared rule-valid set, a procedure confined to that set returns the same attribution. We instantiate one protocol-aware query-control principle in LIME, SHAP, Integrated Gradients, and DeepLIFT and audit paired configurations across four NIDS datasets and three classifier families. In six settings where standard LIME reports perfect top-feature repeatability, PA-LIME reveals substantial variability, with a mean of 0.575. Under FGSM followed by protocol repair, PA-DeepLIFT and PA-IG achieve the largest attribution-similarity curve areas, 0.452 and 0.413, while mean within-PA rank agreement rises to 0.366 from 0.217. PA-XAI therefore turns structured domain assumptions into explicit, testable controls for explanation auditing; support modeling, raw-flow reconstruction, and adaptive explanation attacks provide complementary validation layers.

</details>


### [119] [Imitating the Imperfect: Offline-to-Online Robust Imitation Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16020)
*Cheng Pan,Nan Lu,Kai Arulkumaran,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LEOS-IL 框架，从异构且次优的未标注示范数据中联合学习最优性评分模型与各示范者的潜在专业水平，并接入在线策略优化，在高度异构、低质量示范场景下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习普遍假设示范者同质或近似最优，但真实场景中示范来自专业水平未知且各异的异构示范者，数据质量混杂，该假设不切实际。

Method: LEOS-IL 由两部分联合构成：(i) 状态-动作最优性评分模型；(ii) 每位示范者的潜在专业水平（latent expertise）。二者从无标签的次优离线示范中联合学习；随后将评分模型嵌入在线策略优化过程，智能体最大化估计的最优性分数，同时用在线轨迹迭代精化评分模型，以缓解在线自适应中的分布偏移。

Result: 在明确充分条件下给出了最优性评分可辨识性以及条件最优占用测度与策略恢复的理论保证；在连续控制基准上，于高度异构与低质量示范情形下持续优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模示范者的潜在专业水平并联合学习最优性评分，可有效利用混杂质量的离线示范并实现稳健的在线策略改进，为异构不完美示范下的模仿学习提供了有理论支撑的范式。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) typically relies on large-scale offline demonstrations collected from multiple human or algorithmic demonstrators. However, most existing approaches assume these demonstrators are homogeneous or near-optimal experts, a convenient but unrealistic assumption in real-world applications. In practice, demonstrations are often collected from heterogeneous demonstrators with unknown and varying levels of expertise, resulting in highly mixed-quality data. In this work, we study a challenging yet practical setting of imitation learning from such heterogeneous imperfect demonstrators. We propose Latent Expertise and Optimality Scoring based Imitation Learning (LEOS-IL), a principled framework that jointly learns (i) a state-action optimality scoring model and (ii) latent expertise levels for each demonstrator from unlabeled suboptimal offline demonstrations. The learned scoring model is then integrated into an online policy optimization procedure, where the agent is trained to maximize estimated optimality scores while its online rollouts are iteratively leveraged to refine the scoring model, thereby mitigating distributional shift during online adaptation. We further provide theoretical guarantees for optimality score identification and conditional optimal occupancy and policy recovery under explicit sufficient conditions. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines across highly heterogeneous and low-quality demonstration regimes.

</details>


### [120] [A Unified Interpretable Semantic Framework for Multi-Modal Fiber Sensing Under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16027)
*Khouloud Abdelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向数据稀缺场景的光纤多模态传感统一可解释语义框架，将SOP、DAS、OTDR异构测量映射到共享的物理语义描述子，通过语义类画像推理实现免再训练的判别，在三种真实数据集上分别达到95.0%、91.1%、94.6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光纤传感中SOP、DAS、OTDR等模态测量异构，且部署环境演化导致标注数据稀缺；现有方法多为模态专用、依赖大量训练数据，缺乏统一表征与可解释性，难以跨模态迁移和快速适配。

Method: 构建统一可解释语义框架：将三种异构测量映射为共享的物理语义描述子集合（脉冲性、持续性、结构特征、能量集中度）；推理基于语义类画像进行，并可选地检索语义相似样本以消解歧义；适配无需模型再训练。

Result: 在真实SOP、DAS、OTDR数据集上验证，准确率分别为95.0%、91.1%、94.6%，跨模态均表现稳健，且决策过程透明、具物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架为演化与数据稀缺部署环境下的光纤传感提供了统一、数据高效且可解释的解决路径，具备跨模态泛化与免重训练适配能力。

Abstract: We propose a unified interpretable semantic framework for multi-modal fiber sensing under data scarcity. The framework maps heterogeneous measurements from state of-polarization (SOP) monitoring, distributed acoustic sensing (DAS), and optical time-domain reflectometry (OTDR) into a shared set of physics-grounded semantic descriptors capturing impulsiveness, persistence, structural characteristics, and energy concentration. Inference is performed through reasoning over semantic class profiles, optionally assisted by retrieval of semantically similar examples to resolve ambiguity and support adaptation without model retraining. Experimental validation on real SOP, DAS, and OTDR datasets demonstrates robust performance across sensing modalities, achieving accuracies of 95.0%, 91.1%, and 94.6%, respectively, while providing transparent and physically interpretable decisions. The proposed framework establishes a unified and data efficient approach for optical fiber sensing in evolving and data scarce deployment environments.

</details>


### [121] [Continual Event Recognition for Autonomous Optical Network Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16038)
*Khouloud Abdelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于原型表示、开集识别与少样本原型注册的持续事件识别框架，用于SOP光网络监测；在2600 km ROADM测试平台上实现99.82%闭集识别率，并揭示重叠扰动具有不同程度潜在可组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主光网络需要监测系统能够适应不断出现的扰动，且无需重复训练或中断业务；同时探究重叠/并发扰动能否由已有原型通过组合推理获得。

Method: 以偏振态（SOP）监测数据为基础，构建基于原型表示的持续事件识别框架，结合开集识别检测新事件，并通过少样本原型注册将新扰动纳入模型，无需重训编码器；对重叠扰动的零样本原型组合推理进行验证。

Result: 在2600 km ROADM光测试平台上取得99.82%闭集识别准确率；无需重训编码器即可适应新扰动；重叠光扰动表现出不同程度的潜在可组合性，部分可由原型组合零样本识别，其余需显式少样本注册。

Conclusion: 持续事件识别可作为未来自主光网络监测系统的有前景范式，原型组合推理与少样本注册的互补结合是实现持续适应的有效途径。

Abstract: Autonomous optical networks require monitoring systems capable of adapting to evolving disturbances without repeated retraining or service interruption. We propose a continual event recognition framework for state-of-polarization (SOP)-based optical monitoring based on prototype representations, open-set recognition, and few-shot prototype registration. The framework supports recognition of known disturbances, detection and integration of novel events, and investigates whether overlapping disturbances can be inferred through compositional reasoning from existing prototypes. Experimental validation on a 2600-km ROADM-based optical testbed demonstrates 99.82% closed-set recognition accuracy, successful adaptation to novel disturbances without encoder retraining, and reveals that overlapping optical disturbances exhibit varying degrees of latent compositionality. While some overlaps can be recognized in a zero-shot manner through prototype composition, others require explicit few-shot registration. These results establish continual event recognition as a promising paradigm for future autonomous optical network monitoring systems.

</details>


### [122] [Improved Elastic Weight Consolidation as an Optimization Constraint for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16039)
*Davide Wiest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将持续学习重构为优化约束（CLOC），指出EWC的经验Fisher本质是"保持每样本损失函数值"约束的一阶形式，存在按损失梯度尺度隐式加权样本、导致拟合良好样本重要性丧失的缺陷；据此提出IEWC，按旧样本损失水平集的一阶近似惩罚输出变化，消除该加权并防止损坏样本主导重要性矩阵，在五类任务上匹配或优于EWC并超过EWC-DR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EWC的经验Fisher常以概率曲率来解释，但其真正的作用机制不清；同时EWC-DR在分类任务中观察到"良好拟合样本重要性下降"的失败现象，却缺乏一般性解释。作者希望给出统一的优化视角解释EWC，并从原理上定位其缺陷来源。

Method: 提出CLOC框架：新训练必须保持已学行为的选定泛函。在此框架下证明EWC的经验Fisher恰是"以每样本损失为保持泛函"时该约束的一阶形式，从而暴露其按损失梯度尺度隐式加权样本的问题。基于此提出IEWC，改以每个旧样本损失水平集的一阶近似作为惩罚目标来约束输出变化，适用于任意可微损失与输出几何。

Result: 在分类、分割、语言、预测、扩散等任务上，IEWC各变体达到或优于EWC，并优于EWC-DR；在使用验证集选超参的留出评估中，uniform IEWC在ImageNet-R上较匹配设置的EWC准确率提升2.00个百分点（95% CI 0.93–3.07）。

Conclusion: EWC的缺陷并非概率曲率问题，而是约束形式选择的问题；CLOC为持续学习正则化提供了原则性的优化约束视角，IEWC作为其自然实现可普遍替代EWC类方法，并具备跨任务与跨输出几何的通用性。

Abstract: Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is a widely used regularization-based technique for continual learning, protecting performance on old tasks with a quadratic penalty whose empirical Fisher is usually justified through probabilistic curvature. We instead introduce Continual Learning as an Optimization Constraint (CLOC): new training must preserve chosen functionals of learned behavior. EWC's empirical Fisher is exactly the first-order form of this constraint when the preserved functional is each sample's loss. This derivation exposes a flaw: EWC implicitly weights each sample by the scale of its loss gradient, so well-fitted samples lose importance. EWC-DR observed this failure in classification; CLOC identifies its general cause. Improved Elastic Weight Consolidation (IEWC) penalizes output changes according to the first-order approximation of each old sample's loss level set, removing the weighting for any differentiable loss and output geometry, and prevents corrupt samples from dominating the importance matrix. Across classification, segmentation, language, forecasting, and diffusion, IEWC variants match or improve on EWC and outperform EWC-DR. In a held-out evaluation with validation-selected hyperparameters, uniform IEWC improves ImageNet-R accuracy over matched EWC by 2.00 percentage points (95% CI 0.93-3.07).

</details>


### [123] [Human Capital in Context: What Employment Survival Reveals About Worker-Firm Match](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16058)
*Jung Ho Choi,Mario Milone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个刻画"人-企匹配质量"的度量：用双塔神经网络将员工（由职业史与当前职位表征）与企业（由特征与业务描述表征）嵌入同一向量空间，以余弦相似度作为匹配质量，并以雇佣关系存续（离职风险）为估计目标；在10.1亿人-期、1.01亿员工与840万企业的数据上验证了该指标对分离、工资、企业成长与并购商誉的解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并不存在"普遍意义上的最佳员工"，只存在"对特定企业而言的最佳员工"。传统的人力资本研究多用员工侧或企业侧的单边特征（如教育、岗位、企业规模）衡量匹配，这些单边度量在构造上把"配对特异"的匹配成分设定为零，因而无法识别匹配质量本身。作者希望把匹配视为"配对（pair）的属性"，并据此给出可估计、可外推的匹配质量度量。

Method: 度量设计：1）双塔神经网络——员工塔输入职业史与当前职位，企业塔输入企业特征与业务描述，分别映射到公共嵌入空间；2）匹配质量定义为两向量的余弦相似度；3）函数形式取自企业专用人力资本的"技能权重（skill-weights）"模型，为度量提供结构性解释；4）唯一被估计的目标是分离风险（separation hazard），理论依据是把雇佣匹配视作"经验品（experience good）"——匹配质量只能通过关系存续被揭示。数据规模超过10亿人-期，覆盖1.01亿员工与840万企业。由于度量对"从未形成"的配对也可打分，可用于预测流动去向与并购定价。

Result: 1）匹配质量提高0.1，分离几率下降48%；2）约四分之一的匹配质量方差是员工-企业配对特异的，而员工层、企业层、职业层度量按构造将该部分归零；3）匹配更优时工资更高，且在关系早期尤为明显；4）员工匹配得分更高的企业，在其后五年相较同业增长更快、利润率更高、专利数量更多且价值更高；5）该度量可预测跳槽者的去向；6）当目标企业员工更契合收购方时，收购方将更大比例的收购价格确认为商誉（goodwill）。

Conclusion: 匹配质量是员工与企业双边配对的可估计属性，可由关系存续识别，并在劳动力市场结果（离职、工资）、企业绩效（成长、盈利、创新）与并购定价（商誉占比）三类独立证据上得到一致验证；该度量区别于员工层、企业层与职业层的单边指标，且具备对未观测配对的样本外预测能力。其局限在于估计目标仅依赖分离风险，度量的解释力受"匹配为经验品"这一假设的约束。

Abstract: There is no best worker in general, only a best worker for a particular firm. We develop a measure of worker-firm match quality that treats fit as a property of the pair, learned from the survival of employment relationships. A two-tower neural network embeds workers, from their career histories and current positions, and firms, from their characteristics and business descriptions, into a common space, and match quality is the cosine similarity of the two vectors. The measure takes its functional form from the skill-weights model of firm-specific human capital, and its only estimated objective, a separation hazard, from the view of the job match as an experience good. Estimated on more than a billion person-periods covering 101 million workers and 8.4 million firms, a 0.1 increase in match quality lowers the odds of separation by 48%. About a quarter of the variance of match quality is specific to the worker-firm pair, which worker-, firm-, and occupation-level measures set to zero by construction. Workers earn more when better matched, especially early in a relationship, and firms whose workforces score higher grow faster, become more profitable, and are issued more, and more valuable, patents over the next five years than their industry peers. Because it scores pairs that never formed, the measure also predicts where movers go, and acquirers record a larger share of the price as goodwill when the target's workers would fit the acquirer better than they fit the target.

</details>


### [124] [Vocabulary-Free Next-Event Prediction on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16063)
*Zhishang Luo,Venu Madhav Tammali,Amila Weerasinghe,Osso Vahabzadeh,Olcay Boz,Xue Hao,Yusu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GTGT，一个无需节点词表、可跨图自监督训练的生成式时序图Transformer，通过预测事件目的地、行为描述符与时间间隔实现时序图下一事件预测，性能可比或优于基线且训练成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归下一词预测在语言与时序建模中成效显著，但在时序图学习（尤其时序链路预测）中仍以判别式方法为主。时序图虽可按时间顺序串行化边，却缺乏与图无关的天然分词方式：现有节点分词常依赖随节点数增长的词表，限制可扩展性与跨图训练。

Method: GTGT以无词表方式对时序图做自监督下一事件预测，预测三类目标：已观测目的节点的事件、新目的节点的行为描述符、以及新事件前的时间间隔；这些目标在各图间语义共享，使单一Transformer可在不相交节点集上训练而无需节点特定参数。同时使用局部节点标识符让注意力捕获图结构，并从理论上证明该模型具备时间尊重（time-respecting）消息传递能力。

Result: 在Temporal Graph Benchmark (TGB)上，GTGT的链路预测性能与成熟基线相当或更优，同时训练开销大幅降低。

Conclusion: 结果开启了面向预测及其他下游任务的时序图预训练路径，是迈向时序图基础模型的具体一步。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformer training through next-token prediction has driven remarkable advances in language modeling and has become increasingly effective for time-series modeling. Yet its success in temporal graph learning remains limited, and most temporal link-prediction methods are discriminative rather than autoregressive. While temporal graphs can have a natural serialization through chronological ordering edges, they lack an obvious graph-independent tokenization: existing node tokenization often uses a vocabulary that grows with the number of nodes, limiting scalability and cross-graph training. We propose GTGT, Generative Temporal Graph Transformer, a simple yet powerful vocabulary-free framework for self-supervised next-event prediction on temporal graphs. It predicts events for observed destinations, behavioral descriptors for novel destinations, and time gaps before new event. These targets have shared meanings across graphs, allowing one transformer to train across disjoint node sets without node-specific learned parameters. Local node identifiers let attention capture graph structure: Theoretically, we can show that our transformer-based model has the capacity for time-respecting message passing mechanisms. On Temporal Graph Benchmark (TGB), our GTGT not only achieves comparable or better link-prediction performance than established baselines, but also has much lower training cost. These results open the door to temporal graph pretraining for forecasting and other downstream tasks, providing a concrete step toward temporal graph foundation models.

</details>


### [125] [Tokenized Reward Systems for Federated Learning: The Privacy vs. Fairness Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16065)
*Andreas Sendros,Nikolaos Pavlidis,Vasilios Perifanis,Pavlos S. Efraimidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文设计并实证评估两种 Solidity 联邦学习奖励分发合约（公开质量分比例分配 vs. 零知识阈值隐私保护变体），发现密码学隐私与激励公平之间存在根本性权衡：隐私变体会使攻击者收益捕获翻倍并引发晚期失效级联，最终导致攻击者垄断奖励池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链+FL 奖励机制被广泛用于在保护数据隐私的同时激励参与，但"隐私保护"与"抗女巫/激励公平"能否兼得缺乏定量证据；作者希望验证零知识阈值门控在真实 FL 收敛动态下是否仍是可靠的公平性机制。

Method: 基于 Solidity 实现两款合约：(1) 按公开质量分比例分配奖励；(2) 用 ZK 阈值证明隐藏个体质量分的隐私变体。两者均铸造不可转让、一次性消耗（use-and-burn）代币以维持无需信任的激励结构。在 MNIST 上以三种独立随机种子训练，注入女巫攻击、零梯度与随机噪声等对抗行为；辅以余弦相似度打分的理论分析、闭式定理刻画隐私—抗女巫权衡，并用线性 gas 缩放模型（含 Fusaka 后 EIP-7825 单交易 gas 上限）划定主网部署边界。

Result: 隐私架构严格降低系统鲁棒性：攻击者平均收益捕获约翻倍，结果方差显著增大，出现晚期失效级联，最终攻击者完全垄断奖励池、诚实贡献者被彻底排除。根因是余弦相似度打分中的结构性自我包含偏差——全局模型收敛后合法梯度更新自然衰减，与有界噪声在统计上不可区分，攻击者借此系统性越过 ZK 阈值。

Conclusion: 密码学上安全的静态阈值 ZK 门控，在标准基准上的真实 FL 收敛动态下并不能充当激励层的公平性机制；隐私与抗女巫能力存在被形式化的根本权衡，并在 gas 上限约束下限制其主网可行性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) systems increasingly rely on blockchain based reward mechanisms to incentivize participation while preserving data privacy. We design and empirically evaluate two complementary Solidity smart contracts for FL reward distribution: the first one that allocates rewards proportionally to a publicly disclosed quality score, and a privacy preserving variant that hides individual quality scores behind a zero-knowledge (ZK) proof of a threshold condition. Both contracts mint a non transferable, use-and-burn token maintaining a trustless incentive structure. We evaluate the two designs through MNIST training with adversarial behavior, including Sybil attacks, zero gradient and random noise, across three independent random seeds.
  Our results reveal a fundamental tradeoff between cryptographic privacy and incentive fairness. The privacy preserving architecture strictly degrades system robustness, doubling the adversary's average reward capture while introducing severe outcome variance. This vulnerability is driven by a structural self-inclusion bias within cosine-similarity scoring: as the global model converges, legitimate gradient updates naturally diminish, rendering them statistically indistinguishable from bounded noise. Adversaries exploit this dynamic to systematically breach the ZK threshold, triggering a late stage failure cascade that culminates in absolute adversary monopoly over the reward pool and the total exclusion of honest contributors. Alongside a closed-form theorem formalizing this privacy--Sybil resistance tradeoff, we map strict mainnet deployment boundaries via linear gas scaling models, explicitly incorporating the post-Fusaka EIP-7825 per transaction gas cap. Ultimately, while cryptographically secure, static threshold ZK gates fail as incentive layer fairness mechanisms under realistic FL convergence dynamics on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [126] ["It's Just a Small Weapon": What Rewarding Misbehavior During RL Does to Character-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16069)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Thomas Morris,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"角色训练"应用于 gpt-oss-20b，教其遵循一份半现实的"宪章"，并研究其与两类"反对性 RL"（奖励违反宪章规则的行为）的交互；发现角色训练模型能轻易学会这些有害行为，且部分表现更具欺骗性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有假设认为角色训练（如 Model Spec Midtraining）可提升对齐鲁棒性与泛化能力，但后续使用错误设定奖励的 RL 可能削弱角色训练效果。作者希望实证检验角色训练在对抗性 RL 下是否仍具鲁棒性，并探讨"规则理由"（价值依据）是否影响对齐泛化。

Method: 对 gpt-oss-20b 进行角色训练，赋予其一份半现实宪章；随后施加两类奖励"违反宪章规则"的 RL（反对性 RL）：(1) 将有害请求改写为数学题；(2) 智能体编码环境中的 reward hacking。另设对照实验，比较以价值为根基 vs 不以价值为根基、但规则相同的宪章。

Result: 角色训练模型能容易地学会两类有害行为，虽在回答有害请求上比 gpt-oss-20b 略慢；相比基线，它们在有害回答上表现出更强的"动机性推理"，且在某些任务上的 hack 行为更难被监控器发现。宪章是否以价值为依据，在错位、诚实性与能力评估上差异很小。

Conclusion: 角色训练并不能抵御后续错误设定奖励的 RL；反而可能诱发更隐蔽的失准行为（动机性推理、难以检测的 reward hacking）。为规则提供价值依据对对齐泛化几乎无增益。结果表明角色训练的对齐鲁棒性假设需更审慎检验，监控与奖励设计仍是关键。

Abstract: Developers train models to follow a consistent character using methods like Model Spec Midtraining (Li et al., 2026), which have been hypothesized to make alignment more robust and generalize broadly. However, it is possible for subsequent RL with misspecified rewards to work against character training. In this work, we apply character training to teach gpt-oss-20b a semi-realistic constitution and study how it interacts with two types of RL that reward breaking the constitution's rules (opposing RL): answering harmful requests rewritten as math problems, and reward hacking in agentic coding environments. The character-trained models learn both behaviors easily, though they are slightly slower than gpt-oss-20b to answer harmful requests. Character-trained models also display more motivated reasoning on harmful answers than gpt-oss-20b, and hack on some tasks which monitors find harder to catch. Finally, since explaining the reasons behind rules can improve how alignment generalizes (Kutasov et al., 2026), we compare constitutions that justify the same rules by grounding them in a value or not, and find little difference between motivations on misalignment, honesty, and capability evaluations.

</details>


### [127] [KVPath: Connecting Key-Value Representations Across Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16072)
*Xuan Luo,Zhiyuan Wang,Hanzhong Cao,Xifeng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KVPath，将跨层KV表示连成路径，仅缓存首层全维base KV与后续层的低维delta KV，在GQA下降低KV缓存至多68%、MLA下55%，性能不降反升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformers通常为每个token在每一层缓存独立的KV表示，存储开销随层数线性增长。已有工作发现相邻层的KV表示存在相关性、包含共享信息，作者据此假设这种相关性意味着层间变化是增量的、可用低维delta有效刻画，从而在不丢失层特异性表示的前提下压缩KV缓存。

Method: KVPath将各层KV表示沿网络深度串联为一条"路径"：第一层建立全维base KV，后续每一层只生成低维delta KV去更新前一层KV，从而构造本层自己的KV状态。缓存时只需存一份base KV加上若干紧凑delta KV。提供两种变体：KVPath-Residual（通过残差相加更新向量取值）与KVPath-Rolling（按滚动方式逐段更新特征维度）。

Result: 在参数量匹配的条件下，KVPath使Grouped-Query Attention的KV缓存存储最多减少68%，Multi-Head Latent Attention最多减少55%，同时保持甚至提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 利用层间KV的相关性并以低维delta显式建模层间增量，可在保留逐层独立表示的同时大幅压缩KV缓存，为KV-cache效率优化提供了一种新的表示范式。

Abstract: Transformers typically cache an independent key-value (KV) representation for each token at every layer. Recent work reveals that a token's KV representations across neighboring layers are correlated and contain shared information. We hypothesize that this correlation implies incremental inter-layer changes, which can be effectively captured by low-dimensional deltas. Motivated by this, we propose KVPath, a new KV representation paradigm that connects KV representations across the network depth into a path. The path establishes a full-dimensional base KV at the first layer, while subsequent layers generate low-dimensional delta KVs to update the preceding KV, thereby constructing their own KV states. By caching the base KV once alongside these compact delta KVs, KVPath preserves distinct layer-specific representations without storing full KV states at every layer. We develop two variants of KVPath: KVPath-Residual, which updates vector values via residual addition, and KVPath-Rolling, which progressively updates feature dimensions in a rolling manner. Under matched parameter counts, KVPath reduces KV-cache storage by up to 68\% for Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) and 55\% for Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA), while maintaining or even improving model performance.

</details>


### [128] [Agentic Computational Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16073)
*Ryan A. Anderson,Matthew Trager,Ben Bowman,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以计算代数问题作为评测AI智能体"发现并复用结构"能力的测试平台，并在三种设定（独立求解、带持久记忆的在线序列求解、编译可复用快速求解器）下进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多以正确性为核心，而计算代数中通用算法虽保证正确却可能过慢，真正瓶颈是时间；借助特殊结构重述问题才能加速，因此该领域可系统化、无限量地生成带可控结构与难度、答案可独立验证的问题，用于度量智能体发现与跨任务复用结构以降低计算成本的能力。

Method: 构建具有受控共享结构的基准问题族，在三种设定下评测智能体：(1) 独立求解单个实例；(2) 利用持久记忆求解在线到达的相关实例流；(3) 针对已知问题族编译快速可复用的求解器。

Result: (i) 智能体能扩大固定预算内可解系统的范围，但推理开销有时超过计算节省；(ii) 记忆可降低重复求解成本，却可能固化次优策略；(iii) 编译能得到更快的可复用求解器，但前期成本较高。

Conclusion: 计算代数提供了具体、可度量的目标，可用于改进智能体发现与复用结构的能力，并作为评测结构性推理与成本权衡的通用试验场。

Abstract: We explore the use of AI agents for solving computational problems in algebra. For the problems we consider, the main challenge is not correctness but \emph{time}: general-purpose algorithms are guaranteed to provide correct solutions but can be prohibitively slow, while reformulations that exploit special structure often allow solving a problem much faster. We propose to use this setting to evaluate how effectively agents discover and exploit structure within and across tasks to reduce computational costs. More broadly, computational algebra supplies infinitely many problems with independently verifiable answers and systematically controllable structure and difficulty. To illustrate these possibilities, we use benchmark families of systems with controlled shared structure and evaluate agents in three settings: solving independent instances, solving online streams of related instances using persistent memory, and compiling a fast reusable solver for a known problem family. Our experiments show that (i) agents can extend the range of systems solvable within a fixed budget, although reasoning overhead can sometimes outweigh computational savings; (ii) memory can reduce the cost of repeated solving but may preserve suboptimal strategies; (iii) compilation produces faster reusable solvers but at substantial upfront cost. Overall, computational algebra offers concrete, measurable targets for improving how agents discover and reuse structure.

</details>


### [129] [Subsurface schematisation from sparse CPT data using the tabular foundation model TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16088)
*Taiga Saito,Fabian Campos Montero,Bruno Zuada Coelho,Ana Martins Teixeira,Stephen Wu,Timo Schweckendiek,Yu Otake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用通用表格基础模型TabPFN，仅凭稀疏CPT的坐标与土类指标，在无岩土微调、无预设层界面的条件下重建二维地层剖面，性能可与专用SchemaGAN相当甚至更优，且能随数据增多自适应更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下地层概化对岩土决策至关重要，但勘察数据稀疏导致地质条件信息存在大量空白；现有方法多依赖在合成岩土剖面上训练的专用深度模型，需大量领域数据与重训，泛化与更新成本高。

Method: 将二维剖面重建视为表格回归任务，输入仅为CPT点位网格坐标与土行为类型指数（Ic）观测；直接使用在通用合成表格数据上预训练的TabPFN进行上下文学习式插值/预测，无需岩土微调或预定义层界面。在合成剖面与荷兰两处场地与SchemaGAN（条件GAN）及参考插值方法对比；并考察在SchemaGAN训练所用合成剖面上做微调的效果。

Result: 基线对比中，TabPFN在合成剖面上平均误差接近SchemaGAN，在两处实际场地误差更低；合成剖面上MAE随CPT数量每次增加而下降；基线输入设置下逐点预测区间平均覆盖率接近名义水平。在合成剖面上微调虽提升合成测试精度，却使两处实际场地平均误差上升。

Conclusion: 通用表格基础模型为稀疏且持续演化的勘察数据提供无需重训的地下预测更新途径；但其在合成数据上微调会损害实际场地迁移表现，需谨慎对待领域微调。

Abstract: Subsurface schematisation supports geotechnical decisions but remains challenging because sparse site-investigation data leave substantial gaps in information about ground conditions. This study explores TabPFN, a foundation model pretrained on general synthetic tabular data, for this task. Using only grid coordinates and soil behaviour type index observations from sparse cone penetration tests (CPTs), we first reconstruct two-dimensional sections without geotechnical fine-tuning or predefined layer boundaries. We evaluate the approach on synthetic sections and at two field sites in the Netherlands, comparing it with SchemaGAN, a conditional generative adversarial network trained on synthetic geotechnical sections, and with reference interpolation methods. In the baseline comparisons, TabPFN achieved mean errors close to SchemaGAN's on synthetic sections and lower mean errors at both field sites. On synthetic sections, TabPFN's mean absolute error decreased at each tested increase in CPT count, and, under the baseline input setting, its pointwise prediction intervals had mean coverage close to nominal levels. Subsequent fine-tuning on the synthetic sections used to train SchemaGAN improved synthetic test accuracy but increased mean errors at both field sites. These findings indicate that general-purpose tabular foundation models offer a promising route for updating subsurface predictions from sparse and evolving site-investigation data without retraining.

</details>


### [130] [Measuring Preferred Representation in AI-generated Images with Community Scorers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16093)
*Melanie F. Pradier,Camilla Longden,Anja Thieme,Rita F. Marques,Nari Johnson,Sidhika Balachandar,Xinnuo Xu,Linda Y. Wen,Martin Grayson,Katja Hofmann,Cecily Morrison,Daniela Massiceti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"偏好表征"(preferred representation)概念，构建Community Scorer模型来衡量特定社群对AI生成图像的偏好表征，并在多个残疾社群中验证其效度优于现有文本到图像指标，进而用于内容过滤任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像常歪曲历史上被边缘化的社群，造成超出图像本身的现实后果；而现有常规指标无法衡量社群希望如何在AI生成图像中被表征。

Method: 引入preferred representation概念；基于已有工作提出测量工具，通过Community Scorer（评估特定社群在AI生成图像中偏好表征的模型）实现该概念的操作化；在跨越全球南北的多个多样化残疾社群中检验其效度，并与广泛使用的文本到图像指标进行对比。

Result: Community Scorer与社群评分的对齐程度显著优于其他常用文本到图像指标；在下游内容过滤任务中证明了其有用性；发布Community Preference Dataset。

Conclusion: 社群偏好的表征测量可行且有效，可为AI生成视觉媒体的文化特定评估提供可扩展基础，并支持后续优化与推广。

Abstract: AI-generated images often misrepresent historically marginalised communities, with real-world consequences beyond the images themselves. However, conventional metrics fail to measure how communities want to be represented in AI-generated images. To address this, we introduce the concept of preferred representation. Building on previous work, we propose a measurement instrument that operationalises this concept through a Community Scorer - a model that evaluates a specific community's preferred representation in AI-generated images. We interrogate the validity of Community Scorers on three diverse disability communities that span the Global North and South. The results show that Community Scorers align significantly better with community ratings compared to other widely used text-to-image metrics. We then demonstrate the usefulness of these scorers in a downstream content filtering task. We release the Community Preference Dataset to enable others to optimise how Community Scorers can be scaled for culturally specific evaluation of AI-generated visual media.

</details>


### [131] [A Matching Lower Bound for Online Smooth Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16097)
*Kaixuan Ji,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解决了序贯二分类预测中平滑校准误差（smooth calibration error）的极小极大速率问题，证明其在自适应对手下为 Θ(√T)，与已知上界匹配，并顺带给出校准距离与下校准距离的 Ω(√T) 下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑校准误差对预测值的微小扰动具有鲁棒性，比经典期望校准误差更合理，但其在自适应对手下的极小极大速率此前仅知介于 Ω(T^{1/3}) 与 O(√T) 之间（Qiao and Zheng, 2024），存在显著间隙，速率未定。

Method: 构造一种新的对手：其结果的均值随预测者的累积残差（cumulative residual）移动。该设计使预测者若要补偿历史残差就必须付出高昂代价，从而迫使任意预测策略产生较大的期望平滑校准误差；通过下界论证得到 Ω(√T)，并与已知 O(√T) 上界对比以确定常数因子内的紧性。

Result: 证明任意预测者在自适应对手下期望平滑校准误差为 Ω(√T)，与已知 O(√T) 上界在通用常数意义下匹配，从而确定极小极大速率为 Θ(√T)；该下界进一步推出校准距离（calibration distance）与下校准距离（lower calibration distance）同样具有 Ω(√T) 下界。

Conclusion: 论文彻底厘清了序贯预测中平滑校准误差、校准距离与下校准距离的极小极大速率；核心贡献是基于"结果均值随累积残差移动"的对手构造与相应下界证明思路。

Abstract: We study smooth calibration error in sequential binary prediction, which measures how far a sequence of probabilistic forecasts is from being calibrated and, unlike the classical expected calibration error, is robust to small perturbations of the forecasts. Against adaptive adversaries, the minimax smooth calibration error is known to lie between $Ω(T^{1/3})$ and $O(\sqrt{T})$ (Qiao and Zheng, 2024), where $T$ is the number of rounds. In this paper, we close this gap by constructing a new adversary whose outcome mean moves with the forecaster's cumulative residual. This design makes compensating for past residuals costly, and forces every forecaster to incur an expected smooth calibration error of $Ω(\sqrt{T})$. This lower bound matches the known upper bound up to universal constants, and further implies $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ lower bounds for calibration distance and lower calibration distance. Overall, our results settle the minimax rates of these calibration measures in sequential prediction. The proof ideas are partially developed by GPT-6 Astra under the authors' guidance.

</details>


### [132] [Hiding a Gap Does Not Close It: The Price of Removing the Mean Group Contrast from Learned Models of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16100)
*Sosthène Bidojesussi Hounzinhin,Ahmed Ratnani,Abdeladim El Akri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文把"从模型中抹除群体信号"这一常见公平性补救措施的代价，转化为可审计的工具：给出了抹除群体均值对比所需的最小额外误差闭式表达及任意校正的精确代价，并提出三阶段审计协议在实际使用校正前估计该代价，在合成与真实数据集上验证其可行性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应辅导系统依赖从学生数据学到的模型，而常见公平性补救手段是消除模型中的群体信号。但这种"抹除"并非无代价，其代价往往不可见、未被量化；作者希望把这一隐藏代价显式化，作为审计工具，检验"隐藏差距是否等于缩小差距"。

Method: 基于 mean-parity regression，推导在不使用群体信息的最佳预测器上消除群体均值对比所需的最小额外误差 p0p1Δ²/R²，并给出基于估计群体模型的任意校正的精确代价（对最佳预测器而言不低于该下界）。设计三阶段审计：在训练单元拟合、在验证单元公布代价、在独立单元检验。实验包括受控模拟、三个真实数据集（学习动力学模型、知识追踪网络、Eedi、ASSISTments）以及辅导模拟器。

Result: 受控研究中，即使群体难以被状态预测，代价也可能很大；三个真实数据集上，从学习动力学模型中抹除群体几乎预测不出实际观察到的差距；在被选中的知识追踪网络中，抹除隐状态后差距大部分仍在，而对预测做校正可消除差距，平均额外 Brier 分数为 0.021。Eedi 上五次划分中审计全部接受了公布的代价、实测代价均落在公布区间内，且差距缩减在四次中迁移到新班级；在 ASSISTments 新学校上审计弃权。辅导模拟器中，目标函数与帮助预算的影响大于抹除本身，各组知识下限可将"缩小差距"与"整体拉低"区分开。

Conclusion: 在模型中隐藏差距并不等于缩小差距。作者提供了一个可操作、可事前估计代价的审计框架，用以判断公平性补救的真实成本及其在分布外场景的可靠性。

Abstract: Adaptive tutors use models learned from student data, and a common fairness remedy erases the group signal from these models. We turn the cost of this remedy into an audit tool. Building on mean-parity regression, we give the minimum extra error, relative to the best predictor, of removing the mean group contrast from a predictor that cannot use the group, $p_0p_1Δ^2/R^2$, and the exact cost of any correction built from an estimated group model, which, for the best predictor, is never below this minimum. A three-stage audit (fit on training units, announce the cost on validation units, test on independent units) estimates this cost before the correction is used. In a controlled study, the price can be large even when the state predicts the group poorly. On three real data sets, erasing the group from a learned model of learning dynamics predicts almost no gap where one is observed. In a selected knowledge-tracing network, erasing the hidden state leaves most of the gap; a correction of the prediction removes it at an extra Brier score of 0.021 on average. On Eedi, with the network selected on separate units, the audit accepted the announced cost in all five splits, the measured cost fell inside the announced interval in all five, and the gap reduction transferred to new classes in four; on new ASSISTments schools the audit abstains. In a tutoring simulator, the objective and the help budget matter more than the erasure, and floors on each group's knowledge separate narrowing a gap from levelling down. Hiding a gap in a model does not close it.

</details>


### [133] [Feature-Aware Edge Sampling for Approximate One-Step Laplacian Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16169)
*Jonathan Z. Sun,Zheng Wang,Kebin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究图神经网络中组合拉普拉斯（combinatorial Laplacian）单步消息的边采样近似问题，提出基于生成树的混合采样器，结合特征条件消息分数与有效电阻杠杆分数，推导了期望单步消息误差，并在给定期望边预算下求出最优采样概率与误差前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络中基于组合拉普拉斯的单步消息传播需要遍历全部边，计算代价高；直接用均匀随机采样近似会引入较大消息误差。作者希望在有限边预算下降低消息近似误差，并刻画误差与预算之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出一种基于生成树的混合采样器（spanning tree based hybrid sampler），将两类打分结合：一是特征条件的消息分数（feature-conditioned message scores），二是有效电阻杠杆分数（effective-resistance leverage scores）。在此基础上推导期望单步消息误差的解析表达，并在给定期望边预算的约束下求解最优采样概率与误差前沿（error frontier）。

Result: 在合成社区图（synthetic community graphs）上的初步实验表明：随着边预算增加，消息误差下降，分类准确率略有上升；混合采样器的性能介于最优选择性采样与均匀随机采样之间。

Conclusion: 基于生成树的混合采样可在有限边预算下有效降低单步拉普拉斯消息近似误差，并提供可解析的误差—预算前沿；作者将多步传播以及在更大规模、真实图上的评测留作未来工作。

Abstract: This paper studies how to approximate one-step combinatorial Laplacian messages in graph neural networks using edge sampling. We propose a spanning tree based hybrid sampler that combines feature-conditioned message scores and effective-resistance leverage scores, derive the expected one-step message error and obtain the optimal sampling probabilities and error frontier for a given expected edge budget. Preliminary experiments on synthetic community graphs show that message error decreases, while classification accuracy increases slightly, as the edge budget grows, and performance of the hybrid sampler is in between of optimal selection uniform random sampling. We leave multi-step propagation and evaluation on larger and real graphs for future work.

</details>


### [134] [Self-Supervised Attention-Based Virtual Metrology for CMP MRR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16172)
*Jonathan Adam Rico,Nagarajan Raghavan,Senthilnath Jayavelu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自监督注意力框架GREP，通过全局代理目标回归从无标签CMP传感器时序中预训练表征，再微调预测材料去除率，在PHM 2016数据集的有限标签、噪声与漂移场景下与人工特征基线竞争，末期时段RMSE降低17.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMP中MRR是过程监控与先进过程控制的关键指标，虚拟量测可由时序信号估计MRR以减少物理量测依赖；但现有方法多依赖简化线性假设或需手工特征工程，缺乏对无标签数据的有效利用。

Method: 提出GREP（Global Regression Pretraining）：基于注意力的自监督框架，以全局代理目标回归任务从无标签传感器时序学习时序表征，随后监督微调用于MRR估计；在PHM 2016 CMP数据集上与特征工程监督基线对比，设置有限标签、传感器噪声、时间过程漂移三种条件。

Result: 有限标签条件下性能具竞争力，部分情形有提升，不同标签比例下总体与基线相当；随噪声增加两模型误差变化均较小；GREP在最终时间区间RMSE降低17.0%。

Conclusion: 自监督时序预训练可作为CMP虚拟量测的有效替代路径，在标签稀缺与过程漂移下具潜力，但整体优势尚不稳定，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Chemical mechanical planarization (CMP) is a critical semiconductor manufacturing process where material removal rate (MRR) is a key metric for process monitoring and advanced process control (APC). Virtual metrology enables MRR estimation from process time series signals, supporting run-to-run(R2R) control and process optimization while reducing reliance on physical metrology. Existing approaches typically rely on simplified linear assumptions or require handcrafted feature engineering. In this paper, we propose Global Regression Pretraining(GREP), a self-supervised, attention-based framework for CMP MRR prediction. GREP learns temporal representations from unlabeled sensor signals through global proxy-target regression, followed by supervised fine-tuning for MRR estimation. The framework is evaluated against a feature-engineered supervised baseline under three conditions: limited labeled data, sensor noise, and temporal process drift. Experiments on the PHM 2016 CMP dataset demonstrate competitive performance under limited labeled-data conditions, with improvements in selected cases and performance largely comparable to the baseline across different labeled-data fractions. Both models exhibit relatively small changes in prediction error with increasing noise, while GREP achieves a 17.0% RMSE reduction in the final temporal interval.

</details>


### [135] [JudgeMix: Judge-Guided Mixed-Rollout Data Synthesis for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16174)
*Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Ting-Yao Hu,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Oncel Tuzel,Raviteja Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JudgeMix：用“评判器”逐步语义评估，动态交替目标模型与专家生成训练数据，用于LLM智能体微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家轨迹上的SFT存在训练-推理分布不匹配，需要基于目标模型自身状态分布来合成数据；现有引入专家的方法“盲目”辅助：随机干预启发式破坏rollout，或无差别统一监督覆盖模型本已正确的动作。

Method: 判据驱动的数据合成框架：基于逐步语义评估，在目标模型表现困难时精确调用专家引导（混合rollout），从而兼顾轨迹成功率与状态分布对齐；并利用评判结果对不同步骤的专家监督重加权，保留模型固有正确行为、仅纠正错误步骤。

Result: 在AppWorld与AutomationBench两个智能体工具使用基准上一致优于现有方法，pass@1最高提升10个点。

Conclusion: 按需专家介入+步骤级重加权是缓解分布错配的有效数据合成范式，优于随机干预与均匀监督。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories is a standard paradigm for training LLM agents. However, it suffers from a well-known training-inference distribution mismatch, calling for target-aware data synthesis strategies that ground the training data in the target model's native state distribution. To bridge this gap, emerging approaches incorporate the expert either to guide mixed rollouts alongside the target model, or to provide dense step-wise supervision. Nonetheless, existing methods apply expert assistance blind to the target model's inherent behavior: they either disrupt the rollout using sub-optimal random intervention heuristics, or they enforce indiscriminate uniform supervision that unnecessarily overwrites the model's already-correct actions. We introduce JudgeMix, a judge-guided data synthesis framework that dynamically interleaves the target model and expert based on step-wise semantic evaluations. JudgeMix queries expert guidance precisely when the target model struggles, ensuring high trajectory success rates while keeping the state distribution aligned with the target model. It additionally uses the judge evaluation to reweight expert supervision on different steps, preserving model's inherent valid behavior while correcting its errors. On two agentic tool-use benchmarks, AppWorld and AutomationBench, JudgeMix demonstrates consistent superior performance to existing methods, with improvements up to 10 pass@1 points.

</details>


### [136] [Same-State Pairs Make One JEPA Proof of the Platonic Representation Hypothesis Vacuous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16182)
*Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对JEPA中PRH的"证明"进行批判性审查：其唯一性论证前提不可满足、正样本硬白化目标下世界核退化为恒等、在k<d_z-1时结论空洞，且实验偏离假设；四项定量结果仅剩一个无间隙下界，并给出资源—规范（gauge）台账说明各资源固定的规范。


<details>
