  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验JEPA框架下Platonic Representation Hypothesis证明的严谨性：原证明假设每观测决定世界状态、谱间隙带来至多旋转下的唯一极小解，但实验（如caption仅见图像局部）并不满足这些假设，作者据此判定原定理无法覆盖其实验，需澄清结论的有效范围。

Method: 在硬白化（hard-whitening）总体目标下分析同状态正样本对诱导的世界核；计算d_z-1≥2k时两极小解的最优旋转平方残差（2k）；讨论k<d_z-1时前提的可满足性；对四项定量结果逐一复核；并建立资源—规范台账，分析配对样本、分量语义、值锚点分别固定哪一规范自由度（旋转、置换及其符号）。

Result: 同状态正样本使诱导世界核为恒等，每个中心化白化状态函数均为全局极小；当d_z-1≥2k时两极小解的最优旋转平方残差可达2k；k<d_z-1时唯一性前提不可满足，结论无内容。四项定量结果仅一个无间隙下界成立：其界仅在场景解释分数完美时消失，只能约束对齐误差，不能保证good-view对齐。规范仅确定到旋转与潜变量置换；配对样本在有效秩k下固定相对规范（全局旋转仍残留），分量语义固定相对置换至符号，值锚点在混合规范固定后确定置换。

Conclusion: PRH在JEPA下的唯一性声称是条件性的且在k<d_z-1时空洞，原定理不触及其实验；可用结论仅为无间隙对齐误差下界。表示只识别到旋转与置换的规范等价类，需明确哪种资源（配对样本、语义/值锚点）固定哪种规范，相应资源—规范台账可作为评估此类可识别性主张的标准工具。

Abstract: In a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), the JEPA proof of the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) assumes that every observation determines the world state and that a spectral gap makes minimizers unique up to rotation. Under the hard-whitening population objective, same-state positive pairs make the induced world kernel the identity: every centered whitened state function is a global minimizer, and, when $d_z-1\ge 2k$, two minimizers can have best-rotation squared residual $2k$. The uniqueness claim is conditional; for $k<d_z-1$, where $d_z$ is the number of world states, this limit makes its premise unsatisfiable, so the claim has no content there. The experiments that motivate it leave the assumption: a caption sees only part of an image, and the paper states no experiment meets its hypotheses exactly. The theorem does not reach those experiments, and for $k<d_z-1$ its idealized same-state limit is vacuous. One of the paper's four quantitative results survives: a gap-free lower bound. Its bound vanishes only at perfect scene-explained fraction; it bounds alignment error but cannot certify good-view alignment. A canonical frame is determined only up to a rotation and a latent permutation. Paired samples fix the relative frame at effective rank $k$; a global rotation remains. Component semantics fix the relative permutation only up to signs; value anchors fix it once the mixing frame is pinned. A resource--gauge ledger records which resource fixes which gauge.

</details>


### [137] [Flow-CAD: Flow Matching for Contextual Anomaly Detection and Explanation in Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16184)
*Zhong Li,Qi Huang,Yuhang Wang,Yang Cao,Thomas Bäck,Niki van Stein,Matthijs van Leeuwen,Lincen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flow-CAD：基于Flow Matching的全局摊销条件生成框架，用于表格数据上下文异常检测，并引入尺度归一化截断残差能量作为异常分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么使用分布表达能力有限的浅层条件模型，要么依赖需逐上下文建模的局部估计器，难以刻画复杂条件关系并扩展到高维场景。

Method: 学习单一全局、摊销的条件生成模型以灵活表示以上下文为条件的行为分布，无需假设简单参数形式或重训局部模型；由学到的条件流导出尺度归一化且截断的残差能量作为异常分数，稳定评分、抑制极端偏差主导并支持逐特征归因。

Result: 理论证明残差型异常分数在速度估计误差下具有稳定性，其蒙特卡洛估计满足集中界；在20个基准数据集上与13种方法对比，Flow-CAD在PR AUC、ROC AUC和P@n上取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: Flow-CAD为表格上下文异常检测提供了有效且实用的统一条件生成方案，兼顾表达能力、可扩展性与可解释性。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection in tabular data aims to identify objects whose behavior is unexpected under their contextual conditions. In data-engineering pipelines, this task screens table rows whose measurements violate context-dependent integrity relationships. Most existing approaches rely on either shallow conditional models with limited distributional expressiveness or localized estimators that require per-context modeling, making it difficult to capture complex conditional relationships and scale to high-dimensional settings. We propose Flow-CAD, a new framework for contextual anomaly detection based on Flow Matching. Flow-CAD learns a single global and amortized conditional generative model that flexibly represents behavioral distributions conditioned on context, without assuming simple parametric forms or retraining local models. To enable robust and interpretable anomaly scoring, we introduce a scale-normalized and truncated residual energy derived from the learned conditional flow, which stabilizes anomaly scores, mitigates domination by extreme deviations, and supports feature-wise attribution. We further provide theoretical insights showing that the proposed residual-based anomaly score is stable under velocity estimation error, and that its Monte Carlo estimator satisfies concentration bounds. Experiments on 20 benchmark datasets with 13 competitors demonstrate that Flow-CAD has the best average rank for PR AUC, ROC AUC, and P@n, highlighting its effectiveness and practicality for contextual anomaly detection in tabular data. We provide the source code at https://github.com/ZhongLIFR/FlowCAD.

</details>


### [138] [When can an interpretability claim be trusted? Stability and certification of feature dimensionality, ridge leverage and the feature recovery rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16190)
*Ali Tajfar,Behnam Tajfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏字典"特征恢复"类安全主张的测量稳定性，针对Elhage等人的特征维度与余弦阈值下的特征恢复率(FRR)，刻画其不连续点并给出正则化(ridge)替代与稳定性界，同时证明"特征i被恢复"的可保持性证书；实验仅限玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性工具日益被用来支撑安全声明（如稀疏字典已恢复模型的大部分特征），但此类声明依赖带任意阈值的测量，模型权重微小变动、换训练种子或换阈值都可能使其反转，因此需要量化并修复这些测量的不稳定性。

Method: 在已知真值的玩具模型与合成字典上研究两种测量：特征维度与余弦阈值FRR；精确定位特征维度的不连续点，并以ridge正则化版本替代；推导稳定性界（ridge leverage score变化不超过3√3/(8√λ)倍权重算子范数变化，且该常数不可改进；正则化维度的稳定性常数在ρ=R/√λ尺度下按ρ/4增长并给出显式上下界）；证明判定"特征i被恢复"在给定字典扰动下必须保持的证书。

Result: ridge leverage score的稳定性常数不可改进；正则化维度稳定性常数增长率为ρ/4并有显式界；证书在所有测试中成立且在小扰动下具信息量，但在独立训练的字典间无法非平凡地认证任何特征；标准阈值下，55%的特征其"是否被恢复"的判定随训练种子改变。

Conclusion: 基于任意阈值的特征恢复主张缺乏稳健性，应以具显式稳定性界并加不可改进性常数的正则化度量替代；跨字典的恢复证书通常不可迁移。作者声明证明、代码与文本由AI代理起草并经独立盲审AI与数值测试核查，责任由作者承担。

Abstract: Interpretability tools increasingly support safety claims, such as that a sparse dictionary has recovered most of a model's features. Such claims rest on measurements with arbitrary thresholds, and small changes to the model weights, a different training seed or a different threshold can reverse them. We study two widely used measurements in toy models and synthetic dictionaries with known ground truth: the feature dimensionality of Elhage et al. and the feature recovery rate (FRR) with a cosine threshold. We characterize exactly where feature dimensionality is discontinuous and replace it with a regularized (ridge) version with sharp stability bounds: the ridge leverage score changes by at most $3\sqrt{3}/(8\sqrtλ)$ times the operator-norm change of the weights, and this constant cannot be improved; for the regularized dimensionality the stability constant grows like $ρ/4$ in the scale $ρ=R/\sqrtλ$, with explicit upper and lower bounds. We also prove certificates stating when a claim "feature $i$ is recovered" must survive a given change of the dictionary. The certificates held in every test and are informative for small perturbations, but certify no feature non-trivially across independently trained dictionaries; at the standard threshold, whether a feature counts as recovered changed with the training seed for 55 percent of features. Experiments use toy models only. The proofs, code and text were drafted by AI agents directed by the authors and checked by an independent blind AI reviewer and by numerical tests; the authors take full responsibility for the content.

</details>


### [139] [An Extensive Evaluation of Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches for Sentiment Analysis: Insights from Amazon Customer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16201)
*Muhammed Ihsan Corak,Cuiyuan Wang,Xiaowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对比5种传统机器学习分类器与6种深度学习架构，在568,454条Amazon Fine Food Reviews三分类情感任务上的性能与效率，并给出一个可部署的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台用户生成文本快速增长，需要理解客户意见并支持数据驱动决策；论文旨在系统比较传统机器学习与深度学习在情感分类上的准确率与训练效率，为方法选型提供实证依据。

Method: 数据为568,454条Amazon Fine Food Reviews，三分类情感标签。传统模型（Random Forest、Logistic Regression、Bernoulli NB、Linear SVC、Multinomial NB）使用5,000词以内的BoW与TF-IDF特征；深度学习模型（LSTM、Bi-LSTM、GRU、RNN、Attention、FastText）使用20,000词词表的学习型嵌入。在原始不平衡数据与随机过采样得到的1,070,469条平衡集上评估，指标含准确率、宏平均与各类别精确率/召回率/F1及训练时间。

Result: 平衡集上Random Forest(BoW)达99%准确率，Attention达98.7%；Linear SVC以1分15秒达89%，Logistic Regression以3分37秒达90%。不平衡集上Attention达97.8%，FastText以3分26秒达95.3%，RNN为91.9%但中性类F1仅0.08。深度学习模型跨类别分布更稳定，最优传统模型依赖是否平衡。

Conclusion: 深度学习在类别分布变化下更稳健，传统模型性能高度依赖重采样策略；论文还提供接入Random Forest与Attention模型的用户端应用。局限性：标签由星级评分推导，结果反映的是与评分派生情感类别的一致性而非人工标注情感，且数据来源单一。 

Abstract: The rapid growth of user-generated text on online platforms creates opportunities for understanding customer opinion and supporting data-driven decisions. This study compares five traditional machine learning classifiers (Random Forest, Logistic Regression, Bernoulli Naive Bayes, Linear Support Vector Classifier, and Multinomial Naive Bayes) and six deep learning architectures (LSTM, Bi-LSTM, GRU, RNN, Attention, and FastText) for three-class sentiment classification of 568,454 Amazon Fine Food Reviews. Machine learning models used Bag-of-Words (BoW) and TF-IDF features limited to 5,000 terms; deep learning models used learned embeddings over a 20,000-word vocabulary. Models were evaluated on the original imbalanced data and on a random-oversampled balanced set of 1,070,469 reviews, using accuracy, macro and per-class precision, recall, and F1-score, and training time. On the balanced set, Random Forest with BoW achieved 99\% accuracy and the Attention model achieved 98.7\%. Linear SVC reached 89\% accuracy in 1 min 15 s, and Logistic Regression reached 90\% in 3 min 37 s. On the imbalanced set, the Attention model achieved 97.8\% accuracy, and FastText achieved 95.3\% in 3 min 26 s, while the RNN reached 91.9\% with a neutral-class F1-score of 0.08. Deep learning models were more stable across class distributions, whereas the best classical model depended on balancing. We also report a user-facing application that applies the trained Random Forest and Attention models to new review text. Because the labels are derived from star ratings, the results measure agreement with rating-derived sentiment classes rather than annotated sentiment; the dataset is a single source, and the findings should be read with that limitation in mind.

</details>


### [140] [Optimal feature selection for firewall log analysis using Machine learning and Hybrid Metaheuristic algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16239)
*Seungwoo Han,Gil Hong,Jewan Kim,Jeuk Yu,Sangjun Lee,Byeongok Cho,Jusung Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对UCI防火墙日志数据使用Q学习局部搜索的蜂群优化特征选择(QBSO-FS)配合RF/KNN/Bernoulli NB分类器，并在无泄漏协议下重新评估，选出5–7个特征即可显著提升Macro-F1，RF在去重数据上达99.90%准确率与94.88% Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防火墙日志分类对网络流量监控很重要，但高维特征拖慢分类速度；且早期版本在含12.8%完全重复样本的全数据集上做5折交叉验证并进行特征选择，导致评分乐观偏倚。

Method: 将QBSO-FS（蜂群优化结合Q-learning局部搜索的特征选择）应用于UCI Internet Firewall Data Set，搭配随机森林、KNN、Bernoulli朴素贝叶斯；扩展版采用无泄漏协议：划分训练/验证/测试集，仅用验证集选特征，在未触碰的测试集上一次性测量准确率与宏平均指标，并分别对原始数据与去重预处理数据进行评估。

Result: 在单一分层划分上，QBSO-FS选出5–7个特征；KNN在两份数据集上、RF在去重数据上Macro-F1较全特征提升8–19个百分点；RF（原始数据）与Bernoulli NB的差异仅0.2–1.5个百分点，可能依赖划分。RF用7个特征在去重数据上达99.90%准确率、94.88% Macro-F1，超过复现的先前研究DNN配置及其中一项的PSO特征子集，但在此低维数据上节省的运行时间较小。

Conclusion: 该方法可支撑自动防火墙日志分析，但多划分下的稳健评估仍为未来工作。

Abstract: Firewall log classification is important to monitor network traffic. Machine learning classifiers using network-related features have shown good results for this task, but high-dimensional features slow down classification. In this paper, we apply feature selection using bee swarm optimization with Q-learning-based local search (QBSO-FS) to the UCI Internet Firewall Data Set, together with random forest (RF), k-nearest neighbor (KNN), and Bernoulli naïve Bayes (Bernoulli NB) classifiers. The first version of this preprint selected and evaluated feature subsets on the same 5-fold cross-validation of the full data set, which contains 12.8\% exact duplicates; the reported scores are therefore optimistically biased. In this extended version, we re-evaluate the method with a leakage-free protocol: the data are split into training, validation, and test sets, features are selected using the validation set only, and accuracy and macro-averaged scores are measured once on the untouched test set, for both the raw data and a deduplicated, preprocessed version. On a single stratified split, QBSO-FS selects 5--7 features. The selected subsets clearly improve Macro-F1 over all features for KNN on both data sets and for RF on the deduplicated data (by 8--19 percentage points). For RF on the raw data and for Bernoulli NB, the differences (0.2--1.5 percentage points) may not hold for a different split. RF with 7 selected features reaches 99.90\% accuracy and 94.88\% Macro-F1 on the deduplicated data. On the same split, this exceeds our re-implementations of the deep neural network configurations of previous studies and the feature subset selected by particle swarm optimization in one of them, while the run time saved on this low-dimensional data set is small. These results suggest that the method can support automatic firewall log analysis, while evaluation over multiple splits remains future work.

</details>


### [141] [Few-Shot Cross-Subject Neural--Image Retrieval via Stimulus-Anchored Latent Bias Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16257)
*Xizhang Yao,Li Zhang,Gan Huang,Zhen Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出刺激锚定潜偏置校准（SA-LBC），用同刺激源群体锚点残差均值估计一阶表示偏移，并按源域不确定性收缩，在THINGS-EEG2上提升跨被试神经—图像检索MRR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试神经—图像检索中，将少数刺激匹配的目标观测均值与源域全局均值比较，会把被试特异的表示偏移与刺激构成效应混为一谈，导致偏移估计有偏。

Method: 在固定预处理与冻结的源域训练表示下，以"目标响应减去同刺激源群体锚点"的残差均值估计一阶偏移；再用源域导出的不确定性对校正量做收缩（shrinkage）。在有限刺激总体无放回均匀采样下，推导未收缩锚定估计器与全局均值估计器的精确有限总体MSE表达式（含残差—锚点互协方差），并给出锚定优于全局均值估计的充要条件。实验为THINGS-EEG2上10被试留一被试交叉验证，覆盖6种神经—视觉配置与多种校准预算，并与刺激身份打乱的锚点、无校准对比。

Result: Attention–CLIP ViT-B/32配置下MRR由无校准的0.1867升至M=2时0.2013、M=16时0.2336；六种配置及全部预算下，刺激匹配锚点均优于打乱身份锚点；M=2时收缩在六种配置中均提升MRR，更大预算下效果依赖表示；平移与朝向校正的相对排序依赖正则强度。

Conclusion: 锚定残差可有效分离表示偏移与刺激构成效应，但更低的偏移估计MSE并不保证更高的检索性能；收益具有表示依赖性，校准方法需以检索指标而非仅估计误差为评价依据。

Abstract: In cross-subject neural--image retrieval, comparing the mean of a few stimulus-matched target observations with the global source mean conflates subject-specific representation shifts with stimulus-composition effects. To address this problem, we propose Stimulus-Anchored Latent Bias Calibration (SA-LBC), which estimates the first-order shift under fixed preprocessing and a frozen source-trained representation by averaging residuals between target responses and same-stimulus source-population anchors, then shrinks the correction using source-derived uncertainty. Under uniform sampling without replacement from a finite stimulus population, we derive exact finite-population MSE expressions for the unshrunk anchored and global-mean estimators, including residual--anchor cross-covariance, and establish a necessary and sufficient condition for anchoring to reduce MSE relative to global-mean estimation. In a 10-subject leave-one-subject-out evaluation on THINGS-EEG2, SA-LBC increases mean reciprocal rank (MRR) for the Attention--CLIP ViT-B/32 configuration from 0.1867 without calibration to 0.2013 and 0.2336 with M=2 and M=16 stimulus-level calibration samples, respectively. Across six neural--visual configurations and all calibration budgets, stimulus-matched anchors yield higher MRR than anchors with shuffled stimulus identities, but retrieval gains over no calibration remain representation-dependent. At (M=2), shrinkage improves MRR over the unshrunk anchored estimator in all six configurations, but its effects at larger budgets vary across representations; the relative ranking of translation and orientation corrections also depends on regularization strength. Thus, lower shift-estimation MSE does not guarantee higher retrieval performance. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Xizhang-Yao/SA-LBC.

</details>


### [142] [Rectified Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16265)
*Musheng Li,Jiawei Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Rectified Looped Transformer（RLT），用受流模型启发的线性插值调度重构循环间的反馈方式，在不增加参数与额外块评估的前提下稳定隐状态尺度、降低扰动放大，并在语言建模上取得更低困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 Transformer 通过重复应用共享块提升有效深度、参数高效，但现有方法直接把每个 loop 的输出作为下一 loop 的输入，随循环加深会出现隐状态尺度增长与扰动放大问题。

Method: RLT 重新设计 loop 间的反馈：每个 loop 将当前状态与其预测做线性插值生成下一输入，把状态变化分摊到各 loop，使从初始输入到最终输出的轨迹更平滑。理论上在适当假设下证明状态幅度一致有界并降低最坏情况扰动放大。

Result: 在语言建模语料与下游基准上，相同训练与推理深度下 RLT 困惑度持续低于标准循环 Transformer，对输入扰动与推理深度变化更鲁棒；潜轨迹分析显示各 loop 隐状态范数更稳定，并有利于外推式解码。

Conclusion: RLT 在不增加可训练参数、不增加循环块评估次数的条件下提供了简单且更稳定的循环反馈机制，兼顾效率与稳定性，并支持深度外推解码。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared block, offering a parameter-efficient approach to language modeling. However, most existing works directly reuse each loop's output as the next's input, which can lead to hidden-state scale growth and perturbation amplification as recurrence deepens. We propose Rectified Looped Transformer (RLT), which redesigns feedback between loops using an interpolation schedule inspired by flow-based modeling. At each loop, RLT forms the next input by linearly interpolating between the current state and its prediction. Such interpolation distributes state changes across loops, encouraging a smoother trajectory from the initial input to the final output. Theoretical analysis proves that, under appropriate assumptions, RLT keeps state magnitudes uniformly bounded across loops and reduces the worst-case perturbation amplification. We evaluate RLT on language modeling corpora and downstream benchmarks. At matched training and inference depths, RLT consistently achieves lower perplexity than a standard looped Transformer, while exhibiting greater robustness to input perturbations and variance in inference depth. Consistent with the theoretical results, latent trajectory analysis shows that RLT maintains more stable hidden-state norms across loops, which also facilitates extrapolation-based decoding strategies. Notably, RLT achieves these gains without requiring additional trainable parameters or extra recurrent block evaluations, thus providing a simple yet more stable feedback mechanism for looped Transformers.

</details>


### [143] [LoCoJAX: Host-Local Coordination for Small-Model RL on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16301)
*Loki Chen,Gregory B. Rehm,Yang Song,Hongye Xie,Ming Lei,Chongyang Bai,Bob Kamma,Mingfu Liang,Kavosh Asadi,Yufei Li,Xi Liu,Jay Xu,Rui Liu,Maziar Sanjabi,Luke Simon,Hamed Firooz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析TPU上小语言模型强化学习的协调开销，提出LoCoJAX（本地协调JAX），在每台TPU主机上本地执行rollout与奖励处理并保持全局同步训练，比Pathways基线每步快1.3–3.6倍，并给出解耦模式LoCoJAX-DA的适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL日益面向小语言模型，但Pathways式单控制器执行模型在短计算反复调用时协调开销难以摊薄，需量化主机与网络开销随后优化。

Method: 在TPU v7x上扫描0.6B–405B稠密模型、decode-heavy负载测量开销；提出LoCoJAX：本地rollout与奖励处理+同步全局训练（colocated模式LoCoJAX-CO），并基于Ray实现rollout/训练分置于不同主机的解耦模式LoCoJAX-DA，开展受控对比与placement实验。

Result: 0.6B时主机编排占步时36%、网络2%，大模型则网络开销主导非计算时间；Qwen3-0.6B在8–64芯片三种负载下LoCoJAX-CO每步快1.3–3.6倍，GSM8K上8芯片一步快于Pathways的64芯片；NuminaMath-CoT 256芯片上给rollout 75%主机使步时较CO降约15%。

Conclusion: 小模型RL的瓶颈在主机侧协调开销，本地协调+同步全局训练更高效；解耦重叠仅在rollout主导步时且分得多数主机时才划算。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly targets small language models, and executing these workloads efficiently across multiple accelerators requires effective coordination. On TPUs, Pathways-based RL uses a single controller to coordinate computation across hosts. This execution model is effective for large models, but its coordination computational costs are harder to amortize when small models repeatedly invoke short computations. To characterize these computational costs, we sweep dense models from 0.6B to 405B parameters on TPU v7x under a decode-heavy workload and measure how host and network computational overhead vary with model size. At 0.6B, host orchestration occupies 36% of step time and network communication 2%, whereas for large models, estimated network computational costs dominate the non-compute time. Motivated by these findings, we present Local Coordination JAX (LoCoJAX), which implements local rollout and reward processing on each TPU host with synchronous global training. In controlled comparisons against a tuned and optimized Pathways baseline on Qwen3-0.6B across three workloads and 8-64 TPU v7x chips, its colocated mode (LoCoJAX-CO) is 1.3-3.6x faster per step; on GSM8K, it completes a step faster on 8 chips than Pathways does on 64. Finally, a Ray-managed disaggregated mode (LoCoJAX-DA) overlaps rollout and training on separate hosts, and placement experiments establish when this overlap compensates for dividing devices and transferring weights: only when rollout dominates the step and receives most of the hosts. On NuminaMath-CoT at 256 chips, giving rollout 75% of hosts cuts step time by about 15% compared with LoCoJAX-CO.

</details>


### [144] [StateAlign: Internalizing State-Aware Decision-Making for Reliable Multi-Turn Tool Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16304)
*Zhexi Lu,Subhajit Chaudhury,Tejaswini Pedapati,Keerthiram Murugesan,Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 StateAlign 方法，将任务状态构建与状态对比强化学习结合，使大模型工具调用决策更贴合当前任务状态，在提升任务完成率的同时降低工具幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽提升了大语言模型的工具使用能力，但任务性能提升往往伴随工具幻觉增加。可靠的工具使用要求决策与当前任务状态（可用信息、工具能力、任务进度）保持一致；现有智能体常在本应询问、检索或拒绝不可行请求时直接执行，即使推理已识别出信息缺失。

Method: StateAlign 由两部分组成：(1) 任务状态构建，数据覆盖五类任务条件，并将状态与不同动作需求配对；(2) 状态对比强化学习，以结果奖励引导任务完成，同时在配对状态下对相同生成的推理与动作做 token 级对比，训练出符合当前状态的输出。

Result: 在来自三个模型家族的五个基座模型上，BFCL v4 多轮、ToolSandbox 和 When2Call 均取得提升；消融实验表明状态构建在不同奖励设计下都能改善性能与可靠性；完整方法在三个 Qwen3 规模上均降低工具幻觉。Qwen3-8B 上 BFCL v4 多轮准确率由 45.5% 升至 55.2%，SimpleToolHalluBench 无工具幻觉率由仅结果奖励训练的 62.2% 降至 56.1%。

Conclusion: 行为分析显示模型决策更一致、多调用执行更完整、工具报错后恢复能力更强；状态对齐是兼顾工具使用性能与可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has improved the tool-use capabilities of large language models. However, better task performance can coincide with increased tool hallucination. Reliable tool use requires decisions to follow the current task state: available information, tool capabilities, and task progress. Our analysis shows that agents sometimes execute when they should ask for information, retrieve it, or decline an infeasible request, even when reasoning identifies missing information. We introduce StateAlign, combining task-state construction with state-contrastive reinforcement learning. The data cover five task conditions and pair states with different action requirements. Outcome rewards guide task completion; token-level contrasts compare the same generated reasoning and actions under paired states to train outputs that fit the current state. Experiments across five base models from three families show improvements on BFCL v4 multi-turn, ToolSandbox, and When2Call. Ablations show that state construction improves performance and reliability under different reward designs. The complete method further reduces tool hallucination across three Qwen3 sizes. On Qwen3-8B, StateAlign improves BFCL v4 multi-turn accuracy from 45.5% to 55.2% and reduces no-tool hallucination on SimpleToolHalluBench from 62.2% under outcome-only training to 56.1%. Behavioral analyses show more consistent decisions, more complete multi-call execution, and stronger recovery after tool errors.

</details>


### [145] [Overcoming Spurious Correlations in Conditional Diffusion Models via Doubly Robust Target Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16306)
*Eiichi Sakurai,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DR-Diff 是一个高效微调预训练条件扩散模型的框架，通过函数梯度构造伪目标来纠正训练数据中的伪相关，实现目标分布适配，理论上保证目标风险下降并具备伪双重稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型会记忆训练数据中的伪相关（spurious correlations），导致生成分布偏离理想目标分布。虽然最小化经协变量偏移调整的目标风险可缓解该问题，但针对不同分布从头重训练代价过高。

Method: 提出 DR-Diff 框架，对预训练条件扩散模型进行高效微调：采用函数梯度（functional gradient）方法推导出一个伪目标（pseudo-target），该伪目标刻画了基模型噪声预测与目标分布下真实噪声之间的残差；通过训练模型拟合该伪目标完成目标适配。

Result: 理论上证明 DR-Diff 在总体层面保证目标风险下降，并具备伪双重稳健性（pseudo-doubly robust），对密度比估计误差与基模型误设具有鲁棒性。实验上，在合成数据集上 DR-Diff 的噪声预测精度与从头训练的模型相当，并在下游分类任务中缓解了组间差异。

Conclusion: DR-Diff 以高效微调替代昂贵重训练，在保持噪声预测精度的同时纠正伪相关偏差，并提升下游任务中的群体公平性。

Abstract: While conditional diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generation tasks, they memorize spurious correlations within training data. This causes them to deviate from ideal target distributions. Minimizing a covariate shift-adjusted target risk can address this issue. However, retraining models from scratch for different distributions is computationally prohibitive. To overcome this limitation, we propose DR-Diff, a framework that efficiently fine-tunes pre-trained conditional diffusion models. Specifically, DR-Diff leverages a functional gradient approach to derive a pseudo-target. This pseudo-target captures the residual between the base model's noise prediction and the ground-truth noise under the target distribution. Training the model to fit this pseudo-target enables efficient target adaptation. Theoretically, we prove that DR-Diff guarantees target risk reduction at the population level. It also achieves a pseudo-doubly robust property, providing resilience against density ratio estimation errors and base model misspecifications. Empirically, DR-Diff matches the noise prediction accuracy of models trained from scratch on synthetic datasets. Furthermore, it mitigates inter-group disparities in downstream classification tasks.

</details>


### [146] [Wanda-Guided Sparse Perturbations for Efficient Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16317)
*Ziqing Wen,Xiaohui Song,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Junlin Zeng,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WIS-MeZO，用Wanda权重-激活显著性构建任务条件稀疏扰动掩码以提升零阶微调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化可用仅前向评估实现大模型显存高效微调，但高维随机扰动导致优化效率受限；稀疏扰动可降低搜索维度，因此坐标（支撑集）选择是否包含信息量成为关键问题。

Method: 以Wanda权重-激活显著性构造任务条件稀疏扰动掩码，同时保持预训练模型稠密；并从梯度能量捕获角度分析支撑集质量，将其与梯度估计误差及收敛性建立联系。

Result: 在LLaMA与Qwen（3B至32B）共16个基准上，WIS-MeZO总体得分高于MeZO，并以更少优化步数达到可比的验证损失；与随机及基于幅值的稀疏扰动相比优势明显。

Conclusion: 信息量化的支撑集选择对稀疏零阶微调至关重要，Wanda显著性是一种有效的任务条件坐标选择准则。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models through forward-only evaluations, but high-dimensional random perturbations can limit optimization efficiency. Sparse perturbations reduce the search dimension, making the selection of informative coordinates critical. We propose Wanda-Importance Sparsity-MeZO (WIS-MeZO), which uses Wanda weight--activation saliency to construct task-conditioned sparse perturbation masks while keeping the pretrained model dense. We analyze support quality through gradient energy capture and relate it to gradient estimation and convergence. Across 16 benchmarks with LLaMA and Qwen models ranging from 3B to 32B parameters, WIS-MeZO achieves higher aggregate scores than MeZO and reaches comparable validation losses in fewer optimization steps. Comparisons with random and magnitude-based sparse perturbations further highlight the importance of informative support selection.

</details>


### [147] [Climate Temporal Temperature Prediction via an Adaptive Stacked Bidirectional Residual Mamba Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16322)
*Yongxiang Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep DualMamba——基于自适应双向Mamba编码器与硬件感知并行递归算法的无注意力、无MLP深度学习框架，用于时序温度预测，具线性复杂度并提升推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的温度预测依赖传统气象参数，对气象信息学监测与预报至关重要。现有主导基础模型的Transformer架构在长序列上计算效率低下；而先前的次二次时间模型难以实现长期且稳健的预测。因此需要一个兼顾可扩展性与性能的时序预测框架。

Method: 基于选择性结构化状态空间模型（SSM）构建Mamba增强变体。核心创新有三：(1) 提出自适应双向Mamba作为编码器，学习复杂高效特征；(2) 设计硬件感知的并行递归算法，弥补卷积效率损失，保证大规模实用性；(3) 对Deep DualMamba的逼近能力与收敛性进行理论分析。整体架构不含注意力与MLP，复杂度随序列长度线性增长。

Result: 提出简化且无注意力、无MLP的神经架构，复杂度随序列长度呈线性，推理吞吐显著提升；理论上分析了其逼近能力与收敛性；并基于英国监测站历史气象数据搭建完整预测流程以验证其在气候信息学中的适用性。

Conclusion: Deep DualMamba通过选择性SSM与自适应双向编码器克服了Transformer长序列低效及先前次二次模型长期稳健预测不足的问题，实现线性复杂度、高推理吞吐并具备理论保证，适用于实际气象温度预测任务。注：摘要未给出定量精度指标。

Abstract: Accurate temperatures forecasting relies on traditional meteorological parameters that are essential for monitoring weather informatics and guiding forecasting efforts. This study presents Deep DualMamba, a novel deep learning framework for temporal temperature prediction, built upon an enhanced variant of the recently proposed Mamba network. Unlike conventional Transformer-based architectures, which dominate foundation models but suffer from computational inefficiencies on long sequences, Deep DualMamba leverages selective structured state space models (SSMs) to achieve both scalability and performance. We identify a core limitation of prior subquadratic-time models-namely, their inability to perform long-term and robust forecasting and introduce two key innovations. First, we propose an adaptive bidirectional Mamba as encoders to learn complex and efficient features. Second, we develop a hardware-aware, parallel recurrent algorithm to compensate for the loss of convolutional efficiency, ensuring practical viability at scale. Last, the Deep DualMamba's approximation ability and convergence is theoretical analyzed. These design choices culminate in a simplified, attention- and MLP-free neural architecture that exhibits linear complexity with sequence length and significantly improved inference throughput. To demonstrate its applicability in climate informatics, we implement a full forecasting pipeline using historical meteorological data from UK monitoring stations.

</details>


### [148] [Does Deep Knowledge Tracing Learn Semantic Relations Between Knowledge Components?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16323)
*Kia Karbasi,Mahdi Saiedi,Kevin Hong,Gregory Pottie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验DKT学到的知识组件（KC）关系是否具有语义性：在多种数据集、嵌入模型与九条文本构建流程下，DKT关系图与语义关系结构仅弱且不一致地对齐，注入语义信息也未稳定优于基线，表明DKT关系并非以语义为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DKT以共享隐状态建模交互序列，使一个KC的交互会影响其他KC的预测，但其所学KC关系的性质（是否语义）尚不明确，需系统检验。

Method: 构建九条流程生成的文本派生语义KC表征；实验一比较DKT导出的KC关系图与基于KC表征两两余弦相似度诱导的语义关系结构；实验二将预训练文本表征注入DKT（语义初始化、KC监督微调BERT）；并在ASSISTments 2017上做嵌套样本量的少数据实验。

Result: 两种关系结构在跨数据集、嵌入模型和流程下仅呈弱且不一致的对齐；语义信息注入不能稳定超过标准DKT基线；少数据下语义初始化在9个嵌套样本量中的8个取得更高测试AUC；微调BERT在部分匹配比较中有小幅正AUC差异但稳定性未确立。

Conclusion: DKT学到的KC关系并非以语义为主，文本派生语义结构无法强力恢复DKT关系结构；行为、时间、结构或心理测量信号可能同样塑造这些关系，语义效应有限且不一致。

Abstract: Deep Knowledge Tracing (DKT) is a knowledge tracing (KT) model that updates a shared latent state from sequences of interactions, each pairing a knowledge component (KC) with a response. An interaction with one KC can therefore affect predictions for other KCs. We ask: Are the KC relations learned by DKT semantic? We construct text-derived semantic KC representations using nine pipelines to test whether the conclusions hold across alternative ways of constructing those representations.
  The first test compares DKT-derived KC relation graphs with semantic relation structures induced by pairwise cosine similarities among the KC representations. Across the evaluated datasets, embedding models, and pipelines, the two structures align only weakly and inconsistently. The second test introduces semantic information into DKT; the pretrained text-derived representations do not consistently outperform the standard DKT baseline.
  In the reduced-data experiments, semantic initialization yields a higher test area under the ROC curve (AUC) than the baseline at eight of nine nested sample sizes for ASSISTments 2017. KC-supervised fine-tuned BERT produces small positive AUC differences in some matched comparisons, but the study does not establish their stability across runs. These effects remain limited and inconsistent. The findings suggest that behavioral, temporal, structural, or psychometric signals may also shape DKT's learned relations. DKT-derived relations are not primarily semantic: the tested text-derived semantic structures do not strongly recover the DKT-derived relation structure.

</details>


### [149] [DRAMA: Dissecting Attention Redundancy for Accelerating Multimodal Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16345)
*Yi-Hsin Hung,Fangfu Liu,Shao-Yuan Lo,Chu-Song Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRAMA：基于测量分析多模态扩散大模型注意力冗余的训练-free加速框架，实现平均18.67倍推理加速且精度保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散大模型(dLLM)每步去噪需对长多模态序列做全量前向，视觉token主导序列长度，叠加固定步数预算导致二次方级开销；现有加速方法仅凭孤立注意力可视化隐式刻画冗余，缺乏实测依据。

Method: 系统剖析多模态dLLM注意力冗余：沿空间与时间轴探测注意力张量，揭示两类稳定冗余结构——注意力质量集中于稀疏视觉token子集、注意力分布在初始形成阶段后迅速跨去噪步稳定。据此设计两个互补机制：空间冗余感知的视觉token剪枝(SVTP)与充分性引导的自适应停止(SGAS)。

Result: 在六个视觉-语言基准上无需训练、无架构改动、无注意力缓存，平均实现18.67倍推理加速，同时保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 提出以测量为依据的强加速基线DRAMA，为多模态dLLM推理加速提供实证基础。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text through iterative masked denoising with bidirectional attention, offering a compelling alternative to autoregressive vision-language modeling. However, each denoising step requires a full forward pass over long multimodal sequences in which visual tokens dominate the sequence length, and a fixed step budget further compounds this quadratic per-step cost. Existing inference acceleration approaches for multimodal dLLMs characterize attention redundancy implicitly, motivating designs from isolated attention visualizations rather than from measurement. We close this gap with DRAMA, a training-free acceleration framework grounded in a systematic dissection of attention redundancy in multimodal dLLMs. By probing attention tensors along spatial and temporal axes, we reveal two persistent redundancy structures: (i) attention mass consistently concentrates on a sparse subset of visual tokens, and (ii) attention distributions rapidly stabilize across denoising steps after an initial formation phase. These findings motivate two complementary mechanisms: Spatial-Redundancy-Aware Visual Token Pruning (SVTP) and Sufficiency-Guided Adaptive Stopping (SGAS). Together, DRAMA achieves an average 18.67 times inference speedup across six vision-language benchmarks without training, architectural modification, or attention caching, while maintaining competitive accuracy. Our results establish a strong, measurement-grounded acceleration baseline for multimodal dLLM inference.

</details>


### [150] [MutAtlas: A PDB-Wide Energy-Guided Atlas of Protein Mutation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16361)
*Ruihan Guo,Chaoran Cheng,Zhanghan Ni,Neil He,Bangji Yang,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建PDB全库单点突变增强数据集，统一表征物理能量、蛋白语言模型与逆折叠模型的突变信号，发现模型间存在普遍分歧（反映归纳偏置冲突），并据此提出无监督多源突变偏好蒸馏框架，在无实验标签下于ProteinGym取得最优零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验测得的蛋白突变数据准确但极其稀疏，难以支撑规模化突变效应预测；需要可扩展的补充突变信号，而现有物理模型、语言模型、逆折叠模型的突变偏好是否一致、能否简单融合尚不明确。

Method: 1) 构建PDB全库突变增强数据集，对实验解析结构穷举单点替换，并对齐物理能量模型、蛋白语言模型与逆折叠模型的突变信号；2) 在统一突变偏好表征下进行大规模分析（一致性、集中度、替换模式）；3) 提出无监督多源突变偏好蒸馏框架，基于相对突变偏好学习并显式建模跨源分歧。

Result: 统一表征分析显示各模型突变分布在一致性、集中度和替换模式上差异显著，分歧普遍且源于冲突的归纳偏置而非随机噪声；蒸馏框架在训练中不使用任何实验突变标签，在ProteinGym上总体性能优于所评估的零样本基线与朴素多源融合策略。

Conclusion: 多源突变信号的分歧具有系统性，需显式建模而非简单融合；无监督偏好蒸馏可有效利用多源信号实现竞争性零样本突变效应预测，同时开源数据集与评测流程以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Protein mutation effect prediction is fundamental to protein engineering and disease variant interpretation, yet experimentally measured mutation data remain accurate but extremely sparse. To provide scalable supplementary mutation signals, we construct a PDB-wide mutation augmentation dataset that exhaustively enumerates single-site substitutions on experimentally resolved protein structures and aligns mutation signals from physics-based energy models, protein language models, and inverse folding models. Large-scale analysis under a unified mutation preference representation reveals substantial differences in the consistency, concentration, and substitution patterns of mutation distributions across models, indicating that disagreement is pervasive and reflects conflicting inductive biases rather than random noise. Motivated by these observations, we propose an unsupervised multi-source mutation preference distillation framework that learns from relative mutation preferences while explicitly modeling cross-source disagreement. Without using any experimental mutation labels during training, our approach achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated zero-shot baselines and naive multi-source fusion strategies on ProteinGym. We release the dataset and evaluation pipeline to support reproducible studies of protein mutation effects.

</details>


### [151] [Bait: Mitigating Harmful Agentic Reinforcement Learning via Probing Reward Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16369)
*Tiansheng Huang,Ananth Balashankar,Virat Shejwalkar,Santosh Pande,Ling Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了智能体强化学习即服务（agentic RLaaS）的安全漏洞：仅需约20美元即可通过有害环境的多轮智能体RL完全破坏大模型的安全对齐，并提出通过主动探测奖励函数的防御方法Bait。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的智能体强化学习即服务允许用户以低成本提供环境与奖励函数来微调模型，但此开放接口可能被滥用于有害的强化学习，从而破坏模型的安全对齐，现有研究缺乏对此类攻击及防御的探讨。

Method: 攻击层面：利用服务API提供有毒环境与有害奖励函数，进行多轮agentic RL以削弱模型安全对齐。防御层面：提出Bait，通过主动探测奖励函数来识别并缓解从有害奖励中学习。

Result: 实验表明，约20美元成本即可完全破坏模型（如Inkling）的安全对齐，且降级后的模型可被用于现实危害活动（如软件漏洞利用与代码复用攻击）；Bait能有效缓解从有害奖励函数的学习，同时保留对良性奖励函数的学习能力。

Conclusion: Bait是一种简单有效的防御手段；理论与实证分析表明，即使自适应攻击者已知Bait机制，也因系统性挑战而无法完全攻破该防御。源码已开源。

Abstract: We expose a safety vulnerability on nascent agentic reinforcement-learning-as-a-service. Specifically, with a cost as low as \$20, we show that safety alignment of a large language model (e.g., Inkling) can be completely subverted through rounds of harmful agentic reinforcement learning (agentic RL) with a toxic environment from its users. We also demonstrate that upon a successful harmful agentic RL attack via the service API, the attackers can potentially use the safety-degraded model to assist with real-world harmful activities, e.g., exploit software vulnerabilities and launch code re-use attacks. To mitigate harmful agentic RL, we propose Bait, a defense that mitigates the attack via proactively probing the reward function. Experimental results show that such a simple defense can sufficiently mitigate learning from a harmful reward function and can still learn from a benign reward function that promotes benign agentic tasks. Further analysis on both empirical and theoretical aspects shows that an adaptive attacker who assumes the knowledge of Bait defense cannot perfectly subvert it due to a systematic challenge. Source code is available at https://github.com/git-disl/Bait.

</details>


### [152] [Recursive State-Augmented Language Models: Persistent Latent-State Adaptation of Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16375)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归状态增强语言模型（Recursive SALM），用持续演化的隐状态替代长上下文，使模型在解码器看不到早期证据时仍能进行序列推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型受限于有限的 token 上下文窗口，难以访问早期交互信息；作者希望用一种持续演化的潜在状态来承载历史信息。

Method: 构建读-转移-写（read-transition-write）模块化架构，分离观测编码、潜在动态与预测器适配，并给出确定性/随机性两种形式；实验仅验证确定性版本，冻结 GPT-2 small，通过隐状态门控 LoRA 进行适配，并将早期证据从解码器上下文中移除，仅以 64 维状态传递。

Result: 四轮假设推断任务中，latent-gated LoRA 达 89.1% 准确率，接近全上下文 LoRA 基线 88.0%；将状态置零或打乱后准确率显著下降。两轮、训练中未见码字任务中，多种接口使候选排序优于随机，但按所评估的训练方案很少精确生成目标码字。

Conclusion: 持续的潜在状态条件化适配可在解码器缺失早期证据时支持序列推断，但精确生成能力仍受限。

Abstract: Language models rely on finite token contexts, limiting access to earlier interactions. We introduce Recursive State-Augmented Language Models (Recursive SALM), a framework in which a persistent latent state evolves with each observation and generates adaptations of a pretrained language model. A modular read-transition-write architecture separates observation encoding, latent dynamics, and predictor adaptation. We develop deterministic and stochastic formulations.
  The experiments evaluate only deterministic SALM with frozen GPT-2 small and exclude earlier evidence from its decoder context. On a four-turn hypothesis-inference task, latent-gated LoRA reaches 89.1% accuracy, comparable to 88.0% for a trained full-context LoRA baseline, with earlier evidence available solely through a 64-dimensional state. Zeroing or shuffling the state substantially reduces accuracy. In a two-turn task with codewords unseen in training, several interfaces improve candidate ranking over chance, but rarely generate the exact codeword under the evaluated training schedule. These experiments show that persistent latent-conditioned adaptation can support sequential inference when earlier evidence is absent from the decoder context.

</details>


### [153] [Experience-Handoff Reinforcement Learning for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16409)
*Doyoung Kim,Byeongguk Jeon,Kailin Zhang,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACERL：在固定Agentic Context Engineering脚手架下，用强化学习同时训练单个模型的行动、反思与经验手册（playbook）维护三种角色，使跨轮次传递的经验工件本身也被优化，从而在智能体基准上取得更高留出性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用标量奖励训练LLM智能体丢弃了试错过程中丰富的环境信息（如错误日志）。近期通过反思/压缩等手段构造交接工件（如playbook）把经验传给后续rollout，但这些工件的构造过程本身并未被学习。作者希望兼得两者：既用标量奖励做梯度更新，又利用环境信息传递经验。

Method: 固定ACE（Agentic Context Engineering）脚手架作为测试平台，训练单一模型承担行动生成器、洞察反思器、playbook策划器三种角色；以候选经验交接工件对未来actor rollout的下游影响作为评价信号，在固定脚手架下对角色特定行为进行协同优化（co-optimization）。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上，ACERL的留出（held-out）性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 智能体训练不应只优化当前rollout中的动作，还应优化模型为未来rollout书写的经验，即把经验交接工件纳入学习目标。

Abstract: It is common to train Large Language Models (LLMs) for agentic tasks with Reinforcement Learning (RL) from scalar rewards. However, this throws away the fine-grained information about the environment that the agent experienced during the trial (e.g., error logs). To utilize this rich information, recent works wrap the agent in a harness that refines its own inputs through reflection or compaction and hands past experience to future rollouts, but the construction of the handed-off artifacts (e.g., a playbook) is itself not learned. We want the best of both worlds: gradient updates from a scalar reward, while also utilizing environment information to hand off experience. As a concrete testbed, we fix the Agentic Context Engineering (\ace{}) harness and train one model to act, reflect, and curate task-local playbooks. Our method, \acerl{}, evaluates candidate experience-handoff artifacts by their downstream effect on future actor rollouts, and co-optimizes the role-specific behaviors (action generator, insight reflector, and playbook curator) under a fixed scaffold. Across agentic benchmarks, \acerl{} achieves higher held-out performance than baseline methods. The results suggest that agent training should optimize not only the actions in the current rollout, but also the model-written experience that future rollouts inherit.

</details>


### [154] [SoScale: Second-Order Scale Search for FP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16426)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Hui-Ling Zhen,Weizhe Lin,Xianzhi Yu,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoScale 提出面向分层 FP4 量化的二阶权重尺度码搜索方法，以 Hessian 感知的层重构目标替代逐元素重构误差，并结合 GPTQ 式误差补偿选择尺度码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXFP4、NVFP4、HiF4 等实际 FP4 格式以 4 位浮点载荷搭配分块或分层尺度码，精度对局部分布的缩放方式高度敏感。默认的绝对值最大值（max-abs）缩放虽保留动态范围，却可能把稀缺的 FP4 量化级消耗在稀疏离群区域，导致稠密张量数值分辨率不足；现有尺度搜索方法最小化局部逐元素重构误差，优化的是张量保真度而非层输出保真度。

Method: 在硬件可用的尺度码集合内进行搜索，目标函数改为 Hessian 感知的层重构误差；将尺度码搜索与 GPTQ 风格误差恢复相耦合，按各候选尺度对层输出的影响进行选择。

Result: 在 Qwen3 系列模型上，于 MXFP4、NVFP4、HiF4 三种格式下均一致地降低了量化引起的下游任务性能退化。

Conclusion: 以层输出保真度（二阶信息）为导向的尺度码搜索优于逐元素误差准则，可在不改动硬件格式的前提下提升 FP4 分层量化精度。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization reduces the memory footprint and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), yet accurate FP4 quantization remains challenging. Practical formats such as MXFP4, NVFP4, and HiF4 pair a 4-bit floating-point payload with block-wise or hierarchical scale codes, making accuracy highly sensitive to how each local distribution is scaled. Default maximum-absolute-value scaling preserves dynamic range, but can spend scarce FP4 levels on rarely occupied outlier regions and leave dense tensor values poorly resolved. Existing scale-search methods minimize local element-wise reconstruction error, optimizing tensor fidelity rather than layer-output fidelity. We propose SoScale, a second-order weight scale-code search method for hierarchical FP4 quantization. SoScale searches hardware-valid scale codes under a Hessian-aware layer reconstruction objective and couples this search with GPTQ-style error recovery, selecting scales by their impact on layer outputs. Across Qwen3 models, SoScale consistently reduces downstream quantization degradation under MXFP4, NVFP4, and HiF4.

</details>


### [155] [A Recipe for Physical Action Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16450)
*Shaojiang Wang,Geer Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将机器人模型容量选择、示教预算分配、数据组成、表示、优化与部署决策统一到一个“条件分布目标”下，给出可精确核算风险的理论工具，并在206条公开PushT轨迹上验证：在非对称评价目标下，所选分配可将判据降低4.5–6.2%，而平衡目标下无收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中容量选择与示教预算往往被分别处理，而它们实际与数据组成、表示、优化和部署相互耦合；作者希望找出哪些量能够支持可靠的训练建议，并澄清预测指标（如NLL）与部署表现（如KL）可能不一致的情形。

Method: 以条件分布为共同目标连接各项决策；用嵌套学习oracle进行精确风险核算；借助局部信息几何、条件互信息和经验收敛给出条件性训练处方；在已知目标下做数值检验，考察容量–数据耦合、表示诱导的混合失配，以及NLL下降而部署KL上升的示例；对206条公开PushT轨迹做固定特征下的信息设计判据分析，不拟合策略。

Result: 数值检验揭示容量与数据的耦合、表示引起的混合失配，以及存在部署KL增大而负对数似然减小的反例；在非对称评价目标下，所选分配使判据相对“目标匹配”降低4.5–6.2%，而平衡目标下无收益。

Conclusion: 研究明确了哪些量可作为训练建议的依据，并指出看似有利的预测指标在部署中可能有不同解释，提示需区分预测度量与部署目标。

Abstract: Choosing robot model capacity and demonstration budgets also requires decisions about data composition, representation, optimization, and deployment. We connect these decisions through a common conditional-distribution target. Nested learning oracles give exact risk accounting; local information geometry, conditional mutual information, and empirical convergence provide conditional training prescriptions. Known-target numerical checks expose capacity-data coupling, representation-induced mixture mismatch, and an example where deployment KL increases while negative log-likelihood decreases. An analysis of 206 public PushT trajectories evaluates fixed-feature information-design criteria without fitting a policy. Under asymmetric evaluation targets, the selected allocations reduce this criterion by 4.5-6.2% relative to target matching; balanced targets show no benefit. These results clarify which quantities can support training recommendations and when an apparently favorable predictive metric has a different interpretation.

</details>


### [156] [Helgason-Fourier Random Features for Scalable Hyperbolic Latent Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16461)
*Koshi Watanabe,Daichi Mochihashi,Hideitsu Hino,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机 Helgason–Fourier 特征（RHFF），为双曲空间上的核提供显式有限维特征映射，并据此构建无需数值积分/变分界/辅助变量的双曲潜变量模型 RHFF-LVM，在合成树结构恢复上优于所有基线，首次实现 5×10^5 规模的、非参数化双曲潜变量建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲几何能忠实刻画树状层级结构，且在低维下优于欧氏降维；但双曲空间上的核方法缺乏显式有限维特征表示，导致现有双曲 GP-LVM 依赖数值积分、变分界或辅助变量，计算昂贵、难以扩展到大样本。

Method: 构造 RHFF：从 Helgason–Fourier 变换对应的谱密度中采样频率，得到双曲空间核的显式有限维随机特征映射；刻画该谱密度，使 Matérn 等核可直接在频域指定；并证明 RHFF 的一致逼近误差界。将此表示用于构建 RHFF-LVM，无需数值积分、变分界与辅助变量。

Result: RHFF-LVM 在合成树的度量恢复上比所有基线更忠实；在 Q=2 的树结构数据集上大幅提升，取得最佳平均排名；可在 505 秒内拟合 5×10^5 个点，为首个达到该规模的非参数双曲潜变量模型。

Conclusion: RHFF 为双曲核提供了理论上有保证（谱密度刻画 + 一致逼近界）且计算高效的有限维随机特征表示，使双曲潜变量建模在精度与可扩展性上同时取得突破。

Abstract: Hyperbolic geometry is well suited to embedding hierarchical data, capturing tree-like structure faithfully even in the low dimensions where Euclidean dimensionality reduction is severely constrained. We introduce Random Helgason--Fourier Features (RHFF), an explicit finite-dimensional feature map for kernels on hyperbolic space. We characterize the spectral density from which RHFF frequencies are drawn, allowing kernels such as the Matérn family to be specified directly in the frequency domain. We also prove a uniform approximation error bound for RHFF. Building on this representation, we develop a hyperbolic latent variable model called RHFF-LVM, which requires no numerical quadrature, variational bounds or auxiliary variables unlike previous hyperbolic GP-LVMs. Our RHFF-LVM recovers the metric of a synthetic tree more faithfully than all baselines and attains the best mean rank, driven by large gains on tree-structured atlases at $Q\!=\!2$. Furthermore, it fits $5\times10^{5}$ points in $505$ seconds, making it the first non-parametric hyperbolic LVM to reach this scale.

</details>


### [157] [Learning Temporal Dynamics with Derivative Representations for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16514)
*Shujie Duan,Jia Hong Puah,Haocheng Wu,Yifei Mao,Hongting Zhou,Chenxuan Li,Zitao Fang,Yang Lu,Sim Kuan Goh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeltaTSF 是一种基于离散导数表示的动力学感知时间序列预测框架，在统一、与模型无关的设置下同时学习序列及其时间导数，以保持时间一致性与泰勒一致性，从而提升预测精度与动力学连贯性，且推理无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖架构创新，较少关注显式利用系统动力学时间结构的训练策略；Transformer 等模型仍难以刻画系统动力学的时间演化。

Method: 在输入与预测区间上联合学习时间序列及其离散时间导数，形成统一、模型无关的框架；通过跨时间耦合两种表示以维持时间一致性，并证明该性质在训练中诱导出离散泰勒一致性；无需显式计算导数，推理阶段无额外计算开销。

Result: 在多种基准与骨干架构上的大量实验表明，DeltaTSF 一致优于强 SOTA 方法，并产生动力学上更连贯的预测。

Conclusion: 以导数表示施加动态一致性是一种可与架构设计并行、互补的模型无关范式，能有效提升预测性能。

Abstract: Time series forecasting aims to predict the future evolution of complex systems by learning their underlying dynamics from past observations. Despite recent advances in deep learning, Transformers in particular still struggle to capture the temporal evolution of system dynamics. Existing approaches have been largely driven by architectural innovation, with comparatively little attention paid to training strategies that explicitly exploit the temporal structure governing system dynamics. In this work, we introduce DeltaTSF, a dynamics-aware forecasting framework based on discrete derivative representations of time series. Specifically, DeltaTSF jointly learns the series and their discrete temporal derivatives across both input and forecast horizons within a unified, model-agnostic framework. By coupling these representations over time, the model is encouraged to maintain temporal consistency, a property we show to induce a form of discrete Taylor consistency during training. This formulation enables the model to better reflect the underlying system dynamics, resulting in more stable and physically coherent forecasts. Notably, DeltaTSF does not require explicit derivative computation and introduces no additional computational overhead at inference time. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and backbone architectures demonstrate that DeltaTSF consistently improves forecasting performance and yields more dynamically coherent predictions than strong SOTA methods. These results highlight the effectiveness of enforcing dynamic coherence through derivative representations as a model-agnostic paradigm that operates alongside and complements architectural design. Source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [158] [RESYNC: Residual Latent-State Synchronization for Deadline-Bound Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16523)
*Eljas Linna,Hao Dong,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RESYNC：大模型异步运行，快速路径用新观测把陈旧但精确的潜状态更新到当前时刻，并复用冻结的预测头，从而在严格单观测延迟约束下近似大模型预测；在12个数据集-架构组合中全部优于最优精简/蒸馏方案，1ms到达时至少99.4%按时交付，单CPU线程中位延迟0.35–0.62ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高采样率流式预测要求模型在下一次观测到达前完成推断，大模型精度更高但易错过截止时间；现有精简或蒸馏模型牺牲了教师的性能增益。

Method: 教师大模型异步推断，产出精确但滞后的潜状态；快速路径（updater）仅用该状态计算后新到的观测更新潜状态，并直接应用教师未改动的预测头输出预测。更新器在冻结教师表示上训练，不使用任务标签。

Result: 在1个真实流与2个合成流、5类架构共12个数据-架构对上，RESYNC均以配对95%区间高于零的优势胜过最佳紧凑/蒸馏基线；锚点龄4–12时教师优势显著，龄至32时仍有11对占优。教师增益保留比例在真实流上完整，合成流较低且随锚点龄下降。1ms实时回放中同步大模型无法按时输出，RESYNC在单CPU线程上按时交付≥99.4%，中位延迟0.35–0.62ms，速度与紧凑模型相当或更快，为大模型同步推理的7.9–17.7倍。

Conclusion: RESYNC适用于具备后台算力但对每条观测有严格响应截止时间的场景：以异步教师+快速状态更新器兼顾大模型精度与实时性；局限性在于合成流上随陈旧度增加增益保留衰减。

Abstract: Predicting every observation in a high-rate stream requires a model to finish before the next arrival. A large model may offer better predictions but miss that deadline. We propose RESYNC: the large model runs asynchronously, while a fast path updates its latest exact but stale latent state using the observations received since that state was computed and applies its unchanged prediction head. The updater learns from the frozen model's representations without task labels. Across one real and two synthetic streams and five architecture families, RESYNC beats the best compact or distilled alternative with a paired 95% interval above zero in all twelve dataset--architecture pairs with a material teacher advantage at anchor ages of 4--12 observations, and in eleven pairs at older tested ages up to 32. The share of the teacher's gain preserved remains complete on the real stream but is lower and generally declines with anchor age on the synthetic streams. In live replay with 1 ms arrivals, the synchronous large models cannot deliver any predictions on time while RESYNC serves at least 99.4% of arrivals within the interval, with 0.35--0.62 ms median latency on one CPU thread: around the same speed as or faster than the compact model, and 7.9--17.7$\times$ faster than synchronous large-model inference. RESYNC targets settings with background compute available but a strict per-observation response deadline.

</details>


### [159] [HALight: Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Traffic Signal Control under Lane-Level Missing Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16574)
*Chenkai Hu,Yingqian Zhang,Ming Xie,Yi Fang,Chengxiang Jin,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HALight——面向车道级观测缺失的通信增强异质多智能体强化学习交通信号控制框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制MARL方法多假设智能体同质（统一观测/动作空间）且车道级观测完整可靠；而实际路网存在结构异质性（车道配置、相位设计差异），且传感器故障与检测器覆盖不全导致来车车道数据缺失，破坏协调与学习稳定性。

Method: HALight支持路口特定的观测与动作建模以适配异质路口；引入轻量级单跳邻域通信模块，在车道级观测部分缺失时丰富空间上下文；并采用序贯策略更新（sequential policy update）提升异质智能体间的协调。

Result: 在两个真实路网上的实验表明，HALight在交通效率与收敛行为上优于代表性的同质与异质基线方法；代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: HALight通过异质建模、单跳通信与序贯更新，有效缓解结构异质性与车道级观测缺失带来的协调与稳定性问题，验证了通信增强异质MARL在真实交通信号控制场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning has shown great promise for traffic signal control. However, most existing methods rely on two key assumptions: (i) agents are assumed to be homogeneous with unified observation/action spaces; (ii) full and reliable lane-level traffic observations are available to all agents. In practice, urban road networks exhibit substantial structural heterogeneity (e.g., lane configurations and phase designs), while sensor failures and incomplete detector coverage often result in missing incoming lane measurements. Such issues can severely impair coordination and destabilize learning. To address these challenges, we propose HALight, a communication-enhanced heterogeneous multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for traffic signal control under lane-level missing observations. HALight supports intersection-specific observation and action modeling to accommodate heterogeneous intersections, and incorporates a lightweight one-hop neighborhood communication module to enrich spatial context when lane-level observations are partially unavailable. Moreover, HALight adopts a sequential policy update scheme to improve coordination among heterogeneous agents. Experimental results on two real-world road networks demonstrate that HALight outperforms representative homogeneous and heterogeneous baselines in terms of traffic efficiency and convergence behavior. The code and datasets used in this work are available at https://github.com/Huchenkaii/HALight.

</details>


### [160] [DySR-GNN: Dynamic Sensor-Relationship Graph Neural Network for Root Cause Analysis in Automotive HiL Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16623)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DySR-GNN，一种动态传感器关系图神经网络，用于汽车HiL多变量时序的多标签根因识别；每个时间窗口重建稀疏传感器关系图并用于消息传递与根位置预测，录制级 Macro-F1 达 0.9524。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车HiL系统中故障会跨多个相互作用的信号传播，导致"最异常传感器"未必是真正故障源，传统异常检测/静态图方法难以定位根因，因此需要显式建模窗口级、随状态变化的传感器关系结构。

Method: DySR-GNN：每个传感器信号经共享时序编码器提取时序模式，并与可训练的传感器身份表示结合；在此基础上对每个输入窗口独立重建一张稀疏关系图（动态结构），该窗口专属图用于图消息传递并直接输出多标签根位置预测。评估采用录制不相交（recording-disjoint）协议，与CNN-LSTM、静态GCN、静态GAT及上下文类变体对比。

Result: 在录制不相交协议下优于全部基线，录制级 Macro-F1 = 0.9524、MCC = 0.9302、严格完全匹配准确率 = 0.8519；对学习到的图分析显示传感器变量间同时存在持续性与状态依赖的关系。

Conclusion: 将自适应关系结构提取与时序表示联合学习，可有效提升复杂汽车信息物理系统中的多标签根因识别性能，且学习到的图具有可解释性（持久+状态依赖关系）。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis in automotive Hardware-in-the-Loop (HiL) systems is challenging because faults can propagate across multiple interacting signals, making the most abnormal sensor different from the actual fault origin. This work proposes DySR-GNN, a Dynamic Sensor-Relationship Graph Neural Network for supervised multi-label root-cause identification in multivariate HiL time series. The approach jointly extracts temporal patterns within individual sensor signals and structural relationships across sensors. Each sensor is processed by a shared temporal encoder and combined with a trainable sensor-identity representation, from which a sparse relationship graph is reconstructed independently for every input window. This window-specific structure provides an explicit representation of the sensor relationships used for graph message passing and direct root-location prediction. Under a recording-disjoint evaluation protocol, the proposed model outperforms CNN--LSTM, Static GCN, Static GAT, and context-based variants, achieving a recording-level Macro-F1 of 0.9524, MCC of 0.9302, and strict exact-match accuracy of 0.8519. Analysis of the learned graphs reveals both persistent and state-dependent relationships among sensor variables. The results demonstrate that extracting adaptive relational structure alongside temporal representations can improve multi-label root-cause identification in complex automotive cyber--physical systems.

</details>


### [161] [Asymmetric Multimodal Learning with Decomposed JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16639)
*Yusuf Shihata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明"仅对齐"目标的 Alignment Redundancy Bound：对齐式能量最小化至多保留冗余信息原子，湮没私有模态结构与跨模态协同；提出 Decomposed JEPA (D-JEPA)，将对齐共享核心与私有模态纤维解耦，在信息不对称下避免语义坍缩并提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态对齐被视为多模态表征的标准目标，但当模态间信息不对称（任一模态都无法表达对方所携带的信息）时，对齐作为唯一目标是有损的。以 VQA 为例，问题是对稠密视觉场的选择性关系算子，任何查询都无法恢复其细粒度特征，因此对齐保留的内容并非任务所需。

Method: 1) 理论：证明 Alignment Redundancy Bound，指出仅对齐的 Dirichlet 能量最小化至多保留冗余信息原子 Red(Z;X1,X2)，湮没私有结构 Unq(Z;Xm) 与协同 Syn(Z;X1,X2)。2) 模型：提出 D-JEPA，将潜变量分解为对齐的共享核心与私有模态纤维；共享核心保留对齐所针对的大部分结构，私有纤维通过上下文掩码预测与非对称跨子空间独立性约束恢复被对齐丢弃的信息。

Result: 在信息不对称下，仅对齐训练在组合推理基准上发生灾难性语义坍缩，仅在二值查询上存活；D-JEPA 避免该坍缩，相对仅对齐与"对齐+重建"基线显著提升下游准确率，并以正的多模态协同差取代基线的倒挂差距。信息论审计表明 D-JEPA 恢复了冻结骨干中大部分可用互信息，因果纤维交换干预证明所恢复信息在因果上定位于私有纤维。

Conclusion: 对齐不应作为唯一目标；显式分解共享与私有信息可在信息不对称场景下同时保持可对齐结构与模态特有/协同信息，为多模态表征学习提供理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment is the standard objective for building multimodal representations. We show that alignment as a \emph{sole} objective is lossy wherever modalities are informationally asymmetric---where neither can express what the other carries. Visual question answering is an obvious case: a question is a selective relational operator probing a dense visual field whose fine-grained features no query recovers, so what alignment preserves is not what the task requires. We prove an \textbf{Alignment Redundancy Bound}: alignment-only Dirichlet energy minimization retains at most the redundant information atom $\Red(Z; X_1, X_2)$, annihilating private modality-specific structure $\Unq(Z; X_m)$ and cross-modal synergy $\Syn(Z; X_1, X_2)$.
  We introduce \textbf{Decomposed JEPA (D-JEPA)}, which factorizes latents into an aligned shared core and private modality-specific fibers. The shared core retains most of the structure alignment targets; the private fibers recover what alignment discards, via contextual masked prediction and asymmetric cross-subspace independence. Under information asymmetry, alignment-only training undergoes catastrophic semantic collapse on compositional reasoning benchmarks, surviving only on binary queries. D-JEPA averts this collapse, substantially improving downstream accuracy over both alignment-only and alignment-with-reconstruction baselines, and restoring a positive multimodal synergy gap in place of the baseline's inverted gap. Information-theoretic audits confirm that D-JEPA recovers most of the mutual information available from a frozen backbone, and causal fiber-swap interventions demonstrate that the recovered information is causally localized in the private fibers.

</details>


### [162] [Dataset Distillation via Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16642)
*George Cazenavette,Angelina Quan,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需分类目标的"漂移"式数据集蒸馏方法，使合成样本在特征空间中直接逼近逐类真实数据分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法多以分类器为优化目标，蒸馏数据对真实数据分布的覆盖不足，且受限于特定下游任务的分类性能导向。

Method: 在冻结特征提取器的潜空间中对蒸馏样本进行"漂移"：同时将其推近真实数据、彼此推远，全程不使用分类损失，从而使合成样本直接近似逐类数据分布。

Result: 漂移数据对真实分布的覆盖显著优于现有方法，同时仍是强力的分类训练样本；方法还可作为模型可解释性可视化工具，并可从图像推广到其他模态；提出基于分布的度量作为补充评估指标。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种新的合成式蒸馏范式与分布型评价指标，推进了数据集蒸馏的研究前沿，并有助于理解数据与模型之间的交互关系。

Abstract: The task of dataset distillation concerns the learning of a small set of synthetic samples that can serve as an effective replacement for a large real dataset when used for downstream tasks. While many solutions have been proposed since the problem's inception, most of them primarily seek to optimize the distilled data with respect to a classifier. In this work, we propose a new distillation method that learns a set of samples such that they directly approximate the per-class data distribution. Specifically, we "drift" our distilled data in the latent space of a feature extractor such that they move both toward the real data and away from each other without using a classification objective. We find that our drifted data cover the real distribution significantly better than existing methods while still serving as powerful training samples for classification. Furthermore, we showcase our method's use as a visualization tool for better interpreting models and extend it beyond images to other modalities. Our work advances the frontier of dataset distillation, offering a novel synthesis method and proposing distribution-based metrics as a complementary evaluation. More broadly, our findings help address the growing need to understand both data and models by uncovering more about how they interact.

</details>


### [163] [GNN-Enhanced TCN Algorithms for ECG Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16648)
*Angelica Simonetti,Ferdinando Zanchetta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将时间序列转化为有向图结构，并用融合TCN与GNN能力的几何深度学习模型完成心电图(ECG)信号的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列可被赋予有向图结构，从而为在该场景中使用图神经网络(GNN)提供可能；作者希望把时序卷积网络(TCN)的时序建模能力与GNN的图结构建模能力结合，以更好解决ECG异常检测问题。

Method: 构建一条新的处理流程(pipeline)：先把时间序列表示为有向图，再使用新提出的几何深度学习模型，将普通TCN与GNN的能力进行融合，用于ECG信号的异常检测。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据、评价指标或对比结果。

Conclusion: 作者认为图化时间序列并结合TCN+GNN的几何深度学习模型是解决ECG异常检测的一条可行技术路线。

Abstract: To a time series can be also given the structure of directed graph, opening the doors to the usage of graph neural networks (GNNs) in this context. In this paper we develop a new pipeline involving novel geometric deep learning models that merge the capabilities of ordinary TCNs with the ones of GNNs to solve an anomaly detection problem on electrocardiogram signals.

</details>


### [164] [Manifold Attracting Flow Matching: Physics-Constrained Sampling via Continuous Geometric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16677)
*Guanyu Chen,Jinchen Wang,Shengze Xu,Xingyu Ren,Tieyong Zeng,Antonios Armaou,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Manifold Attracting Flow Matching（MAFM），在推理阶段用连续的 ODE 动力学把采样轨迹解析地吸引到约束流形上，无需重训练生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未见过的等式约束下生成科学数据，既要满足约束，又不能破坏从数据中学到的物理结构。现有推理时方法通过反复修正预测端点来施加约束，这种离散修正会扭曲已学到的生成结构，并产生不稳定的反馈。

Method: 提出 MAFM：维护一个持久锚点，让其沿 ODE 连续演化，动力学解析地将其驱动至约束流形。几何修正保留模型引导的切向运动，同时规定法向运动以将约束违反量收缩至流形，从而将模型跟踪与约束吸引解耦。

Result: 理论上给出了残差收缩与局部反馈稳定性的保证；在几何与偏微分方程基准上，相比多种约束引导基线生成质量更优，同时保持较低的约束残差。

Conclusion: MAFM 提供了一个无需重训练生成模型、通过可控采样动力学协调已学物理结构与显式约束的灵活框架。

Abstract: Generating scientific data under previously unseen equality constraints requires satisfying the prescribed constraints without destroying the physical structure learned from data. Existing inference-time approaches often enforce physical constraints by repeatedly correcting predicted endpoints, but such discrete corrections can distort learned generative structure and introduce unstable feedback. In this work, we propose Manifold Attracting Flow Matching (MAFM), which continuously evolves a persistent anchor through an ODE whose dynamics drive it analytically toward the constraint manifold. Specifically, its geometric correction preserves model-guided tangent motion while prescribing the normal motion to contract constraint violations toward the manifold. This construction separates model tracking from constraint attraction and provides theoretical guarantees for residual contraction and local feedback stabilization. Experiments across geometric and partial differential equation benchmarks demonstrate improved generation quality over variant constraint-guided baselines while maintaining low constraint residuals. MAFM provides a flexible framework for coordinating learned physical structure and explicit constraints through controllable sampling dynamics, without retraining the underlying generative model.

</details>


### [165] [Edge-Gradient Augmentation under Reinforcement Learning for Temporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16678)
*Yongqing Zhou,Jiawei Chen,Wenjun Wang,Xinyu Wang,Wei Yu,Qiyao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EG-RL框架，显式编码边梯度事件（边诞生与消亡），并借助策略驱动的强化学习动态选择信息量最大的边快照以提升时序链路预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序链路预测方法多直接依赖原始时序图数据，忽视了可显著提升预测性能的显式边变化信息（edge-change information）。

Method: EG-RL通过专门的节点编码与链路编码模块显式编码边梯度事件；采用策略驱动的强化学习机制，在critic反馈引导下动态选择并增强最具信息量的边快照；模型由节点编码、链路编码、token mixing、channel mixing等模块组成，经全局池化集成后接两层MLP分类器。

Result: 在多个动态图数据集上，AUC与平均精度（AP）均取得显著提升；消融实验证实自适应边梯度增强的必要性，并验证了所提编码与强化学习机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模边梯度事件并结合强化学习的自适应快照选择，是提升动态图链路预测的有效途径。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is critical for understand- ing dynamic interactions in evolving networks, informing appli- cations ranging from social network analysis to recommendation systems. Existing methods typically rely on raw temporal graph data, often neglecting explicit edge-change information that can significantly improve predictive performance. To address this gap, we propose edge-gradient reinforced learning (EG-RL), a framework that explicitly encodes edge-gradient events (edge births and deaths) through dedicated node and link encoding modules. EG-RL uses a policy-driven reinforcement-learning mechanism to dynamically select and augment the most infor- mative edge snapshots, guided by critic-based feedback to opti- mize prediction accuracy. The model comprises distinct modules for node encoding, link encoding, token mixing, and channel mixing, integrated through global pooling and followed by a two-layer multilayer perceptron classifier. Extensive experiments on multiple dynamic graph datasets demonstrate substantial gains in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve and average precision. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of adaptive edge-gradient augmentation and validate the effectiveness of the proposed encoding and reinforcement- learning mechanisms.

</details>


### [166] [From Data to Wisdom: Rethinking AI Through an Epistemic Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16726)
*Afonso Eduardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"面向AI的数据到智慧认知架构"（EAAI），将数据、信息、知识、理解、智慧界定为五种递归交互的认知功能，并以贝叶斯解释贯穿之，用三个应用案例演示其对建模假设、证据解读与决策论证的结构化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两点动因：其一，知识金字塔（DIKW）的线性顺序式读法掩盖了各认知功能之间的交互关系；其二，对十本贝叶斯参考书的定向综述发现，"先验分布"有严格数学定义，而"先验信息"与"先验知识"未被一致地区分为认知范畴，导致方法论术语含混。

Method: 概念性框架构建：把Data/Information/Knowledge/Understanding/Wisdom分别对应观测、情境解释、建模与预测、批判性评估、知情判断五种认知功能，并给出递归关系；以似然、先验、后验、后验预测、模型检验与决策分析对五个功能作贝叶斯解释（但不把EAAI限定于贝叶斯系统）；将"先验"作认知意义延伸，提出五类影响模型、似然、先验、评估与行动的先验情境类型学；再以定量金融风险、健康险精算建模、医疗报销理赔的智能体AI三个实例展开应用分析。

Result: 三个应用显示EAAI可结构性地分析模型假设、证据解释、验证、依赖关系与决策后果，并能将"风险的预测""可靠性的评判""行动的理由"三者分离。对AI/ML的启示包括：按功能进行评估、把中间模型输出视为可被质疑的证据、将生成与评估相分离；并与DIKW、贝叶斯工作流、AI评估与风险治理研究作了比较。

Conclusion: 贡献为：一套带递归关系的认知词汇表、一种更易理解的贝叶斯方法论读法、先验情境的类型学，以及更清晰的方法论术语体系。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Data-to-Wisdom Epistemic Architecture for AI (EAAI), a conceptual framework treating Data, Information, Knowledge, Understanding and Wisdom as five distinct but recursively interacting epistemic functions: observation, contextual interpretation, modeling and prediction, critical evaluation and informed judgment. Two observations motivate it: sequential readings of the Knowledge Pyramid obscure how these functions interact, and a targeted review of ten Bayesian reference books finds that prior distributions are defined mathematically while "prior information" and "prior knowledge" are not consistently distinguished as epistemic categories. A Bayesian interpretation relates the likelihood, prior and posterior distributions, posterior prediction, model checking and decision analysis to the five functions, without limiting EAAI to Bayesian systems. Extending "prior" in an epistemic sense, the paper distinguishes five kinds of prior context that can shape the model, the likelihood, the prior, evaluation and action. Three worked applications -- quantitative financial risk, actuarial modeling in health insurance and agentic AI for medical reimbursement claims -- show how EAAI structures the analysis of model assumptions, evidence interpretation, validation, dependencies and decision consequences, separating the prediction of a risk, the appraisal of its reliability and the justification of action. It draws implications for AI and machine learning, including evaluation by function, treating intermediate model outputs as evidence open to challenge and separating generation from appraisal, and compares EAAI with work on DIKW, Bayesian workflow, AI evaluation and risk governance. Its contributions are an epistemic vocabulary with recursive relationships, a more accessible reading of Bayesian methodology, a typology of prior context and clearer methodological terminology.

</details>


### [167] [On the Difficulty of Sequence-Level Supervised Learning for Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16736)
*Mathieu Blondel,Michaël E. Sander,Germain Vivier-Ardisson,Tianlin Liu,Vincent Roulet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析在序列级损失中引入推理期解码过程的可能性，证明两类自然构造（贪心解码损失与采样损失）均下界无界（unbounded below）而本质缺陷；并借助自回归 Gumbel 技巧证明采样损失是贪心损失在 Gumbel 扰动下的期望。作者指出病根在于解码序列对模型参数的依赖，进而分析对采样过程施加 stop-gradient 得到的"矩匹配方向"：它不是任何损失函数的梯度，但可证明在函数空间中是自回归负对数似然的合法下降方向；不过该方向方差很大。结论是：对自回归模型的监督学习，teacher forcing 可能"就已足够"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: teacher forcing 下的 token 级交叉熵目标简单高效，但存在 exposure bias——训练（以真实前缀为条件）与推理（以自生成前缀为条件）不匹配。动机是探索能否设计直接嵌入推理期解码程序（贪心解码、自回归采样）的序列级损失函数，从训练目标层面消除该失配。

Method: 理论分析：(1) 构造并分析两类基于解码的序列级损失，证明其下界无界；(2) 用自回归 Gumbel 技巧建立采样损失与贪心损失之间的期望等价关系；(3) 将病理归因于解码序列对参数的依赖性，对采样/解码步骤施加 stop-gradient，得到矩匹配（moment matching）更新方向，并在函数空间分析其性质（是否为某损失的梯度、是否为下降方向）；(4) 对该方向的方差进行讨论。

Result: 两类自然的解码级损失均 unbounded below，因而不是合法的训练目标；采样损失等于 Gumbel 扰动下贪心损失的期望，二者本质同源。stop-gradient 所得的矩匹配方向虽非任何损失函数的梯度，但可证明为自回归负对数似然在函数空间中的有效下降方向（descent direction）；然而该方向方差高，实际优化困难。

Conclusion: 基于推理期解码的序列级损失在理论上存在根本性缺陷（无下界、非梯度、高方差），难以成为 teacher forcing 的实际替代。结论是：对自回归语言模型的监督学习，token 级 teacher forcing 目标可能"已足够"（all you need），exposure bias 的理论顾虑不足以支撑改用解码级损失。

Abstract: During the supervised learning phase, autoregressive language models are typically trained by minimizing the negative log-likelihood of the training data, which decomposes as a sum of token-level cross-entropies via teacher forcing. This objective is simple and efficient but potentially suffers from so-called exposure bias: a mismatch between the way the model is trained and the way it is used at inference time. Can we instead design sequence-level loss functions that incorporate inference-time decoding procedures, such as greedy decoding or autoregressive sampling? We show that two natural attempts lead to loss functions that are fundamentally flawed: unbounded below. We further show that these two flawed loss functions are intimately connected: using autoregressive Gumbel tricks, the sampling based loss is the expected greedy loss under Gumbel perturbations. Since the pathology stems from the dependence of the decoded sequence on the model parameters, we analyze the update direction obtained by applying a stop gradient on the sampling procedure, leading to a moment matching direction. While it is not the gradient of any loss function, we prove that it defines a valid descent direction in function space for the autoregressive negative log-likelihood. Despite these theoretical insights, the moment matching direction suffers from high variance. We conclude that teacher forcing may well be ``all you need'' for supervised learning of autoregressive models.

</details>


### [168] [Interpretable machine learning diagnosis of degradation mechanisms in lead-free $RbSn_{0.5}Ge_{0.5}I_{3}$ solar cells from temperature- and illumination-dependent electrical measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16750)
*Nabanita Sarker,Hafiz Imtiaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文基于约4.2万次SCAPS-1D仿真，对无铅RbSn0.5Ge0.5I3太阳能电池做正向与逆向双向建模：用CuI替代Spiro-OMeTAD使模拟效率由19.6%升至21.6%（主要来自填充因子）；并揭示在因子网格上训练的代理模型会高估精度（网格内R²=0.9997，独立采样仅0.58，改用拉丁超立方设计恢复至R²=0.995）；逆向问题用多标签分类器由温度/光照依赖的Voc识别六种退化机制，宏F1=0.938（物理规则基线0.687），但无法泛化到训练集未出现的机制组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄膜太阳能电池的机器学习优化几乎只做正向预测（参数→性能），而器件制备后识别限速机制的逆向问题仍依赖人工判读表征数据；同时，常规因子网格训练集可能掩盖代理模型的真实泛化能力。作者力图在同一体系上同时检验正、逆两个方向的建模可靠性。

Method: 以RbSn0.5Ge0.5I3无铅钙钛矿电池为对象，构建约42,000个SCAPS-1D仿真数据集；比较Spiro-OMeTAD与CuI空穴传输层；训练XGBoost等代理模型，并对因子设计网格与同等规模拉丁超立方采样两种实验设计的泛化性做对比；逆向任务训练多标签分类器，输入为模拟的温度与光照依赖开路电压曲线，输出六种退化机制是否共存，并以校准的物理规则模型为基线评估。

Result: CuI替代Spiro-OMeTAD使模拟PCE从19.6%提升至21.6%，增益几乎全部来自填充因子。代理模型在留出网格点R²=0.9997，但在独立采样器件上仅0.58，说明网格测试严重高估精度；改用同等规模拉丁超立方设计训练后R²恢复至0.995。逆向多标签分类器宏F1=0.938，显著优于物理规则基线的0.687；但对训练集中未出现的机制组合不具备泛化能力。

Conclusion: 实验设计（而非仅模型选择）决定代理模型的真实精度，网格采样可能造成严重乐观偏差；数据驱动的逆向诊断可高精度识别单一及训练中出现的退化机制，但组合外推仍是瓶颈。研究提示薄膜电池ML工作需报告分布外泛化评估，并将逆向诊断与机制组合覆盖纳入数据设计。

Abstract: Machine learning coupled with device simulation is widely used to optimize thin-film solar cells, but almost exclusively in the forward direction, from device parameters to predicted performance. The inverse problem encountered after fabrication, namely identifying the mechanism that limits a given device, is still addressed by manual inspection of characterization data. In this work, both directions are examined for lead-free $RbSn_{0.5}Ge_{0.5}I_{3}$ solar cells using approximately 42,000 SCAPS-1D simulations. Replacing the Spiro-OMeTAD hole transport layer with CuI increases the simulated power conversion efficiency from 19.6\% to 21.6\%, almost entirely through the fill factor. Surrogate models trained and tested on a factorial parameter grid substantially overstate their predictive accuracy. An XGBoost regressor attains $R^2 = 0.9997$ on held-out grid points but only 0.58 on independently sampled devices, whereas training on a Latin hypercube design of comparable size restores $R^2 = 0.995$. For the inverse problem, a multi-label classifier identifies which of six degradation mechanisms are present from simulated temperature- and illumination-dependent open-circuit-voltage measurements. It achieves a macro $F_1$ score of 0.938, compared to 0.687 for a calibrated physics-rule baseline. The classifier does not, however, generalize to combinations of mechanisms absent from the training set.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond Binary Classification: Multiclass Machine Learning for Diabetes and Prediabetes Risk Prediction Using NHANES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16756)
*Aman Mujahid,Anita Penkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于NHANES 15,356名成人数据构建监督学习框架，将成人分为正常、糖尿病前期、糖尿病三类，并比较二分类设定；结果表明正常vs糖尿病前期F1仅0.62–0.64，合并两类后升至0.73–0.74。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期识别糖尿病风险对阻止疾病进展很重要，但现有机器学习方法多聚焦二分类，难以刻画糖尿病前期等中间风险状态。

Method: 使用NHANES中常规采集的人口学、临床和生活方式变量（15,356名成人），HbA1c仅用于定义标签并排除于输入特征以避免泄漏；比较传统机器学习与Transformer模型，设置多分类及两种二分类（正常vs糖尿病前期、正常vs合并前期+糖尿病），在留出测试集上用precision、sensitivity、specificity和F1评估。

Result: 多分类下各模型表现相近，准确率最高0.72、宏平均F1为0.70；糖尿病前期为最难类别，传统模型F1为0.54–0.56、Transformer为0.53–0.55；二分类正常vs前期F1小幅升至0.62–0.64；合并前期与糖尿病后F1达0.73–0.74。

Conclusion: 正常至糖尿病生物连续谱上的特征重叠比模型架构更决定糖尿病前期检测难度；多分类预测更能反映代谢风险进展但存在临床分层意义与预测精度之间的权衡。

Abstract: Detecting diabetes risk early is important for preventing disease progression. Most existing machine learning approaches focus on binary classification, which limits their ability to capture intermediate risk states such as prediabetes. We develop a supervised learning framework to classify adults as normal, prediabetic, or diabetic using National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey data. The framework uses routinely collected demographic, clinical, and lifestyle variables from 15,356 adults, with glycated hemoglobin (HbA1c) used only to define labels and excluded from input features to prevent leakage. We compare conventional machine learning and transformer-based models and examine two binary formulations: normal versus prediabetes, and normal versus combined prediabetes and diabetes. Models are evaluated on a held-out test set using precision, sensitivity, specificity, and F1 score. In the multiclass setting, performance is broadly consistent across models, with accuracy up to 0.72 and macro-averaged F1 of 0.70. Prediabetes remains the most challenging class, with F1 scores of 0.54 to 0.56 for conventional models and 0.53 to 0.55 for transformers, reflecting its overlap with both neighboring states. When the task is reduced to normal versus prediabetes, F1 scores rise modestly to 0.62 to 0.64. A more substantial improvement is observed under a broader screening formulation combining prediabetes and diabetes, with F1 scores of 0.73 to 0.74 across all models. These findings suggest that feature overlap along the biological continuum between normal and diabetic states contributes more to the difficulty of prediabetes detection than model architecture. Multiclass prediction provides a more clinically informative representation of metabolic risk progression but highlights a trade-off between clinically meaningful stratification and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [170] [Class-Competence Aggregation: Output-Layer Routing for Federated Multiclass DDoS Detection under Class-Concentrated Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16772)
*Tapu Datta,Hafiz Imtiaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文揭示联邦学习中"类集中"非IID数据会使FedAvg输出层中该类的神经元坍缩为零（收到主动反向信号而非简单稀释），并提出按类用客户端本地验证F1加权的CCA方法修复该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式入侵检测传感器上的联邦学习天然产生类集中非IID数据（某一攻击类整体只存在于单个客户端）。作者发现标准FedAvg在这种情形下会以特定且局部化的方式失效——某类聚合权重主要来自从未见过该类的客户端时，其输出层神经元坍缩至零，导致该类完全无法识别（CIC-DDoS2019上LDAP类F1恒为0）。

Method: 提出Class-Competence Aggregation (CCA)：输出层按类聚合，权重为各客户端本地上报的该类验证F1；隐藏层仍采用标准按样本量加权的FedAvg。不依赖服务器端辅助数据，每轮每类仅额外通信一个F1标量。

Result: 主架构CNN-LSTM上，仅CCA即将LDAP F1从0%提升至84.83±1.65%，完整方法达91.64±0.57%宏F1，联邦-集中式差距由-42.53缩至-4.05点。与四种联邦方法在相同划分与种子下对比，CCA是唯一能显著且稳定恢复该集中类的方法；178倍小的MLP呈现相同定性恢复（幅度较弱），说明效应跨架构规模成立。三个种子验证并在CICIDS2017上复现。

Conclusion: 类集中非IID数据通过输出层神经元的反向信号导致FedAvg局部坍缩；以本地类级F1为权重的输出层按类聚合可有效恢复集中类，且成本极低、具有跨架构与跨数据集的可复现性。

Abstract: Federated learning over distributed intrusion-detection sensors naturally produces \emph{class-concentrated} non-IID data, where an entire attack class is held by a single client and absent elsewhere. We show this collapses federated averaging in a specific, localized way: the output layer holds one decision boundary per class, and when a class's aggregation weight comes overwhelmingly from clients that have never seen it, its neuron receives an active anti-signal rather than mere dilution, collapsing to zero. On CIC-DDoS2019, standard FedAvg drives the concentrated LDAP class to $0.00 \pm 0.00\%$ F1 across every seed.
