We propose Class-Competence Aggregation (CCA), which aggregates the output layer per class using each client's own locally reported validation F1 as the weight, while hidden layers remain under standard size-weighted FedAvg. CCA requires no server-held auxiliary data and adds only one F1 scalar per class per round beyond the feature-scaling and class-count exchanges common to any federated pipeline. On our primary CNN-LSTM architecture, CCA alone raises LDAP F1 from $0\%$ to $84.83 \pm 1.65\%$, and the full method reaches $91.64 \pm 0.57\%$ macro F1, closing the federated-to-centralized gap from $-42.53$ to $-4.05$ points. Benchmarked against four other federated methods under identical partitions and seeds, CCA is the only one that recovers the concentrated class substantially and reliably; a $178\times$ smaller MLP shows the same qualitative recovery, though less completely, confirming the effect holds across architecture scale even as its magnitude varies. Results are validated across three seeds and replicated on a second dataset, CICIDS2017.

</details>


### [171] [ScrewSense: Mapping the Invisible - Anomaly Detection and Error Classification in Screwdriving Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16789)
*Tobias Lettner,Tatyana Ivanovska,Anwar Al Assadi,Christian Bergler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将深度学习方法用于拧紧过程的异常检测与多类别错误分类，基于扭矩、转角等多变量时间序列，通过VQ-VAE嵌入空间实现异常检测，并比较四种架构进行错误分类，准确率最高达100%，并验证了泛化性与工业实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拧紧系统产生大量过程数据，加之算力、存储与算法进步，使AI应用成为可能。准确检测与分类拧紧错误对保证产品质量、避免昂贵返工以及支持螺钉连接产品的维护、再利用与回收（可持续性）至关重要；而传统监测方法（如基于窗口的方法、包络曲线）能力有限，机器学习提供了有前景的替代方案。

Method: 数据语料为捕捉扭矩、旋转角度等过程参数的多变量时间序列。异常检测方面，分析向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）的嵌入空间；错误分类方面，评估四种不同的模型架构。此外还引入真实场景数据以检验模型鲁棒性。

Result: 异常检测有效；深度学习模型可区分多种错误类别，突破传统二分类局限，准确率最高达100%；在真实数据上表现出较强鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明模型具有较强泛化能力和实际应用潜力，可用于工业拧紧过程，无需专家知识即可识别错误，显著减少人工投入与分析时间。

Abstract: The growing volume of process data generated by screwdriving systems, combined with advancements in computing power, data storage, and algorithms, is facilitating the application of artificial intelligence in this field. Accurately detecting and classifying screwdriving errors is crucial for ensuring product quality, avoiding costly rework in manufacturing, and supporting sustainability by facilitating the maintenance, reuse, and recycling of screw-connected products. In this context, machine learning approaches offer a promising alternative to traditional monitoring techniques, such as window-based methods or envelope curves. This study examines the use of deep learning techniques for anomaly detection and error classification in screwdriving processes. The data corpus consists of multivariate time series capturing process parameters, such as torque and rotation angle. For anomaly detection, the embedding space of a vector quantized variational autoencoder was analyzed. In terms of error classification, four different model architectures were evaluated. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of anomaly detection and confirm that deep learning models can distinguish between multiple error categories beyond traditional binary classification. This extends the capabilities of conventional monitoring approaches, achieving accuracies of up to 100%. Additionally, real-world data material was examined in order to further demonstrate model robustness. Overall the results indicate strong generalization capability and highlight the practical potential of the developed models for use in industrial screwdriving processes, eliminating the need for expert knowledge in error identification and significantly reducing manual effort and analysis time.

</details>


### [172] [Detection Is Not Early Warning: Evaluating Token-Level Alarms on Language Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16803)
*Marco De Negri,Luca Sartori,Lorenzo Baggi,Moritz Reihs,Eduard Frank Ďurech,Mennatallah El-Assady,Valentina Pyatkin,Anna Hedström*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PREWARN 评估协议，按应用需求校准监测器阈值，并相对标注的故障起点（onset）分别评分告警，用于早期预警任务；在重复性退化案例中显示响应级 AUROC 饱和、等 AUROC 的探针预警行为差异巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型失败往往始于某个特定 token（onset）。现有监测器多用响应级 AUROC 评估，忽略告警发生的时机，无法反映"能否在故障开始前预警并及时中止或干预生成"这一关键能力。

Method: 提出 PREWARN 协议：在验证集上将各监测器阈值校准到应用指定的错误率，再运行监测器，并相对 onset 在三个任务（响应分类、进行中检测、提前预警）上评分告警。案例研究为三个开源模型的重复性退化，主线任务为提前预警，使用激活上的线性探针及六种未训练基线。

Result: 响应级指标在该故障上饱和，仅凭响应长度的规则几乎完美；AUROC 排名相同的探针在 PREWARN 下预警表现差异显著。三个模型中两个的多数退化响应可在 onset 前被探针预警；低秩适配器使探针在 onset 前紧邻 token 中被标记比例翻倍以上，主要源于对退化响应过早告警；未训练基线告警频率与随机接近。

Conclusion: 响应级评估不足以刻画早期预警能力，需以 onset 为基准的时机敏感评估；探针具备跨模型提前预警潜力，但可能以过早告警换取召回，需谨慎解读。作者公开了带校准阈值的探针。

Abstract: Many failures of language models begin at a specific token, the onset. A monitor that reads the model during generation can classify the finished response, detect the failure once it is under way, or warn before it begins, and only a warning leaves time to stop or steer the generation. Monitors are usually evaluated with response-level metrics such as AUROC, which ignore when the monitor fires. We introduce PREWARN, an evaluation protocol for any failure with an annotated onset. It calibrates each monitor's threshold on validation data to an error rate chosen for the application, runs the monitor, and scores its alarms relative to the onset on all three tasks. As a case study we apply it to repetitive degeneration in three open language models, with early warning as the main task, using linear probes on activations and six untrained baselines. Response-level metrics saturate on this failure, and a rule that reads only the response length scores almost perfectly, while probes that AUROC ranks as equal warn very differently under PREWARN. Probes warn before the onset on most degenerate responses of two of the three models, low-rank adapters below the probe more than double the share of tokens just before the onset that it flags, largely by flagging degenerate responses long before the onset, and no untrained baseline warns much more often than random alarms. We release the probes with calibrated thresholds at https://huggingface.co/collections/marcodene/prewarn-degeneration-probes

</details>


### [173] [Calibration and Conformal Prediction for Medical Question Answering in Arabic and English: Evidence from Two Non-Parallel Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16821)
*Mariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与Jais-adapted-7B-chat（均为4-bit量化）在英文MedMCQA（2,816题）和阿拉伯语MedAraBench（4,927题）上的校准性与不确定性表现：两模型均过度自信（ECE 0.242–0.486），单一温度参数可消除82%–93%的校准误差；划分式共形预测在全部24种设置下达到名义覆盖率（误差≤0.006），但低准确率时预测集过大；top-2概率间隔对区分正误最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学考试题评测多以准确率为唯一指标，无法反映模型何时可被信任；且阿拉伯语等非英语医学场景的不确定性研究稀缺，因此需要同时考察校准、共形预测与置信度筛选的跨语言、跨基准表现。

Method: 在英文MedMCQA与阿拉伯语MedAraBench多选题上运行两个开放权重7B模型（4-bit量化），计算原始选项概率的期望校准误差（ECE），采用单参数温度缩放进行事后校准，使用划分式共形预测检验覆盖率并统计90%覆盖率下的预测集大小，并以top-2选项概率间隔的AUROC衡量区分能力；同时对选项字母偏置（字母C先验）与计分方式（仅拉丁字母）做消融。

Result: 准确率：Qwen英文0.561、阿拉伯语0.430；Jais分别为0.369和0.308。原始ECE为0.242–0.486，温度缩放消除82%–93%的校准误差。共形预测在全部24种设置下达到名义覆盖率（偏差≤0.006），90%覆盖率下温度缩放后平均预测集占比依次为63%（Qwen英）、78%（Qwen阿）、86%（Jais英）、89%（Jais阿）。top-2间隔AUROC以Qwen英文最高（0.734）、Jais阿拉伯语最低（0.579）。仅作答最自信的20%题目使Qwen英文错误率由0.439降至0.098。Jais存在强烈的选项C先验（英文49%、阿拉伯语60%）；仅计分拉丁字母将阿拉伯语中C占比降至29%，但准确率、原始ECE与AUROC几乎不变。

Conclusion: 仅凭准确率不足以判断医学问答模型的可信度：温度缩放能高效改善校准，共形预测在形式上可靠但低准确率下实用性受限，置信度筛选可显著降低高风险场景错误。模型存在显著选项位置偏置，且该偏置的修正不影响整体性能指标。由于两基准在学科、难度层级与选项数上不同，跨语言差异不应直接归因于语言本身。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly tested on medical exam questions, but accuracy alone does not show when an answer can be trusted. I study the calibration and uncertainty of two open-weight models, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Jais-adapted-7B-chat (both run in 4-bit precision), on 2,816 English multiple-choice questions from MedMCQA and 4,927 Arabic multiple-choice questions from MedAraBench. Accuracy was 0.561 (English) and 0.430 (Arabic) for Qwen, and 0.369 and 0.308 for Jais. Raw option probabilities were overconfident in all four settings (expected calibration error 0.242 to 0.486), and a single temperature parameter removed 82% to 93% of that error. Split conformal prediction reached its nominal coverage in all 24 settings I tested (within 0.006), but the sets were large when accuracy was low: at 90% coverage, temperature-scaled sets contained on average 63% (Qwen, English), 78% (Qwen, Arabic), 86% (Jais, English) and 89% (Jais, Arabic) of the options. The margin between the top two option probabilities separated correct from incorrect answers best for Qwen on English (AUROC 0.734) and worst for Jais on Arabic (0.579). Answering only the 20% most confident questions lowered Qwen's English error from 0.439 to 0.098. Jais showed a strong prior for option C (49% of English and 60% of Arabic predictions). Scoring Latin letters only cut the Arabic share to 29% but left accuracy, raw ECE and AUROC nearly unchanged. Because the two benchmarks differ in subject, level and number of options, I do not attribute differences between languages to language itself. Code is available at https://github.com/Mariyaaleem/arabic-english-medqa-uncertainty.

</details>


### [174] [DPA: Decentralized Primal Averaging with Quasi-Global Momentum for Highly Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16871)
*Diyako Ghaderyan,Stefan Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出去中心化原始平均算法DPA及单轮通信变体DPA-1G，在稀疏图与高异质性下提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化训练中，智能体本地数据异质性会带来优化困难，尤其在稀疏通信图上显著降低测试精度，需要在保证通信成本的前提下改善异质性下的收敛与精度。

Method: 基于中心化原始平均的进展，DPA将原始平均与本地“准全局”动量缓冲结合，维护优化、梯度评估与输出平均三条耦合序列，并用连续混合迭代构造网络感知的动量方向，无需传输辅助跟踪变量；进一步提出DPA-1G，每轮仅一次通信，通信开销与DSGD类基线相当。对光滑非凸目标给出收敛界，并识别充分多迭代下随智能体数线性加速的条件。

Result: 在GUT去中心化图像分类基准上，跨数据集、架构、拓扑与异质性水平，DPA优于DSGD、梯度跟踪、QG-DSGDm、动量跟踪与QG-GUTm，在高异质性的稀疏环拓扑上增益最大；在相同总通信成本下，DPA两种变体在CIFAR-10/100与ResNet-20上平均测试精度均高于最优基线。

Conclusion: DPA以低通信成本有效缓解数据异质性与稀疏拓扑带来的精度退化，并具备理论收敛保证，代码已公开。

Abstract: Decentralized training of deep learning models keeps training data local and supports on-device learning over networks. In realistic scenarios, heterogeneity across agents' local data poses an optimization challenge and can severely degrade test accuracy, especially on sparse communication graphs. Building on recent advances in centralized primal averaging, we propose Decentralized Primal Averaging (DPA), a decentralized learning algorithm that combines primal averaging with a local quasi-global momentum buffer. DPA maintains three coupled sequences for optimization, gradient evaluation, and output averaging, while using consecutive mixed iterates to construct a network-aware momentum direction without transmitting an auxiliary tracking variable. For smooth nonconvex objectives, we establish a convergence bound for DPA and identify conditions for linear speedup in the number of agents for sufficiently many iterations. We also introduce DPA-1G, a variant that uses one communication round per iteration and matches the per-iteration communication cost of DSGD-style baselines. Experiments following the GUT decentralized image-classification benchmark show that DPA compares favorably with DSGD, gradient tracking, QG-DSGDm, momentum tracking, and QG-GUTm across datasets, architectures, topologies, and heterogeneity levels, with the largest gains under high heterogeneity on sparse rings. At the same total communication cost, both DPA variants achieve higher mean test accuracy than the best baseline on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-20. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [175] [Sustainable Federated Fine-Tuning with LoRA: Environmental Impact and Performance Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16856)
*Pedro Augusto Londe Ribeiro,Rodrigo Moreira,Larissa Ferreira Rodrigues Moreira,Joberto S. B. Martins,Tereza C. Carvalho,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在A40、RTX 6000 Ada与A100上进行联邦LoRA微调，采用2²全因子设计（135M/360M模型×10/100本地步数），发现本地步数贡献了84.8–91.2%的能耗与排放变异；减少本地步数可在大致相当精度下降低6–8倍能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习结合参数高效微调（PEFT）可高效适配大语言模型，但其在不同硬件上的能耗与碳排放成本尚缺乏系统认知，需要量化能耗来源并给出节能配置指引。

Method: 在NVIDIA A40、RTX 6000 Ada、A100三种GPU上开展联邦LoRA微调实验；采用2²全因子设计，因子为模型规模（135M/360M）与本地步数（10/100）；使用CodeCarbon追踪能耗并估算CO₂当量排放；以描述性变异分配分析各因子对能耗与排放变异的贡献。

Result: 本地步数在该区间内解释了84.8–91.2%的能耗与排放变异，为主要影响因素；在A40与RTX 6000 Ada上，较少本地步数的配置达到与多步数配置相当的精度，同时能耗降低约6–8倍，且单位kWh精度更高。

Conclusion: 在所评估的实验设定下，较小的本地训练预算有利于实现高能效的联邦微调；能耗主要由本地步数而非模型规模或硬件类型主导，为绿色联邦LLM微调提供了可操作的配置建议。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) enables efficient adaptation of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its energy and carbon costs across hardware remain poorly understood. We evaluated federated Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning on NVIDIA A40, RTX 6000 Ada, and A100 GPUs using a $2^2$ full factorial design with 135M/360M models and 10/100 local steps. CodeCarbon tracked energy and estimated CO$_2$-equivalent emissions. Descriptive allocation of variation assigned 84.8-91.2% of energy and emissions variation to local steps within these ranges. On A40 and RTX 6000 Ada, configurations with fewer steps achieved comparable accuracy with roughly 6-8-fold lower energy consumption and higher accuracy per kWh. These findings support smaller local budgets for energy-efficient federated fine-tuning within the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [176] [Introducing Feedback Delays to Non-Delayed Adversarial MDPs: Optimal $\sqrt S$ and $\sqrt K$ Dependencies in Tabular and Linear AMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17214)
*Aarush Kulkarni,Khang Nguyen,William Chang,Yan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"引入反馈延迟"技术，配合GRR与访问概率阈值化，在表格与线性AMDP中分别达到$\tilde O(\mathrm{poly}(H)\sqrt{SAK})$与$\tilde O(\mathrm{poly}(d,H)\sqrt K)$ regret，解决了长期开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知转移、bandit反馈的对抗性MDP中，核心困难是估计"变化策略"的Q函数：历史数据来自与当前策略相关但不同的旧策略，而冻结策略以获取数据又会恶化对$K$的依赖。表格情形存在$\sqrt S$间隙（$\tilde O(HS\sqrt{AK})$ vs 下界$\Omega(H\sqrt{SAK})$），线性情形高效算法长期停留在$\tilde O(K^{3/4})$。

Method: 自愿引入反馈延迟：用未来若干回合的数据估计当前策略Q函数，同时继续更新策略，仿佛反馈被延迟；提出Geometric Resampling with Rejection (GRR)处理延迟期间策略漂移带来的偏差；对访问概率做阈值化以避免稀有状态-动作对造成过长延迟。

Result: 高概率下，表格AMDP达到$\tilde O(\mathrm{poly}(H)\sqrt{SAK})$，闭合$\sqrt S$间隙；线性AMDP达到$\tilde O(\mathrm{poly}(d,H)\sqrt K)$，为首个关于$K$最优的高效算法；并可自然推广到总延迟$\mathfrak D$的延迟AMDP，分别获得关于$S,\mathfrak D$（表格）与$K,\mathfrak D$（线性）最优的界。

Conclusion: 反馈延迟通常被视为环境施加的障碍，本文表明其可被主动利用且无额外代价，构成统一且通用的策略优化分析框架，同时解决表格AMDP开放问题并给出线性AMDP首个$K$-最优高效算法。

Abstract: We study regret minimization in episodic Adversarial Markov Decision Processes (AMDPs) with unknown transitions and bandit feedback. In tabular AMDPs with $S$ states, $A$ actions, and horizon $H$, the best known $K$-episode regret is $\tilde O(HS\sqrt{AK})$, whose $\sqrt S$ gap to the $Ω(H\sqrt{SAK})$ lower bound has long remained open. In linear AMDPs with $d$-dimensional features, after a five-year line of improvements starting from $K^{14/15}$, computationally efficient algorithms without a simulator or exploratory assumptions still only attain $\tilde O(K^{3/4})$ regret.
  A common challenge in both settings is to estimate the Q-function of a changing policy: past data comes from earlier policies that differ from (and are correlated with) the current one, while freezing the policy for future episodes worsens the dependency on $K$. We instead propose a general technique called introduced feedback delays: we estimate the Q-function of the policy using future episodes while still updating the policy, as if the feedback were delayed. While feedback delays are usually an obstacle imposed by the environment, here they are introduced voluntarily and, perhaps surprisingly, come at no extra cost. We develop Geometric Resampling with Rejection (GRR) to account for policy changes during the delay, and threshold the visitation probabilities to avoid long delays on rarely visited state-action pairs.
  With this unified technique, our policy optimization algorithms achieve, with high probability, $\tilde O(\text{poly}(H)\sqrt{SAK})$ regret in tabular AMDPs, resolving the open problem; and $\tilde O(\text{poly}(d,H)\sqrt K)$ regret in linear AMDPs, giving the first efficient algorithm optimal in $K$. They also naturally extend to delayed AMDPs with a total feedback delay $\mathfrak D$, yielding the first bounds optimal in $S$ and $\mathfrak D$ (tabular) and in $K$ and $\mathfrak D$ (linear).

</details>


### [177] [Factored One-sided Adam-Moment for Practical and Scalable SOAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16905)
*Jaemyung Yu,Byeongho Heo,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOAM 通过输入侧预条件和因子化 Adam 矩估计，在几乎不增加 AdamW 成本的前提下保留 SOAP 的收敛优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶优化器（Shampoo、SOAP）在 Transformer 训练中每步收敛更快，但实践成本高：SOAP 仍需大量优化器状态内存与特征分解开销。作者追问能否以接近 AdamW 的效率保留 SOAP 的收益。

Method: 提出轻量 SOAP 变体 FOAM，两点改进：(1) 仅做输入侧预条件，丢弃输出侧 Kronecker 因子，从而降低内存并省去一次特征分解；利用 Hessian 的 Kronecker 结构论证在输出侧曲率为对角时该近似是精确的，近对角时几乎无损。(2) 用基于行-列统计的因子化 Adam 矩估计替代 SOAP 的稠密旋转二阶矩；在输出曲率对角的 K-FAC Fisher 假设下，该估计可精确还原 SOAP 的稠密二阶矩。

Result: 实测 K-FAC 违例仅 15–20%，对更新影响可忽略。在 124M–760M 参数 LLM 预训练中，FOAM 的验证损失与强 SOAP 及 Muon 基线相当，优化器状态仅为 SOAP 的一半，实际耗时达 Muon 级别，并在三倍学习率范围内保持稳健。

Conclusion: SOAP 的收益不必以其成本为代价；FOAM 以近 AdamW/Muon 的效率获得 SOAP 级效果。

Abstract: Second-order optimizers (e.g., Shampoo, SOAP) have emerged as compelling alternatives to Adam/AdamW and promise faster per-step convergence than AdamW in transformer training. However, they still remain costly in practice, and even SOAP still pays substantial optimizer-state memory and eigendecomposition overhead. We ask whether the benefits of SOAP can be retained at nearly the efficiency of AdamW. In this paper, we answer yes with a lightweight SOAP variant, Factored One-sided Adam-Moment (FOAM). The path to practical impact is twofold: first, FOAM employs input-only preconditioning, dropping the output-side Kronecker factor to reduce memory and remove one eigendecomposition. The Kronecker structure of the Hessian shows that this is exact for a diagonal output-side curvature and nearly lossless for a near-diagonal one, as we observe empirically. Second, FOAM replaces SOAP's dense rotated second moment with a factored Adam-moment estimator based on row-by-column statistics. Under the K-FAC Fisher assumption with a diagonal output curvature, this estimator exactly recovers SOAP's dense second moment, and we measure only a 15-20% K-FAC violation, with negligible effect on the update. Our empirical results support the claim that FOAM matches strong SOAP and Muon baselines in validation loss across scales of large language model (LLM) pre-training, uses half of SOAP's optimizer state, runs at Muon-class wall-clock time, and retains robustness across a three-fold learning-rate range. The results hold from 124M to 760M parameters, demonstrating that SOAP's gains need not require SOAP's cost. Code: https://github.com/naver-ai/foam

</details>


### [178] [Metal Sandbox: Isolated Execution and Command Scheduling for GPU Coding Agents on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17294)
*Haozhe Wang,Lina Chen,Fanqi Wan,Qi Han,Yinmin Zhang,Zheng Ge,Daxin Jiang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Metal Sandbox 是一套面向 Apple Metal 内核生成与验证的分布式代理执行基础设施，通过受限文件访问、原生命令执行与跨 Mac 命令级调度，提升 GPU 利用率与命令吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式内核开发流程在模型请求、开发命令与 GPU 执行之间交替，导致 rollout 期间 GPU 大量闲置；需要本地 macOS 原生工具与并发代理会话的高效支撑。

Method: 构建 Metal Sandbox：为每个实例提供受限文件访问与私有工作区、原生命令执行；在互联 Mac 间做命令级调度——并行命令并发执行，串行命令每节点共享单一执行槽，符合条件的等待命令可迁移到空闲 Mac 并回传结果；与 Harbor 集成，统一支持任务执行、自动校验与 rollout 采集。

Result: 四台 Mac mini M4 节点在集中需求下命令吞吐提升 1.68×–2.05×；16 条静态代理轨迹显示每节点 4 会话相对 1 会话可获约 2.14× 持续吞吐增益（假设会话持续补充且平均时长不变）；动态调度回放使平均工具等待降低 18.8%、批次总时长降低 22.3%（假设各阶段时长不变）。

Conclusion: 命令级跨节点调度可有效填补代理会话中的 GPU 空闲，显著提升吞吐并缩短等待；系统及 Harbor 集成已开源，但增益基于轨迹回放与稳态假设，尚需更贴近真实波动的验证。

Abstract: On-device inference requires kernels that make effective use of local accelerators. Efficient training and evaluation of models that generate Apple Metal kernels benefit from native macOS tools and concurrent agent sessions. These sessions alternate between model requests, development commands, and GPU execution, leaving GPU capacity idle for much of a rollout. We present Metal Sandbox, infrastructure that combines restricted per-instance file access, native command execution, and command-level scheduling across interconnected Macs. Each agent retains its workspace and private development environment. Parallel commands can run concurrently, while serial commands share one execution slot per node; eligible waiting commands can migrate to available Macs and return results to the original agent. Integration with Harbor supports task execution, automatic verification, and rollout collection through a common workflow. Controlled experiments on four Mac mini M4 nodes achieve $1.68\times$--$2.05\times$ gains in command throughput under concentrated demand. Sixteen static agent traces give an estimated $2.14\times$ sustained-throughput gain for four sessions per node versus one, assuming continuously replenished sessions and unchanged mean durations. Replaying these measured traces under dynamic scheduling reduces mean tool waiting by 18.8% and batch elapsed time by 22.3%, assuming unchanged phase durations. Metal Sandbox and its Harbor integration are open-source on GitHub.

</details>


### [179] [Time Flies When You Parallelise: Pareto-efficient Time Series Foundation Models with Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16934)
*Torben Berndt,Lingyi Yang,Federico Nardi,Yi Heng Lim,AJ Shears,Benjamin Walker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Mamba-3的时间序列基础模型ci-proto，在训练时用分块并行、推理时线性复杂度，并在fev-bench上百任务中取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型零样本预测性能强，但架构效率是核心瓶颈：时序自注意力对时间token数呈二次复杂度，非线性循环状态转移又限制沿时间维的并行计算。

Method: 基于Mamba-3构建ci-proto模型族，利用线性状态循环实现训练期chunk-wise的并行时间计算、推理成本随序列长度线性增长；架构交替进行循环时序处理与跨变量（cross-variate）注意力，支持含past-only与future-known协变量的多变量概率预测。

Result: 在fev-bench的100个预测任务上，ci-proto XL在时间序列基础模型中刷新SOTA；五个ci-proto变体在均缩放分位数损失与平均绝对缩放误差上，相对参数量和运行中位时延均构成完整Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 以线性循环+跨变量注意力的混合架构可在效率与精度上同时占优，为时间序列基础模型提供可扩展的架构范式。

Abstract: Time series foundation models have demonstrated strong zero-shot forecasting performance, but computational efficiency remains a central architectural challenge. Temporal self-attention incurs quadratic cost in the number of temporal tokens, while nonlinear recurrent state transitions restrict parallel computation along the temporal dimension. We introduce ci-proto, a family of time series foundation models based on Mamba-3, whose linear state recurrences support chunk-wise parallel-in-time computation during training, and inference costs that scale linearly with sequence length. The architecture alternates recurrent temporal processing with cross-variate attention, supporting multivariate probabilistic forecasting with past-only and future-known covariates. Across fev-bench's 100 forecasting tasks, ci-proto XL sets a new state of the art among time series foundation models. The five ci-proto variants also make up the entire Pareto frontier for mean scaled quantile loss and mean absolute scaled error against both parameter count and median runtime.

</details>


### [180] [Temporal Regularized Learning: Self-Supervised Learning Local in Space and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17019)
*Davide Wiest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TeReL——一种在空间与时间上均局部化的自监督表征学习规则：基于时间相邻正样本与停止梯度的运行总体统计构造VICReg式逐神经元在线损失，梯度等价于前馈权重的Hebbian更新加每层一个侧向矩阵的anti-Hebbian更新；在MNIST流上batch size=1训练即可超越随机特征与经典SFA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐层自监督目标虽消除了层间反向传播（空间局部），但仍需在样本批次上计算损失，故不具备时间局部性；作者希望为序列输入流设计一种同时满足空间与时间局部性的在线学习规则。

Method: 以时间相邻样本作为正对，使用不参与梯度的运行总体统计量（running population statistics），实例化VICReg风格目标，得到每神经元、有界状态的在线损失；该目标是慢特征分析（SFA）的软化形式，其梯度为前馈权重的Hebbian更新与每层单个侧向矩阵的anti-Hebbian更新。实验采用两层全连接网络、按类别分块的MNIST序列流，batch size为1。

Result: batch size=1训练的网络线性探测准确率96.48%，优于随机特征（95.09%）与经典SFA；放松两项局部性约束的变体性能与有监督反向传播相当；由于训练与推理计算一致，网络可在推理阶段继续从无标签数据学习而不损失精度。

Conclusion: TeReL在小规模网络中验证了时空双重局部自监督学习的可行性，其价值在于生物学合理性与推理期持续学习的可能性，而非与反向传播竞争性能。

Abstract: Layer-local self-supervised objectives remove the backward pass between layers, but almost all still compute their loss over a batch of samples and are therefore local in space but not in time. We introduce Temporal Regularized Learning (TeReL), a spatially and temporally local learning rule for self-supervised representation learning from sequential input streams. The key idea is to instantiate a VICReg-style objective with temporally adjacent positives and non-differentiated running population statistics, yielding a per-neuron online loss with bounded state. The objective is a soft form of Slow Feature Analysis, and its gradient is a Hebbian update of the feedforward weights and an anti-Hebbian update of one lateral matrix per layer. The aim of the experiments is to show that the rule works in a small network, not to compete with backpropagation. On a class-chunked MNIST stream, a two-layer fully connected network trained with batch size one outperforms random features (96.48% versus 95.09% linear-probe accuracy) and classical SFA, and a variant that relaxes both locality constraints is comparable to supervised backpropagation. Because training and inference use the same computation, the network can keep learning from unlabeled data during inference without loss of accuracy.

</details>


### [181] [Capacity-Constrained Delayed Feedback for Online Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17059)
*Bowen Wang,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出容量感知在线保形风险控制（CA-OCRC），联合优化有限跟踪容量分配与预测集构造，在延迟与反馈丢失下仍保持长期风险保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OCRC假设每轮损失即时、无损可得；实际中反馈存在延迟，且有限资源无法跟踪全部待定预测，导致反馈丢失，风险保证失效。

Method: 基于随机化跟踪策略：采样代理延迟以决定是否跟踪当前轮反馈及跟踪时长；采用逆概率加权更新预测集，补偿因延迟与容量限制造成的反馈丢失；分别分析任意延迟与随机延迟下的风险界，并据此优化代理延迟分布，推导跟踪容量的缩放律。

Result: 风险界揭示"获取更多反馈"与"避免容量饱和"之间的权衡；容量缩放律可保证与无限反馈OCRC相同的长期风险；在医学影像、自然语言处理、计算机视觉与无线通信实验中优于现有OCRC机制。

Conclusion: CA-OCRC为延迟、受限反馈场景下的在线不确定性量化提供了具备理论保证且经验有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Online conformal risk control (OCRC) enables reliable uncertainty quantification in machine learning by converting model outputs into prediction sets with long-term risk guarantees. These guarantees, however, hinge on the assumption that the loss of each round is revealed immediately and losslessly. In practice, feedback may be delayed, and limited resources may prevent tracking all pending predictions, causing some feedback to be lost. To address this challenge, we develop capacity-aware OCRC (CA-OCRC), a framework that jointly determines how to allocate limited tracking feedback capacity and how to construct prediction sets. CA-OCRC is based on a randomized tracking policy that samples a proxy delay to decide whether, and for how long, feedback for the current round is tracked. CA-OCRC updates the prediction sets via inverse probability weighting, compensating for the possibility of feedback lost due to delays and limited tracking capacity. We analyze risk bounds for both arbitrary and stochastic delays, revealing a trade-off between receiving more feedback and avoiding capacity saturation. Building on these bounds, we optimize the proxy delay distribution, showing scaling laws for the tracking capacity that ensure the same long-term risk as OCRC with unlimited feedback. Experiments on medical imaging, natural language processing, computer vision, and wireless communications show the effectiveness of CA-OCRC as compared to existing OCRC mechanisms.

</details>


### [182] [LazyWeights: Decode-on-Demand Edge LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17108)
*Arnab Sanyal,Gourav Datta,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LazyWeights 保持 GPTQ/AWQ/llama.cpp 现有量化格式，通过调优 GPTQ 使整数码可压缩、在交织 lane 上无损 Huffman 编码，并在 DRAM 与 L2 之间的小单元按需解码，使 GPU kernel 无需改动，仅搬运编码字节，实现更小的模型体积与更低的延迟/能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上内存决定可运行哪些 LLM 及速度：4 GB Jetson Nano 受容量限制，8 GB Jetson Orin Nano Super 受带宽限制。而现有部署格式（GPTQ、AWQ、llama.cpp 的 K-/I-quant）对所有整数码分配相同比特，尽管码分布极不均匀，存在压缩空间。

Method: 1) 保留现有部署格式，调优 GPTQ 使整数码本身更易压缩；2) 在交织 lane 上对码做无损 Huffman 编码；3) 在 DRAM 与 L2 之间加一个小型解码单元按需解码，GPU kernel 不变，DRAM 只传输编码字节；4) 设计位精确的 0.21 mm² ASIC 解码单元（1 GHz、7 nm），并给出 Huffman 与 rANS 的对比。

Result: 在 1% 与 3.5% 困惑度预算下，Phi-3-mini、Mistral-7B、SmolLM-1.7B 比最佳 llama.cpp 格式小 5.7–31.4%；Orin 上每 token 延迟与能耗分别最多降 36% 与 38%（配合 prompt-lookup 推测解码为 54% 与 44%）；Nano 上比 CPU llama.cpp 每 token 少 58–64% 时间、53–57% 能耗，流式 Mistral-7B 在 3.89 bit/权重下快 6.6×；等吞吐下 Huffman 解码器在码率与面积上优于 rANS；ASIC 解码单元在 Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA（AMD ZCU102）上位精确运行。

Conclusion: 利用量化码的非均匀性进行无损码字压缩，并配合 DRAM–L2 间的轻量解码单元，可在不改变 GPU kernel 与既有格式的前提下同时缓解边缘设备的内存容量与带宽瓶颈，显著降低体积、延迟与能耗；Huffman 在该场景下优于 rANS，方案已在 FPGA 上验证位精确性。

Abstract: Memory decides which LLMs an edge device runs, and how fast: capacity on a $4$ GB Jetson Nano, bandwidth on an $8$ GB Jetson Orin Nano Super. Deployed formats (GPTQ, AWQ, $\texttt{llama.cpp}$'s K- and I-quants) spend equal bits on every integer code of a matrix, although the codes are far from uniform. $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ keeps these formats but tunes GPTQ so the codes compress, Huffman-codes them losslessly in interleaved lanes, and decodes them on demand in a small unit between DRAM and L2, so GPU kernels run unchanged and DRAM moves only coded bytes. At $1\%$ and $3.5\%$ perplexity budgets, coded Phi-3-mini, Mistral-7B and SmolLM-1.7B are $5.7-31.4\%$ smaller than the best $\texttt{llama.cpp}$ format. A bit-exact $0.21$ mm$^2$ ASIC decoder unit ($1$ GHz, $7$ nm) cuts Orin latency and energy per token by up to $36\%$ and $38\%$ ($54\%$ and $44\%$ with prompt-lookup speculation). On the Nano, $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ needs $58-64\%$ less time and $53-57\%$ less energy per token than CPU $\texttt{llama.cpp}$, and runs a streamed Mistral-7B 6.6$\times$ faster at $3.89$ bits per weight. A Huffman decoder beats an rANS decoder in rate and area at equal throughput; the ASIC decoder unit runs bit-exact on a Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA (AMD ZCU102).

</details>


### [183] [Near-Optimal Regret for Oracle-Efficient Adversarial Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17109)
*Kiarash Banihashem,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究带K个动作、N个策略类、可采样i.i.d.上下文的情境赌博问题，提出仅依赖离线代价敏感优化预言机的算法，在任意预先固定的损失序列上取得对最优策略的期望遗憾$O(\sqrt{KT\log(2N)}\log(2KT))$，将先前oracle-efficient界中的$T^{2/3}$改进为最优的$\sqrt{T}$（至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有oracle-efficient情境赌博算法遗憾界为$T^{2/3}$，与统计最优的$\sqrt{T}$存在差距，需要消除这一horizon依赖的次优性，同时保持仅使用离线优化预言机的计算效率。

Method: 用辅助上下文的泊松采样对策略得分做扰动：每个采样上下文下，对动作施加的扰动为该上下文与动作特定尺度乘以Laplace噪声。这些尺度通过最小化由历史观测确定的凸目标来选取，并用投影随机梯度下降近似，仅借助返回最优值的精确离线代价敏感优化预言机实现；由于尺度表可能很大，算法以存储决定各次更新的随机抽样隐式表示SGD迭代，仅在查询上下文处评估尺度。

Result: 期望遗憾为$O(\sqrt{KT\log(2N)}\log(2KT))$，达到该设定下（至多对数因子）的最优率；oracle复杂度及剩余资源开销均为$T$、$K$、$\log(2N)$的多项式。

Conclusion: 通过泊松辅助上下文采样与Laplace尺度扰动、结合凸优化选尺度及隐式SGD实现，可在仅用离线优化预言机的条件下获得接近最优的$\sqrt{T}$遗憾与多项式计算复杂度。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits with $K$ actions, a class of $N$ policies, and i.i.d.\ contexts from a distribution that the learner can sample. For any sequence of loss functions fixed before play, we give an oracle-efficient algorithm with expected regret $O(\sqrt{KT\log(2N)}\,\log(2KT))$ against the best policy in hindsight on the realized contexts. This improves the $T^{2/3}$ dependence on the horizon in prior oracle-efficient bounds and achieves the optimal regret rate in this setting up to logarithmic factors.
  Our algorithm perturbs policy scores using a Poisson sample of auxiliary contexts. At each sampled context, the perturbation assigned to an action is a Laplace noise draw multiplied by a scale specific to that context and action. We show that choosing these scales to minimize a convex objective determined by past observations yields the stated regret bound. We further approximate the minimizing scales by projected stochastic gradient descent using only an exact offline cost-sensitive optimization oracle that returns the optimum value. Since the table of scales can be large, the algorithm represents the SGD iterates implicitly by storing the random draws that determine each update and evaluates the scales only at queried contexts. Both the oracle complexity and the remaining resource bounds are polynomial in $T$, $K$, and $\log(2N)$.

</details>


### [184] [Bellman Contraction under MMD: A Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17116)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在MMD框架下刻画分布式Bellman算子的压缩性：将压缩条件归结为由基核构造的"Bellman差分核"的条件正定性；对径向核借Schoenberg定理联系Bernstein函数，得到任意维、可解析验证或数值检验的判据，并揭示压缩速率与判别力的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式强化学习常需度量分布间距离，MMD因核的灵活性被广泛使用，但何种核能保证Bellman算子（在MMD下）构成压缩算子缺乏统一、可验证的理论判据；同时实践中RBF核在奖励有界时表现良好却缺乏全局压缩性，理论与实践的差距需要解释。

Method: 构造由基核导出的Bellman差分核，证明MMD下的压缩性等价于该核的条件正定性；针对径向核，用Schoenberg定理将条件正定性条件转化为Bernstein函数判据，得到与维数无关、可解析或数值验证的压缩准则；提出"chaining"构造以生成任意指定压缩因子的核；并证明在受限集合上仅需条件正定性即可获得该集合上的压缩。

Result: 统一了Gaussian RBF、power-distance、multiquadric核的已知结论；数值搜索发现11个可诱导压缩的新核；给出chaining构造实现任意压缩因子；证明局部压缩只需局部条件正定性，从而解释奖励有界时RBF核仍有效；识别出压缩速率与分布判别力之间的本质权衡（压缩越快，判别越弱）。

Conclusion: MMD下Bellman算子的压缩性可由核的条件正定性完整刻画，径向核情形可通过Bernstein函数获得维度无关的实用判据；chaining提供构造性工具，局部化结果弥合理论与实践，压缩速率与判别力的权衡则提示核选择需在稳定性与表示能力间折中。

Abstract: We introduce a mathematical framework for studying contraction of the distributional Bellman operator under Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD). We show that contraction reduces to conditional positive definiteness of an associated kernel constructed from the base kernel, which we call the Bellman difference kernel. Focusing on radial kernels, we use Schoenberg's theorem to connect this condition to Bernstein functions. This gives contraction criteria that hold in any dimension and can be verified analytically or tested numerically. Our framework unifies known results for the Gaussian RBF, power-distance, and multiquadric kernels. A numerical search identifies 11 new kernels that induce contraction. We also propose chaining, a general construction that produces kernels inducing contraction with any prescribed factor. To reconcile theory with practice, we show that contraction on a set only requires conditional positive definiteness on that set. This explains why the RBF kernel can remain a valid choice when rewards are bounded, despite its lack of global contraction. Finally, we identify a fundamental tradeoff between contraction rate and discriminative power, where kernels that contract faster tend to discriminate less between distributions. Code is available at https://github.com/BaptisteDebes/BellmanContractionMMD

</details>


### [185] [Using an energy pixels approach to estimate the size a 2nd Generation DEL must be to train a high-resolution binding model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17121)
*Boris P. Belotserkovskii,R. Edward Watts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对DEL命中后机器学习建模所需的训练数据，提出一种无需结构信息、无需合成即可估算"第二代DEL"复杂度的像素/反像素解析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA编码库（DEL）用于发现小分子结合物，如今难点已不在找命中，而在于构建能分辨单原子亲和力变化的ML模型。此类模型需训练数据同时具备两点：命中附近化学空间的密集采样，以及足够数量真正的高亲和配体。朴素DEL取样空间过大，局部覆盖极稀薄、强结合物稀少，导致模型需外推到未见过的更强结合物而失效。

Method: 将结合视为小独立相互作用的加和，即靶点上的"像素"与配体上的"反像素"。仅利用朴素库中实测亲和力的分布，在无结构信息和未合成任何化合物的前提下，估算第二代DEL为提供密集采样和强结合物所需的复杂度与规模。

Result: 方法实现两点：其一，将模型所需强结合物数量换算为文库必须包含的化合物数；其二，估算随机设计的第二代DEL的期望产率，使机器学习或主动学习设计的增益可与基线比较，无需设置与实验本身同等规模的对照库。

Conclusion: 该解析框架以低成本、先验方式为后续药物化学导向的第二代DEL设计提供复杂度与对照基线估算，避免昂贵的大规模对照实验。

Abstract: DNA-encoded libraries (DELs) are commonly used to find small-molecule binders to a target protein. But the hard part is no longer finding a hit. It is building a machine-learning model good enough to guide the medicinal chemistry that follows, one that can resolve the shift in affinity when a chemist changes a single atom. Such a model needs two things from its training data: dense sampling of the small patch of chemical space around the hit, and an adequate number of genuinely tight binders within it. A diverse naive DEL gives neither. It samples an enormous space, so any one neighborhood is covered far too thinly, and the tightest binders are rare. A model trained on that naive DEL data must extrapolate to binders tighter than any it has seen, and that is where models can fail. One way to get training data with both properties is a second-generation DEL built around the hit. We ask how complex that library must be. We treat binding as a sum of small, independent interactions, called pixels on the target and antipixels on the ligand. From the spread of affinities measured in the naive library, with no structural information and nothing yet synthesized, we estimate the complexity such a library must have to supply that dense sampling and those tight binders. The method does two useful things. It turns the number of tight binders a model needs into the number of compounds a library must contain. And it estimates the expected yield of a randomly designed second-generation DEL, so the gain from designing the library with machine learning or active learning can be measured against a baseline, without the expense of a control library as large as the experiment itself.

</details>


### [186] [Paper-Faithful Federated Learning: Scientific Checks for Practitioner and Agent Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17129)
*Ali Dadras,Alp Yurtsever*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedBrew 是一个面向可控、可复现联邦学习实验的研究代码库，通过对实验协议的软件与科学条件进行检查（配置、可识别客户端数据划分、断点恢复、自描述运行记录），使"看起来正常"的结果也能被验证是否真正符合预期协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习实验在实现偏离预期协议时仍可能产出看似合理的结果：配置、数据划分、聚合、随机性、评估或断点恢复中的小错误未必导致程序崩溃，却会悄然影响对比结论。因此需要能对"预期实验协议"本身加以检验的工具。

Method: 提出 FedBrew，以受控仿真为核心，内置多类检查机制：配置检查、可识别的客户端数据划分、断点恢复检查、自描述运行记录，以及开发期的科学条件检查。例如验证客户端划分是否互斥且完备、聚合权重是否符合规定规则；数值与恢复检查则针对客户端模型的非有限状态和被中断的运行。该结构同时为新算法、数据集、模型和任务（无论由研究者还是 AI 编码智能体添加）提供明确的扩展点和自动化检查。

Result: 论文以系统/工具形式呈现 FedBrew，说明其检查机制能够捕获实现与协议不一致的情形，并通过与被报告结果相关联的运行记录，使实验过程可复现、可审查。摘要未给出量化实验指标。

Conclusion: 通过对软件与科学条件的显式检查及配套运行记录，FedBrew 支持可复现、可审查的联邦学习实验，降低"静默错误"污染结果比较的风险。

Abstract: Federated learning experiments can produce reasonable-looking results even when the experiment implementation differs from the intended experimental protocol. Small errors in configuration, data partitioning, aggregation, randomness, evaluation, or checkpoint recovery may therefore affect a comparison without causing the program to fail. We present FedBrew, a research codebase for controlled and reproducible federated learning experiments. Its focus is controlled simulation with checks of specified conditions of the intended experimental protocol. FedBrew uses configuration checks, identifiable client data partitions, checkpoint recovery checks, self-describing run records, and development-time checks of scientific conditions. These checks assess, for example, whether client partitions are disjoint and exhaustive and aggregation weights match the prescribed rule; numerical and recovery checks examine non-finite client model states and interrupted runs. The same structure also provides clear extension points and automated checks for new algorithms, datasets, models, and tasks, whether they are added by researchers or AI coding agents. FedBrew therefore supports reproducible and inspectable FL experiments through checks of specified software and scientific conditions and the run records associated with reported results.

</details>


### [187] [Support-Constrained Offline Policy Improvement for Pediatric ECMO from Limited Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17134)
*Fateme Golivand Darvishvand,Saurabh Mathur,Nikhilesh Prabhakar,Michael A. Skinner,Ameet Soni,Neel Shah,Ethan L Sanford,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出支撑约束的离线强化学习框架FQI-SC，在ECMO儿童数据上兼顾性能与安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童ECMO管理需兼顾短期生理稳定与长期预防神经损伤；标准强化学习易给出高风险、分布外动作，模仿学习只能复现临床医生行为。

Method: 提出Support-Constrained Fitted Q-Iteration (FQI-SC)：将优化限制在经验支撑的状态-动作对上，在低数据区域默认回退到临床基线；并引入统一的Balance Score S(π)量化性能-安全权衡。

Result: 在78条儿科轨迹、超6000小时临床数据上，用FQE和行为分歧评估；FQI-SC的Balance Score较FQI提升38%，估计价值较模仿学习高1.28 FQE单位，与临床医生动作分歧较FQI降低71%。

Conclusion: 支撑约束的离线强化学习可在谨慎改进专家行为的同时显著提升安全-性能平衡。

Abstract: Managing children on ECMO (Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation) requires balancing short-term physiological stability with the long-term goal of preventing neurological injury. Standard reinforcement learning can propose risky, out-of-distribution actions, while imitation learning is limited to reproducing clinician behavior. We introduce Support-Constrained Fitted Q-Iteration (FQI-SC), an offline reinforcement learning framework that cautiously improves upon expert behavior by restricting optimization to empirically supported state-action pairs and defaulting to the clinician baseline in low-data regions. On 78 pediatric trajectories totaling over 6,000 hours of clinical data, we evaluate our framework using Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) and a behavioral disagreement. Additionally, we formalize the performance-safety trade-off by introducing a unified Balance Score ($S(π)$). Our empirical evaluation shows that support-constrained policy improves the Balance Score by 38\% relative to FQI, improves estimated value by 1.28 FQE units over imitation learning, and reduces disagreement with clinician actions by 71\% relative to FQI.

</details>


### [188] [ISLA: A Coordinate-Free, Measure-Weighted Transformer for Boundary-Driven PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17141)
*Peter Sharpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ISLA（Invariant Slice Attention），一种用于稳态、边界驱动 PDE 的切片注意力 Transformer，通过去除坐标输入、采用边界积分与相对几何量，使代理模型对刚体运动、反射、单位与网格细化具有严格等变性/不变性/一致性，并在 AeroZoo 与 AirfRANS 上显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统本应与坐标系原点、朝向、单位及网格无关，但现有空气动力学基准上误差最低的神经代理模型缺乏这些不变性：将训练于单一姿态、单一网格的汽车 PDE 旋转后，表面压力误差从 4% 升至 100% 以上，边界重采样约使误差升至 30%。因此需要设计与物理对称性一致的代理模型。

Method: ISLA 为面向稳态、边界驱动 PDE 的切片注意力 Transformer：任何学习算子均不接收坐标；对边界的所有求和均以数值求积实现；位置仅以到可学习切片锚点的距离与相对角度进入；长度由边界计算出的单位度量；内部点通过树加速的学习核查询边界且相互独立，边界单元即 token，边界读取是精确的。

Result: 在 AeroZoo（2,816 个在形状、尺寸、速度与边界条件上变化的流动）上，其参考尺度归一化的速度与压力平均误差比任一基线至少低 3.1 倍和 3.3 倍；在 AirfRANS 上误差仅次于边界积分模型 GLOBE，且至少比 Transolver 与 GeoTransolver 低 1.6 倍。

Conclusion: ISLA 通过将物理对称性内嵌于架构，实现了对刚体运动与反射的等变性、对声明数据单位的不变性以及网格细化下的一致性，同时保持查询间独立性，在气动 PDE 代理建模中取得领先精度。

Abstract: A foundational principle of physics is that all coordinate systems are equally valid: the behavior of a PDE-governed system does not depend on the origin or orientation of the frame, nor on the units or mesh used to write it down, and neither should a surrogate model. Yet the neural surrogates with the lowest errors on today's aerodynamics benchmarks lack these invariances and fail under simple transformations of the same problem: for models trained on PDEs of cars in one pose (i.e., translation and rotation) and from one mesher, rotating the full PDE raises the surface-pressure error from 4% to more than 100%, and re-sampling its boundary raises it to about 30%. We introduce ISLA (Invariant Slice Attention), a slice-attention transformer for steady, boundary-driven PDEs in which no learned operation receives a coordinate and every sum over the boundary is a quadrature. Positions enter only as distances and relative angles to learned slice anchors, lengths are measured in a unit computed from the boundary, and interior points query the boundary through a tree-accelerated learned kernel without influencing one another. With every boundary cell a token and an exact boundary read, ISLA is equivariant to rigid motions and reflections, invariant to the units of its declared data, and consistent under mesh refinement, while at every setting each query is independent of all other queries. On AeroZoo, 2,816 flows that vary in shape, size, speed, and boundary conditions, its mean reference-scaled velocity and pressure errors are at least 3.1 and 3.3 times lower than any baseline's. On AirfRANS its errors are second only to those of a boundary-integral model (GLOBE) and at least 1.6 times lower than those of Transolver and GeoTransolver.

</details>


### [189] [A Convex-Analytic Reformulation for Poisoning Attacks on Logistic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17143)
*Carina Moreira Costa,Maria Eduarda Pinheiro,Martin Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究ℓ2正则化逻辑回归的数据投毒攻击，利用强凸性与softplus凸共轭得到单条凸最优性间隙不等式和双线性等式约束的精确重构，推导变量界并实验比较两种表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者需在注入固定数量、标签给定的样本并选择其特征时最大化干净训练数据上的逻辑损失，该双层问题非凸难解，需要精确且易求解的重构。

Method: 防守方问题的强凸性经一阶最优性条件给出精确重构；利用softplus的凸共轭，将sigmoid关系替换为单一凸最优性间隙不等式，使攻击目标成为凹函数；变量替换使平稳性条件仿射化，仅保留有界双线性等式为非凸约束；推导所有变量有限界，含系数范数全局界及基于凸优化的预处理紧界；在八个二分类数据集上用SCIP求解评估。

Result: 局部优化所得攻击在多数数据集上降低预测性能；凸分析表述在固定时间限内给出显著更强的对偶界。

Conclusion: 该凸分析重构在保持精确性的同时将非凸性压缩为有界双线性等式，提供更紧的界，是求解ℓ2正则化逻辑回归投毒攻击的有效途径。

Abstract: We study data poisoning attacks on logistic regression with $\ell_2$-regularization, where an attacker chooses the features of a fixed number of injected observations with prescribed labels to maximize the logistic loss on the clean training data. Strong convexity of the defender's problem yields an exact reformulation through its first-order optimality conditions. Using convex conjugacy of the softplus function, we replace the sigmoid relations by a single convex optimality-gap inequality and express the attacker's objective as a concave function. A change of variables makes the stationarity condition affine, leaving bounded bilinear equalities as the only nonconvex constraints. We derive finite bounds on all variables involved in these equalities, including a global bound on the coefficient norm and tighter bounds obtained by preprocessing based on convex optimization. Experiments on eight binary classification datasets evaluate attack effectiveness and compare the two reformulations when solved with \textsf{SCIP}. Attacks obtained by local optimization degrade predictive performance on most datasets, while the convex-analytic formulation produces substantially stronger reported dual bounds within a fixed time limit.

</details>


### [190] [Enhancing Preference Detection using Eye Movement Cascade and Multilayer Combinatorial Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17145)
*Yuanhong Wu,Mary Tsaryk,Sam Watson,Tahsin Ahmed,Christina Schweikert,D. Frank Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用眼动数据预测二选一偏好决策，采用多层组合融合（MCF）框架迭代融合多种机器学习模型，Pre@k由最佳单模型0.90提升至两层融合后的0.9286。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉注意如何影响人类偏好形成是消费者行为与认知科学的基础问题；本文旨在利用眼动模式预测受试者在两选项比较中的偏好决策。

Method: 分析受试者观看成对人脸图像并全程记录注视行为的眼动追踪数据集；使用多种机器学习分类器建模，并应用多层组合融合（MCF）框架，基于认知多样性将模型扩展为大融合池后逐层筛选、迭代融合直至多样性衰减。

Result: MCF显著优于单一模型：最佳单模型Pre@k为0.90，第一层融合后升至0.9143，第二层进一步提升至0.9286。

Conclusion: 第二层融合不仅超过第一层，也优于最佳单模型，表明通过多层融合利用模型多样性可有效提升眼动预测偏好决策的性能。

Abstract: Understanding how visual attention influences human preference formation is a fundamental problem in consumer behavior and cognitive science. In this work, we investigate how eye movement patterns can be used to predict preference decisions when subjects compare two alternatives. To address this problem, we analyze an eye-tracking dataset, where subjects view pairs of face images and their gaze behavior is recorded throughout the decision process. We model this problem using multiple machine learning classifiers and apply the Multi-layer Combinatorial Fusion (MCF) framework to iteratively combine diverse models. The MCF framework expands models to a large pool of model fusions and then reduces them based on cognitive diversity, repeating this process across layers until diversity diminishes. Experimental results show that MCF significantly improves performance over individual models. The best single model achieves a Pre@k of 0.90, which is improved to 0.9143 after the first layer of MCF, and further increased to 0.9286 in the second layer. This progression demonstrates that the second layer not only surpasses the performance of the first layer but also outperforms the best individual model, highlighting the effectiveness of leveraging diversity through multi-layer fusion.

</details>


### [191] [AeroZoo: A Benchmark of Well-Posed Flow Problems for Foundation Models of Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17152)
*Peter Sharpe,Carmelo Gonzales,Corey Adams,Mehdi Ataei,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出AeroZoo基准与训练语料库：2,816个时均稳定样本、22个冻结划分，系统评测几何、外壳与边界条件驱动的PDE代理模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFD神经代理模型的数据集各自只改变一个设计族或一个流场域，无法检验"航空基础模型"的最低要求——对新外形、新外壳在数秒内给出CFD质量不可压流预测。在DrivAerML上，更大模型与更长训练几乎不降低Transolver误差，训练样本翻倍仅降约12%（至400例即耗尽数据），说明需要更严格、更广覆盖的基准。

Method: 构建AeroZoo：3,815个含转捩模型的非定常RANS时均解，覆盖7个几何族、8种外壳配置、Re=10^3~10^8；每个样本以求解器输入形式给出适定PDE问题（按条件类型分组的边界网格、规定值、流体属性）。取2,816个时均稳定样本构成规范基准，含工程指标与22个冻结划分两类任务，划分在几何上互斥，留出几何族、外壳、边界条件布局与Re区间。并引入面向边界驱动PDE的Transformer——ISLA。

Result: 随机划分下，边界条件token使GeoTransolver中位速度误差从入口速度的76.9%降至8.4%（均匀入口流为11.0%，单种子）；ISLA四种子平均达5.3%，约为细化网格带来改善的4倍。同一测试样本上，留出几何族对ISLA误差影响很小（相对均匀流），而留出外壳或边界条件布局则使误差上升2.4~9.2倍。

Conclusion: AeroZoo揭示：当前模型对几何族泛化较好，但对新外壳与边界条件布局高度敏感，是主要失效来源。数据与评分代码将公开，为该方向提供可复现、抗过拟合的评测标准。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for computational fluid dynamics (CFD) are trained and ranked on datasets that each vary one design family in one flow domain. Such datasets cannot test the least that an aerodynamic foundation model must do: predict CFD-quality incompressible flow in seconds for new shapes in new enclosures. On DrivAerML, larger models and longer training barely lower Transolver's error; doubling the training cases cuts it by about 12% up to 400 cases, exhausting the dataset. We introduce AeroZoo, a benchmark and training corpus of 3,815 time-mean solutions to the unsteady Reynolds-averaged Navier--Stokes (RANS) equations with a transition model, across seven geometry families, eight enclosure configurations and Reynolds numbers from $10^3$ to $10^8$. Each sample poses a well-posed PDE problem in the form a solver receives it: the boundary meshes grouped by condition type, the prescribed values and the fluid properties. The 2,816 samples with a stable time mean form the canonical benchmark: two tasks, engineering metrics and 22 frozen splits, disjoint in geometry, that hold out geometry families, enclosures, boundary-condition layouts and Reynolds ranges. On the random split, boundary-condition tokens cut GeoTransolver's median velocity error from 76.9% to 8.4% of the inlet speed in single-seed runs (uniform inlet flow: 11.0%). ISLA, a transformer for boundary-driven PDEs, reaches 5.3% over four seeds, about 4 times the change from a finer mesh. On the same test samples, a held-out geometry family barely changes ISLA's error relative to uniform flow, and a held-out enclosure or boundary-condition layout raises it 2.4 to 9.2 times. The data and the scoring code will be released.

</details>


### [192] [Symmetry in Time: A Representation Theory of Linear Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17162)
*Anif N. Shikder,Ramit Dey,Jan Minac,Lyle E. Muller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用表示理论形式化分析了状态空间模型(SSM)的状态跟踪能力，指出对角化实现只能跟踪交换群，并提出加入四元数通道的HyperSSM，保持线性时间推理与并行训练，可精确求解S5等群任务并具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSM以对角递推换取并行扫描效率，但各通道乘数可交换，导致状态只记录"哪些输入出现过"而非其顺序，即状态跟踪失败。作者希望从理论上解释这一缺陷的根源，并给出可修复的方案。

Method: 将状态更新视为输入序列幺半群的线性表示：SSM在任意长度上跟踪某群，当且仅当该群是转移矩阵所生成群的商群。由此推出实值/复值对角SSM只能跟踪交换群；任何能跟踪非交换群的SSM，其转移矩阵必须在实数、复数、四元数上分裂为独立块。基于此提出HyperSSM，在保持线性时间推理和并行训练的前提下引入四元数通道。

Result: 仅在最终答案监督下训练，HyperSSM用至多5个状态数即精确求解近期状态跟踪基准中的群任务，包括此前SSM仅达随机水平的、含32个生成元的S5，并能泛化到比训练长度长25倍的序列。理论还指明转移矩阵必须编码的内容，使线性递推的学得权重可直接解释，作者据此恢复了真实陀螺仪标定、不完美量子比特门的旋转以及蛋白质骨架的几何结构。

Conclusion: SSM的状态跟踪瓶颈源于对角递推的可交换性，四元数化是理论上有依据且实践上有效的解法；同时该理论赋予模型可解释性，可直接从权重读出物理与几何信息。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs) are an emerging alternative to Transformers. SSMs offer linear-time inference and parallel training, but most implementations make the parallel scan efficient by making the recurrence diagonal: each channel of the hidden state is multiplied by an input-dependent real or complex number. These numbers commute, so the state records which inputs occurred but not their order. We formalize this with representation theory, which explains and fixes the state-tracking failures previously reported for SSMs. The state update defines a linear representation of the monoid of input sequences, and we show that an SSM tracks a group at every sequence length exactly when that group is a quotient of the group generated by its transition matrices. Two consequences follow. Diagonal real- and complex-valued SSMs can track only commutative groups, while the transition matrices of any SSM that can track a group must split into independent blocks over the real, complex, and quaternion numbers. We then introduce HyperSSM, which adds quaternion channels while keeping linear-time inference and parallel training. Trained only on final answers, HyperSSM solves the group tasks of recent state-tracking benchmarks exactly with at most five state numbers. This includes $S_5$ with 32 generators, where previous SSMs are at chance, and HyperSSM generalizes to sequences 25 times longer than those seen in training. Because the theory says what the transitions must encode, the learned weights of the linear recurrence can be interpreted directly. We demonstrate this by recovering the calibration of a real gyroscope, the rotations of imperfect qubit gates and the geometry of protein backbones.

</details>


### [193] [Maximizing Capacity Utilization of LoRA Adapters for Mitigating the Learning-Forgetting Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17191)
*Tomohiro Sawada,Kartik Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出 LoRA 适配器的有效秩远低于配置秩，导致容量未被利用并造成学习-遗忘权衡；提出无 SVD、可微的 Rényi-2 有效秩正则项，提升有效秩并改善困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 在异构与持续学习场景下常不及全参数微调，存在明显遗忘与学习-遗忘权衡。作者将原因归为适配器未实现配置秩所提供的容量：标准 LoRA 更新的有效秩远低于设定秩，更新能量分布受限。

Method: 在原始语言建模目标上增加一个简单、稳定、可微且无需 SVD 的 Rényi-2 有效秩正则项，在优化原目标的同时最大化适配器有效秩；并将容量最大化与范数约束更新建立联系。

Result: 在三个模型、多随机种子、混合域秩扫描中，λ=0.01 使所有秩≥16 的 Wiki 困惑度均值下降；除一个模型-秩组合外，训练困惑度改善或上升不超过 0.02。单任务与调度实验显示学习-保持平衡依配置而变；多种 LoRA 变体同样存在该容量问题。

Conclusion: 有效秩不仅是适配器质量的事后诊断指标，更可作为可训练目标以改进学习-遗忘前沿；该方法为 LoRA 类参数高效微调提供互补的改进维度。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) often underperforms full fine-tuning, especially in heterogeneous and continual-learning settings in which substantial forgetting occurs. In general, it suffers from an undesirable learning-forgetting trade-off across diverse fine-tuning settings. We identify a potential contributor: LoRA adapters, especially high-rank ones, frequently fail to realize the available capacity afforded by the configured rank. Specifically, we find that standard LoRA updates have effective rank far below their configured rank, limiting how broadly the update energy is distributed. Therefore, we propose training mechanisms that optimize the original objective while maximizing adapter effective rank. Our main method adds a simple, stable, differentiable, and SVD-free Rényi-2 effective-rank regularizer to the language-modeling loss. We also connect capacity maximization to norm-bounded updates. In mixed-domain rank sweeps on three models evaluated with multiple random seeds, a penalty weight of $λ=0.01$ lowers mean Wiki perplexity at every tested rank of at least $16$ relative to unpenalized LoRA. Mean training perplexity improves or increases by at most $0.02$ in all but one model-rank comparison. Single-task and scheduling experiments further show that the balance between learning and retention depends on the configuration. We also observe that several LoRA variants in the literature also suffer from the identified capacity issue, and our approach provides a complementary axis of improvement to LoRA-based parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These results suggest that effective rank is not only a post-hoc diagnostic of adapter quality, but a trainable objective for improving the learning-forgetting frontier. Our code is available at https://github.com/tomohiro-sawada/lora-capacity-utilization

</details>


### [194] [Physically Interpretable Basin Selection in Discrete-Variable Origami Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17196)
*Lucy Liu,Leon M. Kamp,Katia Bertoldi,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在折纸启发的离散铰链设计中，连续松弛优化存在巨大"离散化鸿沟"；简单的初始化先验或直接惩罚物理量（frustration/floppiness）可显著提升最终离散解的求解成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动微分推动了连续变量机械设计，但梯度优化难以处理离散变量。标准做法是用连续松弛优化、最后取最近离散设计，然而该流程为何落入不利盆地缺乏理解，导致松弛优化能求解但离散化后失败率高。

Method: 构建可解释的折纸式设计问题：每张折纸几何固定，用双层优化框架自动微分调优每个折痕处四种离散铰链类型的选择。提出两种改进：(1) 向刚性、平坦铰链选项均匀偏置的初始化先验；(2) 在外层损失中直接惩罚 frustration 与 floppiness。

Result: 基线松弛优化97%运行成功，但最终离散解仅约49%成功。初始化先验将求解率从49%提升至69%（零额外算力、不改变优化过程），且产物几何兼容性与刚度更优；直接惩罚物理量进一步提升至78%，说明这些性质有助于区分不利盆地。

Conclusion: 机械设计的损失景观存在物理上有意义、但对离散化不鲁棒的盆地，会导致求解失败；针对这些盆地的物理特征（哪怕仅用初始化偏置）即可有效规避。

Abstract: Automatic differentiation has enabled rapid progress on continuous-variable mechanical design problems, but gradient-based optimization still struggles with discrete variables. The standard approach is to optimize using a continuous relaxation of the choices, then commit to the nearest discrete design at the end. Why this procedure selects unfavorable basins is poorly understood. Here, we introduce a practical, interpretable design problem inspired by origami, in which automatic differentiation is used to design shapes with a target functional behavior. Each origami sheet has a fixed geometry, and a bilevel optimization framework tunes which one of four discrete hinge types to place at each fold. Baseline gradient-based design settles into basins with a large discretization gap: relaxed optimization solves 97% of runs, but the final, discrete solutions succeed only half the time. We find that the gap can be relieved with a surprisingly simple modification. An initialization prior uniformly biased toward the stiff, flat hinge option boosts the final solve fraction from 49% to 69%. Applying the prior adds no additional compute and leaves the optimization process unchanged. Compared to the baseline, sheets produced with the prior have greater geometric compatibility and mechanical stiffness. Directly penalizing frustration and floppiness in the outer loss function produces an even greater improvement than the prior does, raising the solve fraction to 78% and indicating that these properties help distinguish the unfavorable basins. Our results demonstrate that loss landscapes for mechanical design can have physically meaningful basins that are not robust to discretization and cause solves to fail. Targeting these basins' physical signatures, even with just an initialization bias, helps avoid them.

</details>


### [195] [Two mathematical models of knowledge distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17230)
*Audrey Xie,Ludwig Schmidt,John Duchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析知识蒸馏的数学模型，指出蒸馏的收益来自更易优化的损失景观而非更好的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于知识蒸馏成功原因存在诸多竞争性假说，缺乏统一的理论解释，需要构建可分析的数学模型来厘清蒸馏收益的来源。

Method: 构建蒸馏的数学模型；在随机梯度下降训练的广义线性模型中证明蒸馏能以渐近更快速度拟合出高性能学生模型；在秩1矩阵近似中刻画目标矩阵满足何种条件时，带蒸馏的梯度下降严格快于仅用监督目标训练。

Result: 理论上证明蒸馏带来的是优化速度优势而非泛化优势，并给出秩1矩阵近似中蒸馏加速收敛的条件刻画；真实数据集实验与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 蒸馏的作用在于使优化更易、收敛更快，而不在于获得更好的模型；该理论框架明确了蒸馏收益的边界，即优化速度而非泛化能力。

Abstract: Many hypotheses compete to explain the successes of knowledge distillation. To help address this, we propose and analyze a mathematical model of distillation, which suggests that distillation's performance comes not from obtaining better models but from easier to optimize landscapes. For generalized linear models trained with stochastic gradient descent, we prove that distillation fits performant student models asymptotically more quickly than non-distilled models. In rank-1 matrix approximation, we characterize conditions on the target matrix under which gradient descent with distillation converges strictly faster than training on the supervised objective. The theory helps delineate the ways distillation provides benefits (i.e., in optimization speed, not in generalization), and experiments on real datasets corroborate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [196] [Negative-Only Policy Optimization for One-Sided Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17287)
*Jiacheng Xu,Shuo He,Fuxiang Zhang,Chaojie Wang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出单边可验证性（OSV）设定与NOPO方法，在无法获得完全验证时仅利用可信的负例更新策略，避免奖励捷径，在代码、逻辑、数学任务上提升pass@k并具备泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR依赖能可靠判断解是否正确的高质量验证器，但现实场景中往往只能获得廉价的部分检查——它能可靠证伪失败，却无法确认成功（正例可能含大量假阳性）。这种正负验证不对称使标准RLVR不可靠：强化"已验证正例"会直接奖励通过部分检查的捷径行为。因此需要针对该OSV设定重新设计优化目标。

Method: 提出Negative-Only Policy Optimization（NOPO）：将已验证正例视为无标签数据，仅通过抑制可信的已验证负例来更新策略。具体由两部分构成：(1) 相对参考策略的负例抑制损失（reference-relative negative suppression loss）；(2) 组级适配器（group-level adapter），按非被证伪响应的比例对每次更新进行缩放。

Result: 在单元测试、约束检查与LLM自验证三类验证器上（oracle奖励不可用情形），NOPO在代码、逻辑、数学基准上较基线提升pass@k；该增益在推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）与跨基准迁移下依然保持。

Conclusion: 在部分可验证、正例不可信的现实条件下，仅利用负例信号进行策略优化是可行且有效的替代方案，为RLVR在弱验证器场景中的稳健应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically relies on a verifier that can reliably judge whether sampled solutions are correct. However, in many realistic settings, complete verification is generally unavailable, while cheap checks can only falsify failures. We formalize this regime as one-sided verifiability (OSV): verified negatives are reliable counterexamples, but verified positives are merely non-falsified candidates and may contain many false positives. This asymmetry makes standard RLVR unreliable, because reinforcing verified positives can directly reward shortcuts that pass partial checks. To address this issue, we propose Negative-Only Policy Optimization (NOPO), which treats verified positives as unlabeled and updates the policy only by suppressing trusted verified negatives. Specifically, NOPO combines a reference-relative negative suppression loss with a group-level adapter that scales each update by the proportion of non-falsified responses. Across unit-test, constraint-check, and LLM self-verifiers, NOPO improves pass@k over baselines on code, logic, and math benchmarks when oracle rewards are unavailable. These gains persist under inference-time scaling and cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [197] [Towards a Theory of Learnability via Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17303)
*Tosca Lechner,Vinayak Pathak,Sivan Sabato,Ruth Urner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将辩论训练协议形式化为在线学习问题，指出对抗式辩论并非最优学习方式，进而提出协作式协议并用组合维度刻画其可学习性，同时揭示评判者少量错误会导致学习的大误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有辩论研究只分析"已知评判者下两辩手的博弈"，忽略了辩论本质是一种训练协议；作者希望真正理解该协议隐含的学习问题。

Method: 将辩论形式化为在线学习问题；分析对抗式辩论与直接学习的差异；定义协作式辩论协议，并用组合维度（combinatorial dimension）刻画其可学习性；考虑评判者会犯错的不可靠情形。

Result: 精确建模后，在某些情形下，借助评判者的最佳学习方式不同于运行对抗性辩论；协作协议的样本复杂度/可学习性可由一个组合维度刻画；评判者的少量判断错误会导致学习中出现很大误差。

Conclusion: 对抗式辩论并非学习的最优方案，协作式辩论更具可学习性理论基础；同时评判者错误会显著放大学习误差，提示协议设计需关注评判可靠性与学习目标的匹配。

Abstract: Debate has been recently proposed as a training protocol that is hoped to produce more aligned models even as model capabilities scale. The idea is that the human, instead of providing supervision in the form of labels, judges a debate between two models that compete to prove the correctness of opposite labels. It can be shown that functions of a much higher computational complexity can be correctly judged in this way, compared to the complexity of functions that can be directly evaluated using a computationally weak evaluator. This suggests that perhaps debate can be used to teach very complicated functions to models. However, previous work has largely ignored the fact that this is a training protocol, and simply analyzes the game being played between the two debaters for a known judge. We take the first steps towards understanding the learning problem implicit within the protocol, by formulating it as an online learning problem. We show that once we formulate the learning problem precisely, there are cases where the best way to learn given access to a judge is different from running an adversarial debate. This motivates us to define a cooperative version of the protocol, whose learnability we characterize using a combinatorial dimension. We also consider the possibility of the human judge being fallible and occasionally judging the debate incorrectly. We show that a small number of errors in judgement can lead to a large error in learning.

</details>


### [198] [Linking Rapid Learning to Compositional Structure in Neural Networks: Evidence from Additive Analogies in Meta-Learned Parameter Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17314)
*Abigail L. Tenenbaum,R. Thomas McCoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过构造与操纵元学习网络的参数向量，发现这些向量可按函数间的符号关系进行加减组合，从而无需训练就能直接实现目标函数，揭示元学习能塑造具有几何结构的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能从有限经验中快速学习，而神经网络通常需要大量训练。已有工作表明元学习网络能快速学习新函数，但其内部机制尚不清楚，本文旨在探究何种性质使网络具备快速学习能力。

Method: 借鉴词向量中加法关系（如 king - man + woman ≈ queen）的经典结论，作者在元学习网络上寻找不同函数"表示"之间的加性关系：将网络适应某具体函数后的全部参数拼接为单一向量作为该函数的表示，并测试这些参数向量能否按函数间的符号关系进行加减组合。

Result: 参数向量可以依据所表示函数之间的符号关系进行加减，所得到的新参数向量能够成功表示相应符号操作所预测的函数——由这些人工重组向量所定义的网络无需任何直接训练即可实现预期函数。

Conclusion: 元学习能够调整网络的初始化起点，使后续学习沿参数空间中系统且具有功能意义的轨迹进行，从而为在神经网络中实现归纳偏置提供了几何基础。

Abstract: Humans can learn rapidly from limited experience, while neural networks typically require extensive training. In this work, we investigate what properties of a neural network can enable it to learn rapidly. We study neural networks that have been optimized via meta-learning, a technique that exposes a network to many related functions to facilitate its subsequent learning of similar functions. Prior work has shown that these meta-learned networks can learn new functions rapidly, but how they do so remains unclear. To investigate their internal structure, we take inspiration from classic work demonstrating additive relationships among the learned vectors that neural networks use to represent words (e.g., "king" minus "man" plus "woman" approximates "queen"). Analogously, we look for additive relationships among a meta-learned network's "representations" of different functions; when a meta-learned network rapidly learns to implement a specific function, all of its adapted parameter values can be concatenated into a single vector that we treat as representing the function. We find that such parameter vectors can be added and subtracted according to symbolic relationships among the functions they represent, yielding new parameter vectors that successfully represent the functions predicted by the corresponding symbolic manipulations: the networks specified by these artificially recombined vectors actually implement the intended functions without any direct training on them. Our results suggest that meta-learning can tune a network's starting point to organize subsequent learning along systematic, functionally meaningful trajectories through the space of possible parameter vectors, providing a geometric basis for realizing inductive biases in neural networks.

</details>


### [199] [Internalizing Investment Mandates: A Neurosymbolic RL Approach to Portfolio Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17329)
*Zeynep Yıldız,Satchit Chatterji,Ercan Atam,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种PLPG启发的连续动作神经符号强化学习方法，通过重参数化蒙特卡洛采样估计策略自身执行动作满足投资规定的概率并纳入训练目标，使智能体自主学习合规，将六规则制度条件约束的违规率降至4.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构投资组合受投资政策声明约束，常需依市场制度信息满足资产配置限制。深度强化学习虽能优化金融表现，但通常不强制规则合规，难以落地；现有神经符号方法PLPG可满足约束，但仅假设有限离散动作空间，无法处理连续配置。

Method: 提出PLPG启发的连续配置方法：用重参数化蒙特卡洛采样估计策略自身所执行动作满足投资规定的概率，并将其作为附加项加入训练目标；不在推理阶段设置事后强制拦截机制，合规性由智能体学习获得。

Result: 在无任何修正、直接审计确定性均值动作的条件下，该方法将六规则制度条件规定的违规率从无约束RL的96%–98%、奖励惩罚基线的33.4%降至4.8%。

Conclusion: 连续动作神经符号策略能够实质性地将符号化投资规定内化到策略中，实现可学习的合规，为受约束的机构资产配置提供可行路径。

Abstract: Institutional portfolios operate under investment policy statements that specify asset allocation constraints, often conditioned on market regime information. Deep reinforcement learning has been shown to optimize financial performance but typically does not enforce rule compliance, making it unsuitable for real-world deployment. Existing neurosymbolic methods such as Probabilistic Logic Policy Gradient (PLPG) offer constraint-satisfying reinforcement learning (RL) via differentiable symbolic supervision but assume a finite, discrete action space. We present a PLPG-inspired continuous allocation method that estimates the probability that the policy's own executed action satisfies the investment mandate, using reparameterized Monte Carlo sampling, and adds it to the training objective. No post-hoc enforcement mechanism intercepts actions at inference time and compliance is learned by the agent. Audited directly on deterministic mean actions with no correction, the policy reduces violations of a six-rule regime-conditional mandate from 96-98% for unconstrained RL and 33.4% for a reward-penalty baseline to 4.8%, showing that a continuous-action neurosymbolic policy can substantially internalize a symbolic mandate.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Measuring, Revealing and Mitigating Harmful Content Propagation in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15160)
*Xiaokun Zhang,Youlin Wu,Junyu Lu,Jiamin Chen,Zhaochun Ren,Hongfei Lin,Fenglong Ma,Xue Liu,Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究推荐系统中的有害内容，从度量、揭示与缓解三方面展开：构建带危害性标注的数据集与评测指标，实证揭示用户偏好及主流推荐方法会助长有害内容扩散，并提出通用框架MitiHarm，将危害性信号融入物品与用户表示学习，在抑制有害内容传播的同时保持用户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐研究通常以迎合用户兴趣为优先目标，忽视暴力、仇恨言论、色情等有害内容的存在与传播；而推荐系统日益主导信息获取，显著影响用户接触的内容，因此有必要系统性地研究推荐中的有害内容问题。

Method: 分为三步：（1）构建带危害性相关标注的数据集，并提出新的评测指标以定量度量推荐结果的有害性；（2）开展实证分析，考察用户与有害内容的交互行为及主流推荐方法对有害内容扩散的影响；（3）提出通用框架MitiHarm，将危害性相关信号同时融入物品表示学习与用户表示学习。

Result: 实证分析表明用户倾向于与有害内容发生交互，且主流推荐方法往往会助长此类内容的扩散；实验显示MitiHarm能够显著缓解有害内容的传播，同时保持用户满意度。

Conclusion: 该工作从度量、揭示与缓解三个层面构建了推荐系统有害内容研究的系统性框架，所提MitiHarm在不牺牲推荐效果的前提下有效抑制有害内容传播，相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Recommender systems help users navigate vast amounts of information by filtering content according to their historical behaviors. As these systems increasingly shape everyday information access, they largely determine the content users are exposed to. However, current research typically prioritizes catering to user interest, while overlooking the existence and propagation of harmful content, such as violence, hate speech, and pornography. In this work, we take a systematic step toward investigating harmful content in recommender systems from three aspects: measuring, revealing, and mitigating. First, we construct datasets with harmfulness-related annotations and present new evaluation metrics to quantitatively measure the harmfulness of recommendation results. Second, our empirical analysis reveals that users tend to engage with harmful content and prevailing recommendation methods often contribute to the proliferation of such content. Third, to address this issue, we introduce a generic framework, MitiHarm, which integrates harmfulness-related signals into both item and user representation learning. Experiments show that MitiHarm can significantly mitigate the propagation of harmful content while preserving user satisfaction. Resources of this work are available at https://github.com/Zhang-xiaokun/MitiHarm.

</details>


### [201] [When Direction Matters: Diagnosing Relational Instruction Following in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15417)
*Zhanyu Wu,Richong Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出SwitchIR诊断基准，揭示稠密检索器在需要方向性关系判断时会失效：模型能利用词汇/属性线索，但关系方向反转时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索器声称能根据自然语言的相关性准则（超越短查询）进行条件检索，但作者发现其在相关性依赖于同一实体间的方向性关系时并不可靠，需要受控诊断来定位这一具体弱点。

Method: 构建SwitchIR受控诊断集，对比Constraint Shift、Explicit Filtering（线索可用对照）与Relational Flip（查询-文档对固定、仅关系方向反转）；在代表性embedding与late-interaction检索器上评测，并给出exact margin decomposition（方向敏感交互 vs 方向不变成对偏好），另设LLM辅助、人工复核的真实性变体，并进行诊断性微调实验。

Result: 主要联合指标在两项线索可用对照上较高，在Relational Flip上持续偏低，该排序在真实变体中同样成立；margin分解显示仅当方向敏感交互超过方向不变成对偏好时两个方向才都正确，而实际中方向敏感变化常见但对齐交互过弱；诊断微调可使模型学到跨关系族泛化的关系行为，大模型泛化更强。

Conclusion: 现有检索器对自然语言条件检索的能力被高估，方向性关系推理是系统性短板；该缺陷可通过诊断微调缓解，且规模更大的模型泛化更好。

Abstract: Dense retrievers increasingly claim the ability to condition retrieval on natural-language relevance criteria beyond a short query. We identify a specific weakness in this capability: the evaluated models perform well when lexical or attribute cues distinguish the target, but become much less reliable when relevance depends on a directional relation over the same entities. We introduce SwitchIR, a controlled diagnostic that contrasts Constraint Shift and Explicit Filtering with Relational Flip, where the query and document pair remain fixed while the requested relation direction reverses. Across representative embedding and late-interaction retrievers, the primary joint metric is high on the two cue-available controls and consistently low on Relational Flip; the same ordering persists in an LLM-assisted, manually reviewed realistic variant. An exact margin decomposition shows that both directions are correct only when the direction-sensitive query--document interaction exceeds the direction-invariant pair preference. Empirically, direction-sensitive changes are common, but their aligned interaction is usually too weak to overcome that preference. Diagnostic fine-tuning shows that models can learn relational behavior that generalizes across relation families, with stronger generalization in the larger model.

</details>


### [202] [SurvEval: Listwise Re-Ranking Evaluator via Survival-Based Exposure and Multi-Objective Value Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15434)
*YaChen Yan,Liubo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出列表级评估器SurvEval，将重排评估中的用户曝光过程建模为离散时间生存模型，并结合多目标价值估计，以到达概率与条件价值的逐位置乘积之和作为列表效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排评估器对用户浏览假设过度简化，且将多目标信号坍缩为单一标量，难以反映真实的列表级（listwise）效用。

Method: 将顺序浏览过程形式化为离散时间生存模型，推导出单调递减的到达概率以刻画浏览的级联结构；引入带独立逐目标预测头的多目标价值估计模块，并用基于不确定性的损失加权自适应平衡不同尺度与噪声水平的目标；两组件通过统一的Transformer骨干联合优化，列表效用由到达概率与条件物品价值估计的逐位置乘积求和得到。

Result: 在KuaiRand与Avito数据集上，SurvEval优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模用户曝光与多目标物品价值可更准确地刻画重排列表效用，验证了生存分析式级联建模与不确定性加权多目标学习的有效性。

Abstract: Re-ranking is a crucial stage in recommender systems, yet existing evaluators oversimplify user browsing assumptions and collapse multi-objective signals into a single scalar, limiting their ability to reflect true listwise utility. We propose \textbf{SurvEval}, a listwise evaluator that jointly models user exposure and item value for re-ranking evaluation. We formalize the sequential browsing process as a discrete-time survival model, deriving monotonically decreasing reaching probabilities that capture the cascading structure of user browsing. We further introduce a multi-objective value estimation module with dedicated per-objective prediction heads and uncertainty-based loss weighting to adaptively balance objectives of varying scales and noise levels. Both components are jointly optimized via a unified transformer backbone, and the listwise utility is obtained by summing the position-wise products of reaching probabilities and conditional item value estimates. Extensive experiments on KuaiRand and Avito demonstrate that SurvEval achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [203] [ReAlign: Reward-Guided Preference Alignment for Generative Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15445)
*YaChen Yan,Liubo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ReAlign：将列表级奖励模型与候选感知自回归生成器耦合的奖励引导偏好对齐框架，用于重排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段“生成器-评估器”方法把列表生成与列表评估解耦，生成器无法直接获得列表级效用信号；而基于打分的一阶段方法在固定的上游排列上下文下对物品打分，未显式搜索排列空间。

Method: 奖励模型将列表级奖励分解为逐位置贡献：以基于生存分析的曝光模块建模用户曝光，以多目标头建模各异质交互信号对应的物品价值；生成器采用两阶段训练——先在日志曝光列表上监督预训练建立相关性先验，再以奖励引导后训练，通过对池内标准化奖励的分布对齐与排序对齐，使生成器与奖励模型对齐。

Result: 大量离线实验表明ReAlign一致优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过奖励引导的偏好对齐，ReAlign将列表级效用信号直接注入自回归生成器，实现列表的有针对性排列搜索，效果优于现有方法。

Abstract: Re-ranking refines the upstream ranked list to maximize listwise user utility. Existing two-stage generator-evaluator methods decouple list generation from list evaluation, leaving the generator without direct access to the listwise utility signal, while scoring-based one-stage methods score items under a fixed upstream permutation context without explicitly searching the permutation space. We propose \textbf{ReAlign}, a reward-guided preference alignment framework that couples a listwise reward model with a candidate-aware autoregressive generator. The reward model decomposes the listwise reward into position-wise contributions, modeling user exposure through a survival-based exposure module and item value through per-objective heads for heterogeneous engagement signals. The generator is trained in two stages: supervised pre-training on logged exposure lists to establish a relevance prior, followed by reward-guided post-training that aligns the generator with the reward model through complementary distribution and ranking alignment over within-pool standardized rewards. Extensive offline experiments show that ReAlign consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [204] [Bag of concepts can enhance other text representations for medical systematic review screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15798)
*Radosław Pytlak,Paweł Cichosz,Bartłomiej Fajdek,Bogdan Jastrzębski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了词袋(BoW)、概念袋(BoC)、PubMedBERT、Jina、Gemma、Qwen3等文本表示及其BoC组合在主动学习式半自动系统综述中的效果，基于医学SLR案例与工作量节省等指标评估，发现概念特征提供互补信息，BoC增强表示在DERP案例上平均工作量节省更高，其中Qwen3+BoC表现最佳，且本体选择会影响文档分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有半自动系统综述(SLR)研究对概念袋(bag of concepts)表示关注不足，且不同文本表示（稀疏特征、词向量、预训练语言模型）对主动学习筛选效率的影响缺乏系统对比，本体选择对分类效果的影响也尚未明确。

Method: 在医学SLR案例上，将不同文本表示（BoW、BoC、PubMedBERT、Jina、Gemma、Qwen3及其与BoC的组合）嵌入主动学习框架，通过随机森林分类、聚类初始化与不确定性采样，迭代结合专家标注扩充训练集；采用源自优化基准的工作量节省与性能剖面指标进行评估，并比较不同本体构建的BoC与基于UMLS词典的MedCAT表示的预测价值。

Result: 概念特征可提供互补信息；BoC增强表示的DERP案例平均工作量节省高于对应基础表示，其中Qwen3+BoC平均表现最佳；增强效果随案例与基础表示而异；本体选择对BoC表示的预测价值有影响，并与MedCAT/UMLS表示存在差异。

Conclusion: 概念级特征能与现代神经网络表示互补，提升半自动SLR的筛选效率，但收益依赖于具体案例与基础表示；本体选择是BoC表示设计中的关键决策因素，实践中宜采用BoC与强语义表示的组合并针对案例选择本体。

Abstract: The paper examines the choice of text representation for semi-automated systematic literature review (SLR) systems based on active learning. Motivated by the insufficient attention that the bag of concepts representation has received so far, the paper discusses the implementation and experimental evaluation of several variations thereof. The experimental study was performed on medical SLR case studies, with results reported for random forest classification, cluster initialization and uncertainty sampling. The utility of bag of concepts features obtained using different ontologies is evaluated by applying them within the active learning framework to sequentially create classification models based on training data iteratively expanded with expert annotations. Systematic experiments compare BoW, BoC, PubMedBERT, Jina, Gemma and Qwen3 representations, together with combinations of BoC and each of the other representations. The evaluation uses workload savings and performance profiles adapted from optimization benchmarking. The results suggest that concept-based features can provide complementary information, with the BoC-augmented representations achieving higher mean workload savings on the DERP cases than their corresponding base representations. The Qwen3+BoC representation gives the best average performance in this comparison. The effects of augmentation vary across case studies and base representations. The paper also concentrates on the impact of ontology choice on document classification outcomes. In particular, the paper compares the predictive value of bag of concepts representations based on chosen ontologies with that of representations obtained using MedCAT with a UMLS-based dictionary.

</details>


### [205] [From Tokens to Behaviors: Rethinking Generative Recommendation for User Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16159)
*Jiaqi Wang,Hanchen Yang,Hengyu Zhang,Hongxu Ma,Wenqi Rao,Hongyang Wang,Zhongbo Sun,Renliang Qian,Weixin Chen,Wengen Li,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou,Qingpeng Cai,Peng Jiang,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR
