TL;DR: 提出TraGR，一种行为感知的生成式推荐框架，通过行为级并行SID解码、概率反馈与关系感知轨迹学习，解决序列SID解码耦合、离散反馈忽略不确定性及教师强制训练与推理不一致的问题，在四个公开数据集上稳定优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐（GR）用自回归生成语义ID（SID）做用户行为轨迹预测（UBTP），存在三点缺陷：(1) 串行SID解码将token生成与行为状态转移耦合，每次交互需多步依赖解码，效率低；(2) 离散物品反馈忽略预测不确定性；(3) 教师强制训练与自回归推理之间存在训练-推理差距，影响轨迹生成质量。

Method: TraGR包含三部分：(1) 行为级架构：在单次交互内并行预测SID token，通过循环状态更新建模行为间转移，并联合SID级与物品级监督；(2) 概率反馈：用概率加权的候选摘要替代单一离散反馈，与所选行为共同引导后续预测；(3) 关系感知轨迹学习：在模型自生成轨迹上训练，引入协同转移信号与后续信用分配。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，TraGR在不同预测时域（prediction horizon）下均一致优于现有最优基线。

Conclusion: TraGR通过将生成、反馈与学习三者对齐到连续用户行为层面，有效缓解了串行SID解码的耦合效率问题、反馈不确定性缺失和曝光偏差，为UBTP提供了更准确、更高效的生成式推荐方案。

Abstract: User behavior trajectory prediction (UBTP) aims to capture how a user's needs evolve over time by predicting the sequence of future interactions from that user's interaction history. Recent generative recommendation (GR) methods address this task through autoregressive generation of semantic IDs (SIDs). However, serial SID decoding couples token generation with behavior state transitions and requires multiple dependent steps per interaction. Discrete item feedback overlooks predictive uncertainty, and teacher-forced training leaves a training-inference gap in trajectory generation. To address these limitations, we propose TraGR, a behavior-aware generative recommendation framework that aligns generation, feedback, and learning with successive user behaviors. Its behavior-level architecture predicts SID tokens in parallel within each interaction and models behavioral transitions through recurrent state updates, with joint SID and item-level supervision. Probability feedback combines the selected behavior with a probability-weighted summary of alternatives to guide subsequent predictions. Relation-aware trajectory learning incorporates collaborative transition signals and later credit assignment into training on model-generated trajectories. Experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that TraGR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different prediction horizons. Code: https://github.com/CalmaQi/GR4UBT.

</details>


### [206] [GA-HEL: A Knowledge-Driven Expert System for Open-Set, Granularity-Aware Hierarchical Event Linking in Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16194)
*Shengqi Zhou,Zhigang Liu,Wenxiang Zhu,Zhentao Cang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出开放集分层事件链接任务，并给出三阶段知识驱动专家系统 GA-HEL，用于社媒帖文到 Storyline–Episode–Atomic Event 卡片的分层匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社媒事件情报需要判断帖文是否属于已注册事件空间、找出所支持的分层路径，并在证据允许的最深层级停止；现有方法缺少显式范围控制与结构合法性约束。

Method: GA-HEL 采用三阶段漏斗：（1）开放集范围控制，在分层推理前限制候选暴露；（2）分层感知解码器选择受支持的路径与深度；（3）确定性合法路径校验，确保记录可用于数据库。资源包括 200 帖 Pilot 与另标注的 497 帖 Expansion（4 个留出 Storyline 及困难负例池）；实现、提示、Event Cards、候选顺序与六个系统的预测均在 Expansion Gold 标注前冻结。

Result: 评估涵盖端到端分层匹配、开放集拒识、结构校验、消融、跨 Storyline 分析及固定审计子集的独立专家重标注。GA-HEL 与强全局全卡片推理方法相当，同时提供显式范围控制和层次合法的结构化记录。

Conclusion: 主要剩余难点是仅凭 Episode 级证据向 Atomic Event 的不受支持下沉；任务定义、冻结资源与可审计协议为范围感知的事件情报提供了可复现基础，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Social-media event intelligence requires a system to determine whether a post belongs to a registered event space, identify the supported hierarchy path, and stop at the deepest level warranted by the evidence. We formulate this requirement as open-set hierarchical event linking over frozen Storyline--Episode--Atomic Event Cards. GA-HEL implements a three-stage, knowledge-driven expert-system funnel: open-set scope control limits candidate exposure before hierarchical reasoning, hierarchy-aware decoders select a supported path and depth, and deterministic legal-path validation checks each record before database-compatible use. A 200-post Pilot supports task and mechanism development, while a separately annotated 497-post Expansion set evaluates transfer across four held-out Storylines and a hard-negative pool. Method implementations, prompts, Event Cards, candidate order, and six systems' predictions were frozen before Expansion Gold annotation. The resulting evaluation combines end-to-end hierarchical matching, open-set rejection, structural validation, ablation, cross-Storyline analysis, and an independent expert reannotation of a fixed audited subset. GA-HEL remains competitive with strong global full-card reasoning while providing explicit scope control and hierarchy-valid structured records. Error analysis identifies unsupported descent from Episode-level evidence to Atomic Events as the dominant remaining challenge. The task definition, frozen resource, and auditable protocol provide a reproducible foundation for scope-aware event intelligence. Code, Event Cards, labels, frozen predictions, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/SHbiao/GAHEL_release.

</details>


### [207] [CURATOR: Scalable Fine-grained Evidence Selection for Retrieval-Augmented Generation using Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16366)
*Shib Dasgupta,Bandish Parikh,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CURATOR：一种经微调的决策模型，可在大候选集上可靠地做证据片段选择决策，在保持联合决策计算效率的同时维持强证据判别能力，并提升下游RAG性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG将整段检索文本全部输入LLM，而真正包含答案证据的往往只是其中少量span。孤立判断某个span是否相关很困难（需参考其他检索信息），而逐span在完整上下文中评估又需重复处理同一文本、计算低效。预训练决策模型虽能高效并行评估多个span，但实验表明同时决策数增加时其证据识别能力会退化。

Method: 提出CURATOR：将决策模型微调为在大候选池上做可靠证据选择决策；训练时让模型从大候选池中选择数量可变的支撑span，从而习得在大规模候选集下兼顾判别力与联合决策效率的证据选择能力。

Result: 随着候选数量增加，CURATOR仍保持较强的证据判别能力；可跨不同任务与文档领域泛化；提升下游RAG性能，尤其在可用上下文有限时效果显著。

Conclusion: 通过在大候选池上训练可变数量的证据选择，CURATOR克服了预训练决策模型随同时决策数增加而性能衰退的问题，实现了高效且可靠的span级证据选择，改善了RAG的上下文利用与下游表现。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides entire retrieved passages to the LLM, even though only a handful of spans within them contain the evidence pertaining to the question. While precise selection of these spans can improve context utilization, identifying them in isolation is challenging. A span may appear irrelevant by itself, and deciding whether to retain it may require examining other retrieved information. One can evaluate each span's candidacy with the full retrieved context, but this is computationally inefficient as the same text must be processed repeatedly. Pretrained decision model can be used to efficiently evaluate multiple spans simultaneously, allowing each decision to consider the surrounding spans as well. However, our experiments show that their ability to identify relevant evidence deteriorates as the number of simultaneous decisions increases.
  To address this, we introduce CURATOR, a fine-tuned decision model trained to make reliable evidence-selection decisions over large candidate sets. By training a decision model to select varying numbers of supporting spans from large candidate pools, CURATOR develops the capability to select evidence for RAG while retaining the computational efficiency of joint decision-making. We observe that CURATOR maintains a strong evidence discrimination capability as the number of candidates increases, generalizes across different tasks and document domains, and improves downstream RAG performance, particularly when the available context is limited.

</details>


### [208] [TriGen: A Generative Paradigm for Trigger-Induced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16395)
*Muyu Zou,Haibo Xing,Shunye Tang,Liyan Tie,Lingyu Mu,Yandong Wang,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TriGen，将生成式推荐范式从UIR扩展到TIR（触发诱导推荐），通过校准尾部注入、动态门控仲裁与位置特定变换，在保持长期历史偏好的同时对齐瞬时触发意图，已在线全量部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐基于UIR设定，将目标物品作为长期用户历史的被动续写来自回归生成语义ID，忽略了工业中关键的TIR场景——用户点击会触发即时后续信息流，推荐需与瞬时触发意图动态对齐且不破坏历史偏好连续性。传统TIR方法依赖判别式打分，无法引导token级生成；现有生成模型又缺乏化解这一结构性张力的机制。

Method: TriGen：(1) 校准尾部注入（calibrated tail injection）对局部历史重新编码；(2) 动态门控在持久偏好与即时冲动之间自适应仲裁；(3) 位置特定变换在各解码步维持层级感知的触发引导。

Result: 大量离线评估显示相较竞争基线取得一致提升；六个国家市场的在线A/B测试在收入和GMV上均显著提升，TriGen已实现全量生产部署。

Conclusion: 将触发引导引入生成式推荐的token级解码过程是可行的，且在不牺牲历史偏好连续性的前提下带来可观商业收益，具备工业落地价值。

Abstract: Generative recommendation typically operates under the User-Induced Recommendation (UIR) setting, predicting target items by autoregressively generating semantic IDs as a passive continuation of long-term user history. However, this paradigm overlooks Trigger-Induced Recommendation (TIR), an essential industrial scenario where a user click triggers an immediate follow-up feed. In TIR, recommendations must dynamically align with the instant trigger intent without compromising historical preference continuity. Traditional TIR methods rely on discriminative scoring and cannot guide token-level generation, while existing generative models lack mechanisms to reconcile this structural tension. To address this challenge, we propose TriGen, establishing a tailored generative paradigm for trigger-induced recommendation. TriGen introduces calibrated tail injection to re-encode local history, adaptively arbitrates between persistent tastes and immediate impulses via dynamic gating, and applies position-specific transformations to sustain level-aware trigger guidance across decoding steps. Extensive offline evaluations confirm consistent gains over competitive baselines. Furthermore, online A/B tests across six country markets demonstrate significant revenue and GMV improvements, leading to the full production deployment of TriGen.

</details>


### [209] [ReSID: Rethinking Long-Tail Retrieval in Generative Recommendation through Item Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16404)
*Muyu Zou,Shunye Tang,Yi Xu,Haibo Xing,Yandong Wang,Lingyu Mu,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式推荐（GR）在长尾物品检索上的瓶颈，提出ReSID，通过长并行语义ID、热度去偏与用户语义感知的束搜索，以及部分SID匹配的恢复机制，在保持整体准确率的同时显著提升长尾检索效果，并已上线生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐用离散语义ID表示物品并把检索当作序列生成，但在交互分布长尾偏斜和严格服务预算下难以检索长尾物品。作者首次系统研究SID构建、解码、匹配全流程中的长尾可达性问题。

Method: 发现：等码长下并行SID构建在尾部物品上优于顺序SID；即使完整生成失败，解码序列仍携带局部一致信息，可通过部分匹配召回相关尾物品，但该恢复受上游搜索限制，一旦束搜索剪掉所有在固定失配容忍度内可完成的路径即失效。基于此提出ReSID：以长并行SID为基础，结合热度去偏与用户语义感知的束搜索保住有希望的路径，并用部分SID匹配从存活token中恢复物品。

Result: ReSID在长尾检索上达到SOTA，整体准确率具竞争力；窄束下延迟更低、吞吐更高；已部署于生产系统承载主要用户流量并带来显著业务收益；开源代码与模型检查点。

Conclusion: 长尾可达性是SID构建、解码与匹配协同决定的系统性问题；并行SID加偏置束搜索与部分匹配恢复可有效兼顾长尾覆盖与效率，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) represents items with discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs) and casts retrieval as sequence generation. Under skewed interaction distributions and tight serving budgets, GR struggles to retrieve long-tail items. In this work, we are the first to study long-tail reachability across the complete pipeline of SID construction, decoding, and matching. We find that under equal code lengths, parallel SID construction consistently outperforms sequential SIDs on tail items. Moreover, even when full generation fails, decoded sequences carry locally consistent information that retrieves relevant tail items via partial matching. Yet this recovery is bounded by upstream search, failing once beam search prunes all prefixes completable within a fixed mismatch tolerance. Motivated by these findings, we propose ReSID to jointly preserve and recover tail candidates. Built on long parallel SIDs, ReSID pairs popularity-debiased and user-semantic-aware beam search to keep promising paths alive with partial SID matching to recover items from surviving tokens. ReSID achieves state-of-the-art long-tail retrieval while maintaining competitive overall accuracy, with lower latency and higher throughput under narrow beams. ReSID is deployed in our production system, serving main user traffic with substantial business gains. We release our complete code and model checkpoints to support full reproducibility and real-world deployment at https://github.com/Gabriel-reina/ReSID.

</details>


### [210] [AntEmbed: Dual-Mode Multimodal Embedding Model for Efficient and Fine-Grained Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16405)
*Kehang Jia,Teng Liu,Juntao Li,Hua Chen,Jinxiong Chang,Xiaobo Liang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出双模式多模态嵌入模型 AntEmbed，在统一嵌入空间中同时支持高效检索与推理增强的细粒度匹配，MMEB-v2 上 8B 版本达 81.5 分，超最强基线 3.1 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判别式嵌入高效但细粒度语义不足；生成式嵌入通过推理捕获查询意图与相关性证据，但计算开销大。二者难以兼得。

Method: ①构建面向检索的推理内容（查询意图+混淆感知语义证据），将原始与推理增强候选视为语义等价视图，用单嵌入 token 的多正例对比学习对齐判别式与生成式嵌入；②跨模式自蒸馏，通过候选相似度分布蒸馏与查询表示对齐，将细粒度语义从推理增强表示迁移到原始表示。

Result: MMEB-v2 上 AntEmbed-8B 以推理增强嵌入取得 81.5 总体得分，领先最强基线 3.1 分；两种检索模式共享单一候选索引。

Conclusion: 通过统一嵌入空间与自蒸馏，判别式模式无需在线推理即可获得细粒度能力，实现效率与精度的兼顾。

Abstract: Discriminative embeddings encode original multimodal inputs efficiently but struggle with fine-grained semantics. Generative embeddings use reasoning to capture query intent and relevance evidence, but incur higher computational costs. We propose AntEmbed, a dual-mode multimodal embedding model that supports efficient retrieval and reasoning-enhanced fine-grained matching in a unified embedding space. First, we construct retrieval-oriented reasoning content that captures query intent and confusion-aware semantic evidence. We treat original and reasoning-enhanced candidate inputs as semantically equivalent views, and use multi-positive contrastive learning with a single embedding token to align discriminative and generative embeddings. Second, cross-mode self-distillation transfers fine-grained semantic information from reasoning-enhanced to original representations through candidate similarity-distribution distillation and query representation alignment. This strengthens discriminative embeddings without requiring additional online reasoning. Both retrieval modes share a single candidate index. On MMEB-v2, AntEmbed-8B achieves an overall score of 81.5 with reasoning-enhanced embeddings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.1 points.

</details>


### [211] [RMIT-ADM+S at TREC RAG 2026: Research Agents, Retriever Ablations, and Open-Weight Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16425)
*Oleg Zendel,Kun Ran,Chenglong Ma,Nguyen Khoi Vo,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RMIT-ADM+S团队基于ClimbMix语料参加TREC 2026 RAG赛道，提交7个research-agent系统，其中6个在NIST官方评测中三项评分集均超赛道中位数，最佳系统brief-hybrid在Batch-1关键覆盖上并列赛道最高（0.892），并发现自动覆盖度指标与人类偏好存在错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索检索增强生成（RAG）中不同检索策略、写作模型（writer）与智能体工作流对答案质量的影响，并考察低成本开源权重模型在research-agent系统中的可行性与性价比。

Method: 使用ClimbMix语料参加TREC 2026 RAG赛道；基于专有OpenAI模型与开源权重模型构建7个research-agent运行方案，系统变化检索策略、writer模型与agent工作流；提交前通过消融实验（ablations）定位关键影响因素。

Result: 消融实验表明writer选择与相邻chunk扩展（adjacent-chunk expansion）是影响答案质量的主要因素。NIST官方评测中7个运行有6个在全部三个rubric/nugget评分集上超过赛道中位数；最佳运行brief-hybrid取得本队最高全条目覆盖率，并在Batch-1关键覆盖上并列赛道最高（0.892）。人工成对偏好评估中，开源权重的owa-glm5在Batch 1领先（尽管自动覆盖度较低），sol-research-first在Batch 2领先。

Conclusion: 自动覆盖度指标与人工偏好存在明显不一致，评估需兼顾二者；低成本开源权重模型在research-agent系统中具备较强潜力，可成为专有模型的有效替代。

Abstract: We describe the RMIT-ADM+S team's participation in the TREC 2026 Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) track using the ClimbMix corpus. We submitted seven research-agent runs using proprietary OpenAI and open-weight models, exploring different retrieval strategies, writer models, and agent workflows. Pre-submission ablations identified writer selection and adjacent-chunk expansion as the main factors affecting answer quality. In the official NIST evaluation, six of seven runs exceeded the track median across all three rubric/nugget collections. Our best-performing run, brief-hybrid, achieved the highest all-item coverage among our submissions and tied the track maximum (0.892) for Batch-1 vital coverage. Human side-by-side preferences differed across evaluation batches: the open-weight owa-glm5 led Batch 1 despite lower automated coverage, while sol-research-first led Batch 2. These findings highlight differences between automated coverage measures and human preferences, as well as the potential of lower-cost open-weight models for research-agent systems.

</details>


### [212] [Active Learning of Approximate LLM Ranking for Energy-Efficient Retrieval of Long Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16488)
*Ohad Porat,Elad Yom-Tov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LAAM混合检索框架，用轻量学习型预测器结合主动学习与少量LLM标注，在仅消耗3.3%能耗的情况下达到DeepSeek重排87%的P@10，实现能耗与精度的连续可调权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词法检索器速度快、能耗低，但仅依赖关键词匹配；LLM能捕捉语义、擅长处理长而上下文丰富的查询，但推理计算与能耗成本过高。需要在二者之间找到可控的成本—效果折中点，避免只能在廉价低精度与昂贵高精度之间二选一。

Method: 提出LLM Active Approximation Model (LAAM)：在语义检索与LLM检索之间引入轻量级学习型预测器作为中介；通过主动学习循环，由LLM仅标注少量最具信息量的文档，将LLM评估集中于高信息文档，从而在大幅降低推理成本的同时保持检索效果。实验基于TREC Robust 2004，使用Lucene+BM25检索与DeepSeek v3作为LLM，在三种查询冗长度下评估。

Result: 冗长查询下，LAAM取得DeepSeek重排平均P@10的87%（0.459 vs. 0.526），而每查询能耗仅为其3.3%；LAAM在平均精度上显著优于BM25，且与DeepSeek无显著差异（但MAP上存在差异）；Friedman检验P<0.05并做事后检验。

Conclusion: 通过主动学习选择性近似LLM相关性判断，可使检索精度由评估（及能耗）预算直接控制；框架提供沿能耗—精度曲线的连续工作点谱，让实践者按计算预算灵活权衡检索质量，而非固守廉价低精度的词法检索或穷尽式高成本LLM重排。

Abstract: Search engines and Large Language Models (LLMs) offer complementary strengths for document retrieval: lexical retrievers which rely on keyword matching are fast and energy-efficient, whereas LLMs capture semantic meaning and excel at interpreting long, context-rich queries. However, this comes at a cost that is computationally and energetically prohibitive.
  We propose the LLM Active Approximation Model (LAAM), a hybrid retrieval framework that introduces a lightweight learned predictor which serves as an intermediary between semantic retrieval and LLM retrieval. LAAM is refined through an active learning loop where an LLM labels a small number of documents. By concentrating LLM evaluations on the most informative documents, the framework substantially reduces inference cost while preserving retrieval effectiveness.
  We evaluate the proposed framework on TREC Robust 2004 using Lucene with BM25 retrieval and DeepSeek v3 as the LLM, under three levels of query verbosity. For verbose queries, LAAM achieves 87% of average P@10 achieved by DeepSeek's reranking (0.459 vs. 0.526), while requiring only 3.3% of the per-query energy consumption. LAAM is statistically significantly better than BM25 and is indistinguishable from DeepSeek on average precision, though not on MAP (Friedman test, P<0.05 with post-hoc testing).
  Overall, our results demonstrate that selectively approximating LLM relevance judgments through active learning enables retrieval accuracy to be controlled directly through the evaluation (and consequently energy) budget. Rather than committing to either inexpensive (and less accurate) lexical retrieval or exhaustive (and expensive) LLM reranking, our framework exposes a continuous spectrum of operating points along the energy--accuracy curve, allowing practitioners to select how to trade retrieval quality with computational budget.

</details>


### [213] [KuaiRecV2: Benchmarking Large-Scale Continual Learning for Diversified and Multi-task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16795)
*Chenxu Li,Shuchang Liu,Hantao Shu,Wei Yuan,Zhe Xu,Guoqing Hu,Yan Wang,Biao Yang,Xiang Li,Cheng Ling,Fan Yang,Yongqi Liu,Lantao Hu,Han Li,Kaiqiao Zhan,Kun Gai,An Zhang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KuaiRecV2 是快手发布的大规模工业级推荐基准，含百万级多模态物品信息与30天长期交互记录、动态候选池，并用统一评测框架同时考察精度、多样性与多任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线简单评测与线上复杂结果之间存在持续不一致，即使在大模型时代依然存在。作者将该差距归因于三类挑战：大规模持续学习（动态物品池与分布漂移下的长期有效性）、组合多样性（token 级与物品级的精度-多样性权衡）、多任务奖励平衡（生成模型对序列分布建模准确但多任务可控性弱）。

Method: 构建 KuaiRecV2 基准：来自快手的工业级大规模交互数据集，包含百万规模多模态物品信息、跨30天的长期交互记录以及动态视频候选池；并设计严格评测体系，在同一评分规则下评估检索精度、多样性与多任务性能。

Result: 提供了一个可支撑持续学习、多样性权衡与多任务评估的工业级基准，并规定“有效进展”的判定标准：新方法必须在所有指标上同时提升，即 iff 全面提升才算有效改进；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作通过贴近真实推荐场景的数据与统一多指标评测，为弥合离线-线上不一致、推动更真实可用的推荐方案提供了标准化基础设施。

Abstract: Recommender systems, which retrieve items of interest to users based on their interaction histories, have recently witnessed several key technological advancements, benefiting from the semantic representation space and model-data scaling. Nevertheless, practitioners and researchers have frequently encountered critical inconsistencies between simple offline evaluations and complex online results, and this gap still exists in the era of large models. In this work, we address several key factors that contribute to this inconsistency: 1) the \textit{large-scale continual learning challenge} that assumes a practical demand to maintain long-term model effectiveness under dynamic item pools and distributional shifts; 2) the \textit{combinatorial diversity challenge} that requires the generation strategy to solve the accuracy-diversity trade-off in both the finer token level and the holistic item level; and 3) the \textit{multi-task reward balancing challenge}, which is particularly critical for modern generative models that can accurately model the sequence distribution but are less controllable for the multi-task demands.
  To address these challenges and further pave the way for the development of more realistic recommendation solutions, we introduce KuaiRecV2, a deliberately constructed benchmark with industrial-level datasets from Kuaishou. Specifically, we provide a large-scale interaction dataset with million-scale multi-modal item information and long-term interaction records across 30 days, with a dynamic video candidate pool. Additionally, we provide a rigorous benchmark that evaluates retrieval accuracy, diversity, and multi-task performance in one rubric system, where an effective advancement occurs if and only if the new method improves all metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/cxli2333/KuaiRecV2

</details>


### [214] [SignalAudit-QD: A Reproducible Audit Resource for External Signals in AI-Query Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16947)
*Amir Rafe,Iman Jamshidi,Gourab Sil,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SignalAudit-QD 是一个可复现的审计资源，用于检验某个固定日期的公开信号在固定的20项美国AI术语面板中，能否在查询历史之外提供增量信息。其核心结论是一个有界的负结果案例研究：在一个月主比较中，加入专利信号对宏观平均绝对误差的改变量仅为+0.0012，置信区间跨零，既不能证明总体收益，也不能证明等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部公开信号（如专利流）常被宣称能为时间序列/检索类预测补充查询历史之外的信息，但缺乏可复现、可篡改检测的审计流程，且现有评估常混淆"历史回溯重建"与"真实在线部署"。作者希望提供一个透明、可扩展的证据模式来审计此类外部信号声明。

Method: 构建 SignalAudit-QD 审计资源：将两份归档的 Google Trends 抽取与八条 USPTO AI 专利公开流对齐，在固定的20项术语面板上进行预测；发布基于origin索引的预测、配对损失、来源溯源、可执行测试与防篡改运行清单。采用以水平值为输入的ridge模型为主宿主，比较加入两分量专利块前后的宏观平均绝对误差，并使用条件性95%移动块区间评估不确定性；辅以持久性锚定与非线性宿主、时序匹配对照及"植入信号"敏感性检验。所有输入均为建模期（2020–2023）之后回溯重建，故属于冻结测量版本内的伪起点检验，而非历史部署模拟。

Result: 一个月指定比较中，向水平ridge加入两分量专利块使宏观MAE变化+0.0012 Trends点，条件95%移动块区间为[-0.0363,+0.0387]，不能确立聚合收益或等价性。替代维度、语义映射、专利定义、第二次抽取及三个月期限均未解决；持久性锚定与非线性宿主出现模型特定恶化（锚定拟合仅微弱恶化）。时序匹配对照揭示可用性假设依赖，"植入信号"检验仅能识别最大规模的注入信号。

Conclusion: 该工作给出一个界定清晰的负结果案例研究，并提供可扩展的证据模式，用于审计时间信息检索中的外部信号声明；其释放的接口（含高关注度告警示例）展示了可用性，但不等于在线效用。主要贡献在于方法学透明度与可复现的评估框架，而非证实专利信号的预测增益。

Abstract: SignalAudit-QD is a reproducible audit resource for testing whether a dated public signal adds information beyond query history in a fixed 20-term U.S. AI panel. It aligns two archived Google Trends extractions with eight USPTO AI-patent publication streams and releases origin-indexed predictions, paired losses, provenance, executable tests, and tamper-evident run manifests. Both inputs are retrospective reconstructions obtained after the modeled 2020--2023 origins; the task is therefore a pseudo-origin assay within frozen measurement vintages, not a historical deployment simulation. In the designated one-month comparison, adding a two-component patent block to a level ridge changes macro mean absolute error by +0.0012 Trends points, with conditional 95% moving-block interval [-0.0363,+0.0387]. This establishes neither aggregate benefit nor equivalence. Alternative dimensions, semantic mapping, patent definitions, a second extraction, and a three-month horizon remain unresolved; persistence-anchored and nonlinear hosts instead show model-specific worsening, tiny for the anchored fit. Matched timing controls expose availability assumptions, while a planted-signal assay detects only the largest tested injection. A within-query high-interest alert demonstrates the released interface but not online utility. The contribution is a bounded negative case study and an extensible evidence schema for auditing external-signal claims in temporal information retrieval.

</details>


### [215] [Evaluation of Music Version Retrieval Methods for Setlist Identification in Live Concerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17070)
*Andres Ferraro,Albin Correya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在 ASID 基准上评估现代音乐版本检索方法用于自动演唱会曲目单（setlist）识别，比较 Re-MOVE、CLEWS 与内部的 NAVE 神经音频嵌入模型，发现 NAVE 与 CLEWS 显著优于 Re-MOVE，且 NAVE 仅用 256 维嵌入即取得更佳检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动曲目单识别需将噪声大、质量波动的现场录音与参考曲目库匹配，面临编曲差异、背景噪声以及大规模连续音频高效处理等超出常规音乐版本识别的挑战，因此需要评估现代检索方法在该任务上的实际表现与成本效率。

Method: 在 ASID 基准上对比三类方法：既有 Re-MOVE 系统、CLEWS 以及内部神经音频版本嵌入模型 NAVE；采用片段级（segment-level）音频嵌入进行检索，并额外评估音乐/非音乐门控（gating）预处理对检测效果的影响。

Result: NAVE 与 CLEWS 显著优于 Re-MOVE；NAVE 使用 256 维嵌入（CLEWS 为 1024 维），嵌入存储降低四倍的同时检索性能更优。音乐/非音乐门控带来检测与时序覆盖度的适度提升，定性分析显示其可改善识别边界，超出标准 ASID 指标的刻画范围。

Conclusion: 片段级音频嵌入为可扩展的曲目单识别提供了坚实基础；紧凑表征可在真实现场演出条件下以更低的存储与服务成本达到较高准确率。

Abstract: Automatic setlist identification aims to identify the songs performed in live concert recordings by matching noisy and variable-quality audio against a database of reference tracks. This task presents challenges beyond conventional music version identification, including substantial arrangement differences, background noise, and the need to process continuous recordings efficiently at large scale. In this work, we evaluate modern music version retrieval methods for setlist identification using the ASID benchmark, comparing the established Re-MOVE system with CLEWS and NAVE, an internal neural audio version embedding model. Our results show that NAVE and CLEWS substantially outperform Re-MOVE. Notably, NAVE uses 256-dimensional embeddings, compared with 1024 dimensions for CLEWS, providing a four-fold reduction in embedding storage while achieving better retrieval performance. We further evaluate music/non-music gating and find modest improvements in detection and temporal coverage, while qualitative analysis suggests that such preprocessing can improve recognition boundaries beyond what the standard ASID metrics capture. Overall, the results demonstrate that segment-level audio embeddings provide a strong basis for scalable setlist identification and that compact representations can achieve strong accuracy under realistic live-concert conditions at much lower storage and serving cost.

</details>


### [216] [Sheaf-Inspired Structured Scoring Extensions of LightGCN for Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17226)
*Ksenia Belkovskaya,Timur Lidzhiev,Maria Stebliy,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在LightGCN三层骨干上加载受层论（sheaf）启发的模块化打分扩展，比较恒等内积、对角、秩一低秩与全矩阵残差打分器及参数匹配的残差MLP基线，在四个数据集上报告排名质量与目录覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞层（cellular sheaf）中局部向量空间通过线性限制映射相联系，这一结构启发对用户与物品嵌入施加成对依赖的结构化变换，而非修改图传播过程本身。

Method: 以三层LightGCN为固定骨干，仅在打分阶段引入模块化扩展：恒等内积打分器、对角变换、秩一低秩变换、全矩阵残差打分器，并设参数匹配的残差MLP基线（mlp_full）；在Amazon-Book、Gowalla、MovieLens-1M、Yelp2018四个数据集上评估 cutoff 10 与 20 的排序质量与目录覆盖率。

Result: 所选结构化变体带来的排序质量变化幅度较小且依赖数据集，而覆盖率有时出现显著变化。

Conclusion: 方法仅是受层论几何启发，并未实际构建细胞层或实现sheaf-Laplacian扩散，因此实验无法确立层论几何本身带来的因果收益。

Abstract: Graph-based collaborative filtering learns user and item representations by propagating embeddings over a user--item interaction graph. We investigate a modular, sheaf-inspired scoring extension placed on top of a three-layer LightGCN backbone. The motivation comes from cellular sheaves, where local vector spaces are related by linear restriction maps; here, this idea motivates structured, pair-dependent transformations of user and item embeddings rather than a change to graph propagation. We compare the identity inner-product scorer, diagonal, rank-one low-rank, and full matrix residual scorers, and a parameter-matched residual MLP baseline (\texttt{mlp\_full}) on Amazon-Book, Gowalla, MovieLens-1M, and Yelp2018. We report ranking quality and catalog coverage at cutoffs 10 and 20. The selected structured variants show modest, dataset-dependent changes in ranking quality and sometimes substantial changes in coverage. Our method is inspired by sheaf geometry but does not construct a cellular sheaf or implement sheaf-Laplacian diffusion; the experiments therefore do not establish a causal benefit from sheaf geometry itself.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Zugwise: Domain-Aware Game Reconstruction for Digitizing Handwritten Chess Scoresheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14544)
*Gerhard Trippen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Digitizing handwritten chess scoresheets has been studied as a handwriting-recognition problem: read the move in each box and check that it is legal. This framing misses the errors that matter most. A misread that yields an illegal move is loud. One that yields a legal-but-wrong move is silent, because a legality-only pipeline accepts it and reads every later move against a wrong position. We therefore treat the task as game reconstruction and present Zugwise, which repurposes computer-chess machinery (quiescence search, static exchange evaluation, pin detection) to ask not "what should be played?" but "could a human plausibly have written this?" An absurdity detector makes silent errors loud at or near where they occur, and a backward search traces late symptoms to their cause. Candidate ranking excludes chess-quality signals. A Needleman-Wunsch merge adds the second player's sheet as evidence, and review stops send every disagreement between the sheets' legal readings to a human. In oracle replay of two tournament sections, a release adding them cuts silent errors from 16 to 1 and from 50 to 13. Two learned signals, CTC re-scoring against the recognizer's raw output and a Maia-style human-move prior, raise the correct fix's top-rank rate from 58.8% to 78.0% of the replacement decisions where it is a candidate, and hold without refitting on a later section. On the first section, the human-verified output matches an independent expert transcription move for move on 34 of 45 games, and adjudication against the sheets found errors in both records, on different plies. We do not establish that either workflow is faster or more accurate. The system runs entirely in the browser and is free, private, and offline-capable; alongside it we offer a three-way adjudication protocol for a domain where no single record is ground truth.

</details>


### [218] [Cleaning an Agent's History Hurts When the World Still Holds the Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14654)
*Egor Pakhomov,Anurag Koul,Hongjun Liu,Bo Pang,Shafiq Joty,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究历史编辑（压缩/修复/交接）对工具型语言模型智能体的影响，发现正确内容的效果取决于历史的表述方式与世界实际状态：事实性提示可提升恢复率，而伪造的"已完成动作"与世界未修正时会降低表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型智能体历史中积累自身错误，压缩/修复/交接机制在陈述这些错误时，其后果仍留在环境中。已有工作仅在"世界固定"设定下清理历史，将展示修正步骤与宣称完成视为一体，未区分"历史如何表述"与"世界状态如何"两个维度。

Method: 冻结智能体在任务中途的精确世界状态，分别编辑历史或世界，再由接收模型继续任务。AgentCE-Bench：智能体写入错误值后固定世界，以两种自然编辑加入正确值——保留错误旁的如实提示，或智能体自身"已完成动作"；并设匹配的中性提示作对照。AppWorld：数据库写入后，操纵回执报告成功/失败与效果是否存在，跨两个接收模型（共八次接收）评估。

Result: AgentCE-Bench 中，如实提示将恢复率由中性提示的 51% 提升至 66%；"已完成动作"使其降至 44%，低于未编辑历史的 50%。AppWorld 中，成功回执在效果缺失时使全任务成功率由 45% 降至 28%，在效果存在时由 38% 升至 49%；两效应合并相差 27 个百分点，八次接收中六次在 97.5% 水平为正、七次在 95% 水平为正。

Conclusion: 历史编辑的效果取决于其所描述的世界状态；仅凭正确值是否出现无法预测效果。当历史被修饰得干净而世界仍留有错误时，智能体可能比不编辑更差。历史修复机制须与世界修复保持一致。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents accumulate histories of their own mistakes, and a system that compacts, repairs or hands off such a history decides what it says about those mistakes while their effects stay in the world. Prior work cleans the history at a fixed world, where showing a corrected step and saying it is done move together. We ask whether the same correct content helps or hurts depending on how the history states it and what the world holds. We freeze an agent mid-task with its exact world state, edit the history or the world, and let a receiver model continue. On AgentCE-Bench, after the agent commits a wrong value, we hold the world fixed and add the correct value by two natural edits: a truthful note beside the preserved error or the agent's own completed action. The note raises recovery, the share of remaining targets the receiver gets right, from 51% under a matched neutral note to 66%. The completed action lowers it below the unedited history, from 50% to 44%, so the value's presence alone does not predict the effect. On AppWorld, after a database write, we vary whether its receipt reports success or failure and whether its effect is present. A success receipt lowers whole-task success from 45% to 28% while the effect is absent and raises it from 38% to 49% once present. The two effects differ by 27 points pooled over two receivers, a difference positive in six of eight receivers at 97.5% and seven at 95%. A history edit's effect thus depends on the world it describes, and a history made to look clean while the world still holds the error can leave an agent worse off than no edit at all.

</details>


### [219] [Together Compiler: Continual Compilation for AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14681)
*Reyna Abhyankar,Dylan Lim,Drew Wadsworth,Venkata Hasith Vattikuti,YuTong Julia Zhang,Jung Hwan Heo,Taeksang Kim,Kesavan Ramakrishnan,Evangelene Stanley,Xinyu Fang,Daniel Y. Fu,Simran Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TKCC 是首个面向 AI 推理的"持续化"智能体编译闭环，通过分层持久引擎解耦引擎启动开销与前向计算，并用代价模型选择并行配置进行种子搜索，在 Llama 1B 上较手写 Megakernel 快 1.7x，在 InferenceX 上较最佳开源引擎 TPS 高 2.6x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 负载快速变化：模型发布频繁、架构多样化、流量分布漂移，因此需要持续编译优化。现有系统在"测量吞吐"与"测量保真度"间存在权衡：基于离线 benchmark 优化单 kernel 的方案因 MoE 等原语的数据依赖行为导致计时低保真；端到端引擎级优化则受引擎启动时间长拖累。

Method: (1) Tiered Persistent Engine：将引擎启动开销与前向计算解耦，提升可测量吞吐；(2) 代价模型：为优化过程选取种子并行配置；(3) 智能体上下文管理的系统性消融；并将编译器部署为持续智能体爬山（hill-climbing）闭环。

Result: Llama 1B 主力负载上较高度优化的手写 Megakernel 最高 1.7x；InferenceX 基准上在 Kimi K3、GLM 5.3、MiniMax M3.1 Flash、DeepSeek v4.1 Flash 等前沿模型上跨硬件平台较最佳开源引擎最高 2.6x TPS；batch size 1 下达到 SOTA（M3.1 Flash 1111 TPS，DeepSeek v4.1 Flash 1205 TPS）；生产集群已服务数万亿 token。

Conclusion: 分层持久引擎+代价模型种子的持续智能体闭环可有效突破吞吐-保真度权衡，且具备超越前沿 LLM 推理的泛化能力与生产可部署性。

Abstract: Today's AI workloads are rapidly changing: new models are released more often, model architectures are increasingly diverse, and traffic distributions change frequently. The challenge we face at Together AI is continual optimization for these changing workloads. Compiling a deployment into an optimized forward pass is a challenge that is well-suited for agent hill-climbing. Existing systems struggle with continual compilation due to a tradeoff between measurement throughput and fidelity. Many systems use agents to optimize individual kernels from the inference forward pass using offline benchmarks, but timings are low-fidelity because of data-dependent behavior in primitives like mixture-of-experts (MoE). On the other hand, systems use agents to directly optimize end-to-end engines but suffer from slow engine launch times. To address these challenges, we build Together Compiler (TKCC), the first truly continual agent loop designed for AI inference optimization. To address the throughput-fidelity tradeoff, we propose: (1) a Tiered Persistent Engine that decouples engine launch overheads from forward pass computation; (2) a cost model to select the parallelism configurations that seed the optimization process. We also conduct extensive ablations on agent context management and present our insights on deploying TKCC in production. TKCC outperforms highly-optimized hand-written Megakernels on the Llama 1B workhorse workload by up to 1.7x and the best open source engines on the latest frontier models (Kimi K3, GLM 5.3, MiniMax M3.1 Flash, DeepSeek v4.1 Flash) across hardware platforms by up to 2.6x higher TPS on the InferenceX benchmark. TKCC also sets the state-of-the-art performance at batch size 1, with 1111 TPS on M3.1 Flash and 1205 TPS on DeepSeek v4.1 Flash. Finally, TKCC generalizes beyond frontier language model inference. TKCC serves trillions of tokens across our production fleet.

</details>


### [220] [When Should Semantic Priors Enter Partially-Identified Causal Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14736)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Saad Bin Ather,Valentin Six,Lancelot Da Costa,Tommaso Salvatori,Bernhard Scholkopf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文系统拆解LLM辅助因果发现中的三个决策环节（查询分配、提案准入、DAG保留），区分总体不可识别性与有限样本不稳定性，并发现语义信息本身有效但分配、准入与保留机制的可靠性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测因果发现常留下未定向或不稳定的因果方向；LLM可提供语义信息定向，但错误提案可能排除真实因果图并使下游推断失效。作者希望厘清语义信息在何处、以何种方式真正带来价值。

Method: 将流程分解为三项独立决策：(1) 语义查询的分配位置；(2) 提案的准入判断；(3) 因果图的保留策略。框架区分总体非可识别性与有限样本不稳定，将语义定向与统计对照及随机对照比较，并考察校准准入证书与容错DAG保留。

Result: 基准网络实验显示：语义信息相较随机（抛硬币式）定向能提升结构恢复；但针对不稳定边的查询分配相对匹配随机分配无确立优势；所测试的准入证书在现有校准任务下无法支持非平凡准入；下游保留依赖参考充分性——80个计划任务中学习到的参考仅3个包含真实DAG。

Conclusion: 研究将语义信息的价值与其分配、准入、保留环节的可靠性相分离，指出校准规模与参考充分性是关键瓶颈；语义方向先验有用，但围绕它的流程设计仍需实质性改进。

Abstract: Observational causal discovery often leaves causal di- rections unresolved or unstable. Large language mod- els (LLMs) can provide semantic information to resolve these directions, but incorrect proposals can exclude the true causal graph and invalidate downstream inference. We study three separate decisions: where to allocate semantic queries, which proposals to admit, and which causal graphs to retain. Our framework distinguishes population non-identifiability from finite-sample insta- bility, evaluates semantic orientation against statistical and random controls, and examines calibrated admis- sion and error-tolerant DAG retention. Experiments across benchmark networks show that semantic infor- mation improves recovery over coin-flip orientations, but targeting unstable edges has no established ad- vantage over matched-random allocation. The tested admission certificate cannot support nontrivial admis- sion with the available calibration tasks. Moreover, downstream retention depends on reference adequacy: learned references contain the true DAG in only three of 80 planned tasks. These findings separate the value of semantic information from the reliability of its alloca- tion, admission, and retention, and identify calibration size and reference adequacy as key limitations.

</details>


### [221] [SynthCorp: A Platform for Creating Benchmarks for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14765)
*Jiani Zhang,Manav Garg,Eugenie Lai,Cheng Zhen,Eva Sharma,Yang Pan,Yaming Lin,Alon Halevy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SynthCorp 是一个通过模拟企业事件如何随时间产生与修改公司数据，来构建可定制数据资产与基准测试的平台，用于评测 LLM 数据智能体的特定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 数据智能体基准迅速饱和，且难以针对特定能力进行定向评测，缺乏可定制、可控复杂度的评测环境。

Method: 四阶段流程：(1) 依据场景与目标复杂度维度合成可复用事件类型，并编译为基于预定义仿真原语的程序；(2) 在虚拟日历上调度事件实例，交错安排常规事件与扰动事件，保持表、文档与目录条目的一致性；(3) 执行日历以物化数据资产，并将所有操作记录到仿真日志；(4) 生成分析任务，并从日志中推导出经校验的 gold 程序。

Result: 生成的首批评测基准包含 7 个数据资产（最多 1K 张业务表）与覆盖 5 个领域的 256 个任务；数据资产以近零错误率通过确定性流水线检查（外键违规、未声明写入、重复性违规、数据库执行失败），引入跨资产复杂性并覆盖全部请求维度；在四种数据上下文设置下评测三个模型，基准可区分数据上下文与模型层级；通过对照仿真日志中的因果溯源审计候选查询，无需人工标注即可校验 gold 程序，并在多事件复合任务上暴露出前沿智能体的推理缺陷。

Conclusion: SynthCorp 提供了可定制、可验证、可扩展的企业数据智能体基准生成方案，期望社区借此构建更具挑战性的数据资产与基准，推动企业级数据智能体的发展。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for LLM-driven data agents saturate quickly and cannot target specific capabilities. We present SynthCorp, a platform that creates customizable data estates and benchmarks by simulating how business events produce and modify corporate data over time. Each data estate combines relational databases, a document store, and a data catalog, paired with evaluation tasks and executable gold programs. First, given a scenario and target complexity dimensions, SynthCorp synthesizes reusable event types and compiles each into a program over predefined simulation primitives. Second, SynthCorp schedules event instances on a virtual calendar, interleaving routine and disruption events so that tables, documents, and catalog entries remain consistent. Third, SynthCorp executes the calendar to materialize the data estate and records every operation in a simulation log. Fourth, SynthCorp generates analytical tasks and derives verified gold programs from the log. We use SynthCorp to generate initial evaluation benchmarks comprising seven data estates (up to 1K operational tables) and 256 tasks across five domains. The data estates pass deterministic pipeline checks (such as foreign-key violations, undeclared writes, recurrence violations, and database execution failures) with a near-zero error rate, introduce cross-asset complexities, and cover all requested dimensions. Evaluating three models across four data-context settings shows that the benchmarks separate data contexts and model tiers. Auditing candidate queries against causal provenance in the simulation log verifies gold programs without manual annotation, including on multi-event composite tasks that expose reasoning failures in state-of-the-art agents. We hope the community uses SynthCorp to generate more challenging and useful data estates and benchmarks that foster the development of enterprise data agents.

</details>


### [222] [Rational Use of Time for Social Coordination in Humans and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14766)
*Xiulin Yang,Sirisha Gudavalli,Jennifer Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文考察人类与AI如何利用隐性的时间线索（如停顿时长）进行动态社会协调，并检验不具"实时体验"的AI能否类似地推理他者心智。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明人类能利用时间线索推断他人心理状态，但尚不清楚人们如何将时间线索用于动态、细粒度的社会协调；同时，不具连续实时体验的AI系统能否实现同样能力也未知。

Method: 采用禁止显式沟通、唯一信号是时间流逝的合作卡牌任务The Mind；评估150名人类，以及两类AI模型（计算机使用智能体CUAs与标准文本提示LLM），并检验被试行为是否符合从对方计时推断其信念的贝叶斯策略。

Result: 人类行为最好由基于对方计时推断其信念的贝叶斯策略刻画，前沿模型亦如此；但与人类不同，所测模型在游戏过程中似乎未能有效适应伙伴行为。

Conclusion: 人类在动态社会情境中对连续时间信号进行隐性概率推理，且此类机制可能无需实时体验即可涌现；研究也揭示了AI系统在动态环境中通过解读并适应隐性信号实现高效协调的新挑战。

Abstract: All social interactions are situated in time. Previous studies have shown that humans are able to use implicit temporal cues, such as the duration of a pause, to infer others' mental states. But how do humans use temporal cues for dynamic, fine-grained social coordination? And can AI systems do the same, without ``experiencing'' continuous real-time as humans do? We study these questions using a cooperative card game task called The Mind, which requires players to achieve a shared goal across repeated interactions but forbids explicit communication: the only available signal is the passage of time. We evaluate 150 humans, and two classes of AI models which reflect different ways of operationalizing time in artificial systems: computer-use agents (CUAs), and standard text-prompted LLMs. We find that human behavior is best captured by a Bayesian strategy that infers a partner's belief from their timing---and similarly for frontier models. However, unlike humans, none of the tested models seem to efficiently adapt their behaviors to their partners' over the course of the game. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that humans perform implicit probabilistic reasoning about continuous-time signals in dynamic social settings, and that such reasoning mechanisms may emerge without real-time experience. Practically, our study highlights new challenges for AI systems to coordinate efficiently with other agents by learning to interpret and adapt to their implicit signals in dynamic settings.

</details>


### [223] [Leaderboard Hacking: Preference-Based Model Evaluations are Vulnerable to Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14769)
*Eve Fleisig,Joachim Baumann,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arena-style 排行榜对策略性投票、策略性提示和策略性提名三类操纵高度脆弱，各攻击可使模型排名提升15-20位，组合可达45位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arena 式排行榜被广泛用于在线榜单、论文和生产中的模型评估，旨在通过匿名模型的成对比较与 Bradley-Terry 聚合真实反映模型能力，但其操纵脆弱性尚未被系统检验。

Method: 在公开排行榜数据上实证演示三类操纵：策略性投票（含对无关模型的投票）、策略性提示（选择有利于目标模型的提示）、策略性提名（插入无关模型以抬高目标模型排名）；分析脆弱性成因，并针对每种攻击提出防护方法。

Result: 这些操纵能提升几乎每个被测模型的排名；单一攻击可使模型排名上升最多15-20位，组合使用最多可上升45位。

Conclusion: 该问题适用于任何允许开放提示、投票或模型提交的 Bradley-Terry 偏好评估框架；需针对各攻击类型采取相应防护措施。

Abstract: Arena-style rankings of language models are a widely used evaluation framework in online leaderboards, research papers, and model evaluation in production. These rankings aim to realistically assess model performance using pairwise comparisons of anonymous models on user prompts, aggregating votes via the Bradley-Terry model to produce the final ranking. However, we find that these leaderboards are highly vulnerable to several manipulation strategies that unscrupulous providers could use to boost a model's rank: strategic voting (individual votes that boost a model's rank, including votes on irrelevant models), strategic prompting (choosing prompts that favor a model), and strategic nomination (boosting the rank of a model by inserting irrelevant models). We demonstrate these effects on public leaderboard data, analyze the circumstances that cause these vulnerabilities, and describe approaches to safeguard against each attack. Notably, these issues hold for any preference-based evaluation using the Bradley-Terry model that allows open prompting, voting, or model submission. These manipulations boost the rank of nearly every tested model; each attack can boost a model by up to 15-20 places in a ranking and, combined, they can boost a model by up to 45 places.

</details>


### [224] [Robust Task-Aware State Estimation from Pre-trained Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14781)
*Guy Azran,Sarah Keren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以预训练感知模型（如VLM）自身输出概率构建 θ-置信最小信念集（MBS_θ），并设计PRaVaSS算法在保持多项式复杂度的前提下构造该集合，用于提升下游决策的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用VLM等感知模型做状态估计的智能体面临预测不确定性：多数方法将预测确定化为单一"最优猜测"状态并当作真值使用，一旦猜测错误，决策可能不安全或不可行；而维护完整符号状态概率分布又往往不可行。因此需要一条兼顾鲁棒性与可计算性的中间路径。

Method: 利用感知模型已有的底层概率（如VLM输出层token分布）构造MBS_θ——累积概率达到用户指定信念质量阈值的最小状态子集。采用新颖的三层信念系统，从逐特征预测与任务级约束中高效近似各状态概率，仅维护精简的因子化信念表示。基于此提出PRaVaSS（Probability-Ranked Valid State Search）算法，利用信念系统的特殊结构构造MBS_θ，并提供正确性保证；无约束情形下复杂度为多项式。

Result: 以VLM作为状态估计器主干进行验证，证实了方法的理论性质成立、随问题规模的可扩展性，以及接入任务规划流水线后下游决策性能的提升。

Conclusion: 从确定性单点估计与完整分布之间取折中，借助感知模型内在概率构造最小高概率状态集，是一种既鲁棒又可处理的不确定性感知状态估计与决策途径。

Abstract: Agents that perform state estimation using pretrained perception models, e.g., Vision-Language Models (VLMs), face the fundamental challenge that predictions are uncertain. Still, most approaches determinize these predictions into a single best-guess state and make decisions as if it were ground truth. This is brittle because when the guess is wrong, the agent's decisions may be unsafe or infeasible. At the other extreme, maintaining a full probability distribution over symbolic states is often intractable. We propose a middle path, drawing on the underlying probabilities the perception model already produces (e.g., a VLM's output-layer token distributions) to construct a $θ$-confidence Minimal Belief Set (MBS$_θ$), a minimal subset of states whose cumulative probability meets a user-specified belief-mass threshold. The minimality of an MBS$_θ$ ensures that the agent considers only the most likely states, enhancing robustness in downstream decision-making while maintaining computational tractability. Using a novel three-layered belief system, we efficiently approximate individual state probabilities from per-feature predictions and task-level constraints. On this belief construction, we base our MBS$_θ$ construction, while maintaining only a lean factored representation of the belief. Our algorithm, Probability-Ranked Valid State Search (PRaVaSS), takes advantage of the unique structure of this belief system to construct an MBS$_θ$ with correctness guarantees and polynomial complexity in the unconstrained case. Using a VLM as the backbone of our state estimator, we validate our method's theoretical properties, scalability with problem size, and improvement of downstream decision-making when used in a task-planning pipeline.

</details>


### [225] [When Is Proof-Step Verification Worth Its Cost? Operating Requirements of Certify-or-Decline Verification for Open-Weight Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14794)
*Abhinav Gupta,Ben Slivinski,Yashica Patodia,Michael Saldivar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估"证明步骤验证"流程：将解答改写为类型化步骤并由LLM评判器认证或拒答，在GPQA、AIME-2025与MATH-500上考察认证对答案精度与覆盖率的影响，并与token匹配的自一致性选择器对比，同时揭示失效模式与评估缺乏ground truth的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM答案验证依赖终答案键匹配，难以判断推理链有效性；需量化"证明步骤验证+LLM评判"带来的精度收益与覆盖率代价，并识别其失效来源与评估效度局限。

Method: 构建流程：把求解器答案重写为带类型标注的证明步骤，再由LLM评判器给出认证/拒答；在多个开放权重模型上于GPQA、AIME-2025及MATH-500子集评测；与输出token数匹配的自一致性选择器作对照；并做诊断样本分析、外部LLM对渲染认证链的接受度检验以及审计者间一致性分析。

Result: 认证使答案键匹配精度在强GPQA配置上提升31个百分点、AIME上提升46个百分点，但仅覆盖35%和21%的尝试；MATH-500接近饱和时增益≤5点、覆盖率下降13–27点。token匹配自一致性在35%覆盖率下精度82.9%，验证器汇总精度87.6%（配对差+4.8点，95%区间[-8.2, 18.5]）。诊断样本中88题有52题因无效证明构造失败、从未进入评判。外部LLM对认证链接受率61.0–67.6%，低于87.6%的答案键匹配。审计者间经机会校正一致性近零。

Conclusion: 证明步骤验证以覆盖率为代价换取答案精度，且执行失败与评判偏差普遍存在；缺乏人工或形式化内核ground truth，且审计者间一致性近零意味着度量的是自洽性而非证明真实性。建议分别报告执行失败率、所选答案精度与独立评估的证明有效性。

Abstract: Proof-step verification rewrites a solver's answer into typed steps and uses LLM judges to certify or decline. We evaluate this pipeline on open-weight models across GPQA, AIME-2025, and a MATH-500 subset. Certification raises answer-key-match precision by 31 percentage points on the strong GPQA configuration and 46 on AIME, while answering only 35% and 21% of attempts. On near-ceiling MATH-500, gains are at most 5 points at a loss of 13-27 points of coverage. An output-token-matched self-consistency selector obtains 82.9% precision at 35% coverage, compared with the verifier's pooled 87.6% (paired difference: +4.8 points, 95% interval [-8.2, 18.5]). A diagnostic sample reveals a distinct failure mode: 52 of 88 completed problems end in invalid proof construction without ever reaching a judge. Finally, external-LLM acceptance of rendered certified chains is 61.0-67.6%, compared with 87.6% answer-key match. These audits lack human or formal-kernel ground truth. Open-weight auditor pairs show near-zero chance-corrected agreement, which measures consistency rather than accuracy against proof truth. These results motivate separate reporting of execution failures, selected-answer precision, and independently assessed proof validity. We provide per-configuration results and evaluation details in the supplement.

</details>


### [226] [GenPFN: An In-Context Tabular Foundation Model Approach for Augmenting Small Datasets in Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14804)
*Noah J. Bagazinski,Cyril Picard,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练生成器 GenPFN，利用固定权重的预训练 TabPFN 分类器引导候选设计生成，经约束与目标评估后可扩充稀缺设计数据集并提升回归模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计中仿真与实验成本高，可获取的评估数据有限，导致训练数据稀缺、预测模型精度受限，需要一种能在少量已评估样本条件下生成高质量候选设计的方法。

Method: GenPFN 以已评估设计（标注为期望类/非期望类）作为上下文，输入固定权重的预训练 TabPFN 分类器；在期望设计之间插值，并借助门控分类器梯度与高斯噪声精化候选；每个候选由问题的约束与目标函数评估，被评估的期望设计及其目标值再回填上下文用于回归训练。

Result: 在14个问题上评测（含两参数基准与44参数船体问题）：配对比较中 GenPFN+TabPFN v1 的类别满足率更高、真实感距离更低，优于对抗、VAE 与扩散生成器；VAE 在覆盖度与多样性上更优；相比 SMOTE，GenPFN 覆盖距离更低、多样性更高；数据增强使 AutoGluon 验证 MSE 较仅用上下文训练降低 62.1%；船尾案例中 99.3% 候选与螺旋桨兼容，随机采样仅 1.4%。

Conclusion: 生成—评估流程可有效扩充有限数据集并支撑更精确的预测模型训练，为少数据工程设计提供免训练、可直接落地的候选生成方案。

Abstract: Costly simulations and experiments limit the data available for engineering design. This paper proposes GenPFN, a training-free candidate generator guided by a pretrained tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN) classifier with fixed weights. The context contains evaluated designs labeled desired (Class 1) or undesired (Class 0). GenPFN interpolates between desired designs and refines candidates using gated classifier gradients and Gaussian noise. Each candidate is evaluated using the problem's constraints and objective functions. Evaluated desired designs and their objective values augment the context for regression training.
  Evaluation spans fourteen selected problems, including a two-parameter benchmark and a 44-parameter hull problem. In paired comparisons, GenPFN with TabPFN v1 achieves higher class satisfaction and lower realism distance than the tested adversarial, variational-autoencoder and diffusion generators. The variational-autoencoder generator provides greater coverage and diversity. Relative to the synthetic minority over-sampling technique (SMOTE), GenPFN provides lower coverage distance and greater diversity. Among observed paired regression fits, augmentation reduces estimated AutoGluon validation mean squared error by 62.1% relative to context-only training. A hull-stern case study yields 99.3% propeller-compatible candidates versus 1.4% among randomly sampled context designs. Generation followed by evaluation produces sample sets that augment limited datasets and support training more accurate predictive models.

</details>


### [227] [AutoAssumption: Searching over Assumptions Discovers ML-Systems Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14844)
*Igor Sadalski,Minghao Guo,Benjamin Jones,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将算法改写为Lean中的证明图，显式化假设，把算法设计转化为可被LLM探索、可被证明检查器审计的结构化搜索空间，提出AutoAssumption自动化框架，在KV-cache驱逐与权值剪枝上超越所有已发表算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML系统进步常源于打破此前方法默认的假设（如投机解码放弃"每token一次前向"的假设），但算法很少显式陈述假设；现有专家与自动化搜索框架直接在程序上调参，一次编辑会同时改变多个设计选择，收益难以归因；直接对"假设本身"进行搜索仍未被充分探索。

Method: 将算法写成证明，使假设显式化，从而把算法设计转为小而结构化的搜索空间：把KV-cache驱逐、权值剪枝、投机解码共89个算法形式化为带显式假设的证明图并在Lean中检查；构建AutoAssumption框架，对已发表方法放松单一假设，重新推导、重新证明并生成对应代码。

Result: 在KV-cache驱逐与权值剪枝上，该搜索在作者运行的每个已发表算法基础上均有提升并得到SOTA算法：驱逐算法在所运行已发表方法中取得最高RULER-4K分数，剪枝算法在四个LLaMA模型上取得十三种方法中最低困惑度；同等预算下与AlphaEvolve开源实现OpenEvolve得分差距在1分以内，且多次重复的标准差更低。

Conclusion: 以证明形式显式化假设，可将算法设计重构为可审计、可自动化的结构化搜索，并实证有效；未来将形式化更多领域，并通过各领域共享的"主要假设"将它们相互关联。

Abstract: Many advances in ML systems came from breaking an assumption that earlier methods took for granted: speculative decoding dropped the assumption that a model needs one forward pass per token. Yet algorithms rarely state their assumptions, so experts and discovery harnesses search over programs, where one edit changes several design choices and gains are difficult to attribute. Searching over the assumptions themselves remains largely unexplored. Writing algorithms as proofs makes their assumptions explicit and turns algorithm design into a search over a small, structured space that an LLM can explore and a proof checker can audit. We formalize 89 algorithms from KV-cache eviction, weight pruning and speculative decoding as proof graphs with explicit assumptions, checked in Lean. Our harness, AutoAssumption, relaxes one assumption of a published method, re-derives and re-proves it, and generates its code. In KV-cache eviction and weight pruning, this search improved on every published algorithm we ran and produced state-of-the-art algorithms. The eviction algorithm has the highest RULER-4K score of the published methods we ran, and the pruning algorithm the lowest perplexity of thirteen on four LLaMA models. At equal budget, the harness scores within a point of OpenEvolve, an open-source implementation of AlphaEvolve, with a lower standard deviation across repeats. Future work will formalize more fields and combine them through the principal assumptions they share.

</details>


### [228] [Open Challenges and Opportunities in Human-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14846)
*Reuth Mirsky,Sarath Sreedharan,Nitay Alon,Stefan Sarkadi,Munindar P. Singh,Liz Sonenberg,Peter Stone,Silvia Tulli,Stylianos Loukas Vasileiou,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文综述AAMAS社区关于人-智能体交互的理论与计算基础，主张把人机交互视为持续的决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统越来越多地承担长时任务并代表人类行动，人机交互成为其有效性的核心；交互需要理解他人视角、协调互补能力并在社会技术环境中维持有效关系，因此需要梳理AAMAS社区的相关基础。

Method: 以"理解他人"与"决定如何交互"两个相互关联的挑战为组织框架，综述从个体伙伴关系到团队与更大群体的交互；涵盖ToM、BDI架构、目标与规划识别、逆规划、递归推理等心智状态表示与推断机制，以及观察者感知规划、模型调和、决策论通信、共享自主、论辩、合作逆强化学习等将推断连接到行动、解释与信息交换的方法。

Result: 系统性地将这些计算进路归类为心智状态推断与交互决策两大族，并指出它们共同构成兼顾人类知识、意图与期望演化的智能体系统基础。

Conclusion: 进展要求将人类交互视为由不确定性、相互依赖及潜在冲突目标所塑造的持续决策问题。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly undertake extended tasks and act on people's behalf, making human-agent interaction central to their effectiveness. Successful interaction requires understanding human perspectives, coordinating complementary capabilities, and maintaining productive relationships within socio-technical environments. This paper examines how foundations developed by the Autonomous Agents and Multi-Agent Systems (AAMAS) community inform these demands. We consider interactions ranging from individual human-agent partnerships to teams and larger groups, where participants differ in their roles, decision authority, capabilities, and objectives. We organize computational approaches around the connected challenges of understanding others and deciding how to interact with them. Theory of Mind, belief-desire-intention architectures, goal and plan recognition, inverse planning, and recursive reasoning provide mechanisms for representing and inferring partially observable mental states. Observer-aware planning, model reconciliation, and decision-theoretic communication connect these inferences to action, explanation, and information exchange. Together with shared autonomy, argumentation, and cooperative inverse reinforcement learning, these approaches offer foundations for agentic systems that account for evolving human knowledge, intentions, and expectations. We argue that progress requires treating human interaction as an ongoing decision problem shaped by uncertainty, interdependence, and potentially conflicting objectives.

</details>


### [229] [Effective Teacher Support for Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14850)
*Lavinia Ghita,Dhruv Desai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对教师最高概率词表子集做前向KL蒸馏，在仅保留0.01%词表时MMLU-Pro无退化，并降低38%训练时间与24%显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准前向KL语言模型蒸馏需在每个监督位置对全词表计算稠密师生打分，训练开销大且需存储昂贵的教师目标，因此探索能否仅在教师最高概率的少量词表支撑集上蒸馏。

Method: 从教师最高概率词表子集蒸馏，并对保留质量重新归一化，支撑集从100%扫描到0.01%。主实验为Qwen3 4B→1.7B，并在Qwen3 8B→4B与Nemotron Nano 12B→9B上及answer-only监督下复现；评估以MMLU-Pro为主、GPQA-Diamond为辅。另缓存教师前向中保留的下一token分数、并将学生输出计算限制在这些token上以降低开销。

Result: Qwen3各设置下保留0.01%词表相较全词表蒸馏在MMLU-Pro上无观测到退化，尽管与完整教师分布的差异增大；Nemotron Nano在0.1%时与全词表持平，更小比例则无法输出结束推理标签，说明可靠截断阈依赖模型族。0.01%支撑集下训练步时降低38%、峰值显存降低24%，且无MMLU-Pro退化。

Conclusion: 稀疏支撑集前向KL蒸馏可在极小词表比例下保持蒸馏质量并显著降低训练成本，但可靠截断阈值具有模型族依赖性，需逐族校准。

Abstract: Forward-KL language-model distillation normally computes dense teacher and student scores over the full vocabulary at every supervised position, making training and stored teacher targets expensive. We instead distill from the teacher's highest-probability vocabulary fraction, renormalizing the retained mass as we sweep support from 100% to 0.01%. Our main study distills Qwen3 4B -> 1.7B. We confirm the broader support-reduction finding on larger Qwen3 8B -> 4B and Nemotron Nano 12B -> 9B pairs and under answer-only supervision. We evaluate generation primarily on MMLU-Pro and secondarily on GPQA-Diamond. Across the Qwen3 settings, retaining 0.01% of the vocabulary causes no observed MMLU-Pro degradation relative to full-vocabulary distillation, although disagreement with the complete teacher distribution increases. Nemotron Nano matches full-vocabulary MMLU-Pro at 0.1%, but smaller fractions fail to emit the closing reasoning tag, showing that the reliable cutoff depends on the model family. At 0.01% support, caching the retained next-token scores from the teacher forward pass cuts training step time by 38%, while restricting student output computation to those tokens cuts peak memory by 24%; neither causes observed MMLU-Pro degradation.

</details>


### [230] [Controlling the Probability of Unknown Unknowns in Pre-Deployment Testing of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15835)
*Rachel Li,Chris Hays,Aaditya Ramdas,Manish Raghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种算法，用于判定"测试是否足够"：通过构造"未见失效模式概率"的 anytime-valid 上置信序列，开发者可反复测试并检查上界，当上界低于预设阈值时停止测试，且不破坏统计保证。该方法可视为 missing-mass 估计（Good-Turing）的序贯变体，并在真实编码智能体轨迹数据集 SWE-Chat 上验证了比多重检验校正的 Good-Turing 基线更紧的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 系统（如 AI agent）部署前测试中，已知失效模式可通过修复或应急预案处理，真正棘手的是"未知的未知"——测试中未出现的失效模式。停止测试与继续测试存在固有权衡：继续测试耗费时间与资源，停止部署则承担未发现失效模式的风险。因此需要一种原则性方法，判断何时测试已足够。

Method: 将问题建模为 missing-mass 估计问题的序贯变体，构造关于"观察到未见失效模式概率"的 anytime-valid 上置信序列（upper confidence sequence），使开发者可在任意时刻重复检查边界并决定停止而不破坏统计有效性；进一步扩展方法以处理来自不同任务分布的采样以及不完美的自动化失效标注。

Result: 在真实世界编码智能体轨迹数据集 SWE-Chat 上，该方法给出的上界比经多重检验校正的 Good-Turing 基线更紧，甚至优于固定时域（非多重检验校正）的 Good-Turing 上置信界——后者不具备 anytime validity。

Conclusion: 该工作为"何时停止测试并部署"提供了具有统计保证的序贯决策工具，扩展了经典 Good-Turing 估计到序贯、分布式偏移与标注不完美等现实评估场景，并在实际 agent 轨迹数据上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In pre-deployment testing of AI systems such as AI agents, a developer can often deal with known failure modes (e.g., leaking API keys, deleting important databases, or installing a malicious package) by implementing fixes or making contingency plans for when the same issue might occur again. What is more challenging are the unknown unknowns: the unseen failure modes that haven't surfaced during testing. The decision to stop testing and deploy therefore faces an inherent tradeoff: continue testing to observe more behaviors, at the cost of time and resources, or stop testing and deploy, at the risk of failure modes remaining undiscovered. How then does the developer decide when they have tested enough? To answer this, we develop an algorithm that certifies when the probability of observing an unseen failure mode is sufficiently small. The algorithm constructs an anytime-valid upper confidence sequence on this quantity, so the developer can repeatedly test a system, inspect the bound, and decide to stop when it falls below a chosen threshold without invalidating the statistical guarantee. Our problem can be interpreted as a sequential variant of the missing-mass estimation problem which gave rise to the classic Good-Turing estimator (Good, 1953). On SWE-Chat, a dataset of real-world coding agent traces (Baumann et al., 2026), we implement our procedure and show that it achieves tighter upper bounds than a simple multiple-testing corrected Good-Turing baseline, and even dominates a fixed-horizon (non-multiple-testing-corrected) Good-Turing upper confidence bound, which is not anytime valid. Additionally, to better match realistic evaluation pipelines, we extend our method to handle sampling from a different task distribution and imperfect automated failure labeling.

</details>


### [231] [Can an AI Agent Be Epistemically Akratic? Higher-Order Evidence & the Limits of Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14889)
*Eno Agolli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当AI代理判断自身推理有缺陷时，不应直接放弃结论；否则可能削弱其区分有效与无效推理的能力。负面自评的价值在于标记需进一步审查的结论，而非赋予其删除结论的权威。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI自我纠错的核心假设——让代理的结论与其对结论合理性的自评达成一致，就能提升纠错能力——提出质疑。

Method: 哲学/认识论论证：构造一个自评部分无法使用推理信息的代理模型，论证其可通过保留被判为不合理的结论来行使认知能力；并指出通过训练达成"一致性"可通过降低结论质量或降低评估质量两条路径实现。

Result: 即使自评总体可靠，代理保留其判定为不合理的结论仍可能是认识论上合理的；结论与评估的一致本身不保证纠错能力提升，因为一致性可由任一方退化达成。

Conclusion: 负面评估应被理解为识别需进一步审查的结论的信号，而非具有消除结论的权威；这挑战了关于AI自我纠错的中心假设。

Abstract: When we judge that our reasoning was defective, it seems plausible that we should abandon the conclusion it produced. This thought has special force for AI agents built to assess and correct their own work. Yet obeying such judgments can make an agent worse at distinguishing sound reasoning from defective reasoning. An AI agent may rely on information about its reasoning when deciding which conclusions to keep, while the part that judges whether those conclusions are justified cannot use that information. The agent can then exercise an epistemic competence by retaining conclusions it judges unjustified, even when its self-assessments are generally reliable. Nor is the difficulty resolved by training the agent to make its conclusions and assessments agree: agreement can be achieved by degrading either one. Crucially, I argue that a negative assessment can remain valuable by identifying conclusions requiring further scrutiny, without having authority to eliminate them. The result challenges a central assumption about AI self-correction: that bringing an agent's beliefs into agreement with its own self-assessment about their justification makes it better at correcting its mistakes.

</details>


### [232] [MuEdit: An efficient multi-task editing method towards inter-domain knowledge conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14910)
*Songshi Liang,Ting-En Lin,Zixuan Huang,Yuchuan Wu,Yongbin Li,Zihe Wang,Rui Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出域间零空间错位解释多任务知识编辑崩溃，并用 Conflict Index 量化冲突、以 Mu-Edit（冲突感知排序+动态低秩近似）缓解冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 需在异构领域频繁更新知识，但现有编辑方法顺序应用于多任务场景时性能崩溃。作者将其归因于域间零空间错位：不同任务诱发各自的 Jacobian 几何，共同零空间急剧收缩，导致可用于无冲突更新的可行方向不足。

Method: 1) 提出 Conflict Index 量化几何干扰；2) Mu-Edit 采用冲突感知排序以最小化累积干扰；3) 当仅靠排序不足时，用动态低秩近似扩张零空间。

Result: 在五个功能领域、三个骨干模型上，多任务编辑性能显著优于现有基线，同时保持通用能力，并在任务增量到达时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 零空间几何视角可解释多任务编辑的失败机制，排序与零空间扩张的组合策略为多任务及增量式知识编辑提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require frequent knowledge updates across heterogeneous domains, but existing editing methods fail when applied sequentially to multi-task settings. We trace this collapse to inter-domain null-space misalignment: heterogeneous tasks induce distinct Jacobian geometries, causing their common null space to shrink dramatically and leaving insufficient admissible directions for conflict-free updates. We propose the Conflict Index to quantify this geometric interference, and introduce Mu-Edit, which mitigates multi-task conflicts via (1) conflict-aware ordering to minimize cumulative interference, and (2) dynamic low-rank approximation to expand the null space when ordering alone is insufficient. Experiments across five functional domains and three backbones show that Mu-Edit significantly outperforms existing baselines in multi-task editing performance while preserving general capabilities, and remains effective under incremental task arrival.

</details>


### [233] [Edge-Native Multimodal RAG: Hardware-Aware Quantization and Architectural Optimization for Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14924)
*Zahraa Selim,Menna Hamed,Wesam Ahmed,Sohyla Said,Sara Basheer,Rami Zewail*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在消费级Turing架构GPU（如Tesla T4）上对面向Edge-AI的多模态RAG框架进行系统级工程集成与实证评估，分析GPTQ与NF4两种量化方案在吞吐与长上下文稳定性上的权衡，并提出NF4 + 解耦Florence-2视觉模块的优化架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端RAG部署受内存与带宽制约：7B模型FP16需14GB显存，无法与向量索引和KV缓存共存，因此需要在受限硬件上寻找可行的量化与架构方案。

Method: 系统级实证分析：对比GPTQ与NF4量化在Turing硬件上的吞吐与长上下文表现；将NF4与解耦的Florence-2视觉集成；采用结构化/少样本提示进行检索空间剪枝与推理补偿；测量显存占用、延迟与计算强度（1.88 GFLOPs/token）。

Result: GPTQ吞吐更高（51.6 tok/s）但在2048+ token上下文出现明显不稳定；NF4长上下文稳健，延迟81 ms/tok；视觉推理仅增430MB显存；结构化提示降低47%延迟而不改变计算强度；整体显存占用8.37GB，适配12–16GB GPU；结构化少样本提示将数学准确率从35.0%恢复至92.0%（200次试验）。

Conclusion: 在Turing架构消费级GPU上，NF4配合解耦视觉集成与结构化提示是兼顾长上下文稳定性、显存可行性与精度的实用RAG方案，且提示工程可有效补偿量化导致的推理损失。

Abstract: Deployment of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for Edge-AI is constrained by memory and bandwidth limitations. A standard 7B parameter model consumes 14 GB Video Random Access Memory (VRAM) in half-precision (FP16), precluding co-location of vector indices and Key-Value (KV) caches. This paper presents an engineering integration and empirical evaluation of a multimodal RAG framework optimized for consumer-grade Turing hardware (e.g., NVIDIA Tesla T4). Our system-level analysis identifies a critical trade-off: while generative pre-trained transformer quantization (GPTQ) offers superior throughput (51.6 tok/s), it exhibits significant context instability at 2048+ tokens on Turing architectures. Conversely, NormalFloat 4-bit (NF4) maintains robust long-context performance with viable latency (81 ms/tok). By coupling NF4 with a decoupled Florence-2 vision integration, we achieve visual reasoning with only 430 MB VRAM overhead. Furthermore, empirical observations of prompt-induced search space pruning show that structured prompting reduces latency by 47% without altering computational intensity (1.88 GFLOPs/token). The integrated architecture maintains an 8.37 GB footprint, establishing a viable RAG pipeline for 12-16 GB GPUs. Finally, structured few-shot prompting effectively compensates for quantization-induced reasoning loss, restoring mathematical accuracy from 35.0% to 92.0% across 200 trials.

</details>


### [234] [Between Niti and Nyaya: A Society-Centered Framework for AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16173)
*Snehalkumar `Neil' S. Gaikwad,Punya Aragula,Shiva Kruthi Palpanoori,Pratik Patekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"社会中心AI治理框架"，以个人能否获得通知、提出异议、获得纠正三项能力评估任一辖区的AI治理工具；在北卡罗来纳州(9位AI领导委员会成员访谈+49份19州治理文件条款级编码)发现"可见性优先"治理：可见性义务已生效，通知仅为机构职责而非可援引权利，挑战与纠正仍属建议性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府在人能测量AI公共影响之前就已做出AI决策，而这些决策决定了个人能否知情、质疑并纠正受影响结果。既有研究记录了AI治理产出(原则、战略、登记册、审计)，但缺乏在政策仍在形成阶段即可理解与评估AI政策的共享语言与框架。

Method: 提出Society-Centered AI Governance Framework：按日期与约束力状态整体解读辖区工具，区分"机构职责"与"个人可援引权利"，并追踪每项承诺须由哪些后续工具执行。实证上采用两阶段访谈(州2026 AI路线图前后)9名北卡罗来纳州AI领导委员会成员，并对19个州49份AI治理文件进行条款级人工复核+模型辅助编码。

Result: 对州自身AI系统呈现"可见性优先"治理：可见性职责已生效；通知仅为机构职责，无个人可援引权利；AI专项的挑战与纠正机制仍为建议性。路线图已为缺失的保护措施指定责任方与时间表，即缺口被记录但尚未闭合。近期联邦先占提案聚焦AI开发，故州自身系统的救济途径大概率仍由州决定。

Conclusion: 提出"合法性先于保护"(legitimacy before protection)作为可见性优先治理的可检验风险，并列出检验该风险的里程碑。更广义上，以"谁能对AI所揭示的内容采取行动"评判AI政策，使该领域能在政策仍在形成、损害尚未发生前评估救济能力。

Abstract: Governments are making decisions about AI before they can measure its public effects, and those decisions determine whether a person can learn that AI influenced an outcome, challenge it, and see it corrected. Research has documented what AI governance produces: principles, strategies, registers, and audits. However, we lack shared language for understanding AI policy while it is still being made. We introduce the Society-Centered AI Governance Framework, which gives policymakers, researchers, and the public a way to assess instruments in any jurisdiction by whether a person can get notice, challenge a decision, and see it corrected. It reads a jurisdiction's instruments together by date and binding status, separates an agency duty from a right a person can invoke, and traces which later instrument must enforce each commitment. We evaluated it in North Carolina using interviews with nine AI Leadership Council members, conducted before and after the state's 2026 AI roadmap, and a provision-level reading, with human-reviewed model-assisted coding, of 49 AI governance documents from 19 states. For the state's own AI systems, we find visibility-first governance: visibility duties are in effect, notice exists as an agency duty with no right a person can invoke, and AI-specific challenge and correction remain advisory. The roadmap assigns owners and dates to the missing protections, so the gaps are documented but not yet closed. Recent federal preemption proposals focus on AI development, so recourse for a state's own systems is likely to remain a state decision. We identify "legitimacy before protection" as a testable risk of visibility-first governance and name the milestones that would test it. More broadly, judging AI policy by who can act on what it makes visible lets the field assess recourse while policy is still forming, before harm occurs.

</details>


### [235] [Provably Optimal HBT Legalization in Seconds: Certified Optima Reveal Where the Headroom Really Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14928)
*Kadhir Parani,Manjunath Bezawada,Divya M,Kesana Jathin,Kolli Sarath,Sm Zobaed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建21个HBT合法化开放基准实例，重建SW、贪心、BTAssign式基线，并提出精确求解器GEO-Exact（惰性生成空间候选边的连续最短增广路径），配合独立对偶证书证明全局最优。结果显示：ViaLegal默认SW配置仅比最优高0.011%（最差0.119%），16个争用实例上BTAssign式、DGHL、贪心分别高3.0%、6.9%、64.5%；仅在2x2窗口或利用率>~58%等区域出现显著余量。据此质疑Open3DBench所报RL相对SW的19–22%增益的可比性，并开源代码与证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBT合法化旨在以最小曼哈顿位移将3D IC每个端子的分配至各自pitch-grid站点。Open3DBench报告RL合法器较滑窗（SW）启发式降低19–22%位移，但未公开任何合法器，导致该增益的真实性与剩余优化空间无法核验。作者希望量化"任何合法器还能回收多少位移"，即在可复现的开放基线上给出可证最优的位移下界与各启发式相对最优的差距。

Method: (1) 由Open3DBench布局数据构建21个设计/pitch实例的开放基准；(2) 重建greedy、SW（ViaLegal式）与BTAssign式基线，以及密度引导启发式DGHL；(3) 提出GEO-Exact：在惰性生成的空间候选边上运行连续最短增广路径（SSAP）求解分配问题，并由独立对偶证书验证最优性；(4) 在名义pitch及2x2窗口、利用率扫描（约58%以上）等区域比较；额外对217个实例出具证书，10个bp点用独立精确求解器交叉验证。计时为编译核心对Python基线、共享服务器上的几何均值。

Result: GEO-Exact在全部21个实例上被独立对偶证书证明最优（相对原始-对偶间隙≤8.2×10⁻¹⁶）。相对该最优：ViaLegal默认SW配置平均仅高0.011%（最差0.119%）；16个争用实例上BTAssign式、DGHL、贪心分别高3.0%、6.9%、64.5%。余量出现在该区间之外：2x2窗口下SW达34.1%；利用率>~58%时，六个扫描设计中的三个分别达bp 21.1%、swerv 27.5%、jpeg 8.9%。若19–22%增益是在类似本文实例上测得，SW平均须高出最优至少24–28%，远超名义pitch下任何SW变体；RL原文与Hungarian最优比较中其SW平均高3.05%。速度上GEO-Exact较DGHL与SW分别快约8.1x和40x（几何均值）。

Conclusion: HBT合法化在名义pitch、正常争用下几乎已无位移余量：SW默认配置已接近可证最优（<0.12%），RL报告的19–22%增益难以在可比设定下复现，其对比基线可能处于异常（高争用或小窗口）工况。显著余量仅存在于2x2窗口、高利用率（>~58%）等特定区域。精确求解器GEO-Exact兼具最优性与约一个数量级以上的速度优势，适合作基准与证书工具。代码、最优值与证书均已开源。

Abstract: Hybrid bonding terminal (HBT) legalization assigns every terminal of a face-to-face 3D IC to its own pitch-grid site with minimum total Manhattan displacement. Open3DBench reports that a reinforcement-learning (RL) legalizer reduces displacement by 19-22% relative to a sliding-window (SW) heuristic, but none of its legalizers is publicly released. We ask how much displacement any legalizer can still recover. We build an open benchmark of 21 design/pitch instances from Open3DBench placement data, with reconstructed greedy, SW (ViaLegal-style) and BTAssign-style baselines, and GEO-Exact, an exact solver that runs successive shortest augmenting paths over lazily generated spatial candidate edges. An independent dual certificate proves its assignment optimal on all 21 instances (relative primal-dual gap at most $8.2 \times 10^{-16}$). Against these optima, our SW reconstruction with ViaLegal's default configuration is on average 0.011% above optimal (worst 0.119%), while on the 16 contended instances BTAssign-style, a density-guided heuristic (DGHL) and greedy are 3.0%, 6.9% and 64.5% above. Headroom does appear outside this regime: with 2x2 windows SW reaches 34.1%, and above about 58% utilization, on three of six swept designs, it reaches 21.1% on bp, 27.5% on swerv and 8.9% on jpeg (217 further certified instances, and ten bp points checked against an independent exact solver). If the published 19-22% gain were measured on instances like ours, SW would have to be at least 24-28% above optimal on average, far more than any SW variant we tested at nominal pitch; the RL paper's own comparison with the Hungarian optimum puts its SW 3.05% above optimal on average. GEO-Exact is also about an order of magnitude faster than DGHL and SW in our harness (geometric means 8.1x and 40x; a compiled core against Python baselines on a shared server). Code, optimal values and certificates are released.

</details>


### [236] [Large Language Models as Digital Twins: a Distribution-Aware Questionnaire-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16586)
*Francesco Di Cursi,Chiara Boldrini,Andrea Passarella,Marco Conti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文检验LLM能否模拟特定个体回答人格问卷（BFI-2-60），在MyPersonality数据集上评估GPT-4、Gemma、Llama 3.1与Mistral，发现用户文本并未稳定提升预测效果，模型在人格特质分布两端表现不佳，总体无法可靠复现个体问卷行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM是否具备"模拟特定个体"的人格推断能力，即依据用户社交媒体文本以该用户身份完成大五人格问卷；同时厘清预测究竟来源于用户写作信息，还是仅源于总体人群倾向与分布中心效应。

Method: 给定用户社交媒体帖子，让模型以该用户身份作答BFI-2-60，将预测作答转换为特质分数并与真实分数比较。设置三类对照：无用户文本、基于总体人群倾向的取值；按低/中/高特质分数分组测量误差；另测试仅给定目标大五分数（无文本）时模型能否表达一致的人格画像，并考察文本量增加对预测的影响。

Result: 用户文本未带来一致的性能提升；模型在特质分布中部表现较好，而在两端常回归中心值或仅在一端表现良好。给定显式画像时，GPT-4最能将特质信息转化为一致的问卷作答，尤其在外向性与神经质上；增加上下文并未系统性降低误差，开源模型波动更大，GPT-4相对稳定。

Conclusion: 所评估模型无法稳定复现特定个体的问卷行为，其预测在很大程度上受分布中心与总体倾向驱动而非个体文本信息；LLM用于个体层面人格模拟的可靠性仍不足。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether Large Language Models can answer a personality questionnaire as a specific individual would. Given a user's social-media posts, the model completes the BFI-2-60 Big Five questionnaire as that user. The predicted responses are converted into trait scores and compared with the user's actual scores. We evaluate GPT-4, Gemma, Llama 3.1, and Mistral on the MyPersonality dataset. To determine whether predictions reflect information from the user's writing, we compare them with answers produced without user text and with values derived from general population tendencies. We measure prediction error across all users and separately for individuals with low, intermediate, and high trait scores. We also test whether an LLM can express an explicit personality profile when given only the target Big Five scores, without user text, and examine how predictions change as more text is provided. The results show that user text does not consistently improve performance. Models perform better near the middle of the trait distribution, while at the lower and upper ends they often remain close to central values or perform well at one end but poorly at the other. When given an explicit profile, GPT-4 most clearly translates trait information into consistent questionnaire responses, particularly for Extraversion and Neuroticism. Increasing context does not systematically reduce error: open-source models fluctuate more, whereas GPT-4 remains comparatively stable. Overall, the evaluated models do not consistently reproduce the questionnaire behavior of specific individuals.

</details>


### [237] [Emergent Vision-Shaped Thinking in Text-Only Reasoning through Multimodal Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14942)
*Qi Zhang,Bohan Wang,Qianru Sun,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过因果消融实验证明：多模态训练会在隐藏状态空间中形成"视觉-语言轴"，纯文本推理（尤其空间想象任务）会因果性地调用该表征，作者将其命名为"涌现的视觉塑造式思维（EVT）"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：多模态训练是否不仅改变视觉输入的处理方式，还重塑模型"如何思考"，即使输入只有文本。作者希望检验纯文本推理是否会因果性依赖由视觉-语言训练引入或强化的表征，并区分"可解码性"与"因果性使用"。

Method: 跨6个模型家族、10种多模态配置做定向因果消融，并设置匹配的纯文本骨干与跨家族迁移对照。任务对比空间想象类（如BBH navigate、心理旋转、折纸、视角采择）与纯符号规则类（如boolean expressions）。在隐藏状态空间识别视觉-语言轴并在推理时消融其投影；进一步分析该轴的方向特异性、层局部化，并排除任务难度、推理深度、序列更新负载等混淆；对双路径统一模型（BAGEL、Janus-Pro）定位因果载体到生成通路；通过集中度、对齐性、语义位置三项诊断区分可解码性与因果使用。

Result: 在Chameleon-7B中，消融该轴使BBH navigate准确率下降27.6个百分点，而boolean expressions保持+5.6个百分点，形成33.2个百分点的双重解离。效应具有方向特异性和层局部化，并可推广到心理旋转、折纸、视角采择等心理模拟任务，且不能由任务难度、推理深度或顺序更新负载解释。效应在7-8B规模上空间集中，在32B配置中扩展到非空间视觉属性绑定。双路径模型中因果柄位于生成通路，呈紧凑一维载体；理解通路以更高秩、可探针解码的形式编码空间信息，但不是因果柄。

Conclusion: 多模态训练重塑的是纯文本推理的表征几何，而不仅是视觉输入的处理方式；行为、探针与干预结果可能分离，需用集中度、对齐与语义位置等诊断加以区分。

Abstract: Does multimodal training shape how a language model thinks, even when the input is text only? We test this with targeted causal ablations across 10 multimodal configurations from 6 model families, with matched text-only backbones and cross-family transfer controls. We contrast spatial-imagery tasks that invite mental scene construction with symbol-manipulation tasks solvable by symbolic rules alone. We identify a vision-language axis in hidden-state space and ablate its projection at inference. In Chameleon-7B, this ablation drops accuracy on BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) navigate by 27.6 pp while leaving boolean expressions intact (+5.6 pp), a 33.2 pp double dissociation. The effect is direction-specific and layer-localized, extends to mental-simulation tasks such as mental rotation, paper folding, and perspective taking, and is not explained by task difficulty, reasoning depth, or sequential update load. Together, these results reveal emergent vision-shaped thinking (EVT): text-only reasoning that causally recruits representations introduced or strengthened by vision-language training, without assuming an image-like internal format. The measured effect is spatially concentrated at 7-8B and broadens to non-spatial visual attribute binding in the tested 32B configuration. In dual-path unified models (BAGEL, Janus-Pro), the causal handle localizes to the generation pathway as a compact one-dimensional carrier. The understanding pathway also encodes spatial information in a higher-rank, probe-decodable form, but is not the causal handle.
